Apotheosis – Ascension to Godhood
Vol.22

Chapter 2101
They followed the gangue layer for about a thousand feet.
Suddenly, a "bang" sound came from below. Something was blocking Yu Taibai's path.
"We've reached the flint layer. Should we continue digging?" Yu Taibai asked.
The flint layer was a kind of translucent stone crystal. This flint layer was a hundred times stronger than the gangue layer!
Of course, no matter how strong this flint layer was, it couldn't stop Yu Taibai.
"Wait a minute, the direction seems wrong," Luo Zheng said with a frown.
Last time, when he went underground, his inner world immediately reacted.
That brown lump of mud, which was the spirit crow, had a strong attraction to Luo Zheng.
But this time, after going so deep, Luo Zheng's inner world still didn't react. This meant that they had deviated from the right direction!
Speaking of which, Luo Zheng had clearly dug in front of the Tie Family's house and escaped from the top of the cave. In the end, it led to the pond in the Tie Family's backyard.
At that time, Luo Zheng had concluded that the underground cave could be moved freely.
But his mother had told him to go to the Liuren Domain to find her, so there shouldn't be a problem here.
"Go in that direction," Luo Zheng could only rely on his intuition now.
Yu Taibai nodded. He didn't take Luo Zheng to the flint layer. Instead, he moved horizontally through the gangue layer.
But after moving horizontally for a considerable distance, Luo Zheng's inner world still didn't react. In the end, they had no choice but to stop …
"Did Master's wife leave this place?" Yu Taibai said with a frown.
Luo Zheng shook his head, "Mother said she would wait for me here. She wouldn't leave like this."
"But don't forget, the Master's wife you saw in the Dao Struggle wasn't the real Master's wife," Yu Taibai reminded.
The Li Luoshui in the Dao Struggle was only a Fear Incarnation, a copy.
In other words, the real Li Luoshui didn't know that Luo Zheng was coming to find her. Moreover, she didn't have any memories of the Dao Struggle.
If Li Luoshui chose to leave this place before that, how could Luo Zheng find her?
"Right …" Luo Zheng's eyebrows jumped.
In other words, the possibility of him finding Li Luoshui here was not high.
"That's not right." Luo Zheng smiled. "Even if the mother in the Battle of Dao is the embodiment of fear, she still has complete memories. Since she gave me this suggestion, it means that she has sufficient reason to believe that her original body will not leave this place!"
"That makes sense." Yu Taibai shrugged. "Then let's … continue down!"
After saying that, Yu Taibai took two steps back. A desolate power like a mountain erupted from his body. He clenched his fists and punched towards the Flint Layer below.
"Boom!"
Cracking noises immediately came from the solid layer of flint under his feet, as if beans were being fried.
After a short while, Luo Zheng saw the layer of flint under his feet turning into semi-transparent ashes and rapidly collapsing downwards like quicksand...
"What a powerful force!"
Luo Zheng was also secretly shocked.
Even if he relied on the source of power, Luo Zheng's power was not on the same level as Yu Taibai's.
"Follow me." Yu Taibai nodded towards Luo Zheng and jumped into the tunnel he had created.
Without any hesitation, Luo Zheng followed suit and jumped in as well.
The thickness of the flint layer was much thicker than the gangue layer. After falling for five thousand feet, the two of them finally stopped in a narrow space.
As soon as the two of them stopped, they heard muffled noises coming from the wall made of flint on one side of the space, as if something was trying to crawl out of it!
"What's that?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
They had been drilling underground for so long without encountering anything. Now that they could finally hear something, Luo Zheng was naturally looking forward to it.
Yu Taibai stretched out a finger and gently drilled it into the side of the cave. He suddenly knocked with one hand!
"Bang!"
The cave wall suddenly collapsed.
At the same time, Luo Zheng and Yu Taibai heard a roar.
From behind the cave wall, a huge snake head suddenly appeared!
This snake head opened its bloody maw and bit towards Yu Taibai and Luo Zheng, wanting to swallow them whole.
"Hmph." Yu Taibai's expression didn't change in the slightest as he looked at the snake head. Multicolored light flowed out of the veins in his right arm. That was the color of the source of his strength.
"Boom!"
Yu Taibai's casual punch seemed to have the power to shatter everything.
Before the huge snake head could close its mouth, it was turned into powder by the impact of the punch.
And this punch was extremely far away. Luo Zheng could not clearly see the situation in the distance. Presumably, the entire snake had been turned to dust by the power of this punch.
"An Earth Dragon actually dares to sneak attack. But this Earth Dragon is skilled in digging underground. We can follow the hole it made and search for it," Yu Taibai said.
This Earth Dragon's body was two zhang in diameter. The hole in this layer of flint was naturally also two zhang wide.
Luo Zheng and Yu Taibai entered the tunnel created by the Earth Dragon and continued to move forward, but they still didn't find any clues …
They continued to follow the Black Sovereign Dragon's hole. They did not know how far they had traveled.
Luo Zheng suddenly felt that the ground under his feet had suddenly become soft...
"We've reached the Muscle Layer." Yu Taibai stepped on the ground.
Below the Rock Layer was the Flint Layer. Below the Flint Layer was the Muscle Layer.
This Muscle Layer was not as hard as Flint Layer, but it was extremely flexible. Brute force alone could not break it.
Even Yu Taibai could not casually tear apart the Muscle Layer!
Because there were so many obstacles, the depths of the Divine Realm were not so easy to explore. This was also why Li Luoshui had chosen to hide underground.
"We should have reached a very deep place?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Naturally." Yu Taibai said. "If we break through the Muscle Layer and continue down, we will encounter the Black Flame Torrent. That green lava can kill Saints."
"It's that powerful?" Luo Zheng asked in astonishment.
Yu Taibai nodded. "That is the purest energy in the Divine Realm. It is also the foundation that keeps the Divine Realm from collapsing. This is not strange. We can't continue down. Let's find another place to search."
Luo Zheng's face showed reluctance. The underground world was so large. Finding that hole was like finding a needle in a haystack. But he still listened to Yu Taibai's arrangements.
But just as Luo Zheng was about to leave with Yu Taibai, he suddenly felt a trace of movement in his inner world!
Luo Zheng suddenly stopped in his tracks and looked around.

Chapter 2102
"What's wrong?" Yu Taibai asked.
"Over there!"
Luo Zheng pointed. In front of him was an ash-gray rock. After saying this, Luo Zheng went straight for the rock.
When Luo Zheng got close, his inner world suddenly transmitted waves of intense hunger, as if the most delicious food was hidden behind the rock.
"It's here. I can feel it. The 'mud' is behind it." Luo Zheng was very certain.
Yu Taibai nodded.
Even if he wanted to break through the rock, it would not be easy.
The muscles all over his body began to swell. His shoulders had doubled in size in the time it took to breathe. His two thick arms were disproportionate to his entire body.
These rock could not be broken through with brute force.
"Crack!"
His ten fingers abruptly pierced into the stone. Then, he spread his arms to both sides and actually tore open a crack in the stone!
After tearing open the rock, Luo Zheng immediately saw a wide space through the crack!
With the help of the dim light, Luo Zheng could even see the round stone plate!
"It's that cave! This cave can really move underground! "
Luo Zheng's face revealed an excited expression. He was about to step in, but Yu Taibai stopped Luo Zheng.
"I'll go in first." Yu Taibai said indifferently.
Going deep underground, there might be some strange things. Yu Taibai naturally had to consider the issue of safety.
After saying this, Yu Taibai climbed into the crack, and Luo Zheng followed closely behind.
After Luo Zheng entered the cave, he saw a brown thing running not far away. It seemed like it was about to enter the crack next to the cave.
"Don't let that thing escape!" Luo Zheng anxiously said.
That brown thing was the "mud" Luo Zheng needed. It was also the "spirit crow" that his mother spoke of.
"It won't!"
Yu Taibai replied indifferently. His large hand made a grabbing motion in the air.
The crazily running "spirit crow" stopped in its tracks. The "spirit crow" continuously let out "zhi zhi zhi" sounds, but it was caught by an invisible force. No matter how it struggled, it could not escape this invisible force.
Yu Taibai suddenly pulled the spirit crow into his bosom. The spirit crow flew back and fell in front of Luo Zheng and Yu Taibai.
At the same time …
On the walls of the cave, strange figures with long arms and legs appeared once again!
"This cave is a trap." After seeing these figures, Yu Taibai's face became vigilant.
Last time, Luo Zheng also encountered this scene, but he ran away before these strange figures came out of the rock …
Yu Taibai's words made a lot of sense. This cave was probably a trap set up by his mother. If someone came in, they would definitely fall into this cave. They would either have to escape or stay behind to fight against the strange figures in the rock!
This time, Luo Zheng was prepared. Adding on the fact that Yu Taibai was beside him, he naturally did not panic.
He reached out and grabbed the "spirit crow". His fingers followed the "mud" on the spirit crow. Very quickly, Luo Zheng felt a pearl and pulled it out!
Then Luo Zheng said to the pearl: "Mother, it's me. I'm Luo Zheng!"
These spirit crows were all under Li Luoshui's control. These pearls were the core of the spirit crows. Last time, Luo Zheng was able to see his mother through these pearls!
"We need to hurry." Yu Taibai's gaze focused on the walls of the cave.
The figures on the walls were becoming clearer and clearer. The aura they emitted was especially strong. Moreover, they emitted a strong hostility …
In another space.
Li Luoshui's head suddenly opened her eyes.
When she saw Luo Zheng again, her crimson eyes flashed. "It's him. He's back!"
Last time, she had seen her son through the spirit crow, but before she could finish her sentence, the pearl had exploded …
Li Luoshui did not think that Luo Zheng would return to this cave!
Just as Li Luoshui was about to respond, she felt a very familiar thing between Luo Zheng's eyebrows. It was a memory she had left behind!
"When did I leave a memory between his eyebrows?" Li Luoshui was puzzled. But as the crimson light flashed in her eyes, the silver light between Luo Zheng's eyebrows instantly entered the spirit crow's eyeball and entered Li Luoshui's mind.
She obtained the memory of her fear incarnation.
After she fused with the memory, she suddenly realized, "So it was me who told him to come. Moreover, he has already obtained the inheritance of the Land of Dao Struggle!"
"Hualala …"
Li Luoshui's head slowly floated up from the cauldron. The spirit crows quickly transformed into her body.
"All of you stop!"
After she gave the order, the huge figures in the cave immediately stopped moving forward and solidified in the rock.
"Success. Mother, I already know." Luo Zheng stared at the eyeball and said.
Although he did not see Li Luoshui, the memory Li Luoshui planted in his mind had already been absorbed.
Yu Taibai nodded, "That's good!"
Actually, Yu Taibai was still worried about the Takeshi Xuan forest.
Although he believed in Jing Wuhuan and Yu Shenfeng's ability, it was easy for accidents to happen in the forbidden area. He hoped that he could quickly send Luo Zheng to his master's wife and return to the Takeshi Xuan forest.
The two of them waited in the cave for an incense stick's time.
"Kaka …"
Suddenly, an explosion came from the left side of the cave.
There was a crack on the left side of the rock, like a monster with its mouth wide open.
"Hua!"
Immediately after, a figure with long hair flashed by and stood firmly in front of Luo Zheng.
It was Luo Zheng's mother, Li Luoshui, who he saw in the Dao Battle.
However, he only saw his mother's incarnation in the Dao Battle. The one in front of him was his mother's real body.
"Master's wife!"
Yu Taibai respectfully bowed.
Li Luoshui glanced at Yu Taibai, "You've worked hard to personally send Zheng 'er here!"
"It's my duty," Yu Taibai replied.
Li Luoshui nodded and looked at Luo Zheng.
She had absorbed all the memories of her incarnation, but for Li Luoshui herself, this was the first time she had truly met her son.
Li Luoshui tiptoed and reached out to touch Luo Zheng's face. An enchanting smile appeared on her face, "Zheng 'er, please come with me!"
"Yes, mother!" Luo Zheng nodded.
Before leaving with Li Luoshui, Luo Zheng naturally would not forget to activate the Gen Writ.

Chapter 2103
Luo Zheng walked quietly behind Li Luoshui, his eyes looking up and down the cave.
The cave was filled with a faint smell of blood, and there were brown "mud" running around in all directions.
In addition, Luo Zheng also saw huge heads embedded in the rocks.
These heads had their eyes closed as if they were sleeping.
"Mother, what are these creatures? How can they pass through the rocks? "Luo Zheng curiously asked.
After meeting Li Luoshui in the Battle of the Dao, Luo Zheng no longer felt estranged. Luo Zheng felt a sense of closeness and stability beside his mother.
"These Rock Spirits were planted by mother one by one. They are mother's guards," Li Luoshui replied with a smile.
"Planted?" Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
"Yes, they are the favor of the Ancient Gods and possess extremely powerful strength. Even Yu Taibai might not necessarily be able to deal with them," Li Luoshui said.
"No wonder," Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. These Rock Spirits had always given Luo Zheng a lot of pressure. As his mother said, they had the ability to contend against Yu Taibai. That was an existence comparable to a Saint.
The underground caves were connected to one another. Every time Li Luoshui led Luo Zheng to the end of a cave, a new cave would appear. This underground world was far more complex than Luo Zheng had imagined.
On the way, Luo Zheng didn't know how many Spirit Crows he had seen. His inner world was like a restless monster, urging him to devour those Spirit Crows. However, Luo Zheng restrained himself.
Not long after, Luo Zheng passed through another cave and saw a large pot not far away.
Through the eyes of the Spirit Crows, Luo Zheng had seen this pot. At that time, only his mother's head was immersed in this pot.
Below this pot, there was a light green flame slowly wriggling.
"Sit down," Li Luoshui gently pointed.
Luo Zheng nodded and sat cross-legged on the ground beside the pot.
Li Luoshui immediately sat in front of Luo Zheng and looked at Luo Zheng indifferently with a look of joy on her face.
She had directly fused with the memories planted by her Fear Incarnation, but for her main body, this was the first time she had seen Luo Zheng. Naturally, she had a different feeling in her heart.
After a while, Li Luoshui said, "Your father is indeed brave and resourceful. He dared to gamble, and he gambled correctly. If you had followed my suggestion back then, I'm afraid you wouldn't have been able to open the Thousand Fan Domain."
Looking at Luo Zheng's puzzled expression, Li Luoshui continued, "At that time, Mother didn't want to hand you and your sister over to Husband. You two have extremely pure bloodlines and are destined to be extraordinary. However, in the divine territory, the meaning of being extraordinary only stops at the level of a Saint. But now, you want to surpass this limit."
Li Luoshui's words made Luo Zheng a little confused. This time, he wanted to break through his cultivation and seek his mother's help. However, after so long, Luo Zheng still didn't understand. He asked, "But it's precisely because I cultivate the Primal Chaos Secret Technique that I've been stuck at the realm of a lower-level True God. Not to mention a Saint, I'm far inferior to a Perfected True God …"
Li Luoshui smiled and shook her head. She interrupted Luo Zheng and said, "Vassals are ultimately vassals. The path of relying on others will eventually come to an end. Only by opening up your own path can you go further and have infinite possibilities!"
"Vassals? Mother, are you saying that the Saints are vassals? "Luo Zheng asked.
Li Luoshui smiled faintly. "Yes. Not only the Saints, but all the living beings in the divine territory who cultivate vital essence are vassals. The living beings in the Saints' Inner Worlds are the vassals of the vassals. Even if they pass the Saint Mountain and become a Saint, they still need to attach the universe to the divine territory."
Luo Zheng nodded. Even if the Saints were very powerful, they couldn't leave the divine territory.
"But my Inner World is still unable to expand. My cultivation has been stagnant." Luo Zheng voiced his worries.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, Li Luoshui gently waved her hand. Suddenly, there was a clattering sound from the surroundings. A large number of Spirit Crows immediately crawled over.
"What you lack are these Spirit Crows, right?" Li Luoshui asked.
Luo Zheng looked at the large number of Spirit Crows. From the instincts transmitted from his Inner World, he couldn't help but swallow his saliva. Luo Zheng nodded.
"Haha." Li Luoshui smiled helplessly. "The Primal Chaos Secret Technique that your father gave you isn't complete. This incomplete cultivation method naturally can't tell you how to obtain Spirit Crows. In fact, you already have them in your Inner World!"
"They're in my Inner World?" Luo Zheng was a little dumbfounded.
Li Luoshui's slender fingers spread open. In her palm, there was a dark brown Shenwu coin!
"Shenwu coin?" Luo Zheng stared at his mother in bewilderment.
He only saw his mother lightly toss the Shenwu coin. The Spirit Crows immediately pounced on it like a pack of wolves and began to fight for the Shenwu coin.
The fighting was very intense. Soon, a Spirit Crow stood out and swallowed the Shenwu coin into its body!
"These Spirit Crows are fed with Shenwu coins?" Luo Zheng asked in surprise.
Li Luoshui shook her head. "No, it should be said that these Spirit Crows were transformed from Shenwu coins!"
At the beginning of the Divine Realm, the Li Clan, which was also the Sacred Clan, occupied the entire Divine Realm. At that time, Shenwu coins had two uses. One was to cultivate Spirit Crows for cultivation. The other was to write down the rules of the Divine Realm.
These were the generous conditions that Chi You created for his people.
However, those who cultivated with Spirit Crows were naturally not people of the Divine Realm. They were "beings of a higher level" who had established the Sanskrit Domain. Li Luoshui was one of them.
Later on, the people were almost completely wiped out. The True Gods of the Divine Realm were unable to enter the realm of "beings of a higher level". The Shenwu coins could only be used to change the rules on the Governance Slate.
Hearing his mother's explanation, Luo Zheng revealed a bitter smile. He had been painstakingly searching for a way to break through in the end. He didn't expect that it was because the Primal Chaos Secret Technique was incomplete.
Simply put, the Primal Chaos Secret Technique only recorded the method to establish a small Divine Realm. It didn't record how to expand it or how to cultivate it. It was completely dependent on Luo Zheng's luck to get to where he was now.
However, at the stage of condensing Godhood, Luo Zheng was unable to take another step forward. That was until he found this mud …
"You've established the Qianfan Domain for a long time. Now, Mother can help you break through your bottleneck. However, you also need to master the method to cultivate Spirit Crows." Li Luoshui said. "Zheng 'er, prepare yourself!"
Luo Zheng nodded.

Chapter 2104
Luo Zheng had been looking forward to this for a long time, especially the world inside his body, which emitted a strong desire, teasing Luo Zheng all the time.
He didn't resist at all, and a gentle light emitted from his dantian, completely accepting all the spirit crows.
And Li Luo Shui didn't seem to consider Luo Zheng's ability to bear it, or rather, she didn't need to consider it at all!
"Can I enter your inner world to take a look?" Li Luo Shui asked.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, then nodded.
Soon, in Luo Zheng's inner world, the figure formed by Li Luo Shui's consciousness was already floating inside.
And Luo Zheng's inner incarnation stood side by side with her.
"Hu, hu, hu …"
Those spirit crows continuously fell from the sky, simultaneously emitting "zhi zhi" sounds.
The spirit crows' intelligence was very low, and they were completely under Li Luo Shui's control. Although they clearly knew that they would be devoured, they still charged into Luo Zheng's inner world without hesitation!
"Nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier … this power source is actually so abundant." Li Luo Shui glanced indifferently at the sky, and a hint of surprise appeared on her face. "This should be the nine stars that some saint helped you forge, right?"
"En, it's the power source bestowed by Saint Jiu," Luo Zheng answered.
Li Luo Shui revealed a smile. "The three of them are really whimsical, actually directly stuffing the power source to you …"
In Li Luo Shui's eyes, to forge Luo Zheng's inner world into such a state was practically reckless!
She was only halfway through her words when she looked into the distance, and saw several dozen rays of light blossoming in midair …
"Those are … divine dao accumulations?" Li Luo Shui's gaze focused, and then shot towards those rays of light.
Luo Zheng didn't say anything, only following behind his mother.
When Li Luo Shui arrived above Luo Zheng's condensed godhood, she saw over three hundred rays of light emitted from Luo Zheng's godhood.
These rays of light each contained different divine dao accumulations, distributed in various parts of Luo Zheng's inner world.
"This is a multi-layered godhood?" Li Luo Shui's eyes revealed a strange look. "At least three hundred layers of godhood, how is this possible? Right … Boundless Ruler! I know what they are trying to do! "
In Luo Zheng's eyes, his mother was a "leapfrog creature", so she must understand things at a higher level than him.
Why did she start to panic after seeing all these …
"Mother, what's wrong?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Li Luoshui looked at Luo Zheng's godhead, and her face was still full of doubt, "How can you absorb so much Divine Dao? This … is simply impossible! "
The so-called Three Thousand Divine Daos in the Divine Realm were the Three Thousand Divine Abilities that Chi You grasped.
These 3,000 Divine Daos were not prepared for the True Gods and Saints of the Divine Realm, but for the common people.
It wasn't particularly difficult for some True Gods to cultivate one or two types of Divine Daos. However, some extremely talented True Gods could cultivate three to five types of Divine Daos. It was even possible for them to fuse two or three types of Divine Daos together and create something new. That would be their own Dao.
Li Luoshui had never heard of someone like Luo Zheng who had absorbed hundreds of Divine Daos.
"Father gave me a ruler. It's called the Infinite Ruler," Luo Zheng answered truthfully.
"Infinite Ruler!"
When Li Luoshui heard Luo Zheng's words, her body suddenly trembled. She stared fixedly at Luo Zheng, "How can you have the Infinite Ruler? How can the Infinite Ruler appear here? It's impossible for that ruler to appear in the Divine Realm! "
Li Luoshui's eyes were full of disbelief.
"But before I ascended to the Divine Realm, father did give me the Infinite Ruler," Luo Zheng answered truthfully.
Li Luoshui stared at the godhead and murmured, "So that's how it is. No wonder husband is so confident, so confident … Although he doesn't understand many things, he understands the simplest point. He obtained the Infinite Ruler and let you absorb three thousand godheads to obtain the general outline of the Three Thousand Divine Daos. This way, your inner world will be highly compatible with the Divine Realm. He is helping you seek the right to control the Divine Realm! This is his ambition! "
The Divine Realm did not have a ruler, because the ruler was Chi You himself.
The rulers of large and small Forbidden Zones only had a certain degree of authority. Their abilities might be formidable in a certain aspect, but it was not enough to control the entire Divine Realm.
If someone obtained the general outline of the Three Thousand Divine Daos, the inner world they created would be highly compatible with the Divine Realm. This way, they would be able to seize control of the Divine Realm.
But cultivating Chi You's Three Thousand Divine Daos was easier said than done.
No matter how high one's talent was, it was simply impossible to accomplish …
So Li Luoshui had never thought in this direction.
But Li Luoshui never imagined that the Infinite Ruler would fall into Luo Xiao's hands. Where did he obtain it?
"Seeking to control the Divine Realm!" Luo Zheng was also shocked. He never thought that his father would be planning this. The problem was that even his mother didn't know about the Infinite Ruler, so where did Luo Xiao obtain it?
It wasn't difficult to use the Infinite Ruler to contain the Three Thousand Divine Daos. As long as Luo Zheng was given enough time, he would sooner or later be able to integrate the Three Thousand Divine Daos into it.
Now, he already had one-tenth of it. If he continued to comprehend those Dao tablets under the guidance of some almighty person, it wouldn't be long before he could achieve this …
If he really controlled the entire Divine Realm, then Luo Zheng would be an omniscient and omnipotent existence on this ship. Not to mention those Sages, even those Xuan Yuan Guards, what qualifications did they have to resist him?
"Humph, my husband must have some secrets that I don't know about." A trace of annoyance appeared on Li Luoshui's face.
When she was with Luo Xiao, she had told him everything she knew. She didn't think that her husband would still hide things from her. Naturally, Li Luoshui felt a trace of displeasure in her heart.
"Perhaps Father only obtained the Immeasurable Ruler after that?" Luo Zheng spoke up for Luo Xiao.
Li Luoshui smiled faintly. She was undoubtedly angry, but she was still happy that her husband and Zheng 'er could reach this step. So she smiled faintly and said, "The truth will come out one day! Since Zheng 'er has already expanded the inner world to this step, then quickly absorb these spirit crows! "
Under Li Luoshui's guidance, Luo Zheng didn't absorb all of the spirit crows.
More than ninety percent of the spirit crows fell into the sea of chaos, causing the sea to begin to boil.
As for the remaining spirit crows, Luo Zheng let them fall to the ground.
These spirit crows were like rats, rapidly digging holes and burrowing into the ground to hide.
If he wanted to cultivate more spirit crows, he only needed to use shenwu coins to feed them. As long as he gave enough shenwu coins, the number of spirit crows in his inner world would increase.
The forbidden area in Luo Zheng's inner world was also endlessly producing shenwu coins.
However, previously, Luo Zheng didn't understand the use of shenwu coins. Now, with the help of these spirit crows constantly splitting and multiplying, he could easily obtain an almost unlimited number of spirit crows.

Chapter 2105
Previously, Luo Zheng had spent a lot of effort to get a complete spirit crow.
Now, looking at the surging clouds and mist, he was quite shocked …
"Sword cloud, condense!"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly, and a faint sword shadow suddenly appeared in the surging clouds and mist.
The sword shadow split into two, two into four, four into eight …
As it rapidly spread, it suddenly transformed into countless sword shadows. The sword shadows constantly collided with each other, releasing crisp sounds of metal clashing as they constantly collided in Luo Zheng's inner world.
"Zheng 'er's main cultivation is the Emotion Severing Divine Dao …"
Li Luoshui seemed to have thought of something, and a strange luster appeared in her eyes. "I understand … Saint Jiu must have unearthed father's inheritance in the Saint Mountain. The Immeasurable Ruler really came from the Saint Mountain!"
Saint Jiu hadn't been a Saint for a long time, so Li Luoshui didn't know what happened after that.
However, after seeing Luo Zheng cultivate the Emotion Severing Divine Dao and use the Immeasurable Ruler to absorb the 3,000 Divine Daos, she was almost certain that it must have come from her father's inheritance.
And the only possibility for them to obtain this inheritance was the Saint Mountain.
The General Outline of the 3,000 Divine Daos was one of her father's most powerful divine abilities, but it couldn't be used after comprehending the 3,000 Divine Daos. It had to be driven by the Emotion Severing Divine Dao!
Therefore, the other 2,999 divine abilities were all auxiliary, and the most crucial one was the Emotion Severing Divine Dao.
Luo Zheng now had the Immeasurable Ruler and majored in the Emotion Severing Divine Dao. This couldn't be a coincidence. It was very likely that when they obtained the Immeasurable Ruler, they also received guidance …
Li Luoshui sorted out this series of conjectures and felt that her reasoning was very correct!
In the inner world, thousands of creatures raised their heads to look at the dense sword cloud.
The Evil Deities were the same, and so were the various geniuses on the continent …
Although they didn't know how these sword clouds were formed, they knew that this abnormality would certainly bring about great changes to this world.
"Huff, huff, huff …"
All the brown sword clouds gathered in the center of the inner world, surrounding Luo Zheng's godhood, and gradually formed a huge vortex.
This vortex spiraled at high speed and quickly converged in the godhood.
Luo Zheng's godhead expanded at a visible speed, and then expanded again …
The surface of the crystal shard shone with dazzling light.
It wasn't just the main crystal of the Severing Emotions Divine Dao that was expanding. The other colors of godhoods that were attached to the main crystal also began to expand proportionally!
At the same time, Luo Zheng also felt dizzy. In an instant, he seemed to have comprehended hundreds of Divine Abilities!
As it turned out, the Immeasurable Ruler was able to completely absorb the Divine Dao's Dao Essence.
For example, Luo Zheng used the Infinite Ruler to absorb Aunty Jiu's Purple Cloud Divine Dao. However, he was unable to use the complete Purple Cloud Divine Dao. He could only use the most basic Split Thought Control Technique.
The reason was because of the limitation of cultivation.
The first three stages of the divine dao didn't have any restrictions on godhood, but the next three stages of divine arts had requirements on godhood.
Although Luo Zheng had absorbed the supernatural powers, he was only a lower True Divinity so he wasn't able to use the last three levels of supernatural powers. But now that his cultivation had broken through, he had broken through this shackle and instantly comprehended several hundred supernatural powers. Within the Realm of the Gods, he was likely a unique existence!
"I broke through," Luo Zheng's eyes shone.

The size of this new continent was about three times larger than the total size of the previous seven continents combined!
The Chaotic Sea was a place that gave birth to living beings, but these continents were where living beings relied on to survive and prosper. Soon, an even more glorious kingdom would be born on these continents, and there would be even more geniuses and experts …

Chapter 2106

. The other True Gods in the alchemy workshop were dumbfounded. No one dared to move.

"If it wasn't for you back then, perhaps my husband would have succeeded …" Li Luoshui, who only had her head left, stared at Dongfang Chunjun with hatred. "Today, I will completely destroy you!"
The mud kept wriggling around Dongfang Chunjun, gathering toward his dantian …
In the eyes of the True Gods in the Divine Realm, Saints were indeed immortal and indestructible, unless their universe was destroyed.
However, that didn't mean Li Luoshui didn't have a way to kill Saints.
For example, cutting off the connection between Saints and their universe …
"You!"

Chapter 2110
Luo Zheng did not know what was happening in the cave.
He sat cross-legged in that stable space and continued to focus on breaking through to the upper true god realm.
In his inner world …
Sword clouds surged and swarmed toward his godhood.
Although there were no more spirit crows entering his inner world, the number of brown clouds formed by the spirit crows was enough!
"It seems that Zheng 'er should be able to smoothly advance to upper true god," Li Luoshui said calmly.
Li Luoshui's divine clone naturally knew what was happening in the cave. However, in order not to disturb Luo Zheng, she did not reveal anything.
Luo Zheng focused all his attention on that godhood!
However, breaking through to upper true god took a long time.
Two hours later …
As the inner world rumbled once again, the entire world once again expanded outward.
"It's done!" Luo Zheng was pleasantly surprised.
When the inner world expanded again, he felt that his understanding of the various Divine Daos had deepened by a lot!
"Mother!"
Luo Zheng's incarnation floated in front of Li Luoshui.
"Congratulations, Zheng 'er." Li Luoshui had a smile on her face, but the expression in her eyes became unusually complicated.
Luo Zheng sensed his mother's strange mood and asked curiously, "Mother … what's wrong?"
The smile on Li Luoshui's face gradually faded. Then, she said calmly, "Mother will tell you, but don't panic. After all … this is not a dead end for you."
Luo Zheng nodded. He still did not understand.
"I sent you to the chaos outside the divine territory," Li Luoshui said straightforwardly.
"What!"
Luo Zheng was startled, and his eyes snapped open in shock.
He realized that he was no longer in the cave. Instead, he was enveloped by a purple circle of light!
He was full of doubts. He closed his eyes again and focused his consciousness on his incarnation. He stared at Li Luoshui in his inner world and asked, "What happened?"
"When you were breaking through to upper true god, those Saints found me," Li Luoshui said bitterly.
Li Luoshui was afraid of affecting Luo Zheng, so she did not say anything.
But now, in order to let Luo Zheng understand her situation, she had to explain as much as possible …
"What!" Luo Zheng was shocked. "Which Saints?"
"Dongfang Chunjun and the others," Li Luoshui replied.
This was probably the name Luo Zheng didn't want to hear …
Li Luoshui continued, "I have no choice but to send you out of the Divine Domain. In the Primal Chaos, you will continue to fall. You might encounter the Extraterrestrial Demons, but you will still have a chance of survival. If you fall into their hands, you will undoubtedly die."
Her choice was very decisive.
"How could this be, Mother … What about you?" Luo Zheng's face was full of anxiety.
Li Luoshui didn't answer the question. She just said faintly, "Mother is counting on Zheng 'er to avenge me!"
Luo Zheng kept shaking his head. He couldn't believe what Li Luoshui said. Did mother mean that she was going to die?
His face was filled with panic. "Impossible! We haven't been able to find it after so many years. Why did it have to be this time? Impossible! "
If it was because he found Li Luoshui and caused Li Luoshui to expose her position, how could he forgive himself?
At this moment, Luo Zheng's mind was in a mess. He couldn't maintain his usual calmness!
Li Luoshui's avatar floated slightly. She held Luo Zheng's cheeks with both hands and stared at Luo Zheng with her beautiful eyes. "Zheng 'er, you are our last hope! Don't let Mother down! "
She and Luo Zheng had only been together for less than a day. It was too ironic that they were going to be separated by life and death.
Luo Zheng looked at Li Luoshui motionlessly. He only heard her talking non-stop. She was talking about the method to cultivate the spirit crow, telling him about the origin of the Extraterrestrial Demons …
His mother kept talking, but he didn't listen.
After a long time, Li Luoshui's avatar gradually became thinner and finally dissipated in front of Luo Zheng's eyes.
Luo Zheng maintained the same posture and stared blankly ahead.
After maintaining this posture for half an hour, he suddenly shuddered and dispersed the incarnation in his body. He quickly opened his eyes and took out the "Gen" token!
Regardless of whether Luo Zheng was in the universe or in the divine realm, as long as he activated the "Gen" token, he could return to the immortal abode …
He wanted to find Yu Taibai. Maybe he had a way. He wanted to go underground and save his mother again!
But this time, the "Gen" token was ineffective.
Luo Zheng kept trying to activate this token, but it was like an ordinary iron token now. There was no reaction.
After trying countless times, Luo Zheng was filled with anger. He threw the "Gen" token out.
"Dong dong dong dong …"
This sturdy token was knocked around in the narrow space.
The "Gen" token was used for the entire divine realm, including the universe. With Gu Bei's ability, he could take care of everything.
"Could it be that I'm really in the primal chaos?" Luo Zheng thought blankly.
In this space, Luo Zheng couldn't see the scenery outside. He couldn't even feel that he was falling. He could only sit cross-legged and stare blankly.
Although he'd become an upper true god and the size of his internal world had greatly increased, he wasn't in the mood to investigate …
He sat like this for ten days, but the space binding him still didn't change.
But at this moment, he discovered that a crack had appeared in the layer of space binding him!
"Whoosh!"
The moment this crack appeared, a thick brown fog surged in.
"Primal chaos aura?"
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Ordinary true gods and even perfected true gods would view primal chaos aura as highly toxic, but it wasn't a threat to Luo Zheng. After all, his internal world was made up of primal chaos aura.
His mother hadn't lied to him. She really had sent him out of the divine realm …
Thinking about it carefully, his mother hadn't made the wrong choice at the last moment. With the means of the Saints, Dongfang Chunjun would be able to track him down no matter where she sent him to in the divine realm.
Back when he left the Sea of Time and was taken away by Aunt Hanjiu, Dongfang Chunjun couldn't track him down because of Little Qiao's forbidden magic.
Even so, Aunt Hanjiu had taken Luo Zheng countless times in a short period of time to prevent the possibility of him catching up …
So his mother could only send him out of the divine realm. This was almost the only choice!
"Mother might not have died …" Luo Zheng's eyes shone. "Maybe everything can still turn around, but the prerequisite is that I have enough strength!"
After being depressed for ten days, Luo Zheng finally adjusted his mood.
The crack in the space binding Luo Zheng grew bigger and bigger. Luo Zheng could also observe the situation outside through this crack.
Through this gap, Luo Zheng could see a huge oval-shaped gem floating in the air.
He recognized it at a glance. It was the main body of the divine realm. Luo Zheng could even see the red band of light binding the gem!

Chapter 2111
Rapidly descending through the void, the beast bones at the bottom of the primal chaos grew larger and larger in Luo Zheng's eyes.
Cracks continuously appeared in the purple halo that enveloped Luo Zheng, before finally disappearing completely. Luo Zheng seemed to be sitting on a lidless box as he fell.
"At this speed, I'm afraid I'll need a long time to reach the bottom …"
The divine realm was already very close to the bottom of the primal chaos, but using the divine realm as a scale, Luo Zheng was still very far away.
One month later.
Luo Zheng's height was finally level with the beast bones.
But these massive beast bones were also incomparably tall!
Another ten days passed …
When he was about thirty thousand feet from the ground, Luo Zheng leapt out. Flying through the air, he began to slowly descend.
In this vast and enormous world, Luo Zheng was like a speck of dust that had silently descended. He didn't attract any attention.
"This is the bottom of the primal chaos world!"
The ground beneath Luo Zheng's feet was like a vast expanse of snow. As far as the eye could see, it was a vast expanse of pure white.
But this expanse of white was not snow, but bones. This was a wasteland of bones!
There were all sorts of white bones. There were arm bones, rib bones, plate bones, and all sorts of strangely shaped bones.
Some of the bones were only the size of a palm, while others were tens of thousands or even tens of thousands of feet long.
Of course, no matter how large they were, they couldn't compare to those massive beast bones. Those beast bones were comparable to the length of the divine realm. They were like unclimbable mountains in the distance.
"These bones should have come from creatures above their level." Luo Zheng bent down and picked up a bone fragment.
If a True God's body had entered the primal chaos, it would have been completely corroded by the primal chaos energy. It would be impossible for these bones to remain …
These bones had fallen at the bottom of the primal chaos. They had probably been there for countless years, but they were still completely undamaged despite being covered by the primal chaos energy. Perhaps only creatures above their level could do this.
But how could there be so many creatures above their level that had died here?
Luo Zheng's gaze once again fell on the massive beast bones soaring into the sky. From this angle, he could still see the red beam of light.
Yu Taibai said that the red beam of light belonged to the "Otherworldly Demons", but he didn't know what kind of race they were.
While thinking, Luo Zheng suddenly heard a rustling sound around him.
He turned his head and saw seven or eight half-clothed humanoid creatures nimbly shuttling through the skeletons.
"Human?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
From their appearance, they did look like humans, but they were actually crawling on the ground with their hands and feet. Their hollow eyes revealed a greedy and ferocious glint. Occasionally, they would open their mouths and reveal a mouthful of sharp white teeth.
"Sou!"
"Huff huff …"
One of them, with the help of a large piece of bone, leaped up like a nimble monkey and pounced towards Luo Zheng!
Facing this sneak attack, Luo Zheng's expression froze. His five fingers instantly clasped onto this person's head and gently twisted …
"Kacha, kacha, kacha …"
Luo Zheng twisted him like a fried dough twist.
"It's not a human, just a beast that looks like a human …"
As Luo Zheng spoke, he threw the twisted corpse to the ground. If it was a human with intelligence, they wouldn't dare to recklessly pounce on him. At the very least, they would consider the difference in strength between them in advance.
When the others saw this scene, the ferocious glint in their eyes disappeared without a trace. They used their hands and feet to flee in all directions.
Luo Zheng didn't chase after them. Slaughtering these unintelligent creatures was meaningless.
"The red beam of light at the top of that beast bone twines around the entire Divine Realm. Since I've fallen to the bottom of the Primal Chaos, I can go and investigate!" After making this decision, Luo Zheng flew forward.
Just as Luo Zheng had imagined, the bottom of the Primal Chaos was indeed incomparably desolate.
For three days in a row, Luo Zheng would occasionally encounter unintelligent humans. These humans couldn't fly and lived in the Skeleton Wasteland like wild beasts.
Seeing Luo Zheng flying in the air, they would occasionally raise their heads and roar, as if they were venting their dissatisfaction …
On the fourth day, Luo Zheng saw more than a hundred unclothed people using their hands and feet to run in a direction.
"Eh!"
His gaze focused slightly.
In front of these humans, there was a person running wildly.
This person was probably a true "human". It was a youth. He was draped in a pale yellow beast skin. His face was filled with fear as he ran in a random direction …
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng, who was flying in a straight line, suddenly circled in the air in a spiral trajectory and flew towards the youth.
The youth was on the brink of despair.
This time, his luck was extremely good. He had actually found three desolate bones! For three pieces of desolate bones, the risk was worth it.
However, on the way back, his good luck was used up. He was chased by more than a hundred sneaky people …
The youth only knew that he would die here today. He never expected that a person would suddenly fall from the sky.
Although he was extremely surprised in his heart, he only wanted to live right now. His feet did not stop at all, and he continued to run forward madly …
However, before he could run a few steps, he heard a series of dull sounds coming from behind him. A formless wave was stirred up in the air.
"Peng Peng Peng Peng …"
The youth turned his head and saw seven or eight sneaky people lying on the ground. Their bodies were twisted and they were deader than dead.
The remaining sneaky people were intimidated by this scene. They did not chase after him and dispersed in an uproar.
These sneaky people were not completely unintelligent. It was just that their intelligence was low. However, seeing how Luo Zheng could easily kill their own kind, they knew that he was an existence they could not afford to offend.
Seeing the sneaky people disperse, the youth heaved a sigh of relief. He stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
When he saw Luo Zheng walk over, he took out a desolate bone from his waist and knelt on the ground. He raised the desolate bone above his head and said loudly, "This is the result of my scavenging. Great Immortal, please accept it!"
The youth's actions left Luo Zheng baffled.
However, when he heard the youth speak the same language as him, he calmed down.
No matter what, there were humans at the bottom of the Chaos …
However, the language of the Divine Realm was inherited from the Mother World. Therefore, the living beings at the bottom of the Chaos must be inextricably linked to the Mother World.
Luo Zheng stared at the "desolate bone". From its appearance, this desolate bone was no different from an ordinary bone. However, he could sense an extraordinary power from this bone.
"What bone is this?" Luo Zheng asked.
Hearing Luo Zheng's question, the youth revealed a strange expression. It was as if it was inconceivable that Luo Zheng did not know the origin of this bone.

Chapter 2112
Luo Zheng said as he took the desolate bone from the youth and examined it.
There were all kinds of bones in this wasteland formed by bones. These bones were not afraid of the corrosion of the chaotic aura, but there was nothing special about them.
The only thing that piqued Luo Zheng's curiosity was the unique energy contained within the desolate bones.
But then again …
Regardless of whether it was the youth in front of him or the humans who had not gained intelligence, they did not have a high cultivation level, but they were still not afraid of the chaotic aura.
Could it be that they were also considered "creatures of a higher level"? Were they considered otherworldly demons?
But now was not the time to consider this. Just as he kept the desolate bone, the youth cleverly climbed up from the ground and said, "Great Immortal can fly, but can you bring me out of here?"
Luo Zheng also needed to understand this strange place, so he agreed without hesitation. He grabbed the youth and flew forward.
"Do you often come here to pick up these desolate bones?" Luo Zheng asked.
The youth nodded, "Yes!"
"This place is still too dangerous for you," Luo Zheng said lightly.
The humans crawling on the ground were like ants to Luo Zheng, but they could easily kill the youth.
It was not worth risking his life for these desolate bones.
The youth smiled faintly, "This time I was careless and went too deep into the Eternal Wasteland! In the past, I was always at the edge of the Eternal Wasteland! But now, there are more and more people picking up desolate bones, so I can only go deeper to try my luck! "
So this place was called the Eternal Wasteland, Luo Zheng thought to himself. Then he asked, "Are there many people like you who pick up desolate bones?"
"Of course! We do this for a living! "The youth replied without hesitation.
Luo Zheng followed the topic and got a rough understanding of the situation.
So there was a tribe at the edge of the Eternal Wasteland, and this youth was a member of that tribe.
To become stronger here, one had to absorb the power of the desolate bones.
The youth's goal was the same.
However, although the Eternal Wasteland was huge, the desolate bones at the edge were hard to find after countless years of development.
The youth was taking a huge risk to go deep into the Eternal Wasteland.
Of course, with the youth's leg strength, the so-called "deep" wasn't that deep in Luo Zheng's eyes.
Under the young man's guidance, Luo Zheng flew along the Eternal Wasteland.
Not long after, the bones on the ground became fewer and fewer, exposing large patches of black soil. This should be the edge.
Just like the youth had said, the further one went outside the Eternal Wasteland, the fewer of those "shady people" there were.
Other than that, Luo Zheng also saw some humans digging in between the bones. From the looks of it, they were also searching for the desolate bones …
After flying for a while and leaving the range of the Eternal Wasteland, he finally saw a medium-sized tribe.
"We're here!" The young man pointed at the tribe and said with a cunning look in his eyes. "Great Immortal, let's come down first!"
Luo Zheng accepted the boy's proposal.
The people here didn't seem to know how to fly, so it would be too eye-catching for him to fly into the tribe with this young man.
In this strange environment, Luo Zheng had to be careful.
After all, the Extraterrestrial Demons at the bottom of the Primal Chaos could bind an entire tribe. The true experts at the bottom of the Primal Chaos were probably even stronger than Sages.
"Great Immortal, since you're here, please come to my tribe as a guest," the young man said sincerely.
Luo Zheng nodded slightly, then followed the young man to the tribe.
Unexpectedly, just as the two of them crossed the mountain and approached the entrance of the tribe, a large group of tall and sturdy men suddenly rushed out from the tribe!
These people were holding wooden clubs, iron pitchforks, and stones. They aggressively rushed towards Luo Zheng and the young man.
"Jinx! You still dare to come back! "
"I already said that if you, jinx, dare to come back to our tribe!"
"Don't say anything else, get lost!"
Their target wasn't Luo Zheng, but the young man beside Luo Zheng.
These people were cursing angrily as they rushed forward …
Some of them waved their clubs and iron pitchforks, while others threw large stones at them.
The young man let out a strange cry and immediately hid behind Luo Zheng, his body still trembling. "Great Immortal, Great Immortal, save me, these people are plotting to kill me!"
Luo Zheng glanced indifferently at the young man. His expression, voice, and movements were all very frightened, but there wasn't the slightest trace of fear in his eyes …
It was very difficult to fake a person's eyes. This little guy wanted to use him.
The young man let him land in advance so that these people wouldn't see that he could fly, so these people in the tribe dared to rush up unscrupulously.
Luo Zheng's brows were already furrowed.
Even so, facing this group of people rushing over, he gently raised his hand, and wisps of invisible energy condensed.
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
The stones flying in the air suddenly turned into a pile of powder.
As for those people waving their clubs and iron pitchforks, they only felt that their hands were empty, and their weapons had also turned into a pile of powder.
"Great Immortal is so powerful! Great Immortal, help me teach them a lesson. These smelly guys bully me every day, let them have a taste of their own medicine! " The faked fear on the young man's face had completely disappeared, and his eyes were full of excitement.
The faces of this group of men suddenly became very unsightly.
When they saw the young man bring back a stranger, they didn't take it seriously. They didn't expect this person to be so powerful, and even help the young man.
"You, who are you?"
"Why are you helping this jinx?"
The leader of the group, a man with a crew cut, stared at Luo Zheng and questioned.
Luo Zheng said calmly: "I don't intend to help him, but if there's anything, you can talk it out. There's no need to treat a child like this."
This matter originally had nothing to do with him, and besides, Luo Zheng didn't like the feeling of being used.
"A child?" The man with a crew cut laughed coldly, "Since this guy came to our tribe, there have been disasters every year. This is not a child, but a living disaster, and will only bring misfortune!"
"Strange friend, this is our tribe's own matter," another person said.
"I'm not a disaster!" The young man defended himself with a stiff neck, looking very sad, "Sister Xiao Yun can prove it!"
"Hmph! If Xiao Yun hadn't been kind enough to take you in, our tribe wouldn't have fallen to this point … Today, no matter what, you can forget about returning to the tribe! "The man with a crew cut said firmly.

Chapter 2113
The woman was wearing a coarse blouse and a coarse skirt.
Although her clothes were simple and crude, they could not hide her astonishing beauty. It seemed that in such a simple and crude tribe, there should not be such a stunning woman who had appeared.
Luo Zheng's gaze landed on the woman and immediately, he was puzzled.
Similarly, he could not tell what cultivation level she had, but her body exuded a different and powerful aura.
It was as though she was a body-tempering cultivator, but not completely!
Luo Zheng knew that she was not his match, but her strength should be far stronger than ordinary people …
What made Luo Zheng even more curious was that the aura she exuded was very similar to the desolate bone in Luo Zheng's hand!
"Sister Xiao Yun!"
Upon seeing the woman, the youth revealed a look of joy.
When the others heard Xiao Yun's words, their expressions immediately turned ugly.
Xiao Yun's status in the tribe was very high. More importantly, whenever a calamity befell, only Xiao Yun could withstand it …
If Xiao Yun really left, the calamity would befall again, and the people in the tribe would probably die!
Furthermore, their tribe had quite a number of conflicts with another tribe. Conflicts would often erupt, and Xiao Yun had to step in every time to keep the other tribe at bay.
"Xiao Yun, if you leave, what will happen if more sinister people come out?"
"That's right … Furthermore, the Yufei tribe is also restless!"
Upon hearing this, Xiao Yun said indifferently, "Didn't you say that these calamities were brought by Fu Chu? If I were to leave with Fu Chu, wouldn't the calamities leave with him? What are you worried about? "
"This …" The man with the crew cut did not know how to retort.
Ever since Xiao Yun brought Fu Chu back to the tribe, sinister people would often come out of the Eternal Desolate Plains to attack the tribe.
However, before Fu Chu was brought back, it was not as though such things had never happened before …
Seeing that the man with the crew cut was speechless, Xiao Yun sneered and walked in front of Fu Chu, saying, "Follow me home!"
The youth named "Fu Chu" obediently followed behind Xiao Yun.
At this moment, Xiao Yun turned her head again and stared at Luo Zheng, saying, "Were you the one who saved Fu Chu?"
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Xiao Yun's calm face finally revealed a faint smile, "Thank you. My humble abode doesn't have anything to entertain you with, but please come to my house …"
…
…
The simplicity of this tribe was even more exaggerated than Luo Zheng had imagined.
In the middle of an empty hut, there was a fire, and strips of dried meat were hanging in the corner.
After Xiao Yun boiled a pot of water, she sat on one side of the fire and glanced at Luo Zheng. She then said, "You … are a True God who has opened up his Inner World?"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
"Yes," Luo Zheng admitted. "How did you know?"
In order to see through the other party's cultivation, one's own cultivation had to reach a certain level.
If Xiao Yun was a lower True God, this would not be strange. However, Luo Zheng clearly could not sense any cultivation from her. This was the strange part.
Xiao Yun's face revealed a strange expression. She stared at Luo Zheng for a while and smiled slightly. "Where did you come from? You don't seem to know anything …"
Fu Chu chuckled and said, "Sister, I also find it strange. This big brother asked a lot of things along the way, but he doesn't even seem to know about the Eternal Wasteland!"
Fu Chu looked innocent, but he was actually quite scheming. He had already suspected Luo Zheng's origins along the way.
"Forgive me for not being able to answer," said Luo Zheng as he shook his head.
When Luo Zheng first entered the divine realm, Xingwei had at least told him a lot of general knowledge about the divine realm.
This time, Luo Zheng was directly pushed out of the divine realm by his mother and fell into this place. He knew nothing about the world at the bottom of the Chaos!
"Everyone has their own secrets." Xiao Yun did not continue to ask Luo Zheng. After all, Luo Zheng was also Fu Chu's benefactor. She then said, "The reason why I can see through your cultivation is that I'm an Aragami."
"Aragami?" Luo Zheng was slightly stunned. He had never heard of this before. At least, he had never seen an Aragami in the divine realm.
Xiao Yun nodded. "In our place, there are very few True Gods who are pure cultivators. So, I suspect that you're the young master of some big force. You've never left the house, so you don't know anything …"
Under normal circumstances, Luo Zheng should be able to see through Xiao Yun's cultivation at a glance. She was a lower Aragami.
However, Luo Zheng had never seen an "Aragami" before. He could only feel a unique aura from Xiao Yun. Naturally, he could not see through Xiao Yun's cultivation.
"The Aragami that you mentioned, is it related to these desolate bones?" Luo Zheng finally thought of the desolate bones. After all, the aura that Xiao Yun was emitting was exactly the same as the energy of the desolate bones. As he said that, Luo Zheng took out the desolate bones from his hand.
After Xiao Yun saw the desolate bones, her eyes lit up. She nodded and said, "Yes, the power of us Aragami comes from these desolate bones!"
The Aragami had a completely different cultivation system.
At the bottom of the Chaos, all resources were scarce.
Ordinary living beings did not have the resources to cultivate their Life Force. It was even more impossible for them to open up a "small divine realm" like Luo Zheng.
However, this world was fair. At the bottom of the Chaos, there were a huge number of bones …
These bones were like the vast sea. There were so many of them that it was impossible to count them. It could only be described as an infinite number.
Moreover, many of the bones were once left behind by extremely powerful beings …
After countless years of precipitation, these bones were not weathered or corroded. Finally, they became desolate bones.
These living beings at the bottom of the Chaos absorbed the energy within the desolate bones and used it to cultivate themselves. Similarly, their strength would increase by leaps and bounds, eventually becoming a Aragami!
However, although the number of desolate bones was not small, it was ultimately limited.
Just like Xiao Yun's tribe, they relied on collecting desolate bones for a living. Every once in a while, there would be experts coming to purchase them in exchange for necessary resources.
Of course, a portion of the desolate bones would be left for Xiao Yun to cultivate.
In the past half a year, the number of desolate bones that the tribe had excavated had decreased. Today, the entire tribe had accumulated less than twenty pieces.
If these desolate bones were sold, Xiao Yun would not have any desolate bones to cultivate with.
Fu Chu knew that his sister needed extra desolate bones to break through the bottleneck. Therefore, he gritted his teeth and took the risk to enter the Eternal Wasteland. The more dangerous the place, the higher the chances of excavating desolate bones.
He never expected that he would be chased by a group of sneaky people after excavating three desolate bones by luck. Finally, he was saved by Luo Zheng.
"I see …"
Luo Zheng was enlightened and sighed in his heart.

Chapter 2114
"Reaching the Aragami level means that you are a different kind of Body Refiner. However, your body doesn't seem to be as strong as it looks …"
Since the other party already knew that he knew nothing, Luo Zheng might as well ask more clearly.
Body Refiners usually had a strong body.
Just like Luo Zheng, who had cultivated his body to a terrifying level, he was also considered a different kind of Body Refiner.
Even though Little Yun was emanating a powerful aura, her physical body was definitely not strong ….
Xiao Yun's gentle eyes glanced at Luo Zheng. She probably felt that Luo Zheng had asked another stupid question.
Fu Chu, who was at the side, smiled helplessly. This young man really didn't know what kind of person he had brought back. It was as if he didn't belong to this world and didn't have any common sense.
Fortunately, Xiao Yun was very patient. She immediately said, "Friend, if you don't know anything about the Aragami level, you can follow me."
After saying that, she walked out of the thatched hut with light steps.
Luo Zheng didn't hesitate and followed closely behind.
When they arrived at the field outside the thatched hut, Xiao Yun turned her waist and smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng. She reached out her hands and started to take off her clothes.
"You …" Luo Zheng was shocked. He didn't know what she was trying to do.
Who knew that after she took off her coarse clothes, a set of brown night clothes was revealed.
The night clothes were tightly attached to her body, outlining her exquisite figure. There were also strange patterns on the surface of the night clothes.
She blinked her eyes slightly, and a strong aura was emitted from her body!
"Huff … Huff … Huff …"
This aura was emitting a faint yellow light. It was actually visible to the naked eye.
Luo Zheng was still looking at Xiao Yun with a puzzled expression. He didn't know why she was doing this …
In Luo Zheng's eyes, he could feel an extremely primitive energy surging evenly in Xiao Yun's body. It almost covered every part of her body.
Head, neck, chest, waist, and even fingers …
In the Divine Domain, there were very few people who could use their strength like this. Regardless of whether it was Quintessential Origin Energy, they would use it on the blade.
For example, when Luo Zheng used his Origin Energy, he would definitely not spread the energy evenly throughout his body. However, Xiao Yun was actually doing the opposite.
Then, he heard Xiao Yun spit out a word, "Grow!"
Then, Xiao Yun's body began to expand rapidly!
Xiao Yun, who was originally only four feet tall, quickly grew taller than Luo Zheng!
Eight feet …
Ten feet …
Fifteen meters …
Ten zhang.
This seemingly weak and simple woman instantly turned into a small mountain, looking down at Luo Zheng.
The energy in Little Yun's body became extremely violent, and her temperament changed in an instant. She seemed to have turned into a female beast from the depths of the wilderness, and she revealed a strange and powerful beauty!
"This!" Luo Zheng was shocked.
Luo Zheng had seen spells that could turn a small object into a large one, and some magic weapons could do the same.
Apart from that, there were also some special abilities and living beings with special bloodlines that could allow them to grow significantly in size for a short period of time.
Even Luo Zheng could expand his body when he infused his body with the source of power.
However, the process of turning a small object into a large object was completely different from what Xiao Yun was doing!
As Xiao Yun's figure grew, every part of her body grew larger. The aura she contained also became extremely powerful. It was as if this size was her original form!
As for why Xiao Yun took off her clothes in the beginning …
It was probably because after she grew bigger, her clothes would also expand and tear.
As for the night clothes she was wearing, they could expand along with her body, and they were still sticking to her body.
Xiao Yun looked down at Luo Zheng and said, "This is the power of the Rage God."
Now, Xiao Yun's fist was even bigger than Luo Zheng's.
However, after her entire body, including her hair and eyebrows, grew larger, she looked very harmonious and beautiful as usual.
Fu Chu was used to such scenes.
After all, every time there was a problem in their tribe, Xiao Yun would appear.
Xiao Yun was the Goddess of War who protected this tribe. Without Xiao Yun, this tribe would have long ceased to exist.
Because of this, every time the people in the tribe wanted to chase Fu Chu away, they would be stopped by Xiao Yun …
"Sister, you can spar with him!" Fu Chu said with a smile.
Hearing this, Xiao Yun's eyes flashed slightly.
With Xiao Yun's eyesight, she knew that Luo Zheng's strength was not bad. After all, he had the cultivation of a True God.
However, this was a world where the cultivation of the Rage God was the main focus. Cultivating a True God had become an auxiliary method.
"I can try." Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
"Get ready. I'm coming. Be careful. My strength … is very strong," Xiao Yun reminded him.
After saying that, her tall figure suddenly twisted. Gusts of wind blew, and the straw on the thatched huts in the tribe began to sway wildly.
"Whoosh!"
Xiao Yun's "Embroidered Fist" smashed toward Luo Zheng. This fist alone was three meters wide.
Seeing the huge fist coming down, Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
After transforming into a giant, Xiao Yun's strength had indeed increased by many times!
Luo Zheng stood in place and bent his arms in front of him to block. At the same time, the power source in his body suddenly erupted.
"Bang!"
In the blink of an eye, he collided with Xiao Yun's fist that was three meters wide.
In Xiao Yun's eyes, those who focused on cultivating a True God were very weak. Therefore, she deliberately suppressed her Rage God power and only used less than forty percent of her strength.
Although this young man did not know anything and seemed to have a strange background, he still saved her brother's life. It would not be good if she smashed him into meat paste with her fist.
However, at the moment of collision, Xiao Yun felt an extremely strong power from this young man's body!
This power was so strong that it was almost impossible for her to resist!
After blocking, Luo Zheng suddenly pushed back with his hand. The strong power pushed back along her arm, and Xiao Yun's huge body began to retreat.
"Dong dong dong dong dong …"
Every time she took a step back, there would be a muffled sound and the ground would shake.
However, although her body was dozens of times bigger, she was still very agile. After taking seven to eight steps back, she actually flipped over and stabilized herself. She stared at Luo Zheng with a strange light in her eyes. She could not help but exclaim, "You have such strong strength! Again! "
This time, Xiao Yun stopped underestimating him. She exploded with ninety percent of her strength and smashed toward Luo Zheng's head again!
"Bang!"
The two of them collided again.
Luo Zheng stood in place. The muscles on his raised arm bulged as he blocked Xiao Yun's fist with one hand.
When Fu Chu saw this scene, he took a deep breath.

Chapter 2115
The siblings had never seen such a thing before.
Especially Little Yun, she was very clear about how powerful she was.
She had been growing by relying on the desolate bones all these years. Her talent allowed her to utilize the power of the desolate bones to its fullest potential.
Among the Aragami of the same level, there were very few who could surpass her in terms of power.
This young man only used one hand to block her attack that was almost at full strength.
He had yet to train as an Aragami … How terrifying would he be if he really did?
Xiao Yun retracted her fist.
The pale yellow light on the surface of her body flickered slightly. Her body began to shrink and shrink …
Finally, she returned to her original form.
She picked up her clothes and slowly walked towards Luo Zheng. She looked at Luo Zheng with a hint of respect and suspicion.
It was impossible for such a person to be a nobody. However, he knew nothing about the Aragami and this world. This guy was too strange.
"You … Where did you come from?" Xiao Yun could not help but ask. Her curiosity had been piqued.
"Can I not tell you?" Luo Zheng replied.
Xiao Yun pouted. She probably knew that Luo Zheng would not reveal that easily. Instead, she asked, "Then what's your purpose for coming here?"
"Purpose …" Luo Zheng pondered for a moment.
He was thrown down by his mother …
Now, Luo Zheng had gradually understood his background and what he had to face.
If he were to talk about his purpose, his eyes shone with a hint of urgency. "My purpose is to become stronger!"
Fu Chu suddenly interrupted. "Great immortal, it seems that your enemy is very powerful, right?"
"Why do you say that?" Luo Zheng asked.
"You're already so powerful. If you want to become stronger, that's the only reason." Fu Chu shrugged and said.
Luo Zheng did not refute. After his mother told him everything, he did realize that his opponents were unprecedentedly powerful. Just Mu Haiji and Dongfang Chunjun alone were insurmountable mountains. Moreover, they were not the only ones who were hiding in the Divine Dominion!
He wanted to become stronger, and he was very eager!
"If that's the case, you can cultivate the Desolate God. I can teach you the cultivation method." Little Yun had already changed back into her coarse clothes as she spoke in an indifferent tone.
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly. Indeed, he had the idea of training the Aragami.
When he saw those unimaginably huge skeletons at the bottom of the Chaos in the God's Domain, Luo Zheng was deeply shocked.
Moreover, Luo Yan had also summoned a gigantic "Shadow of Nuwa" in the Battle of Dao. Luo Zheng had a vague guess in his heart. It seemed that the creatures in the Chaos or the Mother World could all become gigantic.
Xiao Yun's means deepened Luo Zheng's conjecture.
If the world at the bottom of the Chaos was really focused on the cultivation of the Aragami, Luo Zheng was confident that he could get his hands on the cultivation method of the Aragami.
However, he was unfamiliar with this place, and this world was far more complicated than he had imagined.
Xiao Yun from a remote tribe already had such strength. If he was careless and exposed something, or provoked something, he would be in danger of dying.
In the universe, he was protected by his father. In the God's Domain, he had Yu Taibai and his mother. However, he had nothing here.
"Sure," Luo Zheng said with a smile.
"But there's a condition." Xiao Yun's face revealed a sly smile.
"What condition?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Teach Fu Chu the cultivation method of Vital Energy." Xiao Yun said.
"Sister …" Fu Chu was stunned.
Fu Chu had been plagued by bad luck since he was young. Moreover, his eight extraordinary meridians were sealed, so he could not cultivate the Aragami.
Therefore, his talent could only be used on various wicked ideas.
For example, it was not his first time entering the Eternal Wasteland. Moreover, he had gained something every time. However, because it was too dangerous, Xiao Yun did not allow him to enter at all.
"Sure, but I'll only teach you the method. I won't accept any disciples." Luo Zheng said indifferently.
In exchange, this was very fair.
…
…
Body refiners had never been mainstream in the God's Domain.
After all, the three thousand Divine Daos were all Mystical Powers left behind by Chi You to the Sacred Race. Apart from the Strength Divine Dao, the other Divine Daos were very difficult to be compatible with body refiners.
Among them, the mainstream body refinement method was eight layers of Dao Pedestal and nine stars of Spiritual Pedestal.
However, the body refinement method of cultivating the Aragami opened up another path and constructed another system!
"The power of the Aragami contained in these desolate bones is actually a kind of inheritance," Xiao Yun said.
"Inheritance?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a puzzled expression.
"En!" Xiao Yun replied nonchalantly. "These desolate bones were left behind by an ancient expert. After hundreds of millions of years of sedimentation, not only did the energy within not disappear, it even solidified within the bones, turning them into desolate bones! Therefore, our power is all inherited from those ancient elites! "
Hearing this explanation, Luo Zheng finally understood.
There were countless corpses gathered at the bottom of the chaos. There were also many powerful people among these corpses, which was why such a unique cultivation method was developed.
"The desolate bone in your hand contains the power of the Aragami. You can absorb it," Xiao Yun said. "Until the power of the Aragami permeates your body and every part of your bones!"
Permeate every part …
Luo Zheng thought of the time when he had just started cultivating martial arts.
His goal was to completely cleanse the impurities in his body and remove them.
However, using these desolate bones to cultivate was the opposite of that, completely permeating the body with the power of the desolate bones!
"This is only the lowest level desolate bone, so you won't feel any rejection. Some powerful desolate bones won't be so easy to absorb," Xiao Yun added.
"There are even more powerful desolate bones?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Xiao Yun looked as if it was only natural and smiled slightly. "Of course, there are many kinds of desolate bones in the Eternal Wasteland. Some of the desolate bones' owners were extremely powerful when they were alive! Even after billions of years, or even trillions of years after their death, the power imprints in their bones would not disappear! This kind of desolate bone's power is truly powerful. However, these desolate bones' power will only be passed down to their descendants, so they need to possess their bloodline! "
"Fusing desolate bones requires a bloodline?" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
Xiao Yun nodded. "A few years ago, two desolate bones that contained Chiyou's bloodline were dug out from the Eternal Wasteland! These desolate bones are not something I can absorb, so I can only sell them or exchange them for ordinary desolate bones … "
Upon saying this, Xiao Yun's face revealed a trace of resentment. The power contained in the desolate bones was different, so the effects were different. However, bloodlines were destined at birth.

Chapter 2116
"Desolate bones of the Chiyou bloodline …" Luo Zheng's eyebrows pricked up slightly.
Luo Zheng had learned from his mother that the Li Min clan was a large clan in the Mother World. There were many people with the Chiyou bloodline flowing in their veins.
Those desolate bones were likely left behind by the mighty figures of the Li Min clan …
However, Luo Zheng did not know what had happened in the Chaos to cause so many bones to be scattered.
"Since only people with the Chiyou bloodline can absorb the energy in the desolate bones, what's the point of those people taking them away?" Luo Zheng asked, "Could it be that there are people here with the Chiyou bloodline?"
Little Yun nodded, "It's said that there are … Among the mighty figures on the bone pagodas, some of them have powerful bloodlines!"
"There are actually people here with the Chiyou bloodline." Luo Zheng's heart stirred slightly.
Only the Li Min clan could inherit the Chiyou bloodline.
Could there be Li Min among the Other Realm Demons? Or could it be that they had used other means to obtain the Chiyou bloodline?
If there were really Li Min, they could be a powerful aid …
This was just a thought in Luo Zheng's mind. Before he could figure out the crux of the problem, he could not act rashly.
"Let's not talk about this problem. Don't you have a desolate bone in your hands? I will now teach you the refining method! You must remember it! "Little Yun smiled slightly.
The methods of refining desolate bones were similar, but not everyone could withstand it.
Just like Little Yun's Wrought Steel Tribe, she was the only one who could withstand the power of the Rage God. No one else could absorb the energy in the desolate bones.
"This desolate bone's energy is already ownerless, so it's not difficult to extract it. The most difficult part is how to utilize it." Sitting across from Luo Zheng, Xiao Yun said seriously to Luo Zheng while sitting cross-legged. She was also holding a desolate bone in her hands.
Fu Chu had dug out three desolate bones from the Eternal Wastelands. One of them was given to Luo Zheng as a 'gift', while the other two were naturally given to his Sister Little Yun.
"The technique I'm using is called the Dripping Spring Technique. It was imparted to me by an extraordinary person when I was young." As Little Yun spoke, her hands left the desolate bone, causing it to float in front of her.
Her hands moved around the desolate bone as if she was caressing a baby.
"The crux of the Dripping Spring technique lies in the control of one's psyche. It can be slow or fast, from the inside to the outside, to guide the energy from the desolate bones out bit by bit … The psyche is like a gushing spring, never decreasing, never decreasing …"
The moment Xiao Yun's voice faded, Luo Zheng felt the desolate bone change.
The power of the Rage God that was locked tightly within the desolate bones began to loosen. Finally, it began to slowly gush out like a fountain!
"I see …" Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
After the power of the Rage God surged out, it slowly wriggled in front of Little Yun. Little Yun gently flicked her finger, causing the mist to enter Little Yun's body.
"You have to pay attention when absorbing the power of the Rage God. The moment it enters your body, you have to absorb it evenly as quickly as possible …" Little Yun was already very familiar with this. As she absorbed the power of the Rage God, she reminded Luo Zheng of the essentials.
After about ten breaths, Little Yun finally absorbed the power of the Rage God.
However, when she finished absorbing it, Luo Zheng realized that the power of the Rage God was constantly escaping from her body …
