"This power of the Rage God … Why is it escaping from your body?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Little Yun gave a matter-of-fact look and said helplessly, "This is the problem of the utilization rate that I mentioned. Now, I can only absorb 30% of the power of the Rage God. 70% of it cannot be locked in my body, so it will all be wasted."
The problem that she encountered was something that everyone who practiced the Rage God would encounter.
"Only 30%?" Luo Zheng did not expect that the power of the Rage God in the desolate bone would be wasted so much. "I'll give it a try too!"
"I'm afraid you will waste this desolate bone on your first try," Fu Chu said as he curled his lips.
Desolate bones were too rare for them. Luo Zheng had only seen Little Yun's demonstration once, and he wanted to absorb the power of the Rage God in a hurry.
Little Yun said, "It's fine. I nearly wasted all the power of the Rage God on my first try. Everyone has to experience it personally before they can succeed."
Luo Zheng smiled and gently threw the desolate bone in his hand.
The desolate bone slowly rotated in front of him. At the same time, he used the Dripping Spring technique that Little Yun taught him to draw out the power from within.
This kind of method was really too easy for Luo Zheng.
With a gentle flick of his finger, wisps of power of the Rage God were drawn out from the desolate bone like silk being peeled from a cocoon!
"Eh! Luo Zheng, you … are so fast? "Little Yun took a deep breath as her eyes quickly widened.
With Little Yun's skillful technique, the power of the Rage God that was drawn out was like gushing spring water.
With the Dripping Spring technique, Luo Zheng was able to make the power of the Rage God shoot out. In the blink of an eye, the power of the desolate bone was completely extracted!
Regardless of whether it was the Dripping Spring technique or other techniques, it was just using one's consciousness to draw out the power of the desolate bone …
Luo Zheng's soul was so strong that it was comparable to a Perfect True God. Using his consciousness to draw out the power of the Rage God was too easy.
After drawing out the power of the Rage God, Luo Zheng guided it into his body …
According to Little Yun's instructions, he tried his best to distribute the power of the Rage God evenly in his body.
Little Yun and Fu Chu stared at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng sat on the spot, motionless …
The three of them continued like this for a while before Little Yun said with an incomprehensible expression, "Then, what about the power of the Rage God?"
"I've absorbed it," Luo Zheng said.
"Why didn't you release any of the power of the Rage God!?" Little Yun was really dumbfounded.
Even after cultivating for so long, Little Yun could only use 30% of the power of the Rage God. This was already considered quite good.
It was said that some geniuses from large factions could use 60% or even 70% of the power of the Rage God …
However, this was Luo Zheng's first time absorbing the power of the Rage God. He actually absorbed all of it without leaving out a single bit!
With Little Yun's experience, she couldn't understand it at all!
As for Luo Zheng, he was only slightly confused. However, he understood in the blink of an eye.
Most of the living beings' bodies weren't perfect. There would always be some undetectable loopholes. The absorbed True Origin would be able to escape through these loopholes. The power of the Rage God was the same.
Luo Zheng's body had been refined many times. Back when he was in the universe, he had already cultivated the Leakless Real Body.

Chapter 2117
Luo Zheng slowly stood up while Xiao Yun and Fu Chu stared at him as if they were looking at a monster.
The power of the Rage God was not like the source of strength, Qi, or even Chaos Qi that could be used at will.
It had completely fused with his body …
When Luo Zheng thought about it, the power of the Rage God seemed to be ignited by Luo Zheng.
He felt his body tremble. Be it his muscles, bones, or even his veins, they all felt like they were expanding! Moreover, during this process of expansion, he sensed that his body contained an indescribable force!
Luo Zheng's first time absorbing the power of the Rage God, he had only absorbed a small piece of the lowest grade desolate bone. Naturally, he could not be like Xiao Yun, who instantly turned into a two hundred foot tall giant.
His body only stretched slightly, and in a short period of time, he "grew" by an inch.
Even so, it was enough to make Luo Zheng very excited.
"The power of the Rage God is indeed very unique …" Luo Zheng muttered. Only then did he notice Xiao Yun's surprised expression. He asked, "What's wrong?"
"You, you haven't answered my question!" Xiao Yun said excitedly.
Luo Zheng could absorb all the power of the Rage God. This efficiency was three times higher than Xiao Yun's.
In other words, Luo Zheng's refining of one desolate bone was equivalent to Xiao Yun's refining of three desolate bones. The difference was too great!
How could Luo Zheng explain such a question? If he really had to explain, it would be a long story …
He could only shrug his shoulders and pretend to be confused. "I'm not sure either …"
Xiao Yun stared at Luo Zheng's face suspiciously. From Luo Zheng's previous performance, he was indeed clueless. But once he began cultivating, it had completely exceeded her common sense.
If she had not seen it with her own eyes, she would have found it hard to believe.
Finally, Xiao Yun said faintly, "Maybe you are really suitable to cultivate the Rage God."
When she encountered an unexplainable problem, she could only attribute it to talent.
Xiao Yun did not want to compare herself with such a talented person, but she could not help but feel a sense of defeat.
…
…
In order to become an Aragami, absorbing a piece of desolate bone was far from enough.
The unsatisfied Luo Zheng quickly set his sights on the Eternal Wasteland.
Over the past few days, people from the tribe had been going in and out. They were picking up desolate bones in the periphery of the Eternal Desolate Plains.
Even at the periphery of the Eternal Desolate Plains, they would still be attacked by those "sneaky people". It was actually very dangerous for them. Furthermore, a tribe had hundreds or thousands of people. If they were lucky, they would only be able to pick up two or three desolate bones in a month. It was very inefficient.
Just as Fu Chu had said, the deeper one went into the Eternal Wastelands, the higher the chances of picking up desolate bones.
After all, not many people dared to venture too deep. Back then, Fu Chu wasn't Luo Zheng, but he had almost lost his life.
These problems were not a problem for Luo Zheng.
When he suggested entering the Eternal Wasteland to search for desolate bones, Fu Chu did not miss the opportunity to recommend himself. He smiled slyly and said, "The Eternal Wasteland is so big. If you are not careful, you will get lost. I can be your guide!"
Luo Zheng glanced at Fu Chu indifferently. Bringing this guy along would only be a burden. He waved his hand and a scroll appeared in his hand. "There is no need for a guide. This is a cultivation technique for cultivating Quintessential Essence. You can study it first. If there is anything you do not understand, you can ask me."
According to their previous agreement, Luo Zheng had to impart the cultivation technique for Quintessential Essence to Fu Chu.
Fu Chu was immediately unhappy. However, Xiao Yun had strictly forbidden Fu Chu from entering the Eternal Wasteland. She would not allow him to enter with Luo Zheng.
Not long after, Luo Zheng entered the Eternal Wasteland once again.
In the past few days, he had a rough understanding of the world at the bottom of the Chaos …
The true 'extraterritorial devils' resided in those huge beast skeletons. Little Yun and Fu Chu called those beast skeletons that were as huge as the divine domain 'bone pagodas'. Every bone pagoda was a massive faction.
And in those large factions, some Desolate Gods could transform into giants that were hundreds of meters tall or even thousands of meters tall!
In comparison, Xiao Yun was considered weaker among the Desolate Gods.
"To enter the Bone Pagodas, one has to cultivate to the Desolate God level … Xiao Yun is also a Desolate God. She also wishes to become stronger. Why doesn't she enter the Bone Pagodas?"
As Luo Zheng flew through the Eternal Wasteland, he was guessing in his mind. He always felt that the siblings, Xiao Yun and Fu Chu, had some unspeakable secrets.
However, these did not have much to do with him. After all, Luo Zheng could not even protect himself now. He wished to become stronger so that he could kill his way back to the Divine Domain one day!
"Huff … Huff … Huff …"
He flew along the Eternal Wasteland and quickly went deep into it.
When Luo Zheng first fell from the Divine Domain, he did not know of the existence of desolate bones. He only faintly felt that there was a mysterious aura exuding from the Skeleton Wasteland.
Only now did he understand that this aura came from the power of the Desolate Gods.
After flying for about an hour, Luo Zheng descended from the sky.
Beneath his feet were densely packed skeletons.
But with the naked eye, there was no way to differentiate between the skeletons and the desolate bones.
Luo Zheng squinted his eyes slightly. The Divine Perception in his mind immediately spread out. His Divine Perception covered a radius of tens of miles.
"Using Divine Perception to find the desolate bones is much easier." Luo Zheng revealed a smile on his face.
He had already sensed that there were about seven or eight pieces of desolate bones in this area!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng chose a direction and flew over. With a push of his hand, an invisible energy wave swept up the layers of skeletons.
"Hualala …"
These skeletons were like a white wave that surged out. The skeletons that were hidden at the bottom layer were all swept up.
Luo Zheng's eyes flashed slightly. His hand shot out like lightning and a piece of desolate bone landed in his hand.
After obtaining that piece of desolate bone, Luo Zheng flew to the next direction. Using the same method, he obtained seven or eight pieces of desolate bones in the radius of tens of miles!
With Luo Zheng's method, the efficiency of searching for desolate bones was much faster than ordinary people.
After obtaining the desolate bones, Luo Zheng once again soared into the sky and continued flying deeper. At the same time, he used his Divine Perception to cover a radius of tens of miles.
After repeating this several times, he had about thirty pieces of desolate bones in his hand. During this time, Luo Zheng had alarmed a few sinister people.
Luo Zheng killed a few sinister people. The rest of the sinister people dispersed, and no one dared to sneak up on him again.
After half a day, Luo Zheng had already traveled thousands of miles into the Eternal Wasteland.
Just as Luo Zheng was excitedly collecting the desolate bones, he suddenly heard a shocking wail!
This wail contained an extremely powerful force. As the sound spread, it lifted up countless skeletons on the ground, forming a wave of bones that pushed forward like a tsunami.
Luo Zheng looked in the direction of the wail and saw a gigantic bone shadow tens of thousands of miles away!
He was immediately alarmed.

Chapter 2118
Not long after the giant skeleton let out a wail, another giant appeared next to it.
"It's a humanized giant!" Even from tens of thousands of miles away, Luo Zheng could see it clearly. "This giant suddenly appeared … Could it be that it used the power of the Rage God to suddenly become so huge?"
Little Yun had also told Luo Zheng that although she had cultivated to the level of Rage God, she belonged to the lowest level of Rage God and could only transform to a height of 200 feet.
In this world, the truly powerful Rage Gods could transform to a height of hundreds of miles, or even thousands of miles …
One Li was about two thousand feet high …
A giant that was thousands of miles tall, how huge was it? How much power did such a huge giant possess?
Luo Zheng found it hard to imagine.
Just like how Luo Zheng was standing in front of a giant that was thousands of miles tall, he was simply smaller than a speck of dust!
He never thought that after entering the Eternal Wasteland, he would actually see such an existence.
After the giant appeared, it began to fight with the skeleton …
Every time such a huge monster collided, a huge explosion would erupt. The shockwaves from the huge collision formed a huge storm that spread in all directions.
Skeletons of all sizes were also blown all over the Eternal Wasteland.
"Huff huff huff …"
Waves of cold wind blew over, those skeletons tumbled and swarmed towards Luo Zheng.
If an ordinary human was hit by these skeletons, even if they didn't die, they would most likely lose half their life.
Luo Zheng stood in place, allowing those skeletons to smash on his body. His eyes were still firmly fixed on the distance, observing the battle between the two huge monsters.
"Rumble … Rumble …"
Not long after, Luo Zheng saw that the giant skeleton was actually cut in half by the giant, and then it fell to the ground with a loud bang. The giant had finally obtained victory.
Immediately after, the giant disappeared from Luo Zheng's sight.
"It should have become smaller again," Luo Zheng muttered as he stood in the pile of skeletons. He was almost buried by the skeletons that were blown over.
His body twisted and he drilled out of the pile of skeletons, his face full of yearning.
Now, he had already introduced the Spirit Crow into his inner world. According to the method his mother had taught him, he would be able to increase his cultivation by constantly nurturing it.
It was only a matter of time before he became a Paragon.
However, he was only at the Great Perfection level. He might not be able to fight against those Saints.
Other than the Saints, he was even facing Nirvana Realm powerhouses.
If he could cultivate the power of the Rage God to the extreme, Luo Zheng's strength would increase exponentially … This was the power he was desperately searching for!
Luo Zheng did not stop and continued searching for desolate bones.
After the storm stirred up the Eternal Desolate Plains, it seemed like the desolate bones deep underground had been dug up. Luo Zheng suddenly realized that searching for desolate bones had become much easier.
"There are three desolate bones up ahead …"
"There are actually six desolate bones at a depth of twenty feet!"
"There's only one piece …"
Luo Zheng was like a diligent little bee, shuttling back and forth among these skeletons, searching.
In the process of searching for desolate bones, he would occasionally bump into other people passing by …
These people's auras were very similar to Xiao Yun's, but they were much stronger than Xiao Yun. They should also be Rage Gods.
However, they only glanced at Luo Zheng with a surprised expression. They ignored Luo Zheng and immediately left …
In half a day's time, he had accumulated more than eighty desolate bones! Furthermore, nearly half of the desolate bones were the bigger ones.
"Sou!"
He once again flew dozens of miles away and continued to use his soul power to cover the area with his Divine Sense.
Everything that was covered by his Divine Sense turned into grayish-white images that were projected into his mind.
"Eh!"
This time, Luo Zheng discovered that seven miles in front of him, there was a triangular bone that was about the height of a person. And this triangular bone was emitting a rich power of the Rage God!
"Such a big desolate bone?" Luo Zheng was shocked.
He was still not in the core of the Eternal Wasteland. Only the Heavens knew how big the Eternal Wasteland was.
For safety's sake, Luo Zheng did not plan on going too deep. If he were to encounter a gigantic skeleton that was thousands of miles tall, even with Luo Zheng's current strength, it would be difficult for him to survive.
And this region had been searched countless years ago by countless people. Hence, there were only small pieces of desolate bones left. Luo Zheng had searched for so long, but this was the first time he had found such a huge desolate bone!
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's figure suddenly appeared as he stared at the ground.
"It's a thousand feet below me …"
Without much hesitation, Luo Zheng looked down and suddenly jumped down, drilling into the layers of bones.
After drilling to the bottom, Luo Zheng's hands suddenly clasped together, grabbing onto a desolate bone that was about the height of a person. However, he did not manage to lift it up. The weight of the desolate bone had far exceeded Luo Zheng's expectations!
"It's so heavy!"
The nine stars in Luo Zheng's body rotated slightly. The strength in his arms burst out as he dragged the desolate bone out.
After reaching the surface, Luo Zheng saw the true appearance of the desolate bone.
"It's actually a blood-red desolate bone …" Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise.
This triangular desolate bone should be a scapula. However, it was different from the white jade-like bones. This desolate bone emitted a blood-red glow, and the power of the Rage God was extremely rich.
"I wonder how much of a normal desolate bone this desolate bone is worth …"
Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of joy. He was about to use his spatial ring to store the desolate bone.
At this moment, he suddenly heard the sound of wind whistling in his ears. When he turned his head to look, he saw four figures rushing over in the air.
Luo Zheng's eyebrows frowned slightly as he knew something was wrong.
The spatial ring on his hand flashed slightly, and the desolate bone that was about the height of a person disappeared into it.
"Sou Sou Sou …"
The four figures shot through the air, and in a blink of an eye, they stood in front of Luo Zheng.
The leader was a skinny young man with a pale face. Behind the young man was a young girl in a bright red robe. The other two people were dressed as guards.
"Little Senior Brother, I clearly saw a huge piece of blood-red desolate bone just now, but it disappeared in a blink of an eye!" The red-robed girl said.
The youth's gaze immediately landed on the spatial ring on Luo Zheng's hand. "What's there to say? This guy must have hidden it!"
After the youth said that, he sized up Luo Zheng. His eyes revealed a look of disdain as he said, "No matter who you are, the desolate bones dug up in this area belong to our Canglan Sect. Hand them over!"
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly. He could meet such a person anywhere, even in the depths of the Chaos …
Luo Zheng smiled faintly and replied with a calm expression, "The bones in the Eternal Desolate Plains are ownerless. Naturally, they belong to whoever finds them. Why should I hand them over?"

Chapter 2119
The girl in the bright red robe blinked her eyes and said, "Hey, you better hand over the Blood Desolate Bone obediently. My little senior brother is kind-hearted and will spare your life!"
The young man and the young man could tell that Luo Zheng was a True God and not a Desolate God.
In this world, True Gods were not the mainstream. Some Desolate Gods would open up their inner world to cultivate in order to obtain certain divine powers. However, their main goal was still to cultivate as a Desolate God. All other means were just supplementary.
It was extremely rare to see someone like Luo Zheng who cultivated as a True God.
Luo Zheng's cultivation level and strength was naturally not something they placed in their eyes.
"Is that so?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly. He was still maintaining his self-control.
In this unfamiliar environment, he tried his best not to cause any trouble.
Following that, he smiled slightly. Suddenly, he exerted strength in his legs and his body suddenly shot backwards!
"Whew!"
With such a rapid retreat, Luo Zheng was like a nimble bird. He flew at high speed in the Eternal Wasteland.
The girl and the young man clearly did not expect Luo Zheng to dare to escape. The four of them were stunned. The young man's face immediately revealed a tinge of hostility, "A nameless nobody dares to escape in front of me. What a joke …"
"Hu hu …"
The young man's body emitted a bright yellow glow. His body suddenly expanded!
A hundred feet …
A hundred feet …
In a blink of an eye, he had transformed into a five hundred feet tall giant. He charged straight at Luo Zheng.
Although the young man had become so huge, he was still very agile. His speed was even faster than Luo Zheng's!
The girl looked in the direction that Luo Zheng had fled in and sighed helplessly, "This guy is really stupid. If he had obediently handed over the Desolate Bone, he could still have kept his life. Now that he has angered little senior brother, how can he still live?"
Following that, the girl also flew up. The two guards behind her also followed …
With one chasing and the other rushing, the two of them were extremely fast. In a blink of an eye, they had traversed over a hundred miles.
The young man behind him emitted a powerful aura. When the Yin people crawling on the ground felt this aura, their faces all revealed a look of fear. Almost instinctively, they fled in all directions.
"Even with such a huge body, its speed is still so fast …" Luo Zheng frowned. To his surprise, he didn't get rid of this guy.
Not only that, the distance between the two of them was getting closer and closer.
In the beginning, Luo Zheng was still able to shake off the young man by more than ten miles. But now, the young man had quietly arrived behind Luo Zheng.
"If you run, you'll die anyway. What's the point?"
The young man's cold voice sounded. He flipped his palm and slapped down on Luo Zheng's head.
This young man was several hundred feet tall. Compared to him, Luo Zheng was as small as a mosquito. How could Luo Zheng escape from this slap?
"Hmph!"
Luo Zheng snorted coldly. His eyes finally flashed with a strong killing intent.
His forbearance was only to avoid trouble, but it did not mean that he was afraid of the other party!
His body flipped in the air and the Shadow Bearing Sword was unsheathed!
"Whoosh!"
Accompanied by the crisp sound of the sword, a slender sword was slashed.
A slender sword-light shot out. This sword-light was suffused with a faint cyan light, elongating into a crescent moon shape.
The young man didn't take this sword light seriously at all …
In this Desolate God's world, the only way to survive was to have a strong physical body. In his eyes, the attack of a True God was laughable. Even though he sensed that the sword light was somewhat extraordinary, he wasn't vigilant enough.
A single slap was enough to shatter the sword light. If that wasn't enough, then two slaps would be enough.
But this time, the young man realized that he was wrong.
When his palm came into contact with the sword light, he suddenly felt a shocking pain.
His huge hand was like tofu under the sword light. A huge crack appeared in the middle of his palm. Half of his hand was directly chopped off and heavily fell to the ground!
"This is …" The young man almost couldn't believe his eyes.
This guy, who was as small as a mosquito in front of him, could actually slash out with such a terrifying sword?
Taking advantage of the young man's momentary daze, Luo Zheng charged into the sky.
Previously, Luo Zheng didn't want to start a fight, which was why he had fled. Now that he had made a move, he naturally wouldn't let this person go.
Even if Luo Zheng wanted to let him go, the other party would think that he was afraid of the enemy and pursue him relentlessly!
Luo Zheng flew up along the young man's three hundred meter long arm. In the blink of an eye, he had already climbed to a height of four hundred meters.
The emotions in his eyes disappeared without a trace. The Shadow Bearing Sword in his hand emitted a faint green light. An indescribable might crazily condensed.
"Dao Fusion Sword! Die! "
His body turned, and the Dao Fusion energy that had been brewing in his internal world for a long time exploded.
Luo Zheng's cultivation had greatly increased. He was now an Upper True God. Now that he had the Shadow Bearing Sword, he was much stronger than when he was in the Land of the Dao Battle.
The green crescent-shaped sword light shot out. This attack was more than a hundred times stronger than Luo Zheng's first attack. Its speed was so fast that it was impossible for anyone to react …
While the young man was still in a daze, the crescent-shaped sword light had already silently slashed across his neck.
The young man stared blankly at Luo Zheng. His huge body stood motionless in place …
Soon, his head, which was comparable to a mountain, slid to the side and rolled off his neck.
"Boom!"
It fiercely smashed into the ground, creating a large crater.
The huge body swayed back and forth for a moment before it fell to the ground as well.
Luo Zheng hovered in the air, his chest rising and falling as he panted.
Killing this guy was easier than he had imagined …
Desolate Gods seemed to be easier to kill than True Gods because all of their vitality came from their physical bodies.
If a True God had their neck severed, it wouldn't be a fatal injury.
To a True God, the truly fatal injury would be the destruction of their soul and the shattering of their dantian. Desolate Gods relied heavily on their physical bodies, so they would die after their necks were severed!
"Ah!"
At this moment, a scream came from the distance. This scream came from the red-robed girl.
The girl and the two guards had overestimated the young man's strength, so they only followed behind him slowly. In their opinion, the young man could kill Luo Zheng in the blink of an eye. Luo Zheng couldn't have escaped far.
They never expected that the young man's body would suddenly tremble and his head would be separated from his body.
They didn't even see how the young man's neck was severed …
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
Dense Desolate God power escaped from the young man's huge body. At the same time, the young man's corpse began to shrink. In a few breaths of time, it returned to the size of a normal person.
"You, you, you …" The girl stared at Luo Zheng in fear, her face filled with disbelief.

Chapter 2120
Luo Zheng's expression was surprisingly calm. He lightly swung the Chengying Sword and said indifferently, "Who he is, what does it have to do with me? I only know that he's courting death! "
"You …"
The red-robed girl clenched her teeth. She didn't expect to meet a guy who wasn't afraid of anything.
"I think you're the one courting death!"
One of the two guards who followed closely behind shouted sternly.
When they saw the young man killed, their expressions were extremely unsightly.
The Eternal Wasteland was boundless, and the Canglan Sect was the overlord of this region.
The young man killed by Luo Zheng was the most talented disciple of the Canglan Sect in the last hundred years. Not long ago, he had been accepted as a personal disciple by the Sect Master. Now, he had actually died on a trip outside …
Thinking of the Sect Master's cruel and tyrannical personality, how could the two guards report back?
"Huhuhu …"
The two guards' bodies flickered with a faint yellow light, and their bodies began to expand.
These two guards were slightly stronger than the young man, and their bodies were about five hundred feet tall.
In the blink of an eye, two men as tall as mountains appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
"Don't kill him. He killed Little Senior Brother. When we return to the Sect, we'll definitely be punished. If we capture him, we can share Master's anger," the red-robed girl said coldly.
She was also a personal disciple of the Canglan Sect's Sect Master, so she was qualified to call the young man Little Senior Brother. Her talent was also very high, but her cultivation was much weaker than the young man's.
When the two guards heard this, they also felt that the red-robed girl's words were reasonable. One of the guards stared at Luo Zheng and shouted coldly, "Boy, I advise you to obediently surrender. Don't make any unnecessary resistance, or else you'll be tortured!"
In their opinion, Luo Zheng's sword technique was indeed strange, and its power was simply unreasonable.
However, the young man just died under Luo Zheng's sword because he underestimated the enemy. Now that the two of them were going all out, Luo Zheng had no chance of winning.
"Surrender without resistance?" The corner of Luo Zheng's mouth rose slightly, and he gently raised the Chengying Sword in his hand. "Do you think that's possible?"
"Don't waste words with him. Cripple him," one of the guards said.
The two giants no longer hesitated. One on the left and the other on the right, they were like two huge mountains that surrounded Luo Zheng.
The red-robed girl stared at Luo Zheng. From the beginning to the end, she didn't find the slightest bit of panic on Luo Zheng's face. He was very calm. Naturally, she didn't know where his confidence came from. Or did he have something special to rely on?
When the two huge guards rushed towards Luo Zheng, the surface of the Chengying Sword in Luo Zheng's hand suddenly flickered with two alternating lusters.
One was green while the other was grey. This was the True Meaning of Good and Evil in One Thought!
Although Luo Zheng hadn't completely grasped A Thought of Good and Evil, Luo Zheng had fused with the sword soul of the Shadow Bearing Sword. The two had become one, and Luo Zheng naturally comprehended the divine ability within.
"Good and Evil Frenzy!"
This was a soul attack formed by the Shadow Bearing Sword.
"Buzz buzz …"
The two guards immediately felt as if their brains had been struck by a heavy hammer. The good and evil thoughts in their souls were stimulated at the same time. At this moment, their thoughts were incomparably chaotic. Their eyes were filled with confusion and bewilderment.
In a confrontation of this level, a moment of distraction was enough to determine the outcome!
Luo Zheng's figure was like a swallow flying through the air. The green light on the longsword in his hand suddenly increased. Two crescent-like sword lights shot out from both sides.
"Pu, pu!"
Two muffled sounds were heard.
A thin mark appeared on the necks of the two guards at the same time. Their huge heads immediately fell from their necks.
Even after their deaths, the two pairs of huge eyes still had blank expressions. Their souls were still in a state of chaos.
"Hua hua hua …"
Then the power of the Rage God in their bodies began to wildly dissipate. Their bodies quickly shrank and returned to the size of a normal person.
Luo Zheng held the Chengying Sword in his hand and glanced at the red-robed girl indifferently.
The red-robed girl floated in the air and stared blankly at the headless corpses of the two guards. For a moment, she didn't react.
Little Senior Brother and the two guards had become dead people in a short period of time …
And the opponent was only a True God …
Didn't people who cultivated to the True God Realm have average strength?
At least in the Canglan Sect, very few people chose to cultivate to the True God Realm. Only a few geniuses would try to open up the Inner World. That was only a supplementary method.
How could this guy be so strong?
Luo Zheng's performance had completely subverted her understanding of common sense!
Only now did she understand why Luo Zheng had been so calm and collected.
Until Luo Zheng glanced at her. She couldn't help but shiver. Now she was the only one left!
Her strength was far inferior to Little Senior Brother's and the two guards'. Would this person let her leave safely?
"Whoosh!"
Luo Zheng's figure floated over. The Shadow Bearing Sword in his hand was pointed between the red-robed girl's eyebrows.
No matter which power these four people came from, only by killing them would he be able to prevent any future troubles.
However, Luo Zheng wasn't a bloodthirsty person. This girl had only helped the youth to ridicule him and hadn't attacked him. Therefore, Luo Zheng was somewhat hesitant. He needed a reason to kill this girl. He asked indifferently, "You don't plan to resist?"
Looking at Luo Zheng's indifferent expression, the red-robed girl clenched her teeth and suddenly raised her head. She stuck out her chest and stubbornly stared at Luo Zheng.
The Shadow Bearing Sword's sharp edge had already cut her forehead.
Looking at the fresh blood flowing down her beautiful face, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed with a trace of helplessness.
The Shadow Bearing Sword in his hand spun and was stored in his spatial ring. His figure flipped in the air and flew towards the side of the Eternal Wasteland.
After the red-robed girl saw Luo Zheng's figure completely disappear, she let out a sigh of relief. She slowly descended from the sky and knelt on the ground. Her entire body was limp and powerless. She had just walked through the gates of hell.
Letting that red-robed girl go could be considered as burying a large hidden danger for himself. However, Luo Zheng found it difficult to go against his own principles.
However, the Eternal Wasteland was so vast. No matter how powerful that lousy sect was, it was still a small matter for them to find him. Moreover, he might not stay in that village forever …
…
…
In the tribe, Fu Chu was studying the cultivation technique Luo Zheng had given him.
This cultivation technique was taken by Luo Zheng from the Immortal Mansion's library. It could be considered an extremely unique ability.
Fu Chu was very unwilling at first. After all, it was Sister Xiao Yun's idea to make him cultivate …
However, with his eight extraordinary meridians sealed, he couldn't cultivate the Rage God. It seemed that this was the only path he could take. With the thought that it was better than nothing, he studied it for a while.
He didn't expect that after just a few glances, he would be attracted by this cultivation technique.
The cultivation techniques in the Domain of the Gods could be considered to have been inherited from Chiyou. In the Primal Chaos, cultivating the True God was also the mainstream. However, at the bottom of the Primal Chaos, because of the existence of the "desolate bones", it had fallen into decline …
The cultivation technique Luo Zheng took out was personally created by Gu Bei. Naturally, it was the essence of the essence.

Chapter 2121
"Sis, who do you think that guy is!?" Fu Chu asked curiously as he held the scroll in his hand.
Little Yun pondered for a moment before lightly saying, "He doesn't seem to be from our world."
"Not from our world?" Fu Chu revealed a strange look. "Are there other worlds?"
Little Yun looked up at the sky outside the straw hut. "Of course there are. It is said that our ancestors came from deep space, in an extremely high place. That place is called the Mother World … And we are a group of people that were exiled by that world. We are their descendants …"
Looking at the sky from her angle, there were only patches of brown clouds that were so thick that they could not be dispersed. Those were balls of Chaotic Qi.
"He's from the Mother World?" Fu Chu's eyes flashed with a strange luster.
Little Yun smiled. "I'm just guessing! Cultivate well … Look at Luo Zheng. He has such powerful means even though he is a True God. In the future, if you have the chance to return to the main family, you might be able to prove yourself as a True God. "
Fu Chu curled his lips and continued studying the scroll in his hand. Some of the methods recorded in it made him eager to try them out. He wanted to open up a Divine Sea that belonged to him.
At this moment, a sound came from outside the straw hut. The siblings looked over and saw Luo Zheng.
"You're back." Little Yun revealed a gentle smile.
"How's the harvest? Did you not get a single desolate bone? " Fu Chu revealed a look of dissatisfaction. Although he was not a Rage God, he had rich experience in determining the location of desolate bones. From his point of view, Luo Zheng would probably return empty-handed after entering the Eternal Wastelands.
Noticing Fu Chu's expression, Luo Zheng did not say a word and stepped into the straw hut.
"The appearance of desolate bones is similar to ordinary bones. It is indeed difficult to find. Hundreds of people in the tribe can only find a few pieces a month by luck. Actually, there's no need to rush." Little Yun said unhurriedly.
"I already said that if you bring me along, we won't end up empty-handed." Fu Chu curled his lips.
After Luo Zheng sat down cross-legged, he smiled at the siblings and reached out his hand.
A row of desolate bones neatly appeared in front of him!
Upon seeing the desolate bones, Xiao Yun and Fu Chu were stunned.
Especially Fu Chu. He widened his eyes and stared at Luo Zheng before staring at the desolate bones. "One, two, three, four … twelve pieces! And three of them are especially big! "
Previously, Fu Chu had risked his life to find three desolate bones, and he had nearly died in the Eternal Desolate Plains. He never expected Luo Zheng to find so many desolate bones in a day. This was something he could not accept.
Even Xiaoyun, who was usually calm, was looking at the desolate bones with envy.
Although the tribe received a few pieces of desolate bones every month, they were still the entire tribe's property. Little Yun could not use them for herself, as she was always in need of desolate bones.
This time, Fu Chu had risked his life to get her two desolate bones. Although Xiao Yun had scolded Fu Chu, she was still very happy. It was too precious to her.
Just as the siblings stared with widened eyes, Luo Zheng extended his hand and gently swiped again. Another twelve desolate bones were lined up in front of them.
"…"
"…"
Xiaoyun and Fu Chu stared blankly.
However, they saw Luo Zheng's hand move again. Another twelve desolate bones appeared.
With Luo Zheng's repeated swipes of his hand, rows of desolate bones were laid out in front of the siblings …
Xiaoyun and Fu Chu were surprised and incredulous at first, but towards the end, they were numb!
"This, this … There are a total of 89 pieces." Xiaoyun looked at the desolate bones in a daze. The power of the Rage God exuded from the desolate bones was extremely attractive to her.
Fu Chu also looked at Luo Zheng in a daze. "Did you search the entire Eternal Desolate Plains?"
After all, the area they were in was the most barren area in the Eternal Desolate Plains. The bones had been turned over and sifted countless times by countless people, so the number of desolate bones was extremely rare.
To the powerful Rage Gods, this area was not worth their effort to search for desolate bones. Eventually, it was the tribe's turn to scavenge …
Occasionally, they would be lucky enough to find one or two desolate bones.
However, Luo Zheng was able to find nearly a hundred desolate bones in a day. This was completely unbelievable!
If the large sects were to know of this, they would probably reclaim this area.
"How did you do it?" Xiaoyun's shock only lasted for a short period of time.
She was not a girl who had never seen the world.
If she had not been stranded here, even if there were tens of thousands or hundreds of thousands of desolate bones, she would not be moved, much less a hundred desolate bones. To the truly powerful Rage Gods, it was indeed nothing.
However, to find so many desolate bones in this barren land was another matter altogether.
"Luck," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
Fu Chu rolled his eyes. "Only an idiot would believe in luck!"
Finding two or three desolate bones, or even seven or eight, could be attributed to luck. Eighty-nine desolate bones could also be attributed to luck. Even a child could not be coaxed.
Luo Zheng did not continue explaining. He gently pushed twelve desolate bones in front of Xiaoyun. "These desolate bones are for you. You also need to cultivate."
Although Luo Zheng also needed desolate bones to cultivate, these desolate bones were not difficult to obtain. He had just arrived here and had a temporary home, so he was willing to share it with Xiaoyun.
"These … all belong to me?" Xiaoyun's face turned excited.
She indeed needed the desolate bones to break through her bottleneck, but she knew the value of the desolate bones.
Luo Zheng had obtained so many desolate bones. She was already very satisfied with sharing two or three desolate bones. Seeing Luo Zheng push twelve desolate bones in front of her without any hesitation, she felt guilty, but she could not bear to reject it.
Fu Chu had originally planned on giving Luo Zheng a few more desolate bones to his sister, but seeing Luo Zheng being so generous, he was too embarrassed to continue.
"Yes, but there's still one more desolate bone that I would like to consult you on," Luo Zheng said.
Xiaoyun was somewhat puzzled and immediately asked, "There's still one more desolate bone?"
Luo Zheng's spatial ring flashed once again. With a "Dong" sound, a triangular desolate bone about the height of a person fell to the ground, causing the entire straw hut to tremble.
What appeared in front of them was the blood-colored desolate bone.
This time, Xiaoyun stared at the huge blood-colored desolate bone in a daze. She was completely dumbfounded.
After being stunned for a full four to five breaths, she came back to her senses and urged, "Quick, quickly put it away!"
"What's wrong?" Luo Zheng was somewhat puzzled, but he still kept the blood-colored desolate bone in his spatial ring.
Xiaoyun walked to the straw hut's door and vigilantly looked outside. After locking the straw hut's door, she said, "If this desolate bone is seen by others, it will be troublesome …"

Chapter 2122
Xiao Yun widened her eyes as she sized up the blood-colored desolate bone.
She slowly walked closer and stretched out her jade-like hands to gently stroke the surface of the desolate bone.
Luo Zheng did not say a word as he watched Xiao Yun's actions. From the looks of it, Xiao Yun was planning on drawing out the power of the Rage God from the blood-colored desolate bone …
As Xiao Yun continued to stroke it, her finger gently pressed against the surface of the blood-colored desolate bone.
"Si Si …"
A wisp of blood-red power of the Rage God constantly swirled around her fingertip.
"What a violent power of the Rage God." Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly.
A typical desolate bone's power of the Rage God did not have any special emotions. It was just a strange energy.
However, this blood-colored power of the Rage God contained a violent aura, as if this energy would explode at any moment.
Xiao Yun revealed a matter-of-fact expression as she smiled. Following that, she confined the wisp of Rage God's power between her hands, allowing it to settle.
The power of the Rage God was like a drop of ink in clear water, transforming into an unbelievable shape.
Finally, the power of the Rage God finally settled down, transforming into a blood-colored wolf shadow. The wolf's mouth was wide open, revealing a ferocious look. It emitted an intense violent aura …
"What kind of creature is this?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly.
Xiao Yun gently exhaled and said with a smile, "As expected, it's the Spirit Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone!"
"Spirit Fiend Wolf God?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yea!" Xiao Yun looked at the small blood-colored desolate bone in her hand as her eyes sparkled, "I never expected that I would be able to dig out a bloodline desolate bone in this barren area. Furthermore, it's such a large piece! On the bloodline heritage roll, the Spirit Fiend Wolf God is ranked 61st. It's also a very powerful bloodline. "
Back then, Little Yun had dug out two desolate bones that contained the Chiyou bloodline. They were only the size of an egg, but because the desolate bones contained the bloodline, their value was a hundred times higher than ordinary desolate bones!
"According to what I know, there is a large family clan in the north-leaning bone pagoda. They have passed down the bloodline of the Spirit Fiend Wolf God for generations. They crave for the Spirit Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone. You might not be able to absorb this bloodline desolate bone, but if you sell this desolate bone, you can at least exchange it for fifty desolate bones of this size!" Little Yun's voice was trembling. It was obvious that she was trying her best to calm her excitement.
Fifty pieces of desolate bones that were the height of a person was a shocking amount of wealth.
"This desolate bone … is it really impossible for me to refine and absorb it?" Luo Zheng asked.
He had heard from Little Yun that desolate bones of different bloodlines could not be casually refined unless one had the same bloodline.
Hearing this, Xiao Yun smiled, "You can try."
Luo Zheng naturally did not believe it. Compared to selling the desolate bone, he was more inclined to absorb it himself.
Hence, he stretched out his hand and gently stroked the surface of the blood-colored desolate bone. Using the Dripping Spring technique that Little Yun had taught him, he extracted the power of the Rage God from within.
"Si Si …"
After a while, a blood-colored power of the Rage God slowly surged out.
Seeing the violent power of the Rage God, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently guided it. He never expected that the power of the Rage God seemed to have its own consciousness and refused to submit!
However, the consciousness of the power of the Rage God was too weak. How could it resist Luo Zheng?
After Luo Zheng strengthened his perception, he finally forced it into his body …
The blood-colored power of the Rage God had indeed seeped into his body, but Luo Zheng quickly discovered something amiss.
Normal power of the Rage God did not have any resistance with his body and would instantly merge into it. However, this blood-colored power of the Rage God was constantly repelling his body, preventing it from merging!
After trying a few times, Luo Zheng had no choice but to retract his perception.
Following that, the wisp of power of the Rage God dissipated from his body …
"Indeed, I can't absorb it." Luo Zheng's face revealed a wry smile.
Little Yun smiled faintly, as if she was used to it, "Of course. In this world, bloodline is the greatest advantage. Those large factions that occupy the bone pagodas all have extremely powerful bloodlines! They are born with nobility. It is not something that you … and I can compare with. "
From Little Yun's point of view, Luo Zheng's True God cultivation strength was ridiculously strong. However, compared to the true experts of this world, it was still not enough.
Luo Zheng did not mind, "If that's the case, this blood-colored desolate bone can only be used to exchange for normal desolate bones."
Only with a sufficient amount of desolate bones could he quickly cultivate into a Rage God and increase his strength.
After keeping the blood-colored desolate bone, Luo Zheng asked again, "Right, what is the bloodline heritage list that you mentioned just now?"
Little Yun was already used to Luo Zheng's "ignorance". She explained, "This bloodline heritage list is a ranking created by the large factions that occupy the bone pagodas. It is a list that is ranked according to the strength of the bloodline."
Upon hearing this, Luo Zheng's mind stirred slightly. He asked, "I wonder what the Chiyou bloodline is ranked on this bloodline heritage list?"
Just as his mother said, Chiyou was a top expert in the Mother World, and the Li Min was once one of the few large factions.
The desolate bones at the bottom of the Chaos seemed to have descended from the Mother World. Luo Zheng naturally wanted to know the ranking of the Chiyou bloodline.
"Third," Little Yun answered without hesitation.
"Third …"
"Yes." Xiao Yun said, "The Chiyou bloodline is also an extremely powerful bloodline. In fact, it's not much different from the second-ranked Xuanyuan bloodline. These two great bloodline inheritances have been fighting with each other for countless years."
"Xuanyuan!" Luo Zheng's eyes flashed.
He knew that the Li Min's enemy was the Xuanyuan clan, and that was why they had the Celestial Realm ship.
He did not expect that the conflict between the bloodlines of these two clans would extend to the bottom of the Chaos, this desolate land full of bones …
"Then what is the bloodline ranked first on the bloodline heritage list?" Luo Zheng asked again.
What could be an existence that surpassed Xuanyuan and Chiyou?
"The Nuwa bloodline." Little Yun smiled faintly.
"The Nuwa bloodline …"
Luo Zheng silently memorized it.
He did not know much about the Mother World, but here, he could indirectly obtain some information.
However, Luo Zheng had never heard of the Nuwa clan from his mother. Could it be a large clan in the Mother World?
He even thought of the "Shadow of Nuwa" that Luo Yan had summoned. He heard from his mother that Luo Yan's bloodline was different from his. Could it be that Luo Yan had the Nuwa bloodline?
These were just preliminary guesses.
After understanding enough, Luo Zheng did not ask any more questions. He sat down cross-legged and used the Draining Spring Technique that Little Yun had taught him. He extracted the power of the Rage God from the desolate bones and integrated it into his body.
Just like before, the power of the Rage God did not escape after it entered Luo Zheng's body!

Chapter 2123
In a night's time …
Luo Zheng had refined all seventy-seven desolate bones.
As his Leakless True Body did not emit any of the Rage God's power, his efficiency was more than three times that of Little Yun's.
The Rage God's power permeated every part of Luo Zheng's body. This growth gave Luo Zheng a very direct feeling!
Early the next morning, Luo Zheng left the straw hut. With a thought, he stimulated the Rage God's power in his body.
"Phew … Phew … Phew …"
His fleshly body instantly expanded!
Luo Zheng was originally eight feet tall, but in a blink of an eye, he became thirty feet tall!
Compared to Little Yun and the youths in the Eternal Wasteland, he was naturally far inferior.
However, the growth that Luo Zheng received from refining the desolate bones was very direct.
Luo Zheng stood in his original spot and sized up his hands. The straw hut in front of him only reached his knees …
"This Rage God's power … is very powerful!"
His eyes faintly revealed a look of excitement. This brand new body-tempering technique was very compatible with his body.
If he were to absorb enough Rage God's power, he would be able to transform into a giant that was hundreds of feet tall. How strong would he become?
Luo Zheng was looking forward to the arrival of this day.
In just a few days, he had cultivated to such a level. This rascal was really …
At the straw hut's entrance, Little Yun observed Luo Zheng with a complicated look. She had another plan in mind, but she knew that she could not reveal it at this moment.
Although Luo Zheng was generous, he would not risk his life for her and Fu Chu, unless she could really help him. If he was really trustworthy, he might be able to reveal the secret.
…
…
For the past few days, Fu Chu had been studying the scroll that Luo Zheng had given him.
Whenever he encountered a problem that he did not understand, Fu Chu would consult Luo Zheng.
However, Luo Zheng did not spend most of his time in the tribe. Instead, he would head to the Eternal Wasteland to continue searching for desolate bones.
Luo Zheng's method of searching for desolate bones was very efficient. As long as he released his spiritual perception, he would be able to see everything in a radius of tens of miles. The desolate bones that were hidden within could not escape.
But his luck soon ran out.
After all, no matter how efficient it was, the desolate bones in the Eternal Desolate Plains were limited, especially in this barren land.
After three consecutive days, Luo Zheng had only harvested seven or eight desolate bones.
For ordinary people, this was already a great harvest, but for Luo Zheng, it was still too little.
Hence, Luo Zheng decided to sell the blood-colored desolate bone.
Xiao Yun just asked Luo Zheng to wait.
Luo Zheng's blood-colored desolate bone was no trifling matter. If the large factions knew about it, they would definitely snatch it.
She suggested that Luo Zheng wait for the desolate bones buyers to come to the tribe before Luo Zheng would follow them to the nearest city and auction off the blood colored desolate bones.
Another two days passed ….
Luo Zheng saw three people in black robes flying towards him from the horizon. Without exception, all three of them were Aragami.
In a blink of an eye, the three of them landed on the square in the middle of the tribe. Following that, they walked towards Xiao Yun's straw hut.
"How many desolate bones have you harvested this month?" One of them shouted loudly. His voice immediately spread to every corner of the tribe.
Generations of the Black Iron tribe had made a living by harvesting desolate bones. Only desolate bones could be exchanged for necessities.
Hearing this voice, the strong youths of the tribe walked out of their straw huts and gathered around.
The desolate bones that they had harvested were all placed in Xiao Yun's hands, but they would also supervise and prevent Xiao Yun from embezzling the desolate bones that belonged to the tribe.
Xiao Yun also left her straw hut and said to the leader, "Brother Ji, we have harvested a total of 23 desolate bones this month."
"Only twenty-three?" Brother Ji showed a hint of disdain.
Every month, they had to come to this godforsaken place to purchase desolate bones. These tribes were so poor that it made one's hair stand on end. In a month, they could only accumulate tens or even a few desolate bones. It was not worth the trip.
"The number of desolate bones in this region is decreasing. Our tribe is also unable to go deep into the Eternal Wastelands. This amount is already the result of our hard work," Xiao Yun replied.
The other person among the three smiled faintly and said, "Forget it, Brother Ji. Do you expect them to be lucky enough to dig out hundreds or thousands of desolate bones in this crappy place? Quickly collect the desolate bones. We still have to rush back before it gets dark! "
"Hmph!" Brother Ji coldly snorted, "If there are only about 20 desolate bones next month, then don't make us personally make the trip!"
Xiao Yun could only helplessly smile before handing the desolate bones in her hands to Brother Ji.
Brother Ji threw out a large bag and coldly said, "This is your reward."
Following that, the three of them planned to leave.
At this moment, Xiao Yun suddenly said, "Brother Ji, hold on."
When the leader heard this, he frowned and asked, "What's the matter?"
"My friend has an item that he wants to entrust to your merchant group's auction house. Can my friend go with you and recommend it?" Xiao Yun asked.
"Auction?" Brother Ji was slightly surprised. Typically, items that were qualified to be auctioned were extremely rare treasures. What was there that was worth auctioning in such a remote place?
Brother Ji said with a sullen face, "What crappy item is it that requires our merchant group to auction it?"
Xiao Yun pursed her lips and immediately said, "I can't say for now. I'll just have to trouble Brother Ji to bring my friend back."
With that said, Xiao Yun pointed at Luo Zheng in front of the straw hut.
The Evil Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone was too valuable.
If these people knew that Luo Zheng had a bloodline desolate bone in his hands, they would definitely not allow this desolate bone to enter the auction house. They would probably kill him on the spot, or even wipe out the entire tribe …
Brother Ji glanced at Luo Zheng. Luo Zheng had absorbed quite a number of desolate bones, and was faintly releasing the power of the Rage God.
However, he had only cultivated for a short period of time, and the number of desolate bones he had absorbed was not enough. Naturally, he was not worthy of Brother Ji's attention. Now, Brother Ji was extremely impatient, "I don't have the time!"
With that said, Brother Ji was about to fly away.
However, Xiao Yun quickly walked forward and suddenly stuffed three desolate bones into Brother Ji's hands. She said with a smile, "It's just a small token of my appreciation. I'll have to trouble Brother Ji!"
The three desolate bones were excavated by Luo Zheng, so the people in the tribe naturally did not say anything.
Brother Ji looked at the desolate bones in his hand and revealed a strange expression. He could not figure out what good item this kid had that was worth Xiao Yun using three desolate bones to bribe him.
However, three desolate bones as a reward was already not bad. Furthermore, Brother Ji was only recommending Luo Zheng to his merchant group's auction house. It was a trivial matter.
Hence, Brother Ji smiled at Luo Zheng, "Kid, keep up!"

Chapter 2124
Brother Ji and his group flew up at an extremely fast speed.
"Brother Ji, can that kid keep up?" One of them asked.
Brother Ji, who was at the front, smiled faintly and said, "Whether he can keep up or not will depend on his own ability."
Trying to bribe him with three pieces of desolate bones? That was too cheap. Hence, Brother Ji had never thought of doing things properly. Wouldn't it be better to just leave that kid behind?
"Huff … Huff …"
As soon as he finished speaking, Brother Ji deliberately increased his speed. The other two followed closely behind him.
"Hmm?"
Luo Zheng had been following behind at a leisurely pace.
When he saw the people in front of him suddenly increasing their speed, the corner of his mouth curled up slightly. Naturally, he also increased his speed.
Brother Ji glanced at Luo Zheng behind him faintly. His eyes revealed a trace of astonishment.
That kid was still following behind him for more than thirty meters. He actually did not fall behind.
"Brother Ji, that guy's speed is not slow …"
"Looking at him, he seems to be a beginner Aragami?"
"I think he has the cultivation level of a True God. Moreover, it's not low. That's strange."
The two of them muttered.
Brother Ji chuckled and said, "Who cares what he is. Both of you, keep up!"
As soon as he finished speaking, the power of the Aragami flashed out from Brother Ji's body. His aura became stronger. "Whoosh" He suddenly turned into a graceful swan and shot forward at an even higher speed.
The two behind him looked at each other. They could only activate their own power of the Aragami. Even if they wanted to keep up with Brother Ji, they had to give it their all.
When Luo Zheng saw this scene, his expression was indifferent.
The Chaotic Qi in his body circulated slightly and once again caught up with their speed …
They sprinted for the time it took for an incense stick to burn. Brother Ji, who was at the front, thought that he should have left them behind this time, right? When he turned his head again, his eyeballs suddenly widened.
Luo Zheng was still following closely behind them. He still maintained a distance of thirty meters. Even his expression did not change.
"This guy …" Brother Ji was getting more energetic.
"Brother Ji, slow down …"
"I can't keep up!"
The two of them couldn't take it anymore. Even though their bodies had been nourished by the power of the Rage God and were much stronger than ordinary people, their cultivations were weaker than Brother Ji's. They had pushed their power of the Rage God to the limit and their bodies couldn't take it anymore. At this moment, they were out of breath.
Brother Ji was now arguing with Luo Zheng, so how could he possibly listen?
The power of the Rage God that burst out from his body had become even denser, and his speed had increased by another thirty percent. This was his limit!
Those two people really couldn't keep up, so they simply gave up …
Luo Zheng smiled faintly as he watched Brother Ji leave quickly. His body was as light as a swallow as he flew with the wind, increasing his speed by thirty percent.
In the blink of an eye, Luo Zheng brushed past the two men and chased after Brother Ji.
"Huff huff huff …"
As he swept past, a gust of wind slapped the two men's faces. The two men could only stare at him in shock.
"That guy … can actually keep up with Brother Ji's full speed?"
"Is it possible for a True God to be so powerful?"
In the eyes of these Aragami, being a True God was not a proper occupation. They had never seen a powerful True God before. Therefore, Luo Zheng's speed had completely subverted their knowledge …
After about the time it took for three incense sticks to burn … Brother Ji could no longer hold on.
When he turned around to look, Luo Zheng was still maintaining a distance of a few hundred feet behind him. He was not in a hurry, and his face was calm.
Brother Ji finally gave up. He realized that it was impossible to get rid of Luo Zheng. He could only slow down.
At the same time, he was starting to admire Luo Zheng. After all, in Brother Ji's eyes, Luo Zheng was just a fledgling Aragami. However, his performance gave Brother Ji a feeling that he was unfathomable …
Along the way to the north, Luo Zheng saw large patches of grayish-brown soil.
Now, he knew that there were many areas at the bottom of the Chaos that were covered with skeletons. All the areas that were covered with skeletons were collectively known as the Eternal Wasteland.
The Eternal Wasteland was not suitable for living. For example, Little Yun's Wrapping Steel Tribe belonged to the lowest class. Only they would live at the edge of the Eternal Wasteland and scavenge desolate bones for a living …
But outside of the Eternal Wasteland, there were many cities.
This "Brother Ji" belonged to a deacon called the Falcon Caravan in Sand Heart City.
Little Yun told Luo Zheng that the Bloodline Desolate Bone could not be directly entrusted to the Falcon Caravan for auction.
The Falcon Caravan was just a small caravan. They did not have any credibility. If Luo Zheng directly took it out, the only outcome would be endless pursuit.
She told Luo Zheng to get the right to auction with the Falcon Caravan through this Brother Ji. She would never ask them for the real auction.
After about a day and a half, a huge pit suddenly appeared in front of Luo Zheng. It looked like a huge pit formed by quicksand. And in the middle of the quicksand pit, there was a city.
"It's no wonder it's called Sand Heart City …" Luo Zheng looked down and thought to himself.
After reaching the middle of the huge pit, under Brother Ji's lead, the four of them landed in front of the city gate.
"Luo Zheng, just follow me," Brother Ji said lightly.
Even though Sand Heart City was a remote city, its control was still very strict. There was a huge Sky Restriction outside the city. Anyone entering or exiting had to be checked.
After Brother Ji and the two people behind him showed their caravan nameplates, the guards at the gate let them pass.
However, when it was Luo Zheng's turn, one of the guards extended his spear and blocked Luo Zheng's chest. "Stop!"
"He is a guest of our caravan," Brother Ji said as he threw a light green nameplate to Luo Zheng. Every person who entered the city had to have a corresponding identity. When the guard saw the green nameplate, he let them pass.
After witnessing this scene, Luo Zheng understood why Xiao Yun wanted him to follow Brother Ji into the city. If he wanted to enter the city himself, he would probably have to go through a lot of trouble.
After arriving at Sand Heart City, Brother Ji led Luo Zheng straight to the Falcon Caravan.
After entering the Falcon Caravan's gate, Brother Ji communicated with the caravan people. Following that, an old man wearing a cloth on his head came out.
The old man sized up Luo Zheng and asked, "Young man, what do you want us to sell to the Falcon Caravan?"
Brother Ji and the rest also looked at Luo Zheng. They knew that Luo Zheng probably had something good to sell, but whether it was Xiao Yun or Luo Zheng, they were unwilling to reveal anything. Could it be some precious treasure?
"I want to sell this to your caravan," Luo Zheng said as he took out a large piece of desolate bone.
The desolate bone was about the size of a fist and was completely black.
When Brother Ji and the old man saw the desolate bone, their hearts skipped a beat.
Pure black bloodline desolate bones were ranked very high on the bloodline heritage list. One of them was the Heaven's Heart Rend bloodline, ranked 19th, while the other was the Extreme Yin Nether Glow bloodline, ranked 11th. The value of these two bloodline desolate bones was rather shocking.

Chapter 2125
Luo Zheng shook the black desolate bone and kept it in his Sumeru Ring. He smiled and said, "Can you give me an auction qualification?"
Although the Falcon Caravan also held small auctions, their influence was not as great as the auction house in Sand Heart City.
Some of the rarest treasures were auctioned in Sand Heart City's auction house, and Sand Heart City was directly under the large factions in the bone pagodas.
These large factions that occupied the bone pagodas were the creators of this world's order. Under normal circumstances, they would not covet Luo Zheng's bloodline desolate bone.
Hence, Luo Zheng only needed to obtain an auction qualification from the Falcon Caravan.
The old man's eyes revealed a look of doubt.
Although he had only taken a glance, he could sense the power of the Rage God emanating from the black desolate bone.
This old man was also an old member of the Falcon Caravan. It was not as if the old man had never seen the bloodline desolate bones of Heaven Heart Splitter and Extreme Yin Nether Glow. The aura they emitted was completely different from Luo Zheng's desolate bone.
However, the power of the Rage God in Luo Zheng's black desolate bone was indeed very special …
"Can you let me take a look at the black desolate bone again?" The old man asked.
"This … isn't very good, right?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a wary look.
Most people did not wish to reveal their treasures. The old man probably understood Luo Zheng's intentions. He chuckled and took out a white domino. On the domino, there was a lifelike falcon engraved on it.
"This is the auction qualification of our Falcon Caravan. With this domino, you can auction it in Sand Heart City's auction house." The old man handed the domino to Luo Zheng.
If one did not have a domino from a large caravan, it was not allowed to be auctioned. This was the rule.
After Luo Zheng received the domino, he thanked the old man and quickly left the Falcon Caravan.
Even after Luo Zheng left, the old man still had a faint smile on his face.
However, the moment Luo Zheng left, the old man's smile had completely disappeared. "That kid's black desolate bone has a very special origin!"
"What kind of special method?" Brother Ji asked. He had also sensed that the desolate bone's aura was very unique, but he did not know the origins of the black desolate bone.
"That is neither Heaven Heart Crack's bloodline Royal Aunt, nor is it Extreme Yin Nether Glow's bloodline desolate bone. This black desolate bone has never appeared before … Maybe it's a bloodline desolate bone that has never been discovered before!" The elder said.
No matter what …
The elder could sense an extraordinary aura from the desolate bone, so he concluded that it was not an ordinary item.
"Is that so …" Brother Ji was slightly surprised.
This elder had a very high standing in the Falcon Merchant Group because of his extraordinary eyesight. If he said that the black desolate bone was something good, then it was definitely not wrong.
Thus, Brother Ji asked, "Elder Dou, what do you mean?"
"Immediately bring people to snatch that black desolate bone. Kill anyone who resists." The elder's voice was cold.
"Killing people in the city?" Brother Ji was stunned.
Fighting was forbidden in Sand Heart City, and no one was allowed to violate it. Killing people here was too brazen.
"Just be careful. However, it's fine even if it blows up." The elder sneered.
The Falcon Merchant Group had been in Sand Heart City for many years, so they could still handle such matters.
After leaving the Falcon Merchant Group, Luo Zheng leisurely wandered around the streets. However, after he spread out his perception, he was secretly paying attention to the movements behind him.
Luo Zheng wandered around for a while, and his face was somewhat depressed. "Why haven't they appeared? This Falcon Merchant Group's efficiency is too slow …"
After a while, he sensed that two people had appeared behind him. These two people were sneakily following behind Luo Zheng.
"They have finally taken the bait …" Luo Zheng smiled and immediately turned into a small alley at the corner of the street.
After walking through the alleyway for a while, a masked man blocked his way.
Luo Zheng's expression changed. He wanted to retreat, but the other masked person blocked his path of retreat.
"My two brothers, what is the meaning of this?" Luo Zheng asked with a serious look.
"Hand over the black desolate bone and we will spare your life," the person in front said lightly. There was no emotion in his voice, and the power of the Rage God in his body was in a state that could erupt at any time.
The person behind him was the same. The two of them were probably experts in the Falcon Merchant Group.
"You are … from the Falcon Merchant Group?" Luo Zheng's face revealed a look of surprise.
"Yes or no, your question is meaningless." The person in front raised a finger and said, "Hand it over obediently and you will have a chance to live."
This person is so pretentious …
Luo Zheng sighed in his heart. Following that, a look of pain appeared on his face. "But this desolate bone was painstakingly taken by me …"
"You don't have to explain to me," the person stopped Luo Zheng from complaining. After saying that, the two masked men approached each other.
Seeing that they were about to make a move, Luo Zheng had no choice but to reluctantly take out the black desolate bone.
When the masked man in front saw the black desolate bone, he snatched it from Luo Zheng's hands. After scrutinizing it for a while, he stared at Luo Zheng and gave a strange laugh. "However, I never expected you to be so sensible!"
After saying that, the two of them ran away.
The two masked men turned a few alleys before the person in front took off his mask. He was Brother Ji, who had led Luo Zheng into the city.
"This is the desolate bone that Luo Zheng took out. Its aura is indeed very unique. I will let Old Dou extract the power of the Rage God from it and see what kind of bloodline it is …" Brother Ji touched the desolate bone and his eyes were filled with excitement. If this desolate bone was really priceless, he would have done a meritorious deed for the Falcon Merchant Group.
On the other side, Luo Zheng walked out of a small alley with a faint smile on his face.
He had expected the Falcon Merchant Group to do so.
That black desolate bone was forged by Luo Zheng.
The power of the Rage God was an extremely pure energy. It was passed down from the owner of the bone, and after countless years of accumulation, it was formed.
This energy was easy to absorb, but it was also very easy to be influenced!
Back in the Wiry Steel Tribe, Luo Zheng had injected A Thought of Good and Evil into a desolate bone, allowing the desolate bone to emit a unique aura. Then, he painted the surface of the desolate bone black, creating a unique "desolate bone".
Of course, this was not a bloodline desolate bone …
After giving the Falcon Merchant Group a perfunctory reply, Luo Zheng could use the Falcon Merchant Group's bone token to auction it off.
And in order to protect the large merchant groups, the auction house used an anonymous system. They only knew which merchant group was auctioning, and did not care who was auctioning!
This way, they could ensure their own safety, and also ensure that the Spirit Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone could be successfully auctioned off.

Chapter 2126
After spending a day, Luo Zheng had gained an understanding of Sand Heart City.
Within a radius of two hundred thousand miles, Sand Heart City was the only big city. And the big faction that controlled Sand Heart City was the Misty Sect on top of the Night Condensation Bone Tower.
The Misty Sect was considered a colossus to a normal Aragami.
The auction house in Sand Heart City was personally hosted by the Misty Sect's deacons. Various caravans would hand over their goods to the auction house for auction.
However, the auction house only held an auction once a month. There were still three days before the next auction.
Luo Zheng could only stay in Sand Heart City and wait.
After arriving at Sand Heart City, Luo Zheng understood that the value of desolate bones was not as great as he had imagined.
Everyone here cultivated the Aragami, and these people consumed quite a number of desolate bones. And the desolate bones were the currency they used to trade.
It was no wonder that the Falcon Caravan's' Brother Ji 'was so impatient in the Windy Steel Tribe. For example, the Falcon Caravan's deacons' monthly salary was several pieces of desolate bones. He was under the Falcon Caravan's orders to collect desolate bones. If he collected too little in one go, it would not be worth the trip.
The Windy Steel Tribe's Eternal Wilderness had been excavated countless times. It could be seen as a dried up mine. The people in the tribe could only scavenge for leftovers to survive.
Luo Zheng's harvest was not small, but it was just gathering the 'leftovers'. In this world, it was still nothing. As for the Spiritual Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone, it was Luo Zheng's luck.
"No wonder someone could transform into a thousand mile tall body. As long as there are enough desolate bones, the Aragami can grow indefinitely …"
However, to collect so many desolate bones was not an easy task.
The salary of a normal person in Sand Heart City was only one or two pieces of desolate bones a month. In a year, there were only about twenty desolate bones.
Only by joining a big faction would one be qualified to obtain more desolate bones.
Thinking of this, Luo Zheng had a plan in mind.
Three days later …
Luo Zheng brought the Falcon Caravan's domino to the auction house.
Before setting off, in order to avoid being recognized by the members of the Falcon Caravan, Luo Zheng used the Disguise Technique to change his appearance.
There were already a lot of people gathered in the auction house. These people had obtained the dominoes of the various caravans. Only those who had the dominoes of these caravans were qualified to participate in the auction.
After waiting for a while in the auction house, someone finally came out.
"Do you have the qualifications to participate in the auction?" The one who received Luo Zheng was a fair-skinned woman with a rather cold expression.
Luo Zheng nodded and took out the domino of the Falcon Caravan.
The fair-skinned woman glanced at it. "The Falcon Caravan's domino? They like to randomly distribute dominoes. Our auction house is not a place where any trash can enter. Don't waste my time. "
The fair-skinned woman was not polite at all.
However, the Falcon Caravan was considered the lowest level of caravan in Sand Heart City. Their reputation was indeed bad. However, the Falcon Caravan had been established very early in Sand Heart City. They occupied almost all of the low-end markets around Sand Heart City. The auction house had no choice but to work with them.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm as he said indifferently, "Whether it's trash or not, we'll know once we see it."
Hearing Luo Zheng's confidence, the fair-skinned woman's eyes revealed a trace of surprise. She smiled faintly and said, "Then come with me."
After saying that, she led the way with Luo Zheng following closely behind.
After passing through the hall of the auction house, they walked for about ten feet in a wide corridor before entering a compartment.
There were three middle-aged men sitting in the compartment. These three middle-aged men were the appraisers of the auction house. They were responsible for appraising all kinds of treasures, such as whether they were qualified to be auctioned in the auction house. At the same time, they could give a reserve price and so on.
"It's here." The fair-skinned woman led Luo Zheng in and waved her hand.
Luo Zheng stepped in and the three middle-aged men's eyes fell on Luo Zheng.
The three of them had appraised countless treasures and were also very good at judging people. After noticing Luo Zheng's cultivation, the three of them revealed a trace of surprise in their eyes.
"Eh? That's interesting. You seem to have just started cultivating the Aragami not long ago, but your True God cultivation is so profound? "One of the middle-aged men asked curiously.
"It's indeed rare …" Another man said.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "The path of Dao will eventually lead to the same destination. As long as I can become stronger, what's the difference between cultivating the Aragami and True God?"
Hearing this, the three middle-aged men were slightly stunned.
This statement sounded very fresh …
Those who cultivated True Divinity were all seen as unconventional swordsmen.
That was because the True God could only rely on themselves, while the Aragami could rely on a large number of desolate bones.
The path of the True God was much more difficult in this world!
One of them laughed and said, "You have a point of view! I've heard that cultivating the True God to the extreme is also extremely powerful, but … until now, I've never seen anyone succeed! If you succeed, I'm afraid you'll be the first person in history, right? "
His words were vaguely mocking Luo Zheng.
How could Luo Zheng not understand it? However, he only smiled faintly and did not say anything.
"Take out the item you want to auction." The fair-skinned woman urged.
Every month, the auction house in Sand Heart City would always be packed with people. She had to bring them in one by one, so how could she be willing to waste her time on Luo Zheng?
"Oh, what do you want to auction?" One of the middle-aged men asked.
"I want to auction a piece of Bloodline Desolate Bone." Luo Zheng said.
"Bloodline Desolate Bone?" The middle-aged man's eyes flashed slightly, "The grades of Bloodline Desolate Bones are very complicated. According to the ranking of the Bloodline Desolate Bones, the value varies greatly. Some Bloodline Desolate Bones have very poor bloodlines, and ordinary people are unable to extract the Aragami power from them. Such desolate bones are even worse than ordinary desolate bones. Even if it's a good Bloodline Desolate Bone, it depends on the size, quantity, etc."
While the middle-aged man was speaking, the fair-skinned woman kept staring at Luo Zheng with an indifferent gaze. She could tell that Luo Zheng's cultivation level was too low, and he was holding the Falcon Caravan's domino. It was most likely as the middle-aged man had said, desolate bones with poor bloodlines. For this kid to treat it as a treasure and auction it at the auction house, he was destined to make a fool of himself.
Luo Zheng nodded and gently raised his hand. A blood-colored beam shot out from his spatial ring.
At the same time, a violent aura emanated in the room …
"Boom!"
The blood-colored desolate bone, which was about the height of a person, smashed onto the ground, causing the entire room to shake.
The three middle-aged men and the fair-skinned woman stared at the blood-colored desolate bone and immediately drew a cold breath.

Chapter 2127
Bloodline desolate bones were very particular about heritage.
