The unimaginably large bone pagodas at the bottom of the Chaos were mostly occupied by family clans with bloodlines.
These large factions in turn occupied large swathes of the Eternal Desolate Plains. Over countless years, the amount of desolate bones they had accumulated was unimaginable to ordinary people.
But to these family clans with bloodlines, ordinary desolate bones were nothing to them. What they needed were desolate bones from their own bloodline. Hence, they would often appear rich and generous, willing to spend large amounts of money to purchase them.
More importantly, Luo Zheng's desolate bone was not small in size!
"What bloodline does this desolate bone contain!?" One of the middle-aged men asked.
Luo Zheng's expression was calm as he said, "You can see for yourself."
The middle-aged man suddenly stood up and stepped in front of Luo Zheng. His eyes focused on the bloodline desolate bone.
He flicked his fingers, producing a series of tapping sounds on the desolate bone's surface.
After a while, a blood-colored power of the Rage God appeared …
"The method he uses to extract the power of the Rage God is different from Little Yun's." Luo Zheng thought.
Little Yun used the Draining Spring technique. He did not know what technique this person used, but the effect should be about the same.
The blood-colored power of the Rage God slowly dissipated. As it swirled, it transformed into the shape of a wolf.
When the three middle-aged men saw the tiny blood wolf, they cried out in unison.
"Spiritual Fiend Wolf God!"
"It really is!"
"What a coincidence! Haha! "
The three middle-aged men revealed looks of excitement.

"The Blood Wolf family clan in the North Leaning Bone Pagoda is seeking to purchase the Spiritual Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone. Recently, the price of the Spiritual Fiend Wolf God's bloodline desolate bone has skyrocketed!" The middle-aged man said.
There were very few bloodline desolate bones to begin with. Many people would keep them in their possession after digging them out, and only sell them when the opportunity was right.
For example, when the elders and experts of the Blood Wolf family clan wanted to break through a bottleneck, they would purchase their bloodline desolate bones in bulk. At that time, the bloodline desolate bones could be sold for a high price.
"So that's the case." Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
Actually, Luo Zheng was still a little worried.
Little Yun had warned Luo Zheng that if the Falcon Caravan saw the bloodline desolate bone, they might kill him for it.
But Sand Heart City's auction house …
Now, with Luo Zheng's apparent strength, he was unable to protect the Bloodline Desolate Bone. What if the other party really wanted to snatch it?
Soon, Luo Zheng Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng.
A piece of Bloodline Desolate Bone was expensive, but this auction house belonged to a large faction in the bone pagoda. Such a Bloodline Desolate Bone was not enough for them to risk their own reputation to snatch it.
"Normally, one piece of the Spirit Wolf God's desolate bone can be exchanged for fifty ordinary bloodline desolate bones." The middle-aged man said. The 'one piece' he mentioned was about the size of a fist. It was the standard unit of desolate bones. According to Little Yun, Luo Zheng had made a rough estimate. It was about ten thousand desolate bones. He never expected the middle-aged man's estimate to be higher than his.
He never expected the middle-aged man to carry on, "This is only the starting price. Anything can happen in an auction house. The final price might be much higher than this."
Luo Zheng nodded and immediately asked, "Since this bloodline desolate bone can only be cultivated by the Blood Wolf family clan, why would others bid for it?"
Being asked by Luo Zheng, the middle-aged man was slightly stunned. He probably did not expect Luo Zheng to suddenly ask such a childish question.
The other middle-aged man chuckled, "If others bid for it, it would naturally be useless. Without the Spirit Wolf God's bloodline, they would not be able to absorb the power of the desolate bones! However, the Blood Wolf family clan is willing to pay any price for the Spirit Wolf God's desolate bones. If they are willing to spend a large sum of money to buy it, there will naturally be people who will do business with them. This is not something you can meddle in! "
There were many problems involved in the transactions between large family clans.
When ordinary people found a bloodline desolate bone, they could only sell it. Through layers of transactions, it would eventually flow to those large family clans …

The desolate bones he had were mostly absorbed. Where would he have the desolate bones to buy things?

Probably because it was still early, there were not many people in the hall. Luo Zheng found a corner seat and sat down. Following that, he closed his eyes and waited for the auction to begin.

Those who could enter and participate in the auction were mostly reputable figures in Sand Heart City. Their strength was also much stronger than ordinary Rage Gods.

Skyheart Bone Pagoda, Moonorchid Bone Pagoda …

Chapter 2128

Hearing this, Luo Zheng resisted the urge to leave. His gaze returned to the old man.

"Entry token?" Luo Zheng's eyebrows twitched slightly.
Luo Zheng already knew from Little Yun that not all parts of the Eternal Wastelands could be entered and exited as one wished.
The Eternal Wastelands where the Black Steel tribe was located was already a barren land. Even though there were still some desolate bones in the middle of the Eternal Wastelands, it was too dangerous. Taking a huge risk to mine them was meaningless. The amount of desolate bones in the Eternal Wastelands was far beyond what a normal person could imagine.
However, these Eternal Wastelands could only be entered by people sent by the various large factions. If a normal person were to sneak in to mine desolate bones, it would only lead to death.
"Entry tokens" were tokens issued by the large factions. Only those who held the entry tokens had the right to enter certain Eternal Wastelands!
And a single entry token could bring in hundreds of thousands of desolate bones, or even millions of desolate bones …
There were very few entry tokens in circulation on the market. It was something that the average person could not obtain.
But today, one had appeared in Sand Heart City's auction. Everyone present immediately drew a cold gasp.
"Why would an entry token be auctioned?"

In the eyes of many people, it was illogical for an entry token to appear here.

The old man's words once again exceeded everyone's expectations.

Chapter 2129

Chapter 2130

"You want to leave this place? Where do you want to go? "

Chapter 2131
Luo Zheng did not hide anything from Xiao Yun.

Luo Zheng's bloodline desolate bone was worth quite a bit, but it was impossible for it to be exchanged for an entry token.

"Yes." Little Yun pursed her lips and nodded. "He gave us the desolate bones, taught you the cultivation Dharmic formulations, and saved our lives. He has already done enough. He is someone worthy of our trust, but this concerns the rise and fall of our family clan. I really don't dare … "
Fu Chu looked at Xiao Yun's conflicted look, and his slightly immature eyes turned deep. "Sis, other than our own lives, what else can we lose?"
"Although you have tried hard to cultivate, how much have you improved over the years?" Fu Chu asked again. "Five hundred desolate bones is a huge sum in your eyes, but you have forgotten that on the bone pagoda, even if it's fifty thousand or five hundred thousand desolate bones, it doesn't matter …"
Xiao Yun had always dreamed of the day when the siblings could return to the bone pagoda. Furthermore, they would be able to rely on their own strength to do so.
Therefore, she had always worked hard to collect desolate bones and cultivate diligently.
Fu Chu was unable to cultivate the Rage God, so she got Fu Chu to ask for the cultivation Dharmic formulations from Luo Zheng …
However, Fu Chu knew that no matter how talented the siblings were, without the support of resources, it was impossible for them to achieve that day.
"Therefore, there is no need for Sis to hesitate. That is the last bargaining chip in our hands. Now that this opportunity has appeared, Sis should press forward with indomitable will!" Fu Chu said with a resolute expression.
Suddenly hearing Fu Chu lecturing her, Xiao Yun was surprised. She looked at her brother with a gratified expression. "Fu Chu, you have grown up …"
Xiao Yun made her decision and waited for the opportunity to speak.
On the twentieth day, Luo Zheng had finally refined and absorbed ten thousand desolate bones.
He still had three thousand desolate bones left, but he was not in a hurry to refine them. After all, the journey to the bone pagoda in Heavenly South was still quite far. These desolate bones could be used for cultivation, and at the same time, they could be used as currency in this world. He had to keep some for the journey.
After using his spiritual perception to extract the Rage God's power for more than twenty days, even with Luo Zheng's powerful soul spirit, he still felt a little tired. However, he could not wait to head out of the Wiry Steel Tribe. He wanted to test the results of this period of time.
The Rage God's power had completely fused into his body. This power gave Luo Zheng a more intuitive feeling than the source of power.
Luo Zheng took a deep breath and softly said a word, "Big!"
"Weng!"
A faint yellow luster began to radiate from the surface of his body.
Luo Zheng's body began to grow rapidly …
Thirty feet …
Ten feet …
Twenty feet …
At this moment, when he looked down, the houses in the tribe were only the size of his fist.
And his body was still growing rapidly!
Thirty feet …
Forty feet …
Fifty feet …
Sixty feet!
The current Luo Zheng had truly transformed into a tall giant. The height of the houses in the Wiry Steel Tribe did not even reach the top of his feet.
As for Xiao Yun, Fu Chu, and company, they looked as tiny as ants!
"This power …"
The Rage God's power was boiling in his body, and he had an urge to pour it out.
"Boom, boom, boom …"
Luo Zheng took huge steps.
Every step left a deep footprint on the ground.
After taking dozens of steps, he was already far away from the tribe and arrived at the Eternal Desolate Plains.
"Power …"
Luo Zheng raised his hands abruptly and slammed his body down onto the ground.
"Clang!"
A huge power spread out from his body, forming a faint shockwave that spread out.
"Rumble …"
Following this shockwave, the ground in a radius of dozens of miles began to tremble. He had actually caused a small earthquake!
"This Rage God's power is still incomparable to my source of power, but the speed of its growth is very fast. I am only sixty feet tall. If I transform into a giant that is a thousand miles tall, how powerful would I be?"
After this period of understanding, Luo Zheng also understood that the speed of cultivating the Rage God was dependent on the amount of desolate bones.
Furthermore, Luo Zheng's body was special. It could absorb all the Rage God's power.
If he could find enough desolate bones to provide, his strength would increase even faster.
"It's time to leave …"

Chapter 2132
In the tribe …
Xiao Yun stared at Luo Zheng with a complicated look. She had a look of shame on her face, "Congratulations. In less than a month, you have cultivated to such a level …"
Because of Luo Zheng's 500 desolate bones, Xiao Yun's cultivation level had improved by leaps and bounds.
However, Xiao Yun's utilization of the desolate bones was far inferior to Luo Zheng's. Her absorption of 500 desolate bones was equivalent to Luo Zheng absorbing 160-170 desolate bones.
Xiao Yun could only transform to about 200 feet, but Luo Zheng had already transformed into a 600 feet tall Desolate God, far surpassing her.
It would be a lie to say that she was not jealous of such a speed of growth.
"I'm just lucky. If not for that Bloodline Desolate Bone, I wouldn't have been able to cultivate so quickly," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"You're being humble. Luck is also a form of strength," Xiao Yun said as her eyes flashed with a determined look. Following that, she asked, "Before you leave, can I ask you for a favor?"
Luo Zheng looked at Xiao Yun's face and asked in a noncommittal manner, "What is it?"
He knew that Xiao Yun and Fu Chu's origins were not simple, but he did not have the time to care about others.
"Can you promise me first?" Xiao Yun's face flashed with a sly look.
"No," Luo Zheng denied without hesitation.
Xiao Yun curled her lips. She had expected Luo Zheng to give such a reply. Following that, she said, "But to you, this is a great opportunity. It can even help you escape the Light of Judgement."
"Light of Judgement? What's that? "Luo Zheng asked curiously.
Xiao Yun shook her head when she heard this, "I really suspect that you are not from this world. You don't even know about the Light of Judgement. This is not normal …"
Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders. He was indeed not from this world, so he did not argue.
"In this world, everyone is fighting to enter the bone pagoda. Various large factions and family clans want to occupy the bone pagoda. Do you know why?" Xiao Yun asked again.
Luo Zheng still shook his head.
"Because these Aragami outside of the bone tower will not live for more than a thousand years." Little Yun stood up and looked at the vast world outside of the house. "These people outside of the bone tower, the living beings will never live for more than a thousand years … So, including you and me, they are all lowly people."
"Only a thousand years? What does that mean? "Luo Zheng's face revealed a curious expression.
Luo Zheng had indeed paid attention to the Aragami in Sand Heart City. None of the Aragami were considered old. Including the old ones in Sand Heart City, they were only seven to eight hundred years old.
But in the Divine Realm, any random True God that Luo Zheng encountered had lived for several Divine Epochs.
In comparison, the Aragami in this world were as young as babies.
Although he was curious, he did not delve into the matter.
"Because the Light of Judgement will erupt once every thousand years. That light will illuminate this world and all living beings will be wiped out," Xiao Yun said calmly.
"The Light of Judgement will erupt once every thousand years?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
Xiao Yun replied, "Yes. The only place that can be protected is those bone towers."
"So, we can only survive in the bone towers?" Luo Zheng raised his head and looked into the distance.
Tens of billions of miles away, those bone towers stood there silently. Looking at them from such a far distance, they looked very blurry …
"Yes. Every bone tower is a place that all the major forces will fight for," Xiao Yun replied.
"Of course, the bone towers can save one's life. But why don't they choose to leave this place?" Luo Zheng asked again.
The Aragami that Luo Zheng had seen so far might be ordinary. For example, although Xiao Yun was known as a lower Aragami, her real combat power was about the same as a Divine Warrior.
However, Luo Zheng had seen those Aragami that were thousands of miles tall. Perhaps, there were Aragami that were more powerful than a Saint in this world.
"It's not that easy," Xiao Yun said helplessly, "This world is originally a huge prison. Every living being that is born in this world will be bound by the curse chain. No matter how powerful they are, it's impossible for them to leave …"
"A huge prison …"
Luo Zheng had heard from his mother that this was a huge place of exile. Everyone was cursed by the Ancient Gods.
This kind of saying was similar to a prison. However, he had never heard of the curse chain before.
"What's the curse chain? You said that it's linked to everyone, but I've never seen it before, "Luo Zheng said," If such a thing really exists, why have I never heard of it before? "
Xiao Yun smiled faintly, "The people outside the bone towers are living in ignorance. It's impossible for them to know about the existence of the curse chain. However, when the time limit of 600 years is up, they will all die. In the next cycle, there will be countless untouchables …"
"Wait," Luo Zheng interrupted Xiao Yun's words, "You said that there will be countless untouchables every thousand years? How are they born? "
In a thousand years …
Perhaps some low-level living beings could be born. However, living beings like humans and other large living beings could not be born in a short period of time.
It would take hundreds of millions or even billions of years for living beings to be born at the bottom of the primal chaos …
If the light of judgment killed everyone, then only the large factions and large families in the bone towers would be left.
Therefore, Xiao Yun's words did not hold water.
"Have you seen those sinister people crawling on the Eternal Wasteland?" Xiao Yun asked.
"You mean …" Luo Zheng had a guess in his heart.
Xiao Yun nodded, "Yes, all untouchables gradually gained intelligence from the sinister people. The sinister people will not be killed by the light of judgment."
Those sinister people were like walking corpses, living in this world …
After the light of judgment destroyed all the untouchables, a wave of "sinister people" would regain their intelligence and become a new wave of untouchables.
Among these untouchables, there were very few who had powerful innate talents, or the children of these untouchables had good innate talents. In addition, they were fated to be fancied by large factions. Only then were they qualified to enter the bone towers and extend their lives, escaping the thousand-year limit.
Hearing this secret, Luo Zheng was extremely shocked. He did not expect this world to be far more complicated than he imagined.
This world was countless times crueler than the divine realm …
"As for the curse chain, there are no more than 30 people in this world who can see it." Xiao Yun blinked and looked at Fu Chu, who was not far away, "My younger brother is one of them."
Fu Chu curled his lips, "Do you want to see it? I can help you. "
For some reason, Luo Zheng suddenly had an ominous premonition. He immediately asked, "Am I also bound by the curse chain?"

Chapter 2133
In Luo Zheng's eyes, Fu Chu did not seem to have anything special other than being a little clever.
However, Fu Chu's comprehension of the cultivation technique that he taught Fu Chu was not bad. If he was in the divine territory, he would be considered a genius.
Naturally, Luo Zheng did not expect Fu Chu to have such an ability.
After Fu Chu walked over, he crossed his hands between his thumb and index finger, then slowly separated them. Finally, his index finger touched his index finger, and his thumb touched his thumb.
A unique energy burst out between his four fingers. That energy turned into a faint brown light screen.
Fu Chu's eyes looked through that light screen and smiled faintly. "Of course, you are also bound by the Curse Chain."
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows. He walked forward and looked through Fu Chu's hands.
Through this light screen, there was no change in the scene …
However, he could see that there was an additional chain behind him. He focused his eyes to identify it, and his eyes were filled with surprise. "These … are the Curse Chains?"
The so-called Curse Chains were little golden worms the size of buttons.
These little worms had a hook at the head and another at the tail. The hooks at the head were connected to each other, forming a chain …
Not only did he have this chain, but Fu Chu and Little Yun also had this chain!
"How can this be …"
Luo Zheng had only entered this world for a short time. He did not know when he was bound by this chain.
Fu Chu walked to the door of the straw hut and pointed his hands into the distance. Then, he said to Luo Zheng, "Look again."
Luo Zheng's eyes looked through that light screen again and looked into the distance …
Countless chains were coiling in the air, spreading all the way to an extremely distant place.
"Every living being in this world has a chain like this. Of course, you are not an exception," Fu Chu said faintly.
"Even the most powerful Aragami in this world cannot get rid of these chains. When they encounter the Light of Judgment, they can only hide in the bone tower," Little Yun added.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng's face suddenly showed a bitter expression.
It was a pity that he wanted to return to the divine territory as soon as possible. He did not expect to be bound by the Curse Chain when he landed at the bottom of the Chaos …
"Can't anyone cut the Curse Chain?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes," Xiao Yun replied.
"For example, me." Fu Chu smiled faintly, a profound look that did not match his age appeared on his face.
Luo Zheng was slightly shocked by her words. "Didn't Xiao Yun just say that even the strongest Aragami could not do anything about it?"
"Of course they can't. No matter how strong they are, they can't break free from the curse chains." Fu Chu shook his head. "However, these curse chains were set up by our clan …"
"Your clan?" Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows.
Xiao Yun nodded. "In this world, we are above all the other races. Only a very small number of people know of our existence. They call us the Warden Clan."
The Primal Chaos was an extremely vast world. No one knew exactly how far it was.
But in this world, the Primal Chaos Primal God had turned it into a huge prison.
The people here were all exiled and cursed by the Primal Chaos Ancient God, unable to leave for all eternity.
In fact, once they were exiled in this prison, it was equivalent to a death sentence.
No matter how strong they were, they could not resist the Light of Judgment.
No matter if it was a Nirvana Realm powerhouse or a native Aragami, they would all fall under the purification of the Light of Judgment.
In the past countless years, this kind of thing had repeated countless times. Countless powerful beings had died here.
Of course, the Warden Clan was an exception.
They were a special case in this world. After all, they were born to control this prison.
In the past, the Warden Clan had completed their mission very well. They had never missed a single living being.
A thousand years was a cycle.
Those beings exiled by the Primal Chaos Ancient God came to this world. After a thousand years, they would be baptized and fall.
Then, the cycle would start again.
"I heard that an accident happened eighty billion years ago," Xiao Yun said.
The Warden Clan had existed in this world for far too long. Xiao Yun and Fu Chu were not old, so they had only heard about the past.
"What accident?" Luo Zheng asked.
"A Primal Chaos Ancient God died," Xiao Yun said.
"Primal Chaos Ancient Gods can also die. Could it be just a legend …"
Luo Zheng had heard his mother mention the Primal Chaos Ancient God, but he could not imagine what it was.
However, from his mother's expression, it was an existence that even the people of the Mother World had to believe in. How could such an existence fall?
Eighty billion years was too long. Many legends would change after being passed down. Luo Zheng could not believe the truth.
"How could it be just a legend?" Fu Chu laughed. "That guy's corpse is lying there!"
As he spoke, Fu Chu pointed to the sky.
Far away in the distance, a series of gigantic bone towers could be seen.
Only such a huge thing could be seen from such a long distance away.
After all, the height of this bone tower was comparable to the length of the Divine Territory.
"You're saying that those bone towers are the corpse of that Primal Chaos Ancient God?" Luo Zheng's eyes widened.
"Yes." Xiao Yun nodded. "Nothing can resist the Light of Judgment because the Light of Judgment was originally created by the Primal Chaos Ancient God. However, this Primal Chaos Ancient God's corpse does not fear the Light of Judgment!"
When the huge corpse first descended, it did not change the current situation of the prison.
However, after a few reincarnations, some living beings discovered the use of the Primal Chaos Ancient God's corpse.
The exiles were very smart. They hid in the corpses and avoided the purification of the Light of Judgment. Then, they gradually developed and grew stronger.
More and more exiles accumulated in this prison, and gradually, it became more and more prosperous.
At first, the exiles in the prison were grouped together and did not have any conflicts. After all, surviving in this prison was more important than anything else.
However, the corpse of an Elder God of Primal Chaos had begun to rot and fester over the course of countless years. The life energy left behind in the corpse began to leak out, leaving behind only the 'beast bones' that soared into the sky. In other words, they were bone towers.
During this process, the exiles constantly migrated. Finally, they could only hide in the bone towers.
And as the number of living beings in this world increased, the experts became extremely powerful after using the desolate bones. They began to vie for the bone towers because only the bone towers could protect their lives.
Hence, conflict ensued. Various large family clans began to wage war over this matter. It began with the fight for the bone towers, and then the desolate bones.

Chapter 2134
These things were not a secret to the major forces and clans in the bone tower.
However, the countless lowlifes in the Eternal Wasteland, including the Aragami in the cities, did not know about this.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng realized an important question. "You said that the Light of Judgment purifies this world every thousand years. How many years until the next purification of the Light of Judgment?"
Xiao Yun blinked her eyes and said faintly, "Three more years."
"In other words, within three years, if we don't enter the bone tower, everyone outside the bone tower will die?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes," Xiao Yun said with her head lowered.
"That's a little troublesome …" Luo Zheng smiled bitterly.
He was now in this world and was still bound by the curse chain. He could not leave this world, and he could not escape back to the Divine Dominion.
If he wanted to live, he had to enter the bone tower.
Even those powerful Aragami could not resist the Light of Judgment. Luo Zheng might not be able to avoid being purified.
"But as the clan of the wardens, why are you stranded here?" Luo Zheng frowned and asked.
The clan of the wardens, as the name suggested, existed to manage this huge prison.
However, both Xiao Yun and Fu Chu were terrifyingly weak. It did not make sense.
"Because we chose to betray," Xiao Yun replied.
The clan of the wardens was also an extremely ancient clan.
They were created by the Primal Chaos Primordial God and inherited the will of the Primal Chaos Primordial God. They were used to manage this huge prison and ensure the smooth operation of the curse chain and the Light of Judgment.
No matter what clan they were, after a long time, they would eventually doubt their own actions.
After all, although the clan of the wardens were in charge of this huge prison, they were also trapped in the prison and were treated the same as the exiles …
The Primal Chaos Primordial God had also considered this problem. Every once in a while, he would send down his powerful will and order the clan of the wardens to fulfill their duties.
Under the will of the Primal Chaos Primordial God, the clan of the wardens could only obediently guard this prison for generations.
However, eighty billion years ago, when the corpse of the Primal Chaos Primordial God fell, the will of the Primal Chaos Primordial God disappeared!
"Could it be that the huge corpse is the same creature as the Ancient God of Chaos that built this prison?" Luo Zheng asked.
"That's not it. The elders of our warden clan speculated that this dead Primal Chaos Ancient God might be our master's enemy. Our master was most likely seriously injured, which was why he couldn't send down his will," Little Yun said.
Yes or no, it didn't mean much.
Ever since the will of the Primal Chaos Ancient God stopped descending, the warden race had split into two factions. One was the conservative faction, and the other was the liberal faction.
The Conservative Faction continued to follow the will of the Primal Chaos Ancient God and continued to control the prison.
The liberals, on the other hand, were tired of the endless days and hoped to leave.
The Conservatives accused the Liberals of betraying the Primal Chaos Ancient God, while the Liberals vowed to find a way to leave this prison.
These two factions had been secretly fighting for countless years, using almost all means at their disposal.
"In the end, the liberals lost everything?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yeah," Little Yun's gaze fell on Fu Chu, and a helpless smile appeared on her face. "We are the last of the liberals. After three years, I'm afraid we won't exist anymore."
The warden race had always lived in a special place where they could avoid being purified by the Light of Judgment. Once they left, the Light of Judgment would descend and they would die.
After hearing Little Yun's words, Luo Zheng couldn't help but sigh. He didn't expect this pair of sisters to have such a background.
Luo Zheng's thoughts turned, and then he asked, "Wait, since you are planning to leave this world, why don't you seek the help of the great families and forces in the bone pagoda?"
The goal of the liberals was to leave this prison.
The experts of the great families and forces in the bone pagoda were also exiled here.
Their goal was the same.
If the liberals chose to ally with these exiles, there was no reason for them to lose to the conservative faction.
"Although we want to leave this prison, we won't go so far as to wallow in the mire with those exiles. This is the insistence of our liberal faction's elders," Little Yun said indifferently.
The warden race was the warden race that looked after this prison.
Those exiles were the prisoners of this prison. Even if the wardens wanted to leave this prison, they wouldn't go so far as to cooperate with the prisoners.
Luo Zheng could understand the liberals' thoughts.
"In the end, our elders regretted it, but it was too late." Speaking of this, Little Yun's emotions fluctuated violently, and her eyes revealed resentment. "The liberals were almost completely wiped out. I was the first to escape, and then … Fu Chu also smoothly escaped."
After the siblings escaped, they had been hiding in the Leather Iron Tribe.
"You didn't plan to seek refuge with those exiles?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Of course we did." Little Yun's expression was agitated, and her eyes were filled with hatred. "To me, whether or not I can leave this damn place is no longer important! I want revenge! "
To Little Yun and Fu Chu, their biological parents and the elders of their various clans had been completely wiped out. This was an unresolvable hatred.
"But the conservative faction has already set up an inescapable trap. With our current strength, we can't break through at all," Fu Chu said calmly. There was a maturity on his face that didn't match his age.
Fu Chu couldn't cultivate the Aragami, and Little Yun's strength was weak.
Although she had been working hard to cultivate, she was too lacking in resources. She also didn't dare to enter those big cities, so her cultivation base increased very slowly …
The conservative faction only needed to ensure that the siblings didn't enter the bone tower.
After three years, apart from the exiles in the bone tower, all living beings outside would be purified. Naturally, Little Yun and Fu Chu wouldn't be able to escape, and all future troubles would be eliminated.
"I understand. You want me to enter the bone tower and unite the exiles in the bone tower?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Yes." Little Yun nodded. "Although I don't know where you came from, you have the curse chain. No matter how outstanding your talent is or how high your cultivation base is, you can't jump out of this cage. I believe that you'll make the right choice!"
"I don't seem to have a choice?" Luo Zheng asked with a smile.
Hearing this, Little Yun also smiled.
During the years they had been hiding here, they had always been trembling in fear, not daring to cause the slightest trouble.
This time, the siblings were going all out. They revealed almost all of their secrets to this unknown young man, Luo Zheng.

Chapter 2135
On the horizon …
Luo Zheng was flying forward at a fast speed.
Originally, Luo Zheng wanted to leave with Xiao Yun and Fu Chu.
However, he was rejected by Xiao Yun.
Without absolute confidence, it was very likely that they would be discovered by the "conservative faction" if they rashly approached the bone pagodas.
Therefore, Luo Zheng could only go alone.
Sha Xin City was just a border city and there were no teleportation arrays in it.
After passing through Sha Xin City, Luo Zheng once again entered the Eternal Wasteland.
This Eternal Wasteland was the same as the Eternal Wasteland that Luo Zheng had first arrived in. The desolate bones had dried up after being harvested. It was an Eternal Wasteland that no one was guarding.
"So many Yin People?"
Looking down from the sky, Luo Zheng saw that among the densely packed skeletons, there were tens of thousands of Yin People, like wild horses, running wildly across the wasteland.
There were three kinds of origins of the people in this world.
One was the warden race, which was created by the Primal Chaos Ancient God.
The other type was the exiles, who were also the targets of imprisonment in this prison …
The last kind was the "Pariahs" outside the bone pagodas. All of the Pariahs were created by the Yin People, and if the Pariahs were strong enough, they were qualified to enter the bone pagodas.
"All the strong people in this world are afraid of the Light of Judgement. Only these Yin People who have not awakened their intelligence are not afraid. Then, how were these Yin People born?" Luo Zheng had forgotten to ask about the origins of the Yin People.
The Eternal Wasteland was vast and boundless. Even though Luo Zheng maintained an extremely fast speed, it still took him half a month to cross this place.
After half a month, Luo Zheng finally saw another city.
"Swish …"
Luo Zheng opened up a map.
This map was exchanged by Luo Zheng in Sha Xin City with a piece of desolate bone. It was made very carefully and recorded the locations of some cities and bone pagodas in detail.
"Sacred Bone City." Luo Zheng's eyes flashed. "This city has a teleportation array that leads to Helian City, and Helian City is located on the side of the Eternal Wasteland near the bone pagodas in the south. It is a shortcut."
After putting away the map, Luo Zheng's figure flickered, and he went straight to the Sacred Bone City.
Like the Sand Heart City, the Sacred Bone City was quite vigilant against outsiders. The guards at the city gate blocked Luo Zheng's way.
"My two friends, this is a small token of appreciation from me." Luo Zheng gently flipped his palm and stuffed over ten pieces of desolate bones into their hands.
These guards' monthly salary was only two or three desolate bones. How could they not be tempted by this "huge sum of money"?
Seeing that Luo Zheng's cultivation was ordinary, the two guards waved their hands and let him pass.
After entering the Sacred Bone City, Luo Zheng did not stop. After asking around, he went straight to the Transmission Array of the Sacred Bone City.
This place was hundreds of millions of miles away from Helian City. The cost of the Transmission Array was not low. It was a hundred pieces of desolate bones.
Thankfully, Luo Zheng had kept some desolate bones and used them to pave the way, saving him a lot of trouble. Otherwise, it would take years to reach his destination with his own speed … By the time the Judgment Light descended, he might not even be able to enter the bone pagodas, much less return to pick up Little Yun and Fu Chu.
As the Transmission Array was very far, it took three days to travel through the Transmission Array.
Three days later …
A prosperous city appeared in front of Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng thought that all the cities in this world should be very desolate.
After all, other than the living beings in the bone pagodas, the others could only live for a thousand years. There was no time to build a city that was very vast.
However, Helian City, Sand Heart City, and Sacred Bone City were different.
This city was very close to the bone pagodas. After the living beings in the bone pagodas avoided the Judgment Light, they could return to Helian City. Therefore, the Judgment Light would not interfere with the progress of this city.
"Divine Dominion …"
Luo Zheng looked up and saw a gigantic Divine Dominion hanging high in the sky. It was like a star with a unique shape.
The distant bone pagodas seemed to be just inches away as they stood erect not far away.
The bone pagodas were about the same height as the Divine Dominion. It was unknown how many years it would take to climb from bottom to top. It was difficult to describe how massive it was.
"Why are you in a daze? Why aren't you leaving? "Someone urged.
Luo Zheng glanced at the person nonchalantly before quickly leaving.
After circling Helian City and asking around, Luo Zheng learned the location of the Heavenly South bone pagoda. It was hundreds of thousands of miles away.
After leaving Helian City, Luo Zheng headed south. After flying for a day and night, he saw a flagpole that rose from the ground. And under the flagpole, there was a building with a unique shape. And at the top of the building, there was a stone sculpture about a hundred feet tall.
"Three heads and six arms!"
Luo Zheng's eyes suddenly flashed.
"This is the sculpture of Chiyou!"
In the palace inside the sun, Luo Zheng had seen this sculpture. His memory was still fresh. Naturally, he would not be mistaken.
After obtaining the 'Entry Order', Luo Zheng also knew that the reason why the Eternal Wasteland was dangerous was because it was the battlefield between the Chiyou Clan and the Xuanyuan Clan. Although the Eternal Wasteland was rich in resources, it had become extremely dangerous because of the war between the two clans.
"This should be the stronghold of the Chiyou Clan …"
Seeing the sculpture of Chiyou again, Luo Zheng felt a sense of familiarity. Almost without any hesitation, he landed at the entrance of the building.
However, the door was tightly shut.
There were a few people scattered around. There were both men and women, and they were all leaning against the wall with cold expressions on their faces.
When they saw Luo Zheng's cultivation, their eyes faintly revealed a mocking expression.
"The strength of these people is very strong …" Luo Zheng was also sizing up these people. "They are even stronger than the four Aragami that I fought in the Eternal Wasteland. Moreover, they have been through hundreds of battles. They are all people who kill decisively."
Luo Zheng waited in front of the door for a while. Finally, he could not help but ask the man closest to him, "Why is the door here tightly shut?"
That man had a thin and long scar on his face. His eyes were very small. When he heard Luo Zheng's words, the corner of his mouth slightly raised. "Nonsense? The door is not open yet. Naturally, it's locked. Just wait here. "
"Why do we have to wait here?" Luo Zheng asked curiously.
"Nonsense. Didn't you come here to enter the Eternal Wasteland? Without someone from the Chiyou Clan to bring you, how are you going to enter? "The man said again.
Although the other party kept saying nonsense, Luo Zheng still calmed down and continued to ask, "Can't we enter the Eternal Wasteland with the entry order?"
"Hehe …"
"This kid …"
"What a fledgling."
The people leaning against the wall finally could not help but laugh.
The man with the scar on his face asked, "How did you get the entry order? You don't know shit and you dare to come here? "
"I bought it at an auction." Luo Zheng answered.
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the group of people laughed even harder …
"There really is someone who took the entry order here to the auction house! How many desolate bones did you spend to get it? "The man with the scar on his face laughed and asked.
"What do you mean?" Luo Zheng asked.

Chapter 2136
"I can take as much as I want?" Luo Zheng was surprised.
For this entry token, he had spent 3000 desolate bones. Yet, he could take them as he wished here?
"That is indeed the case. The rules were changed last month, and the entry tokens used to be cheap." A ponytailed girl leaning against the wall said lightly.
The Eternal Wasteland below the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda was very fertile.
Some desperadoes were willing to take huge risks and enter to collect desolate bones.
As long as they could return alive, they could bring back large amounts of desolate bones. Apart from the portion given to the Chiyou clan, they would still have a considerable amount of desolate bones.
With enough desolate bones, their cultivation level would increase by leaps and bounds.
However, as the conflict between the Chiyou clan and the Xuanyuan clan intensified, the Eternal Wasteland became more dangerous. Bringing back desolate bones was almost certain death.
Even those desperadoes realized that the risk was too great. Once they obtained the entry token, they had to be prepared to be cannon fodder. Hence, fewer and fewer people entered the Eternal Wasteland …
As a result, the price of the entry tokens dropped again and again. Finally, it was free to take them.
Entry tokens were rare items in this world. Some merchants smelled the business opportunity and took the entry tokens here. They then ran to other border cities to swindle others.
Sand Heart City was considered a border city. One of the entry tokens ended up in Sand Heart City's auction house, where it was bought by Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had spent the 3000 desolate bones unjustly.
However, Luo Zheng was not interested in the entry token, but the information behind the entry token.
After all, other than collecting desolate bones, he had more important things to do. Hence, his mood quickly calmed down.
"With your bit of cultivation level, the chances of you surviving after entering the Eternal Wasteland are very small. It's not too late to regret now." The ponytailed girl warned him lightly.
Even the most desperate desperadoes did not dare to gamble their lives in the Eternal Wasteland.
Those who dared to enter the Eternal Wasteland either did not care about their lives, or they were confident in their strength.
A newcomer like Luo Zheng was rare.
"Thank you. Since I've chosen to enter the Eternal Wasteland, I'm already mentally prepared." Luo Zheng smiled.
Seeing that Luo Zheng was calm and determined, the ponytailed woman didn't say anything else.
While they were waiting, more and more Aragami arrived and landed right in front of the building.
These Aragami glanced at Luo Zheng. Their faces showed a hint of surprise. However, most of these Aragami had a cold temperament. If it was not related to them, they would not take a second look.
Half an hour later, Luo Zheng heard a muffled sound coming from the door. The door opened.
A woman in green clothes walked out of the door. After looking around, she said faintly, "Everyone, please bring your entry tokens to register. If you don't have one, you can get one for free."
The green-robed woman said, and returned to the gate.
The Aragami who had been waiting outside for a long time walked up the stairs and entered the building …
The ponytailed woman was planning to enter the building as well. However, when she turned around, she saw Luo Zheng staring at the gate in a daze. She thought that Luo Zheng was scared and did not dare to enter the building. She smiled and said, "If you're not mentally prepared, it's better for you to go back. This is not a place for a child to take risks."
The ponytailed woman could tell that Luo Zheng was just a beginner Aragami. She even judged that Luo Zheng's Aragami form was only fifty to sixty feet tall. He was not on the same level as the people around him. Therefore, she called Luo Zheng a child.
Hearing this, Luo Zheng did not know whether to laugh or cry. He took the opportunity to ask, "Is that green-robed woman from the Chiyou Clan?"
"Chiyou Clan?" The ponytailed woman was slightly stunned. She did not know why Luo Zheng suddenly asked this question. She then shook her head and said, "No, she's just a deacon of a sect under the Chiyou Clan."
The Chiyou Clan was considered one of the top forces in this world. There were many sects under them.
These complicated and trivial matters would not be done by the Chiyou Clan.
"Just a deacon of a sect under them …" Luo Zheng's face showed a hint of disappointment.
"If you register with the entry token, someone from the Chiyou Clan will bring you into the Eternal Wasteland." The ponytailed woman reminded him. After that, she entered the building without looking back.
Since he was already here, why would Luo Zheng hesitate? He followed closely behind her and entered the building.
Hundreds of Aragami were gathered in the hall. They were registering in order.
After that, the green-robed woman said, "Most of you here are veterans. However, there are some rules that I have to repeat. You cannot hide the desolate bones that you have obtained. You have to share it with us fifty-fifty. If anyone dares to hide it, kill them without mercy!"
"So it's fifty-fifty …" Luo Zheng was speechless.
After all, the entry token was not given for free. Otherwise, what was the point of occupying the Eternal Wasteland?
"Other than that, if you encounter anyone from the Xuan Yuan Clan, please try your best to kill them!" The green-robed woman said again.
To ordinary people, the Eternal Wasteland was a place of adventure. However, to the Aragami with bloodline in the bone tower, it was a training ground.
Both the Xuan Yuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan hoped to kill each other's descendants as much as possible …
However, the Aragami with bloodline had absorbed the bloodline desolate bones. Their strength was much stronger than ordinary Aragami. How could they be killed so easily?
Often, there would be a Aragami with bloodline that would slaughter a group of ordinary Aragami …
That was one of the main reasons why this place was so dangerous.
After the green-robed woman mentioned some other rules, she began to divide them into groups.
According to the names that were registered, four people would be in a group. They would form a temporary team and travel together.
After the names were handed out, these people would also walk out and exchange positions. At the same time, they would also assess the strength of their teammates.
After all, everyone would want their teammates to be strong. No one would want to drag a burden along.
"Zong Yi, Ji Ming, Niu Qing, Luo Zheng. The four of you will form a group." The green-robed woman read out the name list in her hand.
The scar-faced man, the ponytailed woman, and another burly man walked out.
The three of them were looking left and right, trying to figure out who the last Luo Zheng was. At the same time, Luo Zheng also walked out …
When the scar-faced man saw Luo Zheng, his face suddenly changed. He did not expect that the young man who just came out was that "Luo Zheng".
He said to the green-robed woman with a bitter expression, "Being in the same group as this guy? Can we change to another group? "
The Aragami in the hall gathered their attention. When they noticed Luo Zheng, they also looked at the scar-faced man and the others with pity.

Chapter 2137
Zong Yi looked at Luo Zheng with a look of disgust. He bitterly said to the lady in green, "Then can I give up?"
"Your name has already been registered. If you choose to give up, you will be blacklisted and will not be allowed to enter the Eternal Wasteland in the future," the lady in green said indifferently.
This made Zong Yi completely depressed.
Of course, the ponytailed lady called Mi Qing and the burly man called Ji Ming did not look good either.
This was a matter of life and death. Who would be willing to joke around?
However, since things had already come to this, there was no use in regretting. They could only accept this result.
Who knew that Zong Yi was still unwilling. He changed his mind and stared at Luo Zheng with a fierce expression, "Brat, I'll give you a chance to live. Give up right now!"
If Luo Zheng gave up, there would be other people to fill in.
However, Luo Zheng would be blacklisted and would not be able to obtain the Chiyou Clan's entry token. He would also not be able to enter the Eternal Wasteland.
Ji Ming and Mi Qing also stared at Luo Zheng. They naturally hoped that Luo Zheng would give up.
Seeing Zong Yi jumping up and down in front of him, Luo Zheng's face was indifferent, "Why should I give up?"
"If you don't give up, don't blame me for being impolite," Zong Yi's face revealed a fierce expression, "Furthermore, the Eternal Wasteland is extremely dangerous. You will definitely die there!"
"You're talking nonsense," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
Previously, Scarface had been talking nonsense left and right. Now, he was choked by Luo Zheng's words. He could only stare at Luo Zheng helplessly.
"Sigh, Zong Yi, forget it. Even if there are only three people in our group, the problem won't be too big. At most, we will be a bit more careful," Mi Qing advised.
Ji Ming also laughed, "Dragging a piece of trash won't be too big of a problem!"
Under the persuasion of the two, Zong Yi finally stopped.
After these Aragami were divided into groups, they temporarily rested in the hall.
After waiting for another hour or so, they heard movement from the depths of the hall. Then, someone shouted, "Lord Cheng Wei has arrived!"
Not long after, a young man in white slowly walked over, surrounded by a group of women.
This white-robed young man looked to be less than twenty years old. However, the Rage God power in his body was extremely vigorous. Moreover, there was a unique power hidden within his body that left a deep impression on others.
The white-dressed youth had an amiable smile on his face. He stopped in his tracks and surveyed his surroundings before saying, "Everyone, sorry for the long wait. Now, follow me, Cheng Wei, to the Eternal Desolate Plains. I hope that everyone will not waste this opportunity and try their best to collect as many desolate bones as possible."
"Previously, they said that it was the Chi You Clan that brought us into the Eternal Wasteland. Is this Cheng Wei from the Chi You Clan?" Luo Zheng pondered.
His mother said that she also had the Chiyou bloodline. This bloodline was the inheritance of a race, which could not be distinguished by the naked eye. Luo Zheng also could not tell.
After saying that, Cheng Wei took the lead and left the hall.
The Aragami in the hall followed closely behind …
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
After Cheng Wei soared into the sky, all the Aragami followed closely behind.
The Chi You Clan's stronghold was still a distance away from the Eternal Wasteland. After flying in the air for less than an hour, Luo Zheng saw bits and pieces of skeletons in front of them.
"We are already very close to the Eternal Wasteland," Luo Zheng thought.
Then, Luo Zheng looked into the distance and saw that there was a semi-circular silver light screen in front of them. This silver light screen stretched for tens of thousands of miles and extended to the end of their vision. It was likely that it included the entire Eternal Wasteland.
Cheng Wei led the way in front and flew to the front of the light screen. He took out a round plate with a unique shape from his hand.
He gently threw the round plate. When the round plate touched the silver light screen, a gap appeared on the silver light screen!
"It seems like the major factions in this world use this method to enclose their territories. Therefore, outsiders cannot enter the Eternal Wasteland as they wish …"
Cheng Wei was the first to enter the light screen. The others followed suit.
After everyone entered the light screen, Cheng Wei kept the round plate and said with a kind tone, "Everyone, you can start working now. I'll take my leave first."
As a disciple of the Chi You Clan, they needed to experience life and death situations to grow. The Eternal Wasteland was the battlefield for the Chi You Clan and the Xuan Yuan Clan to train. Naturally, it was the best place to train.
After saying that, Cheng Wei turned into a stream of light and disappeared from the eyes of the Aragami.
"Sigh, the disciples of the Chi You Clan are really extraordinary!"
"That's because they are lucky. They were born with the Chi You bloodline. Even if we have a Chi You bloodline desolate bone in our hands, we still can't absorb the power of the Aragami …"
"Let's not talk about the Chi You bloodline. Any bloodline would be good enough for me!"
"Dream on …"
Bloodlines were predestined from birth.
Ordinary people could not absorb the desolate bone even if they had the bloodline. Just like Luo Zheng who dug up the desolate bone of the Spirit Wolf God, he could not absorb it either.
After Cheng Wei left, the Aragami that were present began to discuss.
Most of these Aragami were not here for the first time. Some of the veterans knew where was the safest and where there were more desolate bones.
During the discussion, Zong Yi, Ji Ming and Mi Qing did not seem to include Luo Zheng in their discussion.
After the three of them discussed for a while, Zong Yi glanced at Luo Zheng and said, "Kid, although I don't want to bring you along, since you are here, you better be smart! I'll bring you to a good place! "
As groups of Aragami left one after another, Luo Zheng's group of four also flew towards a certain direction.
While flying in the air, Luo Zheng silently spread out his spiritual sense and penetrated into the ground …
The moment he penetrated into the ground, his eyes suddenly flashed.
Only then did he understand why these people were willing to risk their lives to enter the Eternal Wasteland!
Just as he entered the Eternal Wasteland, he could sense that there were hundreds of desolate bones hidden underneath …
It was no wonder the Eternal Wasteland of the Black Steel Tribe was abandoned. Compared to this place, it was too barren.
Now, Luo Zheng wanted to move on his own. Even if he were to follow the borders of the wasteland and pick up some that slipped through the net, he would be able to gain quite a bit!
"There's no rush. The deeper we go, the more desolate bones there will be." Luo Zheng calmed himself down and followed behind the three of them.
After traveling for about six to eight miles, Luo Zheng used his powerful spiritual sense to investigate and discovered that there were more and more desolate bones underground. Seeing that he had missed out on so many desolate bones, Luo Zheng felt an unbearable itch in his heart.
"It's here!"
"Sou!"
Zong Yi suddenly stopped in midair and descended downwards.
After the four of them landed, Zong Yi scanned the surroundings and said, "Everyone, split up and search for the desolate bones. Don't go too far. This place is a treasure trove and is relatively safe. Furthermore, there are quite a number of desolate bones here. I have obtained quite a number of desolate bones here previously!"

Chapter 2138
After Zong Yi warned Luo Zheng, he began to search for the desolate bones.
Mi Qing and Ji Ming also each looked for a direction.
These people had different methods of searching for the desolate bones. Mi Qing held a small circular mirror in her hand. From the circular mirror, there was a faint fluctuation of the power of the Rage God. Through that, she could determine the approximate location of the desolate bones.
However, the precision of the mirror was not very high. Even after determining the location, Mi Qing still had to search for a long time.
In comparison, Ji Ming's methods were much more violent.
Ji Ming took out a rake that was hundreds of meters long from his Sumeru Ring. Following that, he stimulated the power of the Rage God in his body, transforming into a giant that was hundreds of meters tall.
He waved the rake and plunged it into the ground. He constantly flipped the bones on the ground.
Wherever the rake plowed, gullies would appear. After the bones were plowed by the rake, small pieces of desolate bones would stick to the rake.
This method of searching for the desolate bones was very efficient.
Mi Qing and Zong Yi looked at Ji Ming's thousand feet long rake with a hint of envy on their faces.
Ji Ming's background was not bad. He belonged to a large sect under the Chi You race. The rake in his hand was not something an ordinary Rage God could possess.
Since the other three had begun, Luo Zheng naturally could not stay idle.
Compared to the other people's various methods, the only thing he could rely on was his Spiritual Sense.
In terms of the usage of Spiritual Sense, be it technique or strength, they were not on the same level as Luo Zheng.
After choosing an area, Luo Zheng, who was standing on top of the pile of bones, lowered his head slightly. His gaze was slightly unfocused. An invisible Spiritual Sense immediately spread out …
The size, shape, and characteristics of the bones within a radius of dozens of miles were all displayed in his mind.
"This … This …"
Luo Zheng's pupils suddenly contracted. A look of pleasant surprise appeared on his face.
Previously, when they were flying in the sky, Luo Zheng had secretly observed.
The Eternal Desolate Plains was indeed very fertile. However, he did not expect the area that Zong Yi had chosen to be so fertile.
Under the probing of his psyche, he discovered that there were thousands of desolate bones underground. Furthermore, there was a desolate bone that was about ten feet long in the deepest part of the ground!
Zong Yi's group were veterans after all. They had been in the Eternal Wasteland for many years, so they knew where the rewards were the greatest and where the safest places were …
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's figure flashed by, stretching out a hand towards the ground.
The invisible energy drilled into the cracks of the bones, including the desolate bones underground.
Then, he suddenly raised his hand …
"Hualala …"
Dozens of desolate bones were pulled up by Luo Zheng and shot towards his right hand.
Luo Zheng flipped his right hand and activated his sumeru ring. The desolate bones entered his sumeru ring on their own accord.
Be it Mi Qing or Ji Ming, they had to rely on luck to excavate the desolate bones. Luo Zheng's precision in excavating the desolate bones was naturally several times more efficient.
In less than half an hour, Luo Zheng had excavated most of the fine desolate bones in the area. Finally, he stopped at an empty space.
"That ten feet long desolate bone is buried ten thousand feet underground. Digging it out will definitely cause quite a commotion …"
Luo Zheng secretly glanced at Mi Qing, Ji Ming and company who were far away.
Following that, an invisible energy formed a high-speed spiral that drilled downwards.
"Ka Ka Ka Ka …"
Under the high-speed rotation of the energy source, countless bones were sent flying.
In a blink of an eye, Luo Zheng had drilled out a large hole …
The huge commotion immediately attracted the attention of Mi Qing, Ji Ming and company.
Mi Qing flicked her ponytail as her eyes revealed a look of surprise.
Ji Ming glanced at him and chuckled. He then focused on his work and continued to swing the rake that was hundreds of meters long ….
"What is that trash doing?"
Not far away, Zong Yi's face sunk. He immediately walked towards Luo Zheng.
On his shoulder, there was a tiny squirrel looking left and right. It was charmingly naive. The squirrel's body was covered in golden glossy fur. This squirrel had a talent. It was extremely sensitive to the power of the Rage God. Many people who searched for desolate bones would tame one and keep it by their side. Zong Yi relied on this tiny squirrel to search for desolate bones.
"Hualala …"
Bones of all shapes and sizes were constantly being dug out of the large hole and piled up around it.
In less than half an hour, the large hole had reached a depth of seventy to eighty feet …
"It's about time!"
Luo Zheng, who was standing at the bottom of the large hole, spread out his hands. His energy source had penetrated more than two thousand feet into the ground. It quietly transformed into an invisible energy ring that tightly wrapped around the ten feet long desolate bone.
"Up!"
Dragging the large desolate bone, Luo Zheng began to slowly rise.
There were countless bones on top of the desolate bone. Pulling it up required quite a bit of effort.
At this moment, Zong Yi stood at the edge of the large hole and looked down.
He was extremely displeased with Luo Zheng's actions. Furthermore, in Zong Yi's eyes, Luo Zheng did not have the means to search for desolate bones. His actions would only add to the chaos.
The Eternal Desolate Plains was extremely dangerous. If it was discovered by the Xuan Yuan family, it would probably lead to a fatal disaster. He planned on warning the kid not to cause any trouble.
But with a glance, he could not understand what was going on.
Luo Zheng seemed to be dragging something with great effort. The desolate bones at the bottom of the large hole were constantly being pushed aside, as if a massive object was about to emerge.
"What the hell is he doing?" Zong Yi stared intently below.
Soon, Zong Yi saw a large bone suddenly appear at the bottom of the large hole. With Luo Zheng's tugging, the entire bone was pulled up by Luo Zheng!
The larger the desolate bone, the richer the power of the Rage God it contained.
Zong Yi looked at it and immediately felt an intense power of the Rage God …
"This is a desolate bone, a large desolate bone that is about ten feet long!" Zong Yi's eyes suddenly flashed with a strange light, "How is this possible!? How did this kid discover it!? "
The first desolate bones to be discovered in the Eternal Desolate Plains were usually the ones on the surface.
There were some large desolate bones that were thousands of feet long, even tens of thousands of feet long …
However, those large desolate bones were already occupied by the experts in the bone pagodas. Ordinary Rage Gods would rarely see them.
But deep underground, there would always be some that slipped through the net.
These slipped through the net were very difficult to discover using ordinary means. Be it the little squirrel beside Zong Yi or the bronze mirror in Mi Qing's hand, they could not sense the depths of the ground.
As for the rake in Ji Ming's hand … it was impossible for him to plough through the entire Eternal Desolate Plains.
This desolate bone that was about ten feet long was equivalent to two to three thousand pieces of desolate bones.

Chapter 2139
In Zong Yi's eyes, it did not matter how Luo Zheng found the desolate bone. What mattered was that this kid was someone he could easily bully.
After Luo Zheng pulled out the desolate bone, he activated the Sumeru Ring on his finger and kept it gently.
The large desolate bone was immediately kept in the Sumeru Space.
Following that, Luo Zheng looked up at Zong Yi, who was at the edge of the crater, and flew up from the bottom of the crater.
"Kid, your luck is not bad." Zong Yi stared at Luo Zheng and said.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "It is indeed luck."
Seeing Luo Zheng's calm expression, Zong Yi frowned slightly. Was this guy pretending to be calm, or was he not as simple as he looked?
Zong Yi had been in the Eternal Wastelands for many years, and was also a bloodthirsty person. He had seen many people who pretended to be pigs to eat tigers.
However, Luo Zheng did not hide his cultivation level. He was indeed a fledgling Rage God, and did not have powerful strength to back him up …
Upon thinking of this, Zong Yi's face turned ferocious. "It can't be considered luck. I was the one who brought you here. If you dig up any desolate bones here, I naturally have a share. This is the rule I set."
"You set the rule?" Luo Zheng looked at Mi Qing and Ji Ming in the distance, and asked, "That is to say, they will also give you a share?"
Zong Yi smiled coldly, "They are them, and you are you. What's the point of saying so much nonsense? Since I saw that desolate bone, I have to share it with you! "
Mi Qing and Ji Ming's strength was about the same as Zong Yi's, how could they share it with him?
"Oh? How much do you want? "Luo Zheng's eyebrows raised slightly.
"Nineteen. Ninety for me, ten for you. With your insignificant strength, you don't need that many desolate bones." Zong Yi observed Luo Zheng's expression, and thought to himself that this kid was afraid. In his opinion, a trash like Luo Zheng would not dare to go against him no matter how much courage he had.
In fact, according to the rules of the Chiyou race, they were forbidden from killing each other. If they were discovered, they would be severely punished.
But in the Wilderness, as long as Mi Qing and Ji Ming didn't say anything, who would know?
Too many people had died here. Many had been killed by the Xuanyuan Clan, and many had been killed by their own teammates. There were people dying almost every day, and no one would investigate thoroughly.
"That's right," Luo Zheng said with a faint smile.
"Hey, at least you know what's good for you." Zong Yi thought that Luo Zheng had agreed, and the smile on his face grew wider.
"I'm saying that your dream was pretty good." Luo Zheng shrugged his shoulders. Then, without looking at Zong Yi, he turned around and was about to leave.
"You! You are courting death! "
A look of anger appeared on Zong Yi's face. Only now did he understand that Luo Zheng had no intention of sharing the profits with him.
As soon as he finished speaking, Zong Yi did not even activate the power of the Rage God. Instead, he charged towards Luo Zheng.
The moment Zong Yi approached Luo Zheng's back, he saw Luo Zheng turn around and stare at Luo Zheng with a gleam in his pitch-black eyes.
"Boom!"
Zong Yi felt a sharp pain in his head, as if he had been hit by a huge rock. He almost fainted.
Just as he was swaying, a long sword flashed in Luo Zheng's hand.
"Puchi!"
The long sword pierced through his neck.
The blade of the Chengying Sword was very thin, and Luo Zheng was very familiar with the bones of the neck and the distribution of blood vessels.
Although the long sword pierced through Zong Yi's neck and looked very exaggerated and terrifying, it did not cause any substantial damage.
"Wu, wu …"
At this moment, Zong Yi did not dare to move at all. He did not even dare to say a word. He stood in place like a puppet, staring blankly at Luo Zheng.
He never expected that the person he called trash would be so fierce. He did not even dare to use the power of the Rage God. If he suddenly enlarged, this sword would directly split his body into two!
Luo Zheng held the Chengying Sword and asked with a calm and gentle smile, "You just said, who is courting death?"
"Whoosh, whoosh!"
Mi Qing and Ji Ming quickly rushed over from afar.
When the two of them saw this scene, their faces were filled with shock …
They never expected Luo Zheng to restrain Zong Yi in one strike. They were momentarily stunned.
"What's the matter?" Mi Qing asked.
"Little brother Luo Zheng, let's talk things over. Everyone being in the same group and being able to take care of each other is fate. Besides, the Chiyou race has rules. We cannot kill each other," Ji Ming advised from the side.
Luo Zheng glanced at the two of them and said, "Rules? Does this guy follow the rules? The desolate bones I excavate have to be split with him 10: 9. May I ask what kind of rule is this? "
When Mi Qing and Ji Ming heard this, they looked at Zong Yi in surprise.
10: 9 …
Zong Yi was too greedy.
He only thought that Zong Yi was easy to bully, but he never expected Luo Zheng's counterattack to be so fierce. He was able to restrain Zong Yi in one strike.
"This, this … Little brother Luo Zheng, I was just being greedy. You are a magnanimous person, so please don't be calculative. I know a few places that have a lot of desolate bones. Everyone should be amiable and dig more desolate bones."
Zong Yi's life was in Luo Zheng's hands, so how could he dare to speak nonsense?
People like Zong Yi and Ji Ming had climbed up from the bottom. They relied on their ruthlessness, but they did not have any pride. Once they admitted defeat, they changed their attitude very quickly.
Luo Zheng smiled and stared at Zong Yi and said lightly, "Regardless of whether you are speaking the truth or not, the next time you dare to fight me, you will definitely die. If you don't believe me, you still have one more chance, but it is also your last chance. You have to grasp it well."
When these words were said, Mi Qing and Ji Ming, as well as Zong Yi's expression changed.
From Mi Qing and Ji Ming's point of view, Luo Zheng had caught Zong Yi hostage because he was caught off guard. Even Zong Yi himself thought so.
As long as Luo Zheng released him, Zong Yi could activate the power of the Rage God and transform into an 800-foot giant. Squashing Luo Zheng to death would be as easy as squashing an ant.
They never expected Luo Zheng to be so arrogant, as though he was not afraid of Zong Yi …
"Sou!"
Luo Zheng's right hand suddenly retracted and pulled out the Shadow Bearing Sword from Zong Yi's neck.
The Shadow Bearing Sword was so sharp that it did not even bring out a drop of blood …
Zong Yi touched the thin wound on his neck and heaved a long sigh of relief.
He sized up Luo Zheng again and thought of Luo Zheng's warning. He could not help but feel a chill run down his spine.
This kid is probably not as simple as he looks.
I'll bear with it and observe the situation. If this kid is really bluffing, it won't be too late to take action …
As for Luo Zheng, he could not be bothered with what Zong Yi was thinking. If he had the time, he might as well dig up more desolate bones.
Although the area was rich in desolate bones, Luo Zheng used his Spiritual Perception to probe and then used his Power Origins to pull them out. His efficiency was terrifying. It could be said that wherever he went, not a single bone was left …
Under Mi Qing and Ji Ming's observation, they could tell something. They were even more curious about Luo Zheng's origins.

Chapter 2140
The four of them traveled another three hundred miles in the wasteland before stopping.
"This is the area," Mi Qing smiled, "This should be the most fertile area in the Eternal Wasteland."
At this moment, the little squirrel on Zong Yi's shoulder began to squeak.
The little squirrel probably smelled the rich Rage God power, which caused it to squeak.
Zong Yi's gaze swept the surroundings, and a vigilant look appeared on his face, "This place is indeed rich in desolate bones, but it's not safe enough."
The Eternal Wasteland was not small, but there was still the possibility of encountering people from the Xuan Yuan clan.
The Rage Gods that entered the Eternal Wasteland all had a common understanding. The fewer desolate bones they dug, the more important it was to survive!
After all, the entry tokens were not worth much, and they only had one life.
"It's not a big problem. This is not the core of the Eternal Wasteland. The elites of the Xuan Yuan clan would not take the risk to come here, right?" Ji Ming said. He was also very interested in the vast amount of desolate bones here.
Although these people were cautious people, they were still taking a huge risk by entering this place.
"Everyone just need to move faster," Mi Qing smiled faintly. She looked at Luo Zheng with a hint of helplessness.
Most of the time, everyone had been watching Luo Zheng excavate the desolate bones.
The desolate bones that this kid dug out alone were probably more than the three of them combined. This kid really took advantage of them.
Luo Zheng did not understand the Eternal Wasteland. He only silently began to inspect the desolate bones. His consciousness was like a net, quietly enveloping a radius of tens of miles …

Eight thousand miles away from Luo Zheng's group of four.
Lord Cheng Wei of the Chi You clan stood in the air. He was dressed in white, and his expression was calm. A faint aura seeped out, as if he was a young master that had walked out of a painting.
And in front of Cheng Wei, there were eight Rage Gods that were seven to eight thousand feet tall, surrounding him.
"I'm a little surprised. When did you small fries become so bold to come to the core of the Eternal Wasteland to mine?" Cheng Wei asked faintly.
This Eternal Wasteland was the training ground of the Chi You and Xuan Yuan Clans.
The core disciples from both sides would slaughter the Aragami from the other side as much as they could. Meanwhile, all the Aragami from the other side would have to carefully avoid Cheng Wei and the other Aragami from the other side in order to excavate the desolate bones.
Therefore, very few Aragami would go to the core area to excavate the desolate bones. That was simply courting death.
However, this time, Cheng Wei was rather surprised. He actually encountered eight Aragami from the Xuanyuan Clan …
The eight Aragami seemed to have come prepared. The moment they saw Cheng Wei, the first thing they did was not to run away. Instead, they activated the power of the Aragami and planned to launch a counterattack!
"Killing a Chiyou clan disciple gives you a million desolate bones!"
"I can even transplant a portion of the Xuanyuan bloodline!"
"Cut the crap! Everyone, get ready!"
Those Aragami had a ferocious look on their faces. They could not suppress the greed in their huge eyes.
Hearing their words, Cheng Wei was a little surprised.
As the saying goes, a great reward would attract the brave. Just a million pieces of desolate bones was not enough to make these Aragami take the risk.
However, transplanting a portion of the Xuanyuan bloodline … was too tempting.
The cultivation in this world did not require much talent. The biggest difference was the bloodline!
Those who possessed the bloodline would be able to activate the bloodline ability after absorbing the desolate bones that contained the bloodline. Such Aragami would be much stronger than ordinary Aragami.
However, the bloodline was innate. Countless people dreamed of changing their own bloodline.
Some of the big clans could indeed transplant their bloodline to other Aragami. However, these big clans wanted to maintain the purity of their own bloodline. How could they simply transplant their own bloodline?
Just like the Chiyou Clan for countless years, they had only transplanted their bloodline to more than ten outsiders. However, all of these outsiders had contributed greatly to the Chiyou Clan!
How could Cheng Wei not be surprised when the Xuanyuan Clan suddenly gave out such a reward?
The eight Aragami were like eight huge mountains, charging straight at Cheng Wei!
"Huff … Huff … Huff …"
Under the impact of their huge bodies, a strong gust of wind was stirred up.

"Chiyou Desolate Body!"
"Wah!"
When Cheng Wei activated the power of the Aragami in his body, his body suddenly expanded and turned into a giant that was thousands of feet tall.
However, the shape of his giant was very different from the ordinary Aragami.
There was a huge head on Cheng Wei's shoulder. The face of the head was exactly the same as Cheng Wei. However, the face was filled with ferocious aura. It was extremely violent.
Other than that, there was another pair of hands on the side of his body!
Two heads and two pairs of hands!
That was the bloodline ability of the Chiyou Clan!
If he absorbed enough desolate bones, he could even grow three heads and six arms!

"You are … courting death!"
Cheng Wei said calmly.
When one of his heads spoke, the other head spoke as well.
However, one of the voices was cold and indifferent while the other voice was abnormally angry. When the two voices were stacked together, it made people feel weird.

As soon as Cheng Wei transformed into a gigantic Aragami, one of her hands reached out and dragged one of the Aragami over to her. At the same time, her third hand held a bamboo sword and pierced through the head of the Aragami.

The bamboo sword was extremely sharp. It pierced through the body of the Aragami from top to bottom.

Three huge heads were chopped off and rolled down from the bodies of the Aragami, hitting the ground …
Cheng Wei had used two heads and four arms to the extreme. It was almost impossible to defend against. Those Aragami were about the same size as Cheng Wei. However, they could not resist at all and could only retreat.

However, in this world, in order to survive, the grudge between the clans could be put aside.

Chapter 2141

The newcomer must have the bloodline of the Xuanyuan clan.

And the number of times the core disciples from both clashed was not many. Both sides would even deliberately avoid each other to avoid a huge conflict.
After all, the core disciples of both clans were nurtured using large amounts of Bloodline Desolate Bones. The death of one would be a huge loss.
Now that Cheng Wei suddenly heard the other party's words, he immediately had an ominous feeling.

According to the Bloodline Inheritance List, each of the major clans had their own experts overseeing them.
Both the Xuanyuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan were among the top three clans, but above them was the Nuwa Clan, and there were other clans …
If the Xuanyuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan were to fight to the death for the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, the final result would be a third party benefiting from the fight between the sandpiper and the clam. Perhaps, the other major clans would take advantage of the situation to invade the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda. This was something that both clans did not want to see.
Therefore, it was impossible for the two great clans to fight to the death over a single Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
Even if he killed Cheng Wei, the Xuanyuan Clan would not be able to enter the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.

Amidst the crazy laughter, two more black dots shot towards them from afar.

Among the younger generation of the Chiyou Clan, the most talented five were Mao Xue, Wei Xia, Cheng Wei, Feng Long, and Tu Bai.
Among them, Wei Xia had the closest relationship with Cheng Wei. They often trained in the Eternal Wasteland …
Hearing the man's words, Wei Xia had been killed by them. How could Cheng Wei not be shocked?
Cheng Wei also understood the man's meaning. The Chiyou Clan and the Xuanyuan Clan had just made an agreement, and at the same time, the Xuanyuan Clan took action. They used their fastest speed to eliminate two of the top five people in the younger generation.
The Xuanyuan Clan took advantage of the time difference. This way, they would greatly weaken the Chiyou Clan's elite battle strength, greatly increasing the Xuanyuan Clan's chances of victory!
Wei Xia was dead, Feng Long was dead, and now it was his turn …

…
Luo Zheng naturally didn't know what was happening in the Eternal Wasteland.
He was still focused on excavating the desolate bones …
In a few hours, he had already accumulated more than 30,000 desolate bones.
With this efficiency, he could probably accumulate hundreds of thousands of desolate bones by entering the Eternal Wasteland a few times a month.
It was no wonder these people were willing to take the risk to enter this place. This way, the efficiency of excavating the desolate bones would increase by several times. Just absorbing these 30,000 desolate bones would probably make him a thousand feet tall.
What if he continued for a month?
