When Luo Zheng released his Spiritual Perception again, he felt something strange. His heart jumped slightly.

Luo Zheng had seen a bloodline desolate bone before, so he knew that different bloodline desolate bones emitted different auras.
"Sou!"
His figure flashed, and he began to dig downward.

Luo Zheng had the Chiyou bloodline, but there were too few bloodline desolate bones in this world. Previously, after Luo Zheng had excavated a bloodline desolate bone, he had never encountered a second one, much less a desolate bone of the Chiyou bloodline.
In his excitement, Luo Zheng reached out his hand and gently touched the surface of the desolate bone. With the help of his Spiritual Perception, he drew out a wisp of the Rage God's power.
"Shua …"

Chapter 2142

The true strong warriors of the various clans were all hibernating in the bone pagoda. They rarely made any big movements.

If he saved Cheng Wei, it would be a chance for him to enter the Chi You Clan.

"Cheng Wei, you stall those two first. I will use my Soul Power to suppress that guy called Lou Ji. The three of you will attack together and kill him. Killing one of them will make things easier."
When Cheng Wei and the others heard Luo Zheng's voice, their faces revealed a strange expression.
Most of the people in this world mainly cultivated as Rage Gods. The reason for that was because of the environment.
However, all of the Rage Gods had a flaw. That was, their souls were not strong enough.
Fortunately, everyone's Soul Power was not strong. In a battle between Rage Gods, there was no need to consider this weakness.

The middle-aged man and the 'Wu Ma Mo' he spoke of were already charging towards Cheng Wei. Cheng Wei instinctively chose to retreat.

As for Luo Zheng who had not activated the power of the Rage Gods, Lou Ji had already ignored him like a mosquito.

Chapter 2143

Chapter 2144

Once Lou Ji killed those Aragami, the three of them would pincer him. He would definitely die today.
Upon thinking of this, Cheng Wei felt a tinge of sorrow.
He was unwilling to die in the Eternal Desolate Plains. If he could obtain a few more Bloodline Desolate Bones, he might even have the qualifications to become the number one person among the younger generation!
In his despair, even his fighting spirit was gradually dissipating …
Seeing the middle-aged man charge at him again, he even had the urge to give up resisting.
At this moment, a Quintessential Essence voice transmission entered his ears, "Cheng Wei! I'll disturb the minds of the two. You wait for an opportunity to kill them! "
"What!?"
Cheng Wei's eyes flashed as his gaze wandered. He saw Luo Zheng a few hundred feet away.
Luo Zheng still did not activate the power of the Aragami. He was like a tiny fly floating in the air. He was inconspicuous, but in Cheng Wei's eyes, he had become a life-saving straw!
"Swish!"
Cheng Wei suddenly grabbed the bamboo sword with one hand. Against the middle-aged man's lightning strike, he fell backwards and used one arm to block his chest.
Bang!
The middle-aged man punched him in the arm.
"Crack!"
The arm immediately bent ninety degrees and was broken by the middle-aged man's punch.
The biggest advantage of the Chiyou Desolate Physique was the ability to grow three heads and six arms. Meanwhile, the physical body of the Desolate Physique was far tougher than the ordinary Aragami. Even if the physical body of the Desolate Physique was injured, the Desolate Physique would be able to recover rapidly.
In comparison, the Xuanyuan Desolate Body had an almost abnormal defensive power.
After sacrificing one of his arms, Cheng Wei flipped his body and the bamboo sword in his hand slashed across a distance of over ten thousand zhang, drawing a huge arc in the air.
His sword went straight for Wu Ma Mo.
"Are you going to risk your life? Unfortunately, your life is already worthless. You can't exchange it for anyone else's life, "said the middle-aged man with a sinister smile.
Cheng Wei's slash at Wuma Mo left him wide open. The middle-aged man was completely capable of killing Cheng Wei before he could strike Wuma Mo!
A cold smile appeared on Wu Ma Mo's face. This Cheng Wei had really given up. Such a clumsy sword could not possibly hit him …
However, at that moment, the middle-aged man and Wu Ma Mo suddenly felt a flash of light at the corner of their eyes.
The two of them looked over and saw Luo Zheng who was as tiny as a fly.
The two of them were stunned. Both the middle-aged man and Wu Ma Mo did not expect that Luo Zheng was still alive.
After Lou Ji attacked, they did not even bother to look. As a member of the Xuanyuan Tribe, it was too easy for Lou Ji to take care of four Aragami.
The moment they saw Luo Zheng, they did not think that Lou Ji was dead. Instead, they were surprised that Luo Zheng could escape from Lou Ji's hands.
However, just as this thought flashed through their minds, they saw Luo Zheng raise the Chengying Sword slightly.
"Weng!"
The dual-colored sword gleam flashed past.
"Kaboom!"
The middle-aged man and Wu Ma Mo instantly felt as if a sledgehammer had hit their heads.
Especially Wuma Mo, who reflexively hugged his head and howled madly.
"Kacha …"
Cheng Wei's sword had already slashed towards Wu Ma Mo. If they were not suffering from the soul attack, the middle-aged man could easily block the sword and Wu Ma Mo could easily dodge it.
However, because of the soul attack, the situation changed!
The bamboo sword slashed onto the golden light on the surface of Wu Ma Mo's body. The golden light was instantly shattered.
After the first slash shattered the golden light, Cheng Wei's second slash followed.
This sword went straight for Wu Ma Mo's chest. With a "puchi" sound, the bamboo sword had only pierced halfway when Cheng Wei's body pressed down and his long sword flicked upwards, directly splitting Wu Ma Mo's head into two halves!
After dealing with Wu Ma Mo, Cheng Wei waved his bamboo sword and stabbed at the middle-aged man again.
However, although the middle-aged man was suffering from Luo Zheng's soul attack, his consciousness recovered very quickly. In less than half a breath's time, his eyes were clear again.
When the middle-aged man saw that Wu Ma Mo was killed, he was shocked.
In this world, there were very few Aragami who could use soul attacks. Of course, this was excluding the true experts of the major clans because those experts were not even born in this world!
That kid was just a fledgling Aragami. The Aragami power in his body was not very rich. How could he unleash such a powerful soul attack?
Although the middle-aged man was shocked and afraid, his mind worked very quickly.
As long as he dealt with this kid, Cheng Wei would still not be his opponent!
He took a step to the right and his huge body did a somersault in the air, heading straight for Luo Zheng.
"Be careful!"
When Cheng Wei saw this scene, she also reminded him.
Luo Zheng floated in the air and watched the middle-aged man's huge body roll over. It was like a huge mountain rolling over.
However, he did not panic at all. As the Chengying Sword in his hand flashed, a smile appeared on his face.
"Weng!"
The middle-aged man suffered another soul attack.
His tumbling body instantly lost its balance and landed heavily on the ground.
Faced with such an opportunity, how could the battle-hardened Cheng Wei let it go?
He flew up and grabbed his bamboo sword with both hands. Before the middle-aged man regained consciousness, the bamboo sword in his hand pierced through from top to bottom …
"Pfft!"
That bamboo sword nailed the middle-aged man's head to the ground.
The middle-aged man's body reflexively struggled for a while and then stopped moving.
Cheng Wei held his bamboo sword tightly. His expression was still a little nervous.
After the middle-aged man died, the Aragami power in his body began to dissipate. His mountain-like body began to shrink rapidly.

Chapter 2145
Cheng Wei never thought that he would be able to escape from death. To him, this was a hopeless situation.
However, in the short span of a dozen breaths after Luo Zheng's appearance, everything was reversed.
Until now, he still had not recovered from the shock …
"Thank you," Cheng Wei said sincerely to Luo Zheng, "I, Cheng Wei, will remember your kindness of saving my life."
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "If I did not help, they would have killed you and come after me. Saving you is also saving ourselves. Don't take it to heart."
Cheng Wei shook his head, "This is different. Without you, I would not have been able to kill them."
"Whoosh whoosh whoosh …"
At this moment, Mi Qing, Zong Yi, and Ji Ming returned to their original forms and rushed over.
"Lord Cheng Wei, are you alright? Lord Cheng Wei was being chased by three people from the Xuanyuan Clan, but you were able to fight them alone. You are probably invincible among the younger generation of the Chiyou Clan … "Zong Yi's face was full of flattery.
In Zong Yi's eyes, Cheng Wei was an unattainable noble.
Now that they had gone through the same ordeal, he naturally wanted to grab this opportunity to get close to this noble. In the future, he might even have the chance to enter the bone tower and join the Chiyou Clan!
When Mi Qing and Ji Ming heard this blatant compliment, their brows furrowed.
As long as one was not blind, one could tell that the key to this battle was Luo Zheng.
If not for Luo Zheng's powerful soul attack, not only Cheng Wei, but all of them would have become corpses.
But in order to flatter Cheng Wei, this Zong Yi spoke nonsense the moment he opened his mouth.
Cheng Wei glanced at Zong Yi. These Aragami were nothing in Cheng Wei's eyes. Zong Yi was more like a clown.
He saw Mi Qing and Ji Ming fighting back, but Zong Yi was standing far away. Clearly, he did not plan to help.
Cheng Wei did not expect these people to save him. After all, they were here to collect the desolate bones. They had no obligation to save the Chiyou Clan. However, this guy did not do anything and still stuck to him. It was very annoying.
He ignored Zong Yi. As a core disciple of the Chiyou Clan, Cheng Wei naturally knew the secret of the bone tower.
Although there were many Aragami in this world, three years later, under the purification of the Light of Judgment, all of them would be wiped out.
Unfortunately, most people didn't know that the Grim Reaper was rapidly approaching.
"We can no longer stay in the Eternal Wastelands. The Xuanyuan Clan has made a big move. It's extremely dangerous. Will you leave with me?" Cheng Wei stared at Luo Zheng and asked.
Cheng Wei naturally wasn't willing to let go of such a guy.
He was determined to recommend him. Even if Luo Zheng was allowed to enter the bone pagoda, he was still qualified.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng shook his head and said, "I still need to excavate desolate bones. I can look for you in the future."
Luo Zheng would not let go of the opportunity to enter the Chi You race, but he had only managed to dig out two Bloodline Desolate Bones after much difficulty entering the Eternal Desolate Plains.
He was a little reluctant to leave now.
"Excavate desolate bones …" Cheng Wei smiled, "I can give you a million desolate bones!"
A million desolate bones was not a small sum to Cheng Wei, but it wasn't like he couldn't take it out.
Such a person disliked owing favors. If Luo Zheng was willing to accept the million desolate bones, he could at least repay the favor of saving his life.
"A million desolate bones," Luo Zheng's eyebrows suddenly twitched.
Such a large amount of desolate bones was indeed tempting to Luo Zheng, but he still rejected it.
Firstly, Luo Zheng felt ashamed to accept it. Secondly, with Luo Zheng's efficiency in excavating desolate bones, it wouldn't be too difficult to accumulate a million desolate bones in the Eternal Wastelands. More importantly, Luo Zheng still needed the Bloodline Desolate Bones.
Luo Zheng's rejection made Cheng Wei a little disappointed.
But on second thought, with this guy's soul power, as long as he wasn't surrounded by especially strong Aragami, he wouldn't be in too much danger.
After all, this guy had obtained the Chi You clan's entry token. If he returned to his clan and reported this matter to his clan's elders, it wouldn't be difficult for him to find Luo Zheng.
Before leaving, Cheng Wei took everything from the Xuanyuan clan trio. He chose a few items and gave them to Luo Zheng. Finally, he inquired about Mi Qing and Ji Ming's names and said, "The desolate bones that the three of you excavate this time, you don't have to share it with my Chi You clan. I'll just help you inform them."
Half of the desolate bones excavated in the Eternal Wastelands had to be given to the Chi You clan. Previously, there were people who were unwilling to share, and the Chi You clan had sent experts to kill them.
Now, no one dared to violate this rule.
Mi Qing and Ji Ming's faces were immediately filled with joy. Luo Zheng's face also suffused a smile.
As for Zong Yi, his expression was extremely ugly. The three people that Cheng Wei mentioned were Luo Zheng, Mi Qing and Ji Ming, but he was not included.
Why would Cheng Wei care about Zong Yi's mood?
After bidding farewell to Luo Zheng, his body rose up and flew out of the Eternal Wastelands …
"Are you still excavating desolate bones here?" Luo Zheng stared at Mi Qing and asked.
Actually, Mi Qing was also a little frightened after experiencing this battle. She originally had the intention of retreating.
However, after hearing that Cheng Wei did not have to share it with the Chi You clan, she naturally wanted to seize the opportunity. After all, every piece of desolate bone they excavated was equivalent to two pieces of desolate bones!
Most importantly, the strength that Luo Zheng displayed gave Mi Qing a sense of security.
She had clearly seen that Luo Zheng had used his own strength to kill Lou Ji …
Although Cheng Wei had also killed two of the Xuanyuan clan's Aragami, it was completely due to Luo Zheng's soul power suppression.
In other words, if not for Cheng Wei, Luo Zheng could have easily killed all of the Aragami by himself!
With such an expert accompanying them to excavate desolate bones, they could almost do whatever they wanted in the Eternal Wastelands. She did not believe that the Xuanyuan clan would send someone even more powerful for them.
Among the four of them, only Zong Yi was unhappy. Luo Zheng, Mi Qing and Ji Ming began excavating the desolate bones in full swing.
The Chi You clan's entry permit had a time limit. Their entry permit had a time limit of 18 days.
On the first day, Luo Zheng harvested more than ten thousand desolate bones. He also dug up two desolate bones that contained the Chi You bloodline. In the following days, Luo Zheng's good luck continued.
On the second day, although he did not dig up any bloodline desolate bones, he dug up a large desolate bone that was more than 20 feet tall! This attracted a wave of envy from Mi Qing.
On the third day, Luo Zheng dug up three bloodline desolate bones. However, the desolate bones did not contain the Chi You bloodline …
Maybe it was because of the Xuanyuan clan's plan to fight for the Heavenly Bone Pagoda, the Eternal Wastelands was very peaceful during this period of time. The Aragami's collection of desolate bones was also very smooth.
18 days later, Luo Zheng had accumulated nearly 200,000 desolate bones. Other than that, he had also obtained about seven to eight different bloodline desolate bones. In total, there were dozens of desolate bones.
The only thing that made Luo Zheng depressed was that there were only four Chi You bloodline desolate bones.

Chapter 2146
After leaving the Eternal Desolate Plains, Luo Zheng and company returned to the stronghold.
According to the rules, the Aragami had to check all the desolate bones they dug up, and then let the Chi You race take half of them before they could leave.
They would use a mystic technique to detect the number of desolate bones dug up by the Aragami. Even if they were stored in a spatial ring, they could not be taken away. They had to be checked by the Aragami.
Luo Zheng and company did not need to share the desolate bones they obtained, but they still needed to go through the process.
"Lord Cheng Wei has already instructed that the Chi You clan will not share any of the desolate bones you obtain. We will exchange the bloodline desolate bones for you at market price." The green-dressed woman smiled sweetly at Luo Zheng after saying that.
Maybe it was because of Cheng Wei, but the green-dressed girl was very polite to Luo Zheng.
Exchanging the bloodline desolate bones at market price was a common practice. After all, the Aragami here did not have any special bloodline, so the bloodline desolate bones were useless. They might suffer a loss by exchanging them at market price, but it was also convenient.
Mi Qing and Ji Ming did not have any objections. They also handed over the bloodline desolate bones they dug up. Following that, someone came in to take them away. After the price was evaluated, they would be given the same amount of desolate bones.
"Exchanging the bloodline desolate bones at market price?" Luo Zheng frowned slightly.
The green-dressed girl noticed Luo Zheng's expression and said with a smile, "Yes, the exchange price will not make you suffer a loss."
"That's not the problem. It's just that one of the bloodline desolate bones is very useful to me. I'm afraid I can't exchange it for it." Luo Zheng said.
The green-dressed girl was in a dilemma. Although Cheng Wei had given the order, the various bloodline desolate bones had to be handed over to the Chi You race for storage.
After all, the large races lacked their own bloodline desolate bones. They could only exchange the bloodline desolate bones with each other.
And Luo Zheng clearly did not have any bloodline. What did he need the bloodline desolate bones for?
After some thought, the green-dressed girl said, "Can I see the bloodline desolate bones you dug up?"
Luo Zheng nodded and took out the bloodline desolate bones from his spatial ring …
"So many?"
Seeing the bloodline desolate bones on the table, the green-dressed girl was surprised.
Mi Qing, Ji Ming, and the surrounding attendants were all stunned.
On the table, there were all sorts of bloodline desolate bones that emitted various colors of light. Green, brown, blood, green …
After listing them out, there were actually more than eighty pieces!
When a normal Aragami entered the Eternal Wastelands, they would be able to excavate at most fifty to sixty thousand pieces of desolate bones. Some of them would only be able to excavate thirty to forty thousand pieces. After all, this Eternal Wastelands was not like other Eternal Wastelands. One would have to take a certain amount of risk and would not dare to venture too deep into the core region.
As for Bloodline Desolate Bones, it would be considered pretty good if one could dig out four or five pieces. Even with heaven-defying luck, one would only be able to obtain about ten pieces. Yet, Luo Zheng had managed to obtain more than a hundred Bloodline Desolate Bones, so it was naturally hard for them to understand.
Even though Mi Qing and Ji Ming had been excavating desolate bones with Luo Zheng, and knew that Luo Zheng had an unparalleled sensitivity towards desolate bones, they never expected him to obtain so many bloodline desolate bones.
"What kind of bloodline desolate bones do you want to preserve?" The green-dressed girl stared at the bloodline desolate bones and asked.
Luo Zheng reached out his hand and picked out four brown desolate bones from the table. He brought them in front of him and said, "I only need these four bloodline desolate bones. The rest of the desolate bones can be exchanged for normal desolate bones."
Seeing the four bloodline desolate bones, the green-dressed girl's beautiful willow-like eyebrows twitched slightly, "You cannot take away the desolate bones that contain the Chiyou bloodline."
The bloodline desolate bones of other clans were only used by the Chiyou clan to exchange for the bloodline desolate bones of the Chiyou clan. If Luo Zheng really needed it, the green-dressed girl could give him a way out. However, the bloodline desolate bones of the Chiyou clan were something they were determined to obtain.
Luo Zheng frowned. His greatest goal was the bloodline desolate bones of the Chiyou clan, so how could he give it to others?
"Forgive me for saying this, but Brother Luo Zheng, even if you take this bloodline desolate bone, it would be useless. Could it be that you can absorb the power of the Rage God within it?" Ji Ming could not help but ask.
"That's right. This bloodline desolate bone might not be of much use to you, but it is extremely important to our Chiyou clan." The green-dressed girl also said in such a manner.
Luo Zheng suddenly said, "What if I can absorb the power of the Rage God within it?"
Upon hearing this, the people in the hall were stunned.
Some of them even had expressions of being at a loss whether to laugh or cry.
Only people from the Chiyou clan could absorb this brown bloodline desolate bone. This was common knowledge. For Luo Zheng to suddenly say such a thing, it was somewhat ridiculous.
The green-dressed girl smiled slightly. Just as she was about to speak, a voice suddenly came from the side of the hall.
"If you can absorb the power of the Rage God within this bloodline desolate bone, the Chiyou clan will reward you with an additional fifty bloodline desolate bones." A silver-haired middle-aged man said as he walked.
This silver-haired middle-aged man had a medium build. His eagle-like eyes emitted beams of light that prevented people from looking into his eyes. What was even more peculiar was that the power of the Rage God he emitted was faintly discernible, as if it had reached another level!
Ever since he entered this world, Luo Zheng had seen several Rage Gods. Although their bodies were strong, Luo Zheng was confident of killing them if he used all his strength.
However, this silver-haired middle-aged man was different. He gave off an unfathomable feeling to Luo Zheng!
When the green-dressed girl saw the silver-haired middle-aged man, she was surprised. She bowed and said, "Lord Diao Yuan actually came in person. Please excuse me for not going out to meet you."
Diao Yuan waved his hand and stared at Luo Zheng. He carefully sized him up and chuckled, "A person of the wilderness actually managed to cultivate to an Upper True God. I really don't know what kind of fortuitous encounter you have!"
Those who lived in the bone pagoda would not call the Rage Gods outside the bone pagoda 'lowly commoners'. After all, it was too disrespectful. He only said 'a person of the wilderness'.
Luo Zheng cupped his hands and asked, "What you said just now, is it true?"
"Of course it's true. However, there's no need to try. You definitely can't do it. Hahaha." Diao Yuan laughed heartily and said, "However, you saved my nephew Cheng Wei's life. He said that you are very resourceful. My Chiyou clan is in need of people. Bestowing you with a Chiyou bloodline is not a difficult matter.
Are you willing? "Diao Yuan had specially come for Luo Zheng.
This time, the Chiyou clan and the Xuanyuan clan had made a bet to determine the ownership of the Eternal Wasteland and the Southern Sky bone pagoda.
However, when the Xuanyuan clan made the bet, they actually killed Wei Xia and Feng Long of the Chiyou clan.
These two people were the strongest of the younger generation of the Chiyou clan. If they lost these two people, the Chiyou clan would most likely lose the bet.
Although the Chiyou clan was furious, the other party had acted according to the rules. They had nothing to say. They could only blame themselves for not being vigilant enough and falling for the other party's trick.
After Cheng Wei returned to the bone pagoda, he recommended this person to Diao Yuan. Diao Yuan did not say anything and directly rushed to the stronghold. He wanted to see if this Luo Zheng was as miraculous as Cheng Wei said.
When everyone in the hall heard Diao Yuan's words, their eyes immediately burned with passion.

Chapter 2147
Transplanting a bloodline was not an easy task. It was said that it required the mighty figures of the Chiyou clan to do it personally.
To most of the Rage Gods in this world, it was no doubt a chance to change their fate.
In the past, only outsiders who had made great contributions to the Chiyou clan had the right to do so.
If not for the pressure from the Xuan Yuan clan, the Chiyou clan would not have given Luo Zheng a chance to absorb him.
Even Diao Yuan thought that Luo Zheng's luck was pretty good to have encountered a rare opportunity. In his opinion, Luo Zheng would definitely be extremely excited to agree to this matter.
Unexpectedly, Luo Zheng only nodded his head lightly, "Of course I'm willing. But before that, I still wish to give it a try. I want to know if I can absorb the power of the Rage God."
Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the green-dressed girl, Mi Qing and company looked at Luo Zheng as if they were looking at an idiot.
This was such a lucky reward, but Luo Zheng did not appreciate it?
Diao Yuan frowned. He was a little angry and could not help but taunt, "Alright, then I'll see if you can absorb the power of the Rage God from our Chiyou clan!"
Luo Zheng did not speak further. He swept the bloodline desolate bones into his hand.
In fact, Luo Zheng himself was unsure if he could absorb the bloodline desolate bones. After all, he had undergone a blood exchange, so he did not know if he could be compatible with the bloodline in the desolate bones.
"Lord Diao Yuan, this will waste the desolate bones …" The green-dressed girl said worriedly.
If Luo Zheng were to extract the power of the Rage God from the desolate bones, he would not be able to absorb it. Then, the power of the Rage God would dissipate.
Letting Luo Zheng experiment with a desolate bone from their clan was like child's play.
"No problem!" Diao Yuan waved his hand and said enthusiastically.
Luo Zheng reached out his hand and gently moved the brown desolate bones' surface. The brown power of the Rage God gushed out like spring water.
"Draining Spring Technique?" Someone saw Luo Zheng's technique and revealed a look of disdain.
Although Little Yun belonged to the clan of the wardens, the Aragami technique that she practiced was not inherited from the clan. She had learned the Draining Spring Technique from somewhere else. Moreover, it was a relatively clumsy technique.
Seeing Luo Zheng use such a clumsy method, these people naturally looked down on him.
When Diao Yuan saw this method, he was even angrier. This Bloodline Desolate Bone was probably going to be wasted.
After Luo Zheng extracted the brown Rage God power, he guided it into his body.
When the power of the Rage God seeped into his body, Luo Zheng immediately felt a different kind of power!
This kind of power contained a primitive restlessness, which even affected Luo Zheng's heart. He had a kind of intimate feeling, and his heart began to violently contract. He had a feeling that he couldn't wait to release the power in his body.
"Whoosh, whoosh, whoosh …"
Wisps of brown Rage God power seeped into his body, but not a single wisp escaped.
In a short while, the power of the Rage God within the Bloodline Desolate Bone was completely absorbed. Everyone in the hall remained silent as they stared intently at Luo Zheng.
Even Diao Yuan was dumbfounded.
If the bloodline did not match, the body would reject the power of the Rage God. Even if it was forcefully absorbed, it would be rejected by the body, dissipating into the world. This was common knowledge …
However, Luo Zheng had completely absorbed it!
More importantly, after absorbing so much power of the Rage God, Luo Zheng did not dissipate a single bit!
Even Diao Yuan himself would waste about 30% of the Rage God's power when refining a desolate bone. Yet, this kid's body was able to store all the Rage God's power. This matter was even more shocking than absorbing the Chiyou clan's bloodline desolate bone!
"You already knew that you have the Chiyou bloodline? Who are your parents? "Diao Yuan asked incredulously.
Diao Yuan could only believe that Luo Zheng was originally from the Chiyou clan.
However, the Chiyou clan did not allow intermarriage with other clans. In this world, everyone from the Chiyou clan was gathered in the Heaven's Spirit Bone Pagoda. It was impossible for anyone to be left out.
Now, Luo Zheng had suddenly appeared, and he actually had the bloodline of the Chiyou clan. Diao Yuan could not understand it.
"You will not know my parents." Luo Zheng smiled. "However, Lord Diao Yuan seems to want to fulfill his promise?"
Diao Yuan looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression. After a while, he said, "If I know of your origins, I will fulfill my promise. Those bloodline desolate bones are all yours. After you pack them up, follow me."
"I still want to excavate desolate bones …" Luo Zheng said.
"I'll give you as many desolate bones as you need, but don't even think of getting a single entry token." Diao Yuan said coldly.
"…"
With Diao Yuan saying this, Luo Zheng could not reject him.
The green-dressed woman was good at reading people's expressions. She knew that this was not a trivial matter, so she sent people to exchange for Luo Zheng's other bloodline desolate bones. As for the remaining three Chiyou bloodline desolate bones, she naturally did not mention them.
After these matters were settled, Luo Zheng bade farewell to Mi Qing and company before leaving with Diao Yuan.
Mi Qing and Ji Ming looked at Luo Zheng's departing figure and sighed. They never expected that the fellow who had been mining with them for the past few days was from the Chiyou clan.
However, even if he was from the Chiyou clan, his strength was still unexplainable. They had never seen such a powerful True God cultivator in this world.
High in the sky, Luo Zheng was following behind Diao Yuan.
"Lord Diao Yuan, where are you bringing me to?" Luo Zheng asked.
"Just call me Uncle Diao." Diao Yuan said.
"Uncle Diao." Luo Zheng called out.
"Although I do not know your origins, do you know how long you would have lived if you had not come here?" Diao Yuan asked.
Luo Zheng thought for a moment and said with a smile, "Three years?"
"You know?" Diao Yuan's eyebrows pricked up.
There were very few people in the wilderness who knew of the Light of Judgment. And these few people would desperately squeeze into the bone pagoda to avoid the misfortune that came once in a thousand years.
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded and said honestly, "This is the reason why I came here."
"I'll bring you back to the Chiyou Spirit Bone Pagoda to see how pure your bloodline is. If you are really from my Chiyou clan, my clan will definitely treat you well." Diao Yuan said.
Even though he had seen Luo Zheng absorb the bloodline desolate bones with his own eyes, Diao Yuan still found it hard to believe. After all, what happened to Luo Zheng was illogical …
He mainly cultivated as a True God, had powerful spiritual powers, and possessed the bloodline of the Chiyou clan. He found it hard to imagine that these characteristics would appear in an outsider he did not know.
"How pure is it?" Luo Zheng did not understand what Diao Yuan meant.

Chapter 2148
Luo Zheng nodded and silently followed behind Uncle Diao.
Previously, after absorbing the Bloodline Desolate Bone, Luo Zheng felt that his body had undergone a strange change.
Along the way, he felt that something was about to sprout in his body. Although the feeling was faint, it was real!
Cheng Wei was from the Chiyou race. After he transformed into a Desolate God, he had an extra head and an extra pair of hands. This was the Bloodline Desolate Body of the Chiyou race. He wondered if he could be the same as Cheng Wei if he absorbed enough Bloodline Desolate Bones.
After half a day, Diao Yuan brought Luo Zheng back to Helian City.
Helian City was one of the few huge cities in this area. It was heavily guarded. When Luo Zheng passed by the city, he was very careful and did not dare to cross the line.
Diao Yuan brought him into Helian City. Suddenly, seven to eight beams of light shot out from the city.
Luo Zheng sized them up. These people were probably the upper echelons of Helian City. Although they did not have bloodline, their cultivation was very profound.
"Lord Diao Yuan!"
"Lord Diao Yuan!"
These people cupped their hands at Diao Yuan.
Helian City belonged to the Baihui Sect, which was a subordinate of the Chiyou race. Diao Yuan had a high position even in the Chiyou race. Therefore, these people were naturally very respectful to Diao Yuan.
Apart from being respectful, their eyes were also faintly landing on Luo Zheng.
Previously, when Diao Yuan borrowed Helian City, he said that he had something important to do. This important matter must be the young man behind Diao Yuan.
Diao Yuan asked with an indifferent expression, "Is the teleportation array ready?"
The leader nodded, "It is ready. We await Lord Diao Yuan at any time!"
"Bring me there," Diao Yuan said.
The leader waved his hand and the others dispersed. Then, he led the way and flew towards the west of the city.
In a huge square in the west of the city, a huge teleportation array had been opened. It was like an exquisite lotus leaf emitting a soft pink light. It was slowly rotating.
After Diao Yuan and Luo Zheng landed, they walked towards this exquisite teleportation array.
"Weng …"
As the transmission array rotated, a soft light suddenly wrapped Luo Zheng and Diao Yuan.
In just a split second, the scenery in front of Luo Zheng's eyes constantly flashed by. At this moment, his movement speed seemed to have increased by a million times, ten million times … In a short period of time, he had traversed a long distance!
The principle of this Transmission Array was different from the one Luo Zheng had seen before.
After a short while, a white jade wall appeared in front of Luo Zheng!
The end of the wall could not be seen from left to right, nor could it be seen from bottom to top!
"This wall …"
Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment before he immediately reacted. This was a bone pagoda!
This enormous beast bone was about the same height as the Deity Domain. It was probably hundreds of trillions of kilometers tall. Although the beast bone was relatively thin, it was probably hundreds of trillions of kilometers wide.
Right now, he was standing right below the bone pagoda. He was even smaller than a speck of dust. Naturally, he couldn't see the whole picture of the bone pagoda.
Standing under such a huge bone pagoda, Luo Zheng was even more shocked.
"This is the Heavenly Spirit Bone Pagoda. It belongs to our Chiyou Clan." Diao Yuan's face was somewhat proud.
Any clan that could occupy a bone pagoda in this world was a super clan.
Even if some clans had special bloodlines, they could only share a bone pagoda with other clans. But the Heavenly Spirit Bone Pagoda completely belonged to the Chiyou Clan!
"I've heard that these bone pagoda are the remains of an ancient god. Then how powerful was this ancient god when he was alive?" Luo Zheng exclaimed.
Imagine the incomparably huge body of an ancient god. Even if the Deity Domain was enormous, it could still be trampled underfoot by an ancient god. That is, if an ancient god had legs.
"That's not something we can think about," Diao Yuan said indifferently. At the same time, he looked at Luo Zheng with surprise.
Many people knew that these beast bones were the remains of an ancient god, but very few people would try to find out how powerful this ancient god was. This brat …
Luo Zheng nodded and followed Diao Yuan into the Heavenly Spirit Bone Pagoda.
At the bottom of the Heavenly Spirit Bone Pagoda, there was a giant crack that was millions of feet high. When Luo Zheng entered the crack at the bottom of the bone pagoda, he was immediately enveloped by a strange aura.
When this aura enveloped him, Luo Zheng felt slightly oppressed.
At the same time, Luo Zheng seemed to hear a strange cry …
This sound seemed to be telling Luo Zheng something. It constantly lingered in Luo Zheng's ears, but when he listened carefully, he couldn't hear anything. When he wasn't paying attention, the sound seemed to come from the depths of his soul. It was a very strange feeling.
Diao Yuan noticed the change in Luo Zheng's expression and said, "Although the ancient god is dead, its will won't dissipate quickly. It might stay here forever. You'll get used to it after a period of time."
Even if a True God died, the powerful aura of their body would last for a long time, let alone an ancient god.
Luo Zheng naturally understood, but he still had other questions. "The people of this world are absorbing the power of the desolate bones. Can the remains of the ancient god be absorbed?"
Suddenly hearing Luo Zheng's question, Diao Yuan suddenly laughed. "Hahahaha, good idea. If you can absorb the power of the Rage God in this bone pagoda, you'll be invincible in this world. But this is just a thought. Let's not talk about refining the power of the Rage God in this bone pagoda. Even if you use your full strength, you won't be able to damage it. Even the almighty of my Chiyou Clan can't do it …"
These people had lived in the bone pagoda for countless years. If Luo Zheng could think of something, there was no reason for them not to.
But for countless years, no one had been able to damage the bone pagoda, not even a crack!
These bone pagoda were filled with huge cracks. The Chiyou Clan lived in the huge space formed by these cracks. But these cracks were not opened by the Chiyou Clan. They existed in the first place.
According to their speculations, the ancient god was severely injured before falling into this world. The mottled cracks on the bone pagoda were also left behind at that time.
If the corpse of the ancient god was intact and there were no cracks on the bone pagoda, then no one in this world could hide in it.
There was a precedent for this.
For example, the Heavenly Desolate Bone Pagoda. There were no cracks on the bone pagoda, and no one had been able to open a crack on it. Therefore, the Heavenly Desolate Bone Pagoda was not occupied by any clan. Therefore, it was worthless.
The two flew along the cracks. The space in front of them suddenly narrowed. It was so narrow that only one person could pass through.
But after a while, it suddenly opened up. What appeared in front of Luo Zheng was a huge space. In this space, there were three big cities. The buildings in these cities were orderly. Many Rage Gods were hurriedly shuttling back and forth.
"Have we arrived?" Luo Zheng asked.
Diao Yuan shook his head. "These are only the three major sects under our Chiyou Clan. Our Chiyou Clan is at the top."
As he spoke, he pointed at the dome above. On one side of the dome, there was a wide crack. On one side of the crack, there was a row of steps that extended to an extremely high place.

Chapter 2149
Diao Yuan and Luo Zheng flew to the top of the dome and climbed up the stairs.
After climbing for a distance, Luo Zheng saw a statue of Chiyou with three heads and six arms.
Diao Yuan walked forward and bowed towards the statue of Chiyou. He said to Luo Zheng, "This is the leader of our Chiyou race. You have to worship him sincerely before you can join our race."
Although Diao Yuan was able to bring Luo Zheng into the bone pagoda smoothly, it was actually very difficult to obtain the qualifications to enter the bone pagoda.
The Judgement Light that occurred once in a thousand years would take away anyone's life. Those who knew of this would fight to join the bone pagoda. Even if it was just a job as a guard in a sect under the Chiyou race, they would still be willing …
After all, only by joining the bone pagoda could one live longer.
As for walking up the stairs and entering the territory of the Chiyou race, it was the dream of countless Aragami!
It meant obtaining a Chiyou bloodline, which meant countless possibilities.
Every member of the Chiyou race was abnormally respectful towards Chiyou. When entering or leaving the territory of the Chiyou race, they had to bow to the statue of Chiyou.
Luo Zheng absorbed a piece of bloodline desolate bone in front of Diao Yuan. Although Diao Yuan could not understand the reason behind it, there was no doubt that he had treated Luo Zheng as one of his own race.
Luo Zheng did not hesitate.
According to his mother, Chiyou was his grandfather. He followed Diao Yuan's example and bowed.
After that, the two of them walked up the path again. Soon, Luo Zheng saw a blazing fire in the cave ahead. It illuminated a huge and spacious cave.
And in this cave, there was a huge palace. This was the true territory of the Chiyou race.
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh!"
Six to seven people flew over from the territory.
The leader had messy hair and a muscular body. His upper body was naked. Before he arrived, his voice could be heard, "Haha, Diao Yuan, are you going to bestow a portion of our race's bloodline to this kid?"
When Diao Yuan saw this person, he showed a slightly displeased expression. He only replied coldly, "So what if I am? Qi Chang, do you have any objections? "
Although the Chiyou Tribe was very united internally, there were always some disputes.
According to the rules of the Chiyou Race, only ten bloodlines could be given out in a thousand years, and each bloodline was extremely precious.
The bloodline of these Chiyou people was not pure. Even if both parents were Chiyou people, their children might not have the bloodline of Chiyou people. Therefore, there were often disputes among the Chiyou people over these ten bloodlines.
Therefore, it was even more difficult for outsiders to obtain the Chiyou bloodline.
This time, there were only three years left before the thousand years. There was only one Chiyou bloodline left to be transplanted. Naturally, all the people in the Chiyou Tribe were fighting for the ownership of this bloodline.
The burly man with messy hair was called Qi Chang. One of his brothers was the Sect Master of the Blue Moon Sect, a subordinate of the Chiyou Clan. For the sake of his brother, Qi Chang naturally tried his best to obtain this bloodline.
Seeing that it was about time, a sudden change occurred in the Chiyou Race.
The fall of Wei Xia and Feng Long led to a situation where there was no one in the younger generation that could be used.
Cheng Wei safely returned to the Chiyou Race and recommended Luo Zheng to the Chiyou Race.
When the upper echelons of the Chiyou Race heard of Luo Zheng's methods, they decided to first test Luo Zheng. If Luo Zheng was really as strong as Cheng Wei said, then the last Chiyou bloodline would be transplanted to Luo Zheng.
Diao Yuan was ordered to bring Luo Zheng back. However, he did not expect Luo Zheng to refine the desolate bones of the Chiyou bloodline in front of him. The situation had changed unexpectedly. Diao Yuan did not know what to do and could only bring Luo Zheng back first.
However, Qi Chang did not know. He only thought that Luo Zheng was here to fight for the bloodline. Naturally, he was angry from the bottom of his heart.
"How would I, Qi Chang, dare to have any objections to the Grand Elder's decision? However, I am suspicious. Can this kid really pass the test? " Qi Chang sized up Luo Zheng and laughed, "I don't know where he managed to cultivate his True God cultivation. These people in the wilderness are always strange …"
Although Luo Zheng had absorbed a Chiyou bloodline desolate bone, the amount was too little. Qi Chang could not detect it.
Hearing Qi Chang's words, Luo Zheng's brows furrowed slightly. No one would be too happy to be mocked and ridiculed by a stranger.
Diao Yuan smiled coldly, "Don't worry. There is no problem for him to pass the test. I believe in Cheng Wei. That child would not lie. However, there is something that will disappoint you. Luo Zheng does not need to pass the test!"
Qi Chang's face darkened and asked, "What do you mean? The Grand Elder plans to directly transplant the bloodline to him? "
"Heh," Diao Yuan looked at Qi Chang's expression and was secretly pleased. He deliberately did not explain and turned to Luo Zheng, "Follow me! I will bring you to see the Grand Elder! "
"Shua!" Qi Chang blocked in front of Diao Yuan, "Speak clearly! Otherwise, don't even think of leaving! "
Diao Yuan stared coldly at Qi Chang and asked, "What? Do you want to fight? "
Qi Chang's face was gloomy, but he stood firmly in front of Diao Yuan. The atmosphere was tense.
Luo Zheng's face revealed a helpless smile. This Diao Yuan … couldn't it be better if he spoke clearly?
Seeing the confrontation between the two, a light laughter drifted over, "Diao Yuan, no matter who wants to join my Chiyou clan, they must pass my test. Even if this child is as special as Cheng Wei said, it is unavoidable. Didn't we agree from the beginning?"
The voice was like a silver bell, pleasant to the ear. Even without seeing the person, one could tell that the person with the voice was extremely beautiful.
Luo Zheng looked in the direction of the voice and saw a dazzling figure mysteriously appear not far away, floating in the air.
It was a woman with snow-white skin. Her feet were bare, and her slender body was naked. Only a few strands of pink silk wrapped around her private parts. Her silver hair was tied up behind her head, each strand emitting a dazzling silver light. Her beautiful face was bright and moving. Her gentle eyes blinked a few times, quietly sizing up Luo Zheng.
As soon as this woman appeared, the atmosphere immediately eased.
Qi Chang cupped his hands and said, "Grand Elder, you are right. I think this is Diao Yuan's intention!"
This woman was the Grand Elder? Luo Zheng was also shocked.
Diao Yuan glanced at Qi Chang and said to the Grand Elder, "I, Diao Yuan, have no such intention! I want to see you, Grand Elder, not to transplant a bloodline, because Luo Zheng doesn't need the Chiyou clan's bloodline! "
The Grand Elder didn't understand Diao Yuan's words and continued, "If he really has the strength to fight against the young geniuses of the Xuanyuan clan, then he must transplant a part of our clan's bloodline. After all, according to the bet, the one to fight must be a disciple of our clan. Diao Yuan, you mean …"

Chapter 2150
When the Grand Elder, Qi Chang and the others heard this, their faces were filled with confusion.
Qi Chang's face sank as he stared at Diao Yuan and asked, "Diao Yuan, do you know what nonsense you are saying?"
The Grand Elder's delicate and fair face also had a strange look. She stared at Diao Yuan and asked, "You just said that Luo Zheng was originally a member of our clan?"
Diao Yuan obviously knew that these people would not believe it. If he had not seen Luo Zheng absorb a bloodline desolate bone with his own eyes, he would not have believed it either.
There were a total of 7,000 people in this world with the Chiyou bloodline. All of them were gathered in the Heaven's Spirit bone pagoda, so it was unimaginable for an outsider to possess the Chiyou bloodline.
"Yes, I saw it with my own eyes. He absorbed a desolate bone with the Chiyou bloodline." Diao Yuan nodded and said.
Even though Diao Yuan was so sure, the others still had looks of disbelief, especially Qi Chang. He stared at Diao Yuan warily and said, "Diao Yuan, are you up to something?"
Diao Yuan was unhappy again as he sneered, "Since you don't believe it, then let the Grand Elder test Luo Zheng. I believe that Luo Zheng can pass the test. Letting him obtain an additional bloodline of our clan is not a bad idea!"
If Luo Zheng did not need a bloodline transplant, then the remaining bloodline in the past thousand years would naturally land in the hands of Qi Chang's Sect Master brother.
When Qi Chang heard Diao Yuan's words, he immediately fell silent. Although he did not get along with Diao Yuan, if what Diao Yuan said was true, then this matter was clearly beneficial to Qi Chang.
"Don't argue." The Grand Elder's figure fluttered as she came in front of Luo Zheng. Her mesmerizing eyes stared at Luo Zheng and said, "Whether you possess the Chiyou bloodline, we will know with a test. Qi Chang, go inform the clansmen to bring out this batch of children who are six years old. I'll go make preparations. Luo Zheng, you will participate in the Blood Purification Ceremony with my clansmen."
Saying that, she smiled at Luo Zheng. With a slight twist of her slender waist, she disappeared into the distance.
Not long after …
In a square in the Chiyou clan, four to five hundred Chiyou clansmen were gathered. They were all whispering to each other.
"Why is the Blood Purification Ceremony brought forward?"
"I heard that an outsider possesses our clan's bloodline, so it was brought forward."
"How is this possible? Our bloodline is noble, how can an outsider casually possess it? "
"Could that bloodline have been stolen?"
"That's impossible. What do you think a bloodline is? That you can steal it just because you want to? Even the First Elder doesn't have the ability to transplant a bloodline … "
Luo Zheng and Diao Yuan were standing on one side of the square. When they heard the discussions, Diao Yuan smiled awkwardly and said, "Our Chiyou people attach great importance to our bloodline. It is inevitable that we will be hostile to you. Don't take it to heart."
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. Naturally, he wouldn't argue with these people.
Everyone waited here for a while before a gentle white light flew over. The Grand Elder had already gracefully landed in the center of the plaza. She smiled and said to everyone, "The Blood Purification Ceremony was originally supposed to be held four months later. Let's bring it forward this time."
The Blood Purification Ceremony was specially used to test the purity of the bloodline. It was usually held once every few years. This Blood Purification Ceremony was specially prepared for the children of the Chiyou Clan who had just grown up.
"Why should it be brought forward?!"
"My Lu 'er just turned six yesterday. It's too unfair to hold the Blood Purification Ceremony today!"
"Yes, holding the Blood Purification Ceremony for an outsider? Don't you take that guy too seriously? What if it's a lie? "
The Chiyou Clan generally believed that they should wait for the children to grow up before testing them. This would increase the purity of the bloodline.
In fact, the purity of the bloodline was determined at birth. It was just that these clansmen wanted to fight for greater benefits for their children, so they set the Blood Purification Ceremony at the age of six.
The First Elder unhurriedly stretched out her hand and gently waved at the crowd. The clansmen in the plaza immediately quieted down. The First Elder said softly, "Those who don't want to participate in the Blood Purification Ceremony can leave. Those who stay can start now."
After that, the First Elder gently clapped her hands. An oval-shaped array suddenly appeared in the center of the plaza. As the light in the array kept flashing, an oval-shaped jade stone floated out.
This jade stone was the size of a washbasin, glowing with a faint milky white light. There were no flaws on its surface, like a huge and complete egg.
When the white jade stone appeared, the First Elder turned to Luo Zheng and said with a faint smile, "Luo Zheng, you can come here."
Luo Zheng nodded and was about to walk over.
At this time, a clansman on the other side of the plaza said, "Why should an outsider be tested first?! My Wu 'er and Ling' er want to be tested first! "As he said that, the clansman came forward with a boy and a girl in each hand.
Seeing this scene, Luo Zheng stopped in his tracks and shrugged his shoulders. He wouldn't fight with the children for it anyway.
The First Elder shook her head slightly. These clansmen had always been very repulsive of outsiders. She was somewhat helpless, but it was inconvenient for her to say more. She could only say, "In that case, let's test Wu 'er and Ling' er first."
Wu 'er and Ling' er were a pair of twins. They had just turned six years old. The two children were very happy when they saw the beautiful white jade stone and walked around it.
The First Elder squatted in front of the two children and smiled sweetly at Ling 'er. She reached out and gently pinched Ling' er's hand.
Ling 'er's big eyes blinked a few times and asked, "Big Sister First Elder, why are you holding my hand?"
"Because it will hurt a little," the First Elder said. Her fingertip flickered with a white light as thin as a needle, and she gently pricked Ling 'er's fingertip.
Several drops of blood shot out from Ling 'er's fingertip.
Ling 'er felt pain from her fingertip and was about to open her mouth and cry. The First Elder's finger lightly touched her forehead, and the pain immediately disappeared.
As for the drops of blood, they were led into the air by the First Elder. After they merged into a ball, they splashed onto the jade stone.
A little bit of dark red blood bloomed on the surface of the jade stone. In the blink of an eye, the blood was absorbed by the jade stone.
"Buzz …"
The jade stone began to tremble. The originally pure white jade stone began to turn a faint red light, and it was constantly turning red.
"It's already at the third stage! And the color is still changing! "
"Fourth stage!"
"This Ling 'er of Bo Xu's family is probably at the fifth stage or above, right?"
"I already said that Ling 'er's bloodline purity is definitely good. It's definitely good!" The Chiyou clansman who came over with the two children said excitedly.
After more than ten breaths, the color of the jade stone no longer changed and became stable.
The First Elder announced faintly, "Ling 'er's bloodline is at the upper sixth stage, which is also a quasi-seventh stage. Congratulations to Bo Xu!"
Bloodline purity was divided into ten stages. A sixth stage bloodline was already considered a top grade in the Chiyou clan. In the future, it would be of great use in the Chiyou clan.

Chapter 2151
Bo Xu stepped forward and picked up Ling 'er, throwing her high into the air and cheering.
The Chiyou people around the square also began to cheer …
The sense of racial crisis in this world was very strong. They had to maintain their race's strong bloodline talent.
Ling 'er's performance meant that they had a successor. In the past thousand years, there had only been two sixth-grade bloodline warriors, and Ling' er was the only seventh-grade bloodline warrior. Naturally, it was worth celebrating and cheering!
Ling 'er, on the other hand, was still in a daze. She had no idea what was going on.
Next, it was her brother Wu 'er's turn.
The First Elder did the same. He pierced Wu 'er's finger again and drew a few drops of blood, sprinkling it on the jade stone.
"Drip …"
"Buzz …"
After the blood was absorbed by the jade stone, it vibrated again and began to turn red.
But the speed of the change was very slow.

"Wu 'er's bloodline is not good …"
"Well, after so long, he's only second-grade. I'm afraid he can't even reach third-grade."
"Sigh, the dragon has nine sons, but each son is different. Even if they are twins, the difference in bloodline purity is huge."
After a while, the First Elder announced, "Wu 'er's bloodline is third-grade lower."
This time, Bo Xu silently stepped forward and picked up Wu 'er. He patted his son's back gently. Bloodline purity was completely determined by luck. No one could change it. His family had Ling' er, which was already a blessing from the heavens.
After Ling 'er and Wu' er's bloodline purity was tested, more and more people carried their own children up.
Most of these children's bloodline purity was between third-grade and fifth-grade.
Third-grade was qualified, and fifth-grade was excellent.
Those who passed were a little depressed, but those who got excellent were in high spirits.
Every Blood Purification Ceremony was like this. Some families were happy, and some were sad. The First Elder had seen it many times, so he was very indifferent.
After the children were tested, the First Elder said to Luo Zheng, "Now it's your turn!"
She was really interested in whether Luo Zheng had the Chiyou bloodline or not.
Luo Zheng walked to the front of that oval-shaped jade stone and glanced at it. After absorbing blood for a while, this jade stone would turn milky white again. When it was dripped with blood, it would turn red. The deeper the red, the purer the bloodline would be …
The problem was that Luo Zheng's blood was golden. Would the jade stone be able to detect it?
"Give me your hand," the Great Elder said with a smile.
"Can I do it myself?" Luo Zheng said.
The Great Elder shook her head and grabbed Luo Zheng's arm. She condensed a milky white needle in her palm and stabbed it toward Luo Zheng.
However, the result was beyond the Grand Elder's expectations.
She heard a "ding" sound. The light needle was unable to pierce through Luo Zheng's arm!
"Eh?"
The Great Elder looked at Luo Zheng in surprise. She didn't expect Luo Zheng's arm to be so sturdy. She couldn't even pierce his finger!
Luo Zheng smiled helplessly and said, "I told you to let me do it myself …"
She blinked and condensed another milky white needle. This time, the power of this needle was several times stronger than before.
"Ding!"
She still couldn't pierce Luo Zheng's finger!
An incredulous expression appeared on her pretty face. She glared at Luo Zheng for two or three breaths. Then, she condensed a milky white light again. Under the dense light, a thin white light needle spiraled and emitted an astonishing power.
The light needle shot toward Luo Zheng's fingertip with a "whoosh" sound. This time, it finally pierced through Luo Zheng's finger.
"Drip, drip, drip …"

Diao Yuan, Qi Chang, and the other clansmen saw the golden blood and felt strange. They had never seen blood of this color before.

After this piece of jade absorbed the golden blood, it rumbled with a "weng" sound.

The clansmen looked at the jade stone and began to discuss it.

As long as they could prove that Luo Zheng had the Chiyou Bloodline, he wouldn't need a blood transplant. This was a good result for Qi Chang.
Diao Yuan frowned and stared at the jade stone. He had a feeling that things weren't so simple.
After ten breaths, the jade stone's surface was still suffused with a faint pink light.
"Wastelanders from the outside aren't good enough. Is he one of our people's offspring?"
"The second rank is too ordinary …"

The First Elder's eyes flashed with disappointment. This result didn't satisfy her, but she still smiled and said, "Congratulations. You do have our blood, but it's a pity it's of a lower rank."
Luo Zheng wasn't too disappointed.
His mother had told him that he had the purest Li Clan blood, and was the most legitimate descendant of the Chiyou Clan. But in the end, his blood had been changed. This result was acceptable.
What was important was that he had proven himself to the Chiyou Clan. As long as he stayed in the Chiyou Clan, he'd accomplished his goal.
Just as Luo Zheng nodded, the jade stone beside him suddenly made a "kacha" sound.
Everyone's eyes turned to the jade stone.
A blood-red crack appeared on the surface of the jade!
"Crack!"
"Crack!"
"Crack!"
Every time there was a cracking sound, there would be one more blood-colored crack.
Not long after, the surface of the originally flawless white jade was covered with blood-red cracks.
Hum!
With a tremor, the jade stone's color began to change.
From a faint pink to a bright red, the entire jade stone was a dark red, as if it would bleed from the surface!
Crack crack crack crack …
Soon, the jade stone shattered into countless pieces before everyone's eyes.
The plaza, which had been abuzz with discussion, suddenly fell silent. Everyone was stunned by this sudden scene.

Chapter 2152
Seeing this, the first elder was also a little confused.
The others did not know much about the Blood Purification Ceremony. They only knew that this jade stone could test the purity of one's bloodline, but they did not know the principle behind it.
However, the first elder understood because this jade stone was given to her by her father.
This Crying Blood Stone was very fragile, but it could stimulate the power hidden in one's bloodline. If the power hidden in one's bloodline was stronger, it could stimulate its color.
The Crying Blood Stone could not only test the bloodline of the Chiyou Clan, but also the bloodline of other clans. However, the array laid out was different, and the color changed was also different.
The first elder herself had an upper eighth-grade bloodline, which was a quasi-ninth-grade bloodline. Once, when she dripped her blood on the Crying Blood Stone, the stone turned dark red!
That was because she was the first generation successor of her father, and the purity of her bloodline was far from what other Chiyou Clan members could compare to!
Now, the Crying Blood Stone had turned dark red, but it was broken. What did this mean?
"Luo Zheng, you stay here. I'll be back soon." The First Elder gave him a deep look. She reached out and gently brushed the broken pieces of the Crying Blood Stone. All of the Crying Blood Stone fragments immediately entered her hand.
"Whoosh!"
Her soft waist gently twisted, then she turned into a white light and fled into the distance.
At this time, whispers sounded in the square again …
These Chiyou Clan members were not stupid. They naturally understood Luo Zheng's special ability, and many of them even looked at Luo Zheng uneasily.
"You brat, how did you do it? You actually broke the Crying Blood Stone!" Diao Yuan laughed.
Not to mention that the Crying Blood Stone was broken, the color of the Crying Blood Stone just now was enough to rank it among the ninth-grade bloodlines.
One had to know that in so many years, including the first elder, there were only two people who could rank among the ninth-grade bloodlines in the Chiyou Clan!
"I don't know either." Luo Zheng also looked puzzled.
The first elder shuttled back and forth in the cave for a while, then fled into a narrow cave. After several twists and turns, she arrived in front of a jade wall in a short while.
After standing steadily, she softly called, "Father."
"Purple Jade, what's the matter?" A faint voice came from behind the jade wall. This voice seemed to be unusually tired, but it contained a hint of solemnity.
"The Bloodcry Stone is broken," Purple Jade said lightly.
"If it's broken, then it's broken. This Bloodcry Stone is extremely fragile. The slightest carelessness will cause it to shatter. It's normal that you've used it for so many years. I'll make another one for you in the future," the voice said indifferently, not taking it to heart.
Hearing this, the cub frowned slightly and continued, "The Bloodcry Stone wasn't broken."
"Oh? How did it shatter? "the voice asked curiously.
"Today, Diao Yuan brought back an outsider. Diao Yuan said that this outsider has our bloodline, so I brought forward the Blood Purification Ceremony. I didn't expect that after the outsider tested his bloodline, the Crying Blood Stone would turn bright red and then shatter …" The cub recounted what happened during the Blood Purification Ceremony.
"The Bloodcry Stone was shattered during the Blood Purification Ceremony?" The voice immediately turned serious. "The bloodline of an outsider can shatter the Bloodcry Stone!"
"What does this mean?" Purple Jade asked strangely.
"That means that guy's bloodline is far beyond the tenth grade!" The exhaustion in the voice disappeared without a trace and was replaced by a trace of excitement.
Purple Jade still asked with a puzzled expression, "Beyond the tenth grade? Father, didn't you say that the bloodline of our Chiyou Clan is only at the ninth grade? "
"Haha, compared to you, it is indeed only at the ninth grade. The highest Chiyou Clan in this world can only reach the ninth grade because … the bloodline of exiles like us isn't very pure." The voice revealed a trace of helplessness.
The bloodline was inherited from one's parents and ancestors.
Most of the Chiyou Clan in this world were inherited from a few exiles of the Chiyou Clan.
Purple Jade's father was an exile from the mother world, so Purple Jade's bloodline was far purer than that of other Chiyou Clan members!
However, if Purple Jade was the child of other Chiyou Clan members, the bloodline would be weakened.
Although there were occasional miracles where the child's bloodline might be better than Purple Jade's, the possibility of this was too small …
"The Crying Blood Stone that I gave you can, at the very most, detect a rank ten bloodline. That's because rank ten is already more than enough for you all. I never expected someone to be able to shatter this Crying Blood Stone. I want to see that person." That voice indifferently said.
Purple Jade's eyebrows suddenly raised. "Father is coming out of seclusion?"
The voice behind the jade pendant was silent for a moment before faintly smiling. "We exiles have all been cursed by the Primal Chaos Ancient God. Only by hiding in the bone pagoda can we preserve our lives. Seclusion is just an excuse …"
"Rumble …"
The surface of the jade disc continued to crack, and as the cracks spread, the entire jade disc quickly collapsed.
A thin middle-aged man appeared from behind the jade disc. This middle-aged man's eyes were calm, but there was a strong sense of exhaustion that lingered. His eyebrows were somewhat similar to Purple Jade's.
Seeing her father's appearance, Purple Jade was stunned.
For countless years, Purple Jade had never seen her father's appearance.
For as long as Purple Jade could remember, she knew that her father was behind this jade disc.
However, she could only hear her father's voice and had never seen him. Even after her mother passed away, her father had never left this jade disc.
The other exiles in the bone tower were the same. They sealed themselves in the jade disc and rarely showed themselves …
Once, when the Chiyou Clan was in a life-and-death crisis, another exiled in the bone tower had left the jade disc and taken action.
Purple Jade remembered that the exiled exiled had displayed incredible power. In the blink of an eye, he had twisted hundreds of Xuanyuan Clan members into pieces and resolved the Chiyou Clan's crisis.
This meant that it wasn't that her father couldn't leave this jade disc, but that he didn't want to …
Purple Jade had always been brooding about this. After all, she didn't even know what her father looked like.
She never thought that her father would leave the jade disc because Luo Zheng had shattered the Blood Cry Stone!
For a moment, Purple Jade was stunned as she stared blankly at this unfamiliar face.
A helpless smile appeared on the middle-aged man's face. He stepped forward and gently stroked Purple Jade's white and elastic cheeks. He said faintly, "It's not that Father doesn't want to leave the jade disc. As exiles, we are constantly tortured by the Chaos Ancient God's curse. We can only curl up in this small space as much as possible …"
"Mm." Purple Jade blinked. Although her father's appearance was unfamiliar, she was very familiar with his voice.

Chapter 2153
Luo Zheng was still waiting in the square.
Not only did the Chiyou people not disperse, but it was said that after the Crying Blood Stone was broken, it attracted even more people.
The Crying Blood Stone was very important to the Chiyou people. After all, the purity of each clansman's bloodline needed to be tested by this stone. After the test, the Chiyou people would cultivate according to the purity of the bloodline.
The higher the purity of the bloodline, the more rewards and resources the clansmen would receive.
"Now that the Crying Blood Stone is broken, how can we hold the Blood Purification Ceremony in the future?"

The middle-aged man concealed his cultivation, but Luo Zheng still perceived a different aura from him!

After the Great Elder and the middle-aged man arrived, the Great Elder pointed at Luo Zheng and said, "Father, he is Luo Zheng."
The square quieted down again. Even Diao Yuan could not help but take a few steps back.
Although the cub was the Chiyou people's Great Elder, the clansmen knew very well that the cub was not the strongest in their clan.
The real almighties of the Chiyou people were hidden in the bone pagodas. Many clansmen could not see them in their entire lives.
No one knew exactly how powerful these powerful beings were. There were only many versions of legends circulating within the clan. One of the most popular legends was that a powerful being from the Chiyou Clan had killed hundreds of people from the Xuanyuan Clan.
After all, many clansmen had witnessed it, and many of them were still alive.

As soon as he said this, Luo Zheng suddenly raised his eyebrows.
In this world, there were quite a few people who could see through his True God cultivation, but this middle-aged man actually knew that he cultivated the True Intent of Dao. This person's eyesight was actually so formidable.
"This is a little strange. The middle-aged man was already very interested in Luo Zheng's background after listening to Purple Jade's story. After all, Luo Zheng's bloodline purity was beyond his imagination.

In this world, almost all of the living beings mainly cultivated the Aragami. They relied on the vast amount of desolate bones that were left behind during the desolate era.
There were only a few who cultivated to True Divinity.
For example, Purple Jade had also received his teachings, opened up the Inner World, and even condensed a Godhead. However, Purple Jade did not go too far on this path.
The reason was that it was too difficult to cultivate to True Divinity in this world. In comparison, cultivating the Aragami was much easier.
Luo Zheng had actually cultivated to the Upper True God realm, and had begun to comprehend the True Intent of Dao. Unless he was personally taught by those old freaks, it was impossible to reach this step.
'What sharp observation skills …'
Luo Zheng was slightly surprised.
Facing the middle-aged man's question, he had some concerns in his heart and did not know how to answer for a moment.
Who knew that the middle-aged man had actually judged Luo Zheng's thoughts and immediately said, "Our Chiyou Race is also very united in the Mother World. No matter where you come from, as long as you don't endanger our race, we will treat you as a family."
Luo Zheng was silent for a moment, and then said, "Can I not say it now?"
"Yes," the middle-aged man smiled slightly, "But I need to test your bloodline again."
In fact, the middle-aged man already had some speculations about Luo Zheng's origin, but he still needed to check the purity of Luo Zheng's bloodline first.
"Okay," Luo Zheng nodded, and once again extended his hand to the middle-aged man.
The middle-aged man stretched out his hand and lightly touched Luo Zheng's finger, and a drop of bright golden blood oozed out from Luo Zheng's arm.
Luo Zheng could not see what method the middle-aged man used to draw his blood.

The drop of blood from the middle-aged man's fingertip suddenly merged with Luo Zheng's golden blood.
Then, a small magic array appeared in the air, enveloping the mixed blood and spinning in the air.
Not long after, the small formation suddenly burst out with a domineering aura.
It was as if a giant beast was hidden in the fingertip-sized drop of blood. At the same time, the middle-aged man's own drop of red blood was completely swallowed by Luo Zheng's golden blood!
The middle-aged man always had a faint smile on his face, but at this moment, his expression changed.
His eyes suddenly burst with a bright light, and he looked at Luo Zheng with a face full of disbelief, and said excitedly, "Your, your bloodline …"

Diao Yuan and the others looked at the middle-aged man's reaction, and felt quite puzzled.
After all, the middle-aged man was a supreme existence in their clan, and they might not be able to see him in their lifetime.

Chapter 2154
Fu Er looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression and said in a daze, "There's no mistaking it. It's the same blood law. The power of the bloodline in my blood can't resist at all. Only someone with a royal bloodline can do that!"

The nine tribes were Yu Yi, Fang Yi, Huang Yi, Bai Yi, Chi Yi, Feng Yi, Xuan Yi, Yang Yi, and Chu Yi.
When the Li Tribe was at its most prosperous, each tribe was a huge monster in the mother world.
Fu Er's bloodline was inherited from the Feng Yi Tribe.
Fu Er gave the Chi You Tribe members in the bone tower a Crying Blood Stone and defined their bloodline as between grade one and grade ten.
In fact, even a grade ten bloodline was not enough in the mother world.
For example, Fu Er's bloodline was about grade thirteen according to the Crying Blood Stone.
If the bloodline was tested with the Crying Blood Stone, it would not be detected, but the power contained in Fu Er's bloodline was not enough to break the stone.
Therefore, when Fu Er heard the cub say that the Crying Blood Stone broke during the bloodline test, he was very surprised!
Only the eighty-one Demon Gods of the Li Clan had a higher bloodline than Fu Er. Their bloodlines could be considered as fourteenth-grade.
Even the fourteenth-grade bloodline of the eighty-one Demon Gods could not easily devour Fu Er's blood.
Fu Er's strength was not the strongest among these exiles, but his bloodline was the highest. The only possibility that his blood could be devoured so easily was a royal bloodline.

At first, Luo Zheng did not completely trust these Chi You Tribe members. He had always had concerns in his heart. For example, these exiles were exiled here because they betrayed the Chi You Tribe, or because they committed a big mistake in the Chi You Tribe and were imprisoned in this prison …

The concerns in his heart gradually dissipated.

Chi Yi was the youngest among this group of people. After countless years, he still maintained his youthful appearance. He was also the last Chi You to be exiled to this prison.

Hearing Chi Yi's words, these people were stunned. Pairs of eyes stared at Luo Zheng.

A Royal Bloodline didn't need to be so courteous.
But if Luo Zheng was a direct descendant of Chi You, then it was different.

As for Diao Yuan, Qi Chang, and the other elders, they naturally knelt in front of Luo Zheng.

"Child, don't ask so many questions. Quickly lower your head …"
The clansmen around the square were somewhat confused, but they also prostrated themselves on the ground. In the entire square, only Luo Zheng was left standing.

Hearing Luo Zheng's words, the oldest person slowly stood up and continued to stare at Luo Zheng. His face was filled with grief and indignation. "Did our Li Tribe lose? Why were even the Great Tribal Chief's children exiled to this world?! "
In his eyes, the Royal Bloodline was the pride of the Li Tribe, and the Great Tribal Chief's children surpassed the Royal Bloodline. They were the pride of the pride.
Now that even the Great Tribal Chief's grandson had been exiled to this world, one could imagine the Chi You Tribe's current situation!

They thought that Luo Zheng was also exiled to this world.

The others also looked at each other in dismay.
Other than being exiled, was there any other way to enter this world?
There was a vast distance between this world and the Mother World. With Luo Zheng's cultivation base, it was even more impossible for him to cross such a long distance …
One of the people's eyes suddenly lit up. He then asked, "Could it be that you came from the ship above the water bone pagoda?!"
Regarding that ship, the Aragami of this world had long observed it.
They saw a huge ship falling rapidly. It was estimated that it would crash into this world in a few years.
No one knew who built the ship, but all the races were preparing. As long as the ship crashed, they would swarm in and loot everything inside.
The resources in this world were too scarce. Other than relying on the desolate bones to cultivate, there were almost no other cultivation resources.
Every race was paying attention to the movements of the ship, including the Chi You Tribe. They had even made a detailed plan to determine the location where the ship would crash, and were ready to go and take a share of the loot.
However, not long ago, an accident happened to the ship, and it once again rose into the sky above the Chaos!
However, at that time, the most powerful race in this world, the Nuwa Tribe, made their move and firmly bound the ship to the top of the water bone pagoda.
The old man, Fu Er, Chi Yi, and the others were also staring at Luo Zheng. It was really possible!
"Yes." Luo Zheng nodded. "My mother told me that the ship was built by Chi You himself. I was not born in the Mother World, but on that ship. As for the situation in the Mother World, it should be similar to what you think. The Li Tribe was indeed defeated. That was why my grandfather built such a ship to let the people escape."

Chapter 2155

Chapter 2156

Chapter 2157

Luo Zheng was only an Upper True God. However, he showed signs of cultivating two True Meanings of Dao. His soul was also much stronger than ordinary Aragami. With his strength, it would not be difficult for him to fight against a Middle Aragami.
Moreover, when he arrived at the Chiyou Clan, he would still have time to devour the bloodline desolate bone.
Hearing the old man's answer, Purple Jade smiled and nodded. "Purple Jade understands!"
After the exiles left, Purple Jade ordered someone to bring the envoy of the Xuan Yuan Clan over.
Not long after, a few streaks of light flew over from the horizon.
The person in the lead had a handsome face. He had sharp eyebrows and bright eyes. His face was full of pride.
His cultivation level was only a Middle Aragami. However, he did not panic when he stepped into the territory of the Chiyou Clan.
When he saw the Great Elder, he did not show any courtesy. He just took out a jade page and flicked it at Purple Jade. He said arrogantly, "Take this. This is a letter of challenge from the Xuan Yuan Clan!"
"How dare you!"
"How dare you!"
Seeing the young man's actions, Diao Yuan, Qi Chang, and the other Chiyou tribesmen were enraged.

The young man smiled and said, "My Xuanyuan Clan is one of the most powerful clans in the world. Of course, we don't need much confidence. My clan has selected five people to fight. In my opinion, it's a waste of time. I, Xian Ze, alone is enough."

A genius had appeared out of nowhere in the Xuanyuan Clan. It was said that the entire Xuanyuan Clan had poured in all their resources just to nurture this one person.
