"My father said that you don't dare to touch me," Xian Ze said calmly.
"What's there to not dare? I'll kill you right now." The aura in Qi Chang's body rose. It was like a wild ancient beast, ready to devour someone at any time.
As an elder of the Chiyou Clan, Qi Chang's strength was comparable to Diao Yuan's. It was more than enough to kill a Middle Aragami.
The pressure from this aura pressed down on Xian Ze, forcing him to take two steps back. However, there was still a faint smile on his face, and he said fearlessly, "You can try!"
"Alright!" Qi Chang's temperament was irritable. How could he tolerate such a junior provoking him?
Seeing that he was about to attack, Zi Yu suddenly stretched out her hand and blocked Qi Chang. She said indifferently, "The Xuanyuan Clan killed my clansmen in the Eternal Wasteland. According to the agreement, life and death are not counted. If my Chiyou Clan kills him here, it's against the rules."
"But …" Qi Chang was unconvinced.
Zi Yu ignored Qi Chang and continued, "I've received this letter of challenge. The time is set to three months later. My Chiyou Clan will accept the challenge. You may leave."
Xian Ze looked at Zi Yu with a slightly disinterested expression. He had personally sent the letter of challenge on behalf of the Xuanyuan Clan. Naturally, there was a layer of provocation in it. However, he did not expect the Chiyou Clan's great elder to be so indifferent, as if he did not care at all. Naturally, he was disappointed.

Hearing this, Luo Zheng, who had not said anything, frowned slightly.

"What?" Xian Ze had just turned around.
Slap!
No one saw how Zi Yu attacked, but a clear palm print had already landed on Xian Ze's face.
Zi Yu raised its jade-like hand and said coldly, "You're an envoy from the Xuanyuan Clan. Although I can't kill you, you've lost your manners. This lesson is unavoidable. Go back and tell your elders to teach you how to behave."
As the Chiyou Clan's great elder, Zi Yu's strength was unfathomable, second only to the exiled from the clan.
This slap was not light. The palm print swelled up in the blink of an eye, and half of Xian Ze's cheek swelled up.

Hence, if the clansmen wanted to obtain more bloodline desolate bones, they had to be allocated according to the purity of their bloodline and the talent they displayed. Chapter 2158
The tribe of the Chiyou Clan was divided into two areas.

Some of the Aragami who had devoured enough Aragami energy would have their physical bodies doubled in size. In the beginning, it would be extremely difficult for them to even walk.
Chang Hu also encountered this problem.

Chang Hu fell heavily onto the ground.
The wide and flat drill ground trembled.
The mountain-sized stone lock smashed towards the other side, where a dozen Chi You youths were gathered!

"Sou!"

As for Chang Hu himself, he was already scared out of his wits when he saw this scene. He sat on the ground and stared blankly at the stone lock that was flying away. When he thought of the next scene where blood splattered, he actually closed his eyes …
Luo Zheng saw that the stone lock was only two to three feet away from those young men of the Chiyou Clan. However, Diao Yuan was unable to catch up with it.
Seeing this, Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and grabbed the air.
"Boom!"
In that instant, the stone lock seemed to have crashed into an invisible wall, forcefully falling from the sky.
"Ka ka ka ka …"
Under the sudden impact, countless cracks appeared on the surface of the stone lock. In the end, it turned into a pile of gravel. The gravel scattered all over the ground.

As for those young men of the Chiyou Clan, they were already scared out of their wits. Every one of them was pale. Just now, they all thought that they were dead for sure.

However, the power of the Rage God was completely integrated into the body. This was a pure method of refining the body. It was impossible to extract the power from the body!
Luo Zheng used the power source of the nine stars of the Spiritual Pedestal Tier and comprehended the Extra-Dao Mystical Power, Power Retrace. He could control this invisible power as he wished. This kind of technique of using power made Diao Yuan gasp in amazement.

Chapter 2159

Chapter 2161

Cheng Wei and Luo Zheng stood in front of Diao Yuan.
After all, they were all from the same clan. If Diao Yuan really killed Duo Mo because of this, it would damage the harmony …
After all, Diao Yuan was a senior and also an elder of the clan. Being bullied by a little fellow from the clan and not willing to back down even half a step, it was impossible for him to back down. Therefore, he really wanted to kill Duo Mo.
At this moment, a voice was heard, "Diao Yuan, as an elder of our clan, don't you think it's too much to bully a junior?"
They turned their heads and saw a woman in yellow clothes standing not far away. This woman's face was covered with a thick layer of frost.
"Mother!"
Duo Mo saw the woman and called out with a joyful look on his face.
This woman in yellow clothes was Elder Chun of the Chiyou Clan, and also Duo Mo's mother.
Elder Chun's strength was extremely powerful, even above Diao Yuan. She had also made many contributions to the Chiyou Clan. However, she was extremely protective of her own people, especially her son.
"Elder Chun," Diao Yuan said coldly, "don't tell me you don't know the identity of this person next to me? I've been ordered to come here, but your son refused. How am I supposed to explain this to the grand elder? "
"Identity? Isn't he just a wastelander?! "Duo Mo was even more arrogant now that he had someone to back him up.
Elder Chun's gaze focused on Luo Zheng, her expression complicated. "I've heard that even the Bloodcry Stone couldn't determine the purity of his bloodline. A royal bloodline, the grandson of the great chieftain. As Chiyou Clan members, we should do our best to serve him."
When she finished speaking, Elder Chun bowed deeply to Luo Zheng.
Cheng Wei and Duo Mo were dumbfounded by Elder Chun's words. Wei Cong and the others were stunned as well.
They didn't know what a royal bloodline was, but they did know the great chieftain's …
The exiles had given birth to the Chiyou Clan here, so naturally they'd passed down some of the Chiyou Clan's heritage.
They'd heard all sorts of legends about the great chieftain since childhood. After all, every time they entered or exited the Chiyou Clan's territory, they had to pay their respects to the statue of Chiyou. In their hearts, the great chieftain was a spiritual totem. No one was allowed to tarnish him, and no one dared to tarnish him.
Now that Elder Chun said Luo Zheng was the great chieftain's grandson, they couldn't help but be dumbfounded.
"What … what's going on?" Cheng Wei stared at Luo Zheng in a daze. He knew Luo Zheng wanted to transplant his bloodline and shouldn't have come to the cave so quickly. He hadn't expected Luo Zheng to have the Chiyou Clan's bloodline!
This was too much of a coincidence, too much of an exaggeration. He found it hard to believe.
"Mother, what do you mean …" Duo Mo's imposing manner suddenly shrank. If it weren't for his mother, Duo Mo wouldn't have believed it even if he was beaten to death.
Elder Chun ignored his son and continued, "However, we must win the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda against the Xuanyuan Clan this time. The five people sent must be the elites of the clan. Luo Zheng's cultivation is still shallow. If we give him only three months to cultivate, I'm afraid he won't be able to take my son's place. Why don't we wait for three months before Duo Mo gives up the cave?"
In the end, Elder Chun was still partial to his son. The implication was that even if Luo Zheng's status was extraordinary and his bloodline was pure, they still had to rely on strength to speak in this special period.
"That's right. With his strength, if he takes my place, I'm afraid he'll be smashed into meat paste," Duo Mo agreed with his mother.
Diao Yuan couldn't help but pat his head. Encountering this unreasonable mother and son really gave him a headache.
At this time, Cheng Wei blinked and smiled slightly, "Whether he's strong or not, we'll know after a fight. Elder Diao Yuan, what do you think?"
Before Diao Yuan could speak, Duo Mo quickly responded, "Yes! I wonder what this … Brother Luo Zheng thinks? "

Chapter 2162
Seeing that Luo Zheng agreed so easily, Elder Chun had a suspicious look on her face.
However, she knew her son's strength very well. Among the younger generation of the Chiyou Clan, other than Cheng Wei and Mao Xue, Duo Mo really had no opponent.
Diao Yuan did not agree with Cheng Wei's suggestion. Why should he fight with Duo Mo?
However, since Luo Zheng agreed so quickly, Diao Yuan had nothing to say.
On one side of the Na Desolate Cave was a wide wasteland. This impromptu duel immediately attracted the attention of many people.
"Sigh! Sigh! Who is Duo Mo fighting with? "A woman with neat bangs came up and asked curiously.
The woman was less than 20 years old. Under her neat bangs was an extremely beautiful face. Her big eyes were looking at Luo Zheng who was not far away.
"Mao Xue, you're here too?" Cheng Wei glanced at Mao Xue.
Mao Xue was the strongest Mid-level Aragami in the Chiyou Clan. Her bloodline purity was not high, but there was some special mutation in her bloodline. It was said that this was very rare.
"I heard a lot of noise outside. Who is that young man? I've never seen him before," Mao Xue said.
Cheng Wei smiled. "I've mentioned him to you a few days ago. He's Luo Zheng who saved my life."
"So it's him. Interesting! According to what you said, if Luo Zheng is really that amazing, won't Duo Mo lose for sure? "Mao Xue asked again.
"Probably. Just watch and you'll know." Cheng Wei shrugged.
Under everyone's gaze, Duo Mo softly uttered the word "grow". Then, his body quickly expanded.
In the blink of an eye, Duo Mo turned into a giant thousands of feet tall. Then, his voice thundered, "It's your turn, little guy!"
In Duo Mo's eyes, the Aragami power in Luo Zheng's body was pitifully weak. Even if he activated the Aragami power, he would only be a little guy in front of Duo Mo.
However, Luo Zheng did not plan to fight seriously. He did not want to waste time on Duo Mo.
"No need. I don't need to activate the Aragami power to defeat you …"
Luo Zheng stood on the ground and looked up at the mountain-like Duo Mo.
"Really? Let's give it a try then … "
Duo Mo sneered, and his huge palm slammed down on Luo Zheng. In his opinion, slapping Luo Zheng to death was no different from slapping an ant.
Luo Zheng stood in place. His eyes flashed slightly, and a powerful soul pressure spread out.
Elder Chun had been paying attention to Luo Zheng's movements. Seeing Luo Zheng so calm, she speculated whether Luo Zheng had some extremely powerful trump cards or some special magical weapon.
Who would have thought that Luo Zheng would erupt with such a powerful soul power in an instant!
Before Duo Mo's slap landed, he felt as if his head had been smashed by a sledgehammer. His face instantly turned pale.
With a plop, he knelt on the ground.
"You …" Duo Mo roared. He gritted his teeth and swept his gaze towards Luo Zheng.
However, just as he was about to move, his mind trembled once more. His soul shook violently, and golden stars appeared on his forehead.
"Boom!"
This time, Duo Mo fell to the ground …
Duo Mo, who was lying on the ground, still refused to accept the result. He struggled to get up, but his body shook violently again. He was paralyzed on the ground, and he fainted.
"Pfft …"
Seeing this, Mao Xue couldn't help but laugh. She asked Cheng Wei, "Are you sure this is a fight? Isn't the difference too big? "
Cheng Wei shrugged. "An unequal fight. I expected it. Duo Mo is just asking for humiliation."
Most of the time, the True Gods in the Divine Dominion would use soul attacks as a sneak attack. However, they would never rely on soul attacks completely. After all, the opponent had a soul-type defensive magical weapon. Using soul attacks would be useless.
However, the souls of the Aragami in this world were pitifully weak.
Luo Zheng could kill an Aragami of the same level as him without any scruples.
An Aragami like Duo Mo was almost defenseless in front of him.
"Is this enough?"
Luo Zheng slowly walked over, stared at Elder Chun, and asked.
"So your soul power is so powerful. However, it's unfair to rely on such a method to win," Elder Chun said with a complicated expression.
In front of Luo Zheng, Duo Mo had no power to fight back.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly. "Everyone has their strong points. Elder Chun, are you saying that I can't use my advantage?"
Although Elder Chun was a woman, her temperament could be considered heroic. After thinking for a while, she said frankly, "It's my son's skill that is not good enough. He should give up the Na Huang Cave."
"If you knew it would be like this, why did you bother?" Diao Yuan couldn't help but twitch his mouth.
…
On the walls of the Na Huang Cave, fine bone patterns densely covered the walls.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand and gently stroked these bone patterns. "These bone patterns are naturally produced by the beast bones of the Primal Chaos Ancient God. They can actually guide the power of the Aragami. How strange …"
At the bottom of the Na Huang Cave, there was only enough space for one person to sit. After Luo Zheng entered and waited for a while, he felt a rich aura of the power of the Aragami being emitted.
The power of the Aragami flowed along the bone patterns, gradually gathering towards Luo Zheng.
"Such rich power of the Aragami …"
Above the Na Huang Cave, there were more than ten people constantly refining the desolate bones, sending the pure power of the Aragami into the Na Huang Cave.
They specialized in refining the desolate bones, and their techniques were skillful and experienced. Their refining speed was more than ten times faster than Luo Zheng's!
Luo Zheng's mind flickered, and the power of the Rage God seemed to have a life of its own. It formed a torrent that gathered in Luo Zheng's body, soaked in, and diffused …
The power of the Aragami filled every bone, every piece of flesh and every strand of hair.
Just like that, six days later …
"Elder Diao Yuan, Luo Zheng has already absorbed about two million pieces of desolate bones. Do you want to continue?" Wei Cong asked.
Three million pieces of desolate bones was a relatively large number for a low-ranked Aragami.
If it were anyone else, absorbing two to three hundred thousand pieces of desolate bones would cause them to experience the signs of a physical calamity. However, Luo Zheng did not show any signs of any changes. He continued to absorb the power of the Aragami like a bottomless pit.
"Continue!" Diao Yuan said with a smile.
Previously, when the elder said that he would provide Luo Zheng with ten sets of bloodline desolate bones, Diao Yuan was still worried. Now, he saw that his worries were completely unnecessary.
This rascal's physique was not as simple as it looked on the surface.
Since Elder Diao Yuan had said so, Wei Cong no longer had any concerns. Since Luo Zheng's appetite was so big, he might as well send a few people to refine the desolate bones for Luo Zheng.
Large amounts of desolate bones were once again poured into the Na Huang Cave, being absorbed by Luo Zheng.
However, after another six days, Luo Zheng had once again absorbed more than two million pieces of desolate bones, but he still did not show any signs of changes.

Chapter 2163
Luo Zheng did not know how much power of the Aragami his body could contain. He did not know how much power of the Aragami he would need to inject into his body before he could face the calamity.
The ranks of the Aragami were also divided into upper, middle, and lower ranks, which corresponded to the upper, middle, and lower ranks of the True Gods.
This division was also created by the exiles. However, it was not strict in nature. That was because there was a huge difference in strength between the lower Aragami. For example, Xiao Yun who could transform into a hundred feet tall was considered a lower Aragami. However, those who could transform into a three to four hundred feet tall were still considered lower Aragami …
When the height of the body exceeded eight hundred feet, it would be defined as a middle Aragami.
However, there was a threshold for becoming a middle Aragami. When the time came, the first calamity would be triggered. If one could not overcome the calamity, his body would immediately disintegrate and he would end up in a miserable state.
"It's been half a month and I can still absorb the power of the Aragami!"
In this narrow Na Huang Cave, Luo Zheng stretched his body. He could clearly feel the changes in his body.
A powerful force that was difficult to control was flowing rapidly in his body. If the Na Huang Cave was not too small, he would really want to stimulate the power of the Aragami to see how far he had cultivated.
Before entering the Na Huang Cave, Diao Yuan had warned Luo Zheng not to stimulate the power of the Aragami in the cave.
If it was a normal cave, he could transform into a giant and collapse the entire cave without suffering any damage. However, the Na Huang Cave was naturally formed by the cracks in the bone tower. No matter how powerful the force was, it would not be able to expand it.
There was a tragedy in the Chi You Tribe. A Aragami had stimulated the power of the Aragami in the Na Huang Cave. His body instantly expanded by a hundred times and he was forcefully 'squeezed to death' in the cave.
While thinking, Luo Zheng was absorbing the pure power of the Aragami …
"Eh?"
The pale yellow power of the Aragami could not be absorbed into his body. It was slowly circling around the Na Huang Cave.
"Finally saturated?" Luo Zheng had such a thought.
However, the people outside the Na Huang Cave did not know about it. They were still pouring the power of the Aragami into the Na Huang Cave.
"Enough!"
Luo Zheng's voice was transmitted to the outside of the Na Huang Cave.
Upon hearing this, Wei Cong immediately revealed a look of joy, "Enough, enough. There's no need to refine any more desolate bones!"
Although the Chi You clan had quite a large reserve of desolate bones, these desolate bones were not only supplied to the Chi You clansmen, but also to the three major sects under their jurisdiction.
Luo Zheng alone had consumed millions of desolate bones. It was more than ten times or even a hundred times more than the average clansman!
Even a genius like Mao Xue had only absorbed 800,000 desolate bones before being saturated. Back then, she was seen as a monstrous figure in the Chi You clan, but now, Luo Zheng had absorbed millions of desolate bones. Wei Cong and company no longer knew how to look at Luo Zheng.
"Brother Luo Zheng, stay in the Na Huang Cave and don't come out. I'll inform Elder Diao Yuan!" Wei Cong said from above.
After the time it took for an incense stick to burn, Diao Yuan flew over. He looked down at Luo Zheng from the crack at the top of the Na Huang Cave and said, "You actually stayed in the Na Huang Cave for half a month. You have a big appetite!"
Luo Zheng smiled and immediately asked, "What's the next step of the physical calamity?"
"After the Rage God's power is saturated, the physical calamity will immediately descend. Eat this Calamity Avoidance Pill that the Grand Elder gave you." After saying that, Diao Yuan threw down a shiny pill.
Luo Zheng stretched out his hand to grab the pill. Without any hesitation, he stuffed it into his mouth.
This Calamity Avoidance Pill was used to resist the physical calamity. It was also refined by the exiles of the clan.
The stronger the body, the more powerful the physical calamity. Luo Zheng had absorbed so much Rage God's power, so the physical calamity he would face would definitely be extraordinary. The Chi You clan naturally had thorough considerations.
The resources in this world were extremely scarce, so refining the Calamity Avoidance Pill was not easy. Among the tens of thousands of people in the Chi You clan, only three had been given the Calamity Avoidance Pill. Luo Zheng was the fourth.
When Luo Zheng swallowed the Calamity Avoidance Pill, the medicinal essence quickly dissolved. Luo Zheng felt his body begin to shrink.
"Kacha, Kacha …"
Luo Zheng felt that his bones were constantly being squeezed, and the friction made a strange sound.
It was not only his bones that were being compressed, but also his organs. His muscles that were as hard as steel, and even his meridians. His body began to squirm involuntarily.
"What's going on …" Luo Zheng frowned. Although he tried his best to resist the shrinking force, his hands and shoulders could not help but tremble.
"The physical calamity has begun." Diao Yuan's face suffused a solemn look.
He was also sitting cross-legged above the Na Desolate Cave. He gently shook a small golden pouch in his hand. Pieces of Bloodline Desolate Bones rolled out from the pouch.
When Wei Cong and the others saw the Bloodline Desolate Bones, their eyes revealed looks of envy.
Ordinary Desolate Bones were not considered precious to the Chi You clan. After all, the Chi You clan controlled several pieces of the Eternal Desolate Plains. Digging up Desolate Bones did not require the clansmen to do it personally.
However, the Bloodline Desolate Bones in this world were too few. Even the clansmen did not receive a lot of them.
"When you transcend the physical calamity, you have to absorb all the Rage God's power. Do not waste a single bit!"
As Diao Yuan said that, he reached out his hand and gently grabbed the Bloodline Desolate Bones. Wisps of brown Rage God's power emanated from the Bloodline Desolate Bones and immediately surged into the Na Desolate Cave.
"Si Si Si …"
At this moment, Luo Zheng could no longer speak. His face was cold and his teeth were chattering. His body was trembling like a sieve.
"When transcending the physical calamity, absorbing the Bloodline Desolate Bones is the greatest test. My clan's disciples are usually given one Bloodline Desolate Bone as a reward, but you need ten. The more Bloodline Desolate Bones you have, the stronger the Chi You Desolate Body you form, but … the physical calamity will also be more difficult! This will also be a difficult test for you. "Diao Yuan muttered.
From Diao Yuan's point of view, the elder's request for Luo Zheng to absorb ten Bloodline Desolate Bones was too hasty. However, the elder was an expert from the mother world. His foresight was not something Diao Yuan could compare with. Even so, Diao Yuan was still slightly worried.
"Bo!"
Just as Diao Yuan's voice faded, he heard an explosion coming from the Na Desolate Cave.
Luo Zheng's left chest had ruptured. A half-foot long wound had appeared on the side of his heart!
Golden blood spewed out from the wound and splattered on the walls of the Na Desolate Cave!
Now, Luo Zheng no longer had the time to absorb the Rage God's power from the Bloodline Desolate Bones. Furthermore, the Rage God's power in his body was already saturated. He should not have been able to absorb any more Rage God's power.

Chapter 2164
Previously, when Luo Zheng absorbed a Bloodline Desolate Bone, it made Luo Zheng feel as if something was about to emerge from his body.
The entire process was not painful.
However, when the brown Rage God power drilled into his wounds, it was like a red-hot knife cutting through his flesh. There was a searing tearing sensation.
"Uh …"
Luo Zheng clenched his teeth and could not help but grunt coldly.
Be it Diao Yuan or the elder, they had warned him that the pain of the physical calamity was unbearable. As for Luo Zheng, he had made sufficient mental preparations.
After all, he had experienced similar pain several times. Some of the pain was unbearable, but he had managed to survive them all.
Although the physical calamity was described as terrifying, it was ultimately just pain on the physical level.
However, after experiencing it, he realized that although the pain was only on his body, it reached his soul!
"Bo!"
On Luo Zheng's back, another wound tore open. Golden blood shot out like a blood arrow, hitting the wall. At the same time, more Rage God power was injected into his body through the wound.
"Si …"
He clenched his teeth and took a deep breath.
As time passed, the number of wounds on Luo Zheng's body increased.
At this moment, his entire body looked like he had been dismembered. From head to toe, his body was covered with wounds. Some were a foot long, while others were two to three inches long. There was not a single spot that was unscathed. And the Rage God power in the Bloodline Desolate Bone was constantly drilling into his body through the wounds.
"The second set of Bloodline Desolate Bone …" Diao Yuan took out another golden bag. Dozens of brown Desolate Bones the size of a fist rolled out.
Two hundred Bloodline Desolate Bones were a set. Absorbing ten sets of Bloodline Desolate Bones required Luo Zheng to consume 2000 pieces …
And now, the entire Chi You clan only had about 10,000 Bloodline Desolate Bones. That was to say, Luo Zheng alone needed to consume a tenth of the Chi You clan's stockpile of Bloodline Desolate Bones!
The muscles on Luo Zheng's body were almost completely torn apart. There was a temporary pause in the physical calamity.
He heaved a sigh of relief as he sat motionless on the spot. Just a slight movement of his body would cause intense pain throughout his body.
However, this tranquility only lasted for a few breaths of time. It was just the calm before the storm.
"Pa!"
Soon, Luo Zheng felt an explosion in his chest. One of his ribs had broken without any warning …
Luo Zheng's bones were even tougher than a grandmist treasure artifact after cultivating to such a degree.
An ordinary True Divinity wouldn't be able to shake his body at all, but under the descent of this Mortal Body Tribulation, even such tough bones couldn't resist at all. They constantly shrank and then broke apart …
"Crack!"
Another explosive sound was heard. This time, it was the leg bone that was broken.
The sounds of explosions grew louder and more frequent, like firecrackers exploding inside Luo Zheng's body. Every time his bones exploded, the surrounding wounds would spray out a mist of blood.
Even in the God Realm, Luo Zheng had never suffered such a serious injury. This Mortal Body Tribulation was comparable to walking through the gates of hell. Luo Zheng doubted whether he could endure it!
As the bones inside Luo Zheng's body began to break, his spine couldn't support him anymore. Luo Zheng, who had been sitting cross-legged, fell limply to the ground. With every bone in his body broken, he didn't even have the strength to get up.
The Desolate God's power that had forcefully drilled into Luo Zheng's body also drilled into Luo Zheng's broken bones. This further intensified the pain Luo Zheng was suffering.
Above the Na Desolate Cave, Diao Yuan was silently watching everything.
The Mortal Body Tribulation was a kind of calamity, but it was also an opportunity.
If one couldn't endure this calamity, their body would completely collapse, and they would die.
If one could endure this calamity, they would be reborn and possess the Chiyou Desolate Physique.
Everything depended on whether Luo Zheng could endure it or not!
"Your body is too strong. No wonder you were bestowed with a bloodline desolate bone …" As Diao Yuan spoke, he refined the third set of bloodline desolate bone.
The Mortal Body Tribulation was a general term. When one's body reached a certain level of strength, it would automatically collapse. From the outside to the inside, there were a total of five tribulations. They were the Flesh Splitting Tribulation, Bone Shattering Tribulation, Tendon Rupturing Tribulation, Blood Boiling Tribulation and Marrow Injury Tribulation. These were the Mortal Body Five Tribulations.
Bystanders could not interfere in this process. This was because the Mortal Body Tribulation was not caused by external factors like the Heavenly Tribulation.
Just now, when Diao Yuan heard the sound of bones shattering coming from Luo Zheng's body, he knew that Luo Zheng was undergoing the second tribulation.
If one could survive the Flesh Mountain Five Tribulations, his entire body would be reconstructed. It was equivalent to being reborn. And the reborn body would be infused with the bloodline desolate bone's power. It would possess the Chiyou Desolate Physique of the Chiyou clan!
With all his bones broken, Luo Zheng lay paralyzed on the ground, motionless.
After a while, Luo Zheng's meridians began to rupture …
This degree of pain was enough to make Luo Zheng hover between life and death. Other than passively enduring it, he did not have any means to resist.
And while Luo Zheng was undergoing the Mortal Body Tribulation, he did not notice that the golden blood that sputtered out of his body had quietly fused into the walls of the Na Desolate Cave.
There were many fine bone patterns in the walls. The Chiyou clan used these bone patterns to channel the Rage God's power.
But at this moment, Luo Zheng's golden blood was slowly flowing down the bone patterns, seeping into the tiny cracks in the bones.
Beneath the Na Desolate Cave was still a part of the bone pagoda. The golden blood was constantly flowing down the bone pagoda through the tiny cracks …
A hundred feet …
A thousand feet …
A thousand feet …
A hundred thousand feet …
A million feet …
The bone pagoda that stood outside was only a part of this beast bone. There was still about one-sixth of its length underground, about four million feet.
After Luo Zheng's golden blood flowed down such a long distance, it fused into a bone buried deep underground. This bone was several times longer than the bone pagoda. This bone was the spine of the Primal Chaos Ancient God!
Half of the Primal Chaos Ancient God's skeleton was buried deep underground. The bone pagoda that was exposed outside was the towering ribs in its chest cavity!
"Drip, drip, drip …"
The golden blood dripped into the cavity inside the spine, gathering into a palm-sized ball of blood. This ball of blood seemed to have gained intelligence all of a sudden. It rolled in the space inside the spine and began to jump forward at a high speed …
As if there was a unique force, this ball of golden blood moved at an unimaginable speed. Hundreds of millions of miles were crossed in less than half an incense stick's worth of time.
When this ball of golden blood reached the end of the spine, it seeped into a huge skull …

Chapter 2165
This huge skeleton had been in this world for eighty billion years.
Before the body of the Primal Chaos Ancient God had fallen, the exiles of this world would have been purified by the Light of Judgment within a thousand years.
After the skeleton of the Primal Chaos Ancient God had fallen, the exiles had a place of refuge.
These powerful exiles were also in awe of the corpses of the Primal Chaos Ancient Gods.
The exiles knew that more than half of the Primal Chaos Ancient God's skeleton was buried underground. They also knew that the head of the Primal Chaos Ancient God was a natural refuge.
However, no one dared to dig it up …
Even if the skeleton was placed in front of the exiles, they would not believe that a Primal Chaos Ancient God had died just like that.
Such a powerful Primal Chaos Ancient God had surpassed the level of living creatures. The boundary between life and death was blurred to it.
In the huge head, there was a world the size of a divine territory. However, this world was not a real world in the ordinary sense. If one had to make an analogy, this world was roughly equivalent to an ordinary person's "mind." This was a world formed by one's spirit.
After the consciousness of the Primal Chaos Ancient God split apart, it created such an illusory world. And in this illusory world, there were tens of thousands of spiritual bodies. These spiritual bodies existed in a special form in this world. They could think independently, but they didn't have individual personalities.
The combination of these spirit bodies was equivalent to the soul of the Primal Chaos Ancient God.
"Beep, beep, beep …"
After Luo Zheng's blood was sent into the head of the Primal Chaos Ancient God, it was discovered by a spirit body.
Purely visually, the spirit body was a small ball of light that emitted a green halo. However, this form was only a reflection of the outside world. If it could be integrated into the mind of the Primal Chaos Ancient God, the spirit body's image would be more complete.
In fact, in the world of the Primal Chaos Ancient God's mind, these spirit bodies were also divided into ugly and beautiful. They had their own language system, could marry, could reproduce, and had their own social system. Of course, all of this was formed by the Primal Chaos Ancient God's soul …
"Buzz, buzz, buzz …" The halo on the surface of the small green ball of light fluctuated gently. Its consciousness was changing, and the consciousness was the language of these spirit bodies. "A new blood sample has appeared. I have to report to the Brain Master."
"Buzz …"
Then, with a flash of green light, the small ball of light disappeared on the spot.
For spirit bodies, in this enormous' mind world ', their movement speed was determined by the speed of thought of the Primal Chaos Ancient God. Therefore, the movement speed of spirit bodies was almost limitless. In an instant, they could cross a distance of hundreds of billions of kilometers.
The spiritual body appeared in the center of the mental world. There was a huge halo here, and there were huge light balls fixed on the halo. There were about a hundred huge light balls on the halo, and these huge light balls were the "brain masters" that the small light balls mentioned.
Most of the time, these brain masters were in a state of deep sleep.
When the small ball of light appeared, one of the Brain Masters naturally woke up.
"9527, why have you come?"
The Brain Lord flashed slightly, emitting a unique buzzing sound as he asked with his consciousness.
The small light ball immediately released its own consciousness to communicate. "We have found a new blood sample. It seems to belong to the Chi You Tribe, but the smell is more fragrant. Do we need to observe the original owner?"
"Give up, 9527. It is unrealistic to count on these people …" The Brain Master sighed. "We have secretly observed every exile here. None of them are qualified, and the descendants of the exiles are even worse … Since the third truth was changed, there is not much hope."
"But Brain Master, didn't you teach us that we should never give up no matter what?" The small light ball buzzed, as if it was not convinced.
At this time, another Brain Master began to flash. "Haha, that is just to make life more interesting for you little fellows in this world."
On the surface of the small light ball 9527, a ray of red light appeared, indicating that it was angry. "You are the Brain Masters. How can you do this? I request to examine this blood sample!"
"Give up, 9527. Swimming happily in this world is more interesting than exploring these things, isn't it?" The Brain Master persuaded. "Don't disturb me. I am sleepy …"
As he spoke, the light on the surface of these Brain Masters gradually dimmed, and they fell into a deep sleep again …
The surface of the small light ball emitted a strong red light, constantly buzzing, trying to wake these "Brain Masters," but none of them responded.
"If that is the case, I will send the blood sample myself," the small light ball said angrily.
These spiritual bodies had different levels of authority. Spiritual bodies like the small light ball had very low levels of authority. In the past, it would not have been able to do this because as long as one of the Brain Masters was awake, the authority would take effect. The small light ball had to ask the awakened Brain Master for help.
But over the years, the Brain Masters had become lazier and more lax. Sometimes, all the Brain Masters would go into hibernation together. This was the case now.
At this time, the small light ball could overstep its authority and have some authority that only the Brain Masters could use.
So the golden blood continued to fly in the skull, all the way to the center of the Primordial Chaos Ancient God's mouth.
When the Primordial Chaos Ancient God's physical body was still alive, there used to be a huge tongue here. But as the Primordial Chaos Ancient God decayed and the physical body disappeared, the tongue naturally no longer existed.
But in the center of the mouth, there was a funnel-shaped spiral.

Every time, the Brain Masters would be full of hope, but in the end, they would inevitably be disappointed …
After countless disappointments, the Brain Masters had given up. They no longer had any hope for the trash in this world.
The small light ball was different from the Brain Masters. The Brain Masters had lived for too long and seen too much, so they lost interest in many things. But the small light ball was different. It was still young, and it hoped for a miracle to happen …

The golden blood was sent to the center of the mouth.

Luo Zheng had clearly survived the first Five Tribulations of the Physical Body. He did not expect that he would have to go through it all over again. Luo Zheng was not mentally prepared at all.
The old man looked at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression and said, "So you still don't know …"
Luo Zheng indeed didn't know that he had been injected with blood marrow. "Know what?"
"When you underwent the Physical Body Tribulation, you should have fused with other bloodlines," the old man explained.
"Other bloodlines?" Luo Zheng looked at his own body. Indeed, he didn't notice anything strange. "It seems that nothing has changed."

There was indeed blood marrow of a Primal Chaos Ancient God in the Na Desolate Cave. However, even though the old man was experienced and knowledgeable, he couldn't tell what was wrong with transplanting the Primal Chaos Ancient God's bloodline. Besides, it was only the old man's inference.
"You've just passed the body tribulation and still need to consolidate it. Let's not investigate this matter for now …"

The exiles were naturally very happy. Meanwhile, Purple Jade once again arranged for Mao Xue and the others to cultivate intensively, preparing for the battle with the Xuanyuan Clan.
During these three months, Luo Zheng continued to absorb the power of the Rage God.
In addition to cultivating the Rage God, Luo Zheng also paid close attention to the world inside his body …
Back then, his mother had injected a large number of spirit crows into Luo Zheng's Inner World. Luo Zheng used most of the spirit crows to strengthen his own Godhood.
However, a considerable number of spirit crows remained.
These spirit crows were like rats, living underground in Luo Zheng's Inner World.
As long as the spirit crows didn't come into contact with the Sea of Chaos, they wouldn't be vaporized. These spirit crows continued to divide and reproduce, and their numbers steadily increased.
Every once in a while, Luo Zheng would send the spirit crows into the primal-chaos sea, where they would transform into a brown mist that would strengthen his godhood. The cycle continued, and his cultivation base continued to grow stronger day by day.
During this time, the old man would occasionally visit Luo Zheng in the cultivation area. Every time, he would ask Luo Zheng if there were any special changes in his body.
Luo Zheng was naturally surprised, but there were no special changes in his body.
The only thing that was slightly strange was the bone piece in his chest.
When Luo Zheng absorbed the power of the Rage God, this small diamond-shaped bone piece would always guide most of the power of the Rage God to him.
However, when Luo Zheng looked inside himself, the bone piece didn't have the slightest reaction. Luo Zheng didn't delve into this matter.
Three months passed in the blink of an eye …
Right below the bone tower, hundreds of Chiyou people filed out.

There was a huge bone ship in front of Purple Jade.
In this world, the other materials were abnormally rare. Only bones were nearly endless. These bones had not decayed after countless years, and the material was also very strong. Naturally, they were suitable to be used to make some things, such as this thousand-zhang-long ship.
"The day has finally come." Mao Xue smiled slightly and landed lightly on the bone ship.
Luo Zheng, Cheng Wei, and the others jumped on the bone ship without saying a word.

The Chiyou's bone tower didn't need these elders to guard it. After all, there were exiles in the bone tower, and other races couldn't invade.

However, the price of a draw was Shang Long's life!
After Cheng Wei fought in the third round, the Xuan Yuan Clan naturally wouldn't let Tan Xuan fight.
If they let Tan Xuan fight directly, he would probably be killed by the angry Cheng Wei.
"Gong Lu, you go." Ji Zhuo ordered.
"Yes." Gong Lu, who had been waiting for a long time, cupped his hands towards the Grand Elder and was about to fight.
At this time, Ji Zhuo said in a low voice: "Be careful. This Cheng Wei is very strong. Three of our clansmen died in his hands. If you can't beat him, you can directly admit defeat."
A trace of bitterness appeared on Gong Lu's face.
After all, Cheng Wei, who he was about to fight, was not an ordinary person.
Moreover, Tan Xuan had just killed his opponent. Now, he had to face Cheng Wei's anger. He was really unlucky.
The power of the Rage God was released from Cheng Wei's body. His aura slowly rose, and his figure also slowly grew taller. His white robe rustled, and the bamboo sword in his hand also grew sharply in the wind.
Facing Gong Lu of the Xuan Yuan Clan, Cheng Wei still showed courtesy. He slightly cupped his hands towards the other party and said indifferently: "Just now, your Xuan Yuan Clan also said that in the arena, life and death are up to fate."
Gong Lu blinked and nodded: "Yes, so what?"
"Then, you can leave it to fate …"
As soon as he finished speaking, Cheng Wei moved.
He leaped into the air like a wild ancient beast.
His thousand feet tall body jumped up and rushed to a height of several hundred thousand feet. The bamboo sword in his hand flipped and drew an emerald green sword light in the air. From top to bottom, it emitted a resolute aura, and directly slashed towards Gong Lu.
Gong Lu saw Cheng Wei fighting so desperately right from the start. Probably because he wasn't mentally prepared, he immediately became timid.
In a battle between experts, once one's mentality showed weakness, one would be very passive.
Gong Lu flipped and barely dodged Cheng Wei's slash.
"Boom!"
However, just as Cheng Wei landed on the ground and took a step forward, another emerald green sword light followed closely.
Seeing this, Gong Lu clenched his teeth and stabbed the slender long Mo Sword in his hand, aiming straight for Cheng Wei's chest.
He planned to force Cheng Wei to retreat. After all, if Cheng Wei wanted to slash him, his Mo Sword would definitely pierce through him …
He didn't expect Cheng Wei to completely ignore the Mo Sword that was about to pierce through his chest. Even if both sides would suffer, he still wanted to kill Gong Lu!
How could this be …

Chapter 2175
Cheng Wei was filled with anger. Every move he made was a desperate one. How could he hold back at all?
The bamboo sword transformed into streaks of emerald green sword light that furiously swept toward Gong Lu.
Gong Lu was forced to flee the entire time. He wanted to occasionally counterattack, but that would immediately lead to Cheng Wei's even more brutal suppression.
Clang, clang, clang!
The bamboo sword, which was hundreds of feet long, slashed against the modao several times, producing crisp sounds that shook the heavens.
As Gong Lu retreated, he blocked dozens of sword strikes in a flurry. Finally, he revealed a serious flaw.
Cheng Wei's eyes flashed. He seized this flaw and slashed at Gong Lu with his sword.
Buzz, buzz, buzz …
Gong Lu saw that he could not dodge. The golden light around his body intensified. He wanted to use the Xuanyuan Desolate Body to forcefully withstand this attack.
However, when Cheng Wei's seemingly light bamboo sword slashed his body, Gong Lu realized how naive his thoughts were.
Puchi!
The bamboo sword exploded with a sharp cutting power, and the golden light on the surface of Gong Lu's body was shattered. He only felt a chill in his right arm, and his gigantic arm, which was hundreds of feet long, was instantly cut off.
After this arm was cut off, the power of the Rage God contained within it quickly began to dissipate. The huge arm quickly shrank and flew to an unknown corner.
"I admit defeat! I admit defeat! "Gong Lu shouted loudly.
He threw away his modao. With a rumble, he knelt on the ground. Instantly, two large pits appeared on the ground.
If he did not admit defeat, he would probably end up like Shang Long and be killed by Cheng Wei.
Even though Gong Lu admitted defeat, Cheng Wei still did not show any mercy.
In her fury, Cheng Wei wanted to kill someone. But when she saw that her sword was about to chop down, the long sword stopped halfway and could no longer move … …
Diao Yuan had appeared behind him at some point. He was already the same size as Cheng Wei. Diao Yuan's two fingers gently clamped the bamboo sword.
"Come back. He admitted defeat. You've already won," Diao Yuan said calmly.
Although life and death did not matter in the arena, Gong Lu had already knelt on the ground and admitted defeat.
If Cheng Wei took his life again, it would cause the Xuanyuan Clan to retaliate. This was not something the Chiyou Clan wanted to see.
Before leaving, Zi Yu had also received instructions from his father, Fu Er.
Try not to get into a conflict with the Xuanyuan Clan. Even if you lose, it doesn't matter.
In the eyes of the exiles, the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda was no longer important. What was important was Luo Zheng.
After all, Luo Zheng knew how to get out of this prison …
This time, he let Luo Zheng come out with Purple Jade just to give Luo Zheng some experience.
Cheng Wei gradually calmed down and snorted at the trembling Gong Lu. "Consider yourself lucky!"
Soon after, he retreated with Diao Yuan. Meanwhile, Gong Lu also retrieved his severed arm and returned to the Xuan Yuan Clan's side with his tail between his legs.
Cheng Wei won the third match.
Relying on Cheng Wei, the Chiyou Tribe finally regained some face.
On the other hand, the members of the Xuanyuan Clan all revealed cautious expressions.
"That Cheng Wei's strength is indeed extraordinary," said a black-faced Elder of the Xuanyuan Clan.
Another Elder had some doubt on his face. "This Cheng Wei's strength is not bad, but in the Eternal Wasteland, he can't fight one against three. Even if he really defeated Yun Ang, Tong Wen, and Wu Shuo, it's impossible for him to kill all three of them. It's not to the extent that none of them can return …"
"There should be some other secrets that we don't know," said the black-faced Elder with a frown.
Ji Zhuo smiled faintly. "Whether there are secrets or he really has strength, we'll still follow the original plan. Xia Yun, you fight. If Xia Yun loses, Xia Feng will fight."
In the Xuanyuan Clan, Xia Yun and Xia Feng were second in rank after Xian Ze.
Before Xian Ze rose to power, Xia Yun and Xia Feng had always been the focus of the Xuanyuan Clan's nurturing. However, Xian Ze's sudden appearance overshadowed the siblings' radiance.
In Ji Zhuo's eyes, as long as Xian Ze was here, the Xuanyuan Clan would have no worries.
The Heavenly South Bone Pagoda was theirs!
"Yes, Grand Elder!"
The green-clothed girl bowed to Ji Zhuo. She was the Xia Yun that Ji Zhuo had mentioned.
"Yun 'er, be careful." Behind Xia Yun, her brother Xia Feng warned.
Gong Lu was almost powerless against Cheng Wei, which made Xia Feng a little nervous. He was afraid that something would happen to his sister.
"Yun 'er knows." Xia Yun smiled.
"If you can't beat him, just admit defeat," added Xia Feng.
"Pfft …"
Xian Ze, who was still sleeping on the ground, couldn't help but laugh. "Xia Feng, aren't you too cowardly? You want to admit defeat before the fight even starts? "
"What does it have to do with you?" Xia Feng replied coldly.
Many people in the Xuanyuan Clan didn't like Xian Ze, but he was too monstrous. Everyone gave in to him.
However, Xia Feng had never given Xian Ze any face.
Xian Ze didn't get angry. He just chuckled and said, "I hope you won't ask me to save you in the end." Then, he turned around and continued sleeping on the ground.
Cheng Wei continued to fight in the fourth match.
In the past three months, Cheng Wei's cultivation had improved a lot in the Na Huang Cave, but there was still a certain gap between him and Mao Xue.
Strictly speaking, he was a little stronger than Gong Lu, but the real gap was definitely not as big as it was in that battle. It was just that Gong Lu was flustered and couldn't resist at all …
Xia Yun's strength was far stronger than Gong Lu's.
Facing Cheng Wei's crazy attacks, she retreated in an orderly manner while fighting steadily without any panic.
When Cheng Wei's desperate spirit was exhausted, Xia Yun took the initiative and gradually gained the upper hand. The pure black thin sword in her hand was like a boneless spirit snake, forming a cage and firmly trapping Cheng Wei inside.
Cheng Wei was like a trapped beast. No matter how hard he tried, he couldn't escape.
"Brother Cheng Wei is going to lose. He's no match for that green-clothed girl," said Luo Zheng indifferently as he watched the two giants fight.
"This Xia Yun is actually so powerful …" Mao Xue blinked her big eyes and said.
When Purple Jade, Diao Yuan, and Qi Chang saw Xia Yun's strength, their expressions didn't look too good.
They didn't expect the young Aragami of the Xuanyuan Clan to grow so quickly!
According to the order of battle, this Xia Yun shouldn't be the strongest, but the strength she displayed was already far from Cheng Wei.
Even Mao Xue might not be able to take her down, not to mention there were two people behind the Xuanyuan Clan. Could the Chiyou Clan only count on Luo Zheng?

Chapter 2176
Xia Yun calmly waved the black rapier in her hand.
After turning into a Aragami, her judgment of distance and space had become complicated. Therefore, the Aragami's fighting methods were rough and violent.
It was much more difficult for an ordinary person to be a middleman and a giant to be a middleman.
However, Xia Yun was able to control all of this to the smallest detail. It was a terrifying ability.
Every strike of her sword was very careful and just right.
There were a few times when she could have taken Cheng Wei's life. However, the black rapier only made a light cut on Cheng Wei's body, leaving a non-fatal wound.
Soon, more and more wounds appeared on Cheng Wei's body. His white robe was soaked in blood.
"You've already lost. Stop struggling." Xia Yun pursed her lips and said.
Cheng Wei did not listen. His attacks became fiercer and fiercer.
With no other choice, Xia Yun gently put away the black rapier. She lightly tapped the ground with the tip of her foot, creating a large pit that was dozens of feet deep. Taking advantage of Cheng Wei's attack, she jumped up. The black rapier was like a dancing giant snake, piercing downwards.
"Puff!"
The rapier directly pierced through Cheng Wei's arm.
The bamboo sword suddenly flew out and nailed to the ground. Without the support of the Aragami's power, the bamboo sword that was hundreds of feet long suddenly turned back to three feet long …
At the same time, Xia Yun's black rapier was wrapped around Cheng Wei's head. As long as she used her strength, Cheng Wei's head would be separated from his body.
Seeing this scene, the Chiyou people were so nervous that their throats were hanging.
The Chiyou people had already lost Shang Long. If Cheng Wei fell as well, they would not be able to accept it.
However, Cheng Wei was stubborn. He knew that he was no match for Xia Yun, but he refused to admit defeat!
"Admit defeat …" Xia Yun said. She tilted her head and thought for a while. Then she said, "If you are angry, I apologize to you on behalf of Tan Xuan. He should not have killed Shang Long by mistake."
The muscles on Cheng Wei's face trembled.
After a while, he calmed down and sighed softly, "A defeat is a defeat. It's my own fault for not being able to get justice. You don't have to apologize to me."
"Okay." Xia Yun nodded lightly. She carefully took a few steps back and slowly left.
Cheng Wei looked at Xia Yun's back with a complicated expression …
This woman was neither arrogant nor rash. Even if she had the upper hand, she was always vigilant. Her state of mind was always the same. He was ashamed of his inferiority and accepted his defeat wholeheartedly.
In the fifth match, Cheng Wei lost.
As soon as Cheng Wei lost, the people from the Xuan Yuan Clan relaxed.
In their eyes, there was no suspense in the following battle.
Only Mao Xue was left on the Chi You side.
According to their speculations, Mao Xue's strength was only on par with Xia Yun's.
Even if Xia Yun lost, there was still Xia Feng, who was stronger.
As for Xian Ze, if he wanted to lie on the ground, then so be it …
At this moment, Xian Ze who was lying on the ground was in a bad mood.
Originally, he thought that after the Chiyou Clan defeated Xia Yun and Xia Feng, these fellows in the clan would come to him and beg for his help. Then, he would reluctantly go out to fight and turn the tide. Wouldn't that be great?
However, a single Xia Yun was able to take care of all of them. His Immortal Grace was completely useless, so how could he be happy?
"Looks like the outcome has been decided …"
"The Heavenly South Bone Pagoda should be controlled by the Xuanyuan Clan!"
"Hahaha, we've finally chased those annoying Chi You fellows out! Flying Sickle City will belong to the Python Tiger Clan in the future! "
Discussions broke out at the edge of the wasteland.
The battle between the top factions often affected the fates of many people.
Naturally, there were those who were happy and those who were worried. Those who relied on the Chi You Clan naturally had ugly expressions. If the Xuan Yuan Clan won this battle, there would be no place for them in Flying Sickle City …
After Cheng Wei lost, Mao Xue would fight.
This battle was extremely crucial. After all, in the eyes of most people, Mao Xue was the pillar of support for the Chi You Clan.
If Mao Xue lost, the Chi You Clan would be completely defeated.
Mao Xue and Xia Yun didn't have much interaction between them. After the two met, they began an intense battle.
As the bloodline was passed down from generation to generation, the purity of the bloodline would become thinner and thinner. However, there would occasionally be miracles.
When it was passed down to Mao Xue, her bloodline had mutated.
Her Chi You Desolate Physique was covered in a layer of unique blood patterns.
Why would Mao Xue's bloodline change like this? Even the exiles couldn't explain it …
Because of the existence of these unique blood patterns, Mao Xue had a considerable advantage in speed. Speed was the most important aspect of a huge Aragami.
Therefore, as soon as the two girls started to fight, Mao Xue quickly gained the upper hand.
However, after Xia Yun's battle with Cheng Wei, she became more and more courageous. The black rapier in her hand danced in all directions, forming an impassable wall that firmly blocked Mao Xue outside.
Mao Xue turned into a streak of blood-red light and continuously attacked Xia Yun's defenses. However, her efforts were all for naught and she was eventually forced back by Xia Yun.
As Xia Yun defended against Mao Xue's attacks, she followed suit and forced Mao Xue into a corner.
She had set up a net from the very beginning. Only when Mao Xue stepped into this net did she seal off the exit of this net.
Soon, Mao Xue found that her advantage was being countered and she couldn't use it at all …
Finally, Mao Xue stuck out her tongue and said helplessly, "I've lost."
Xia Yun nodded and didn't say anything else. She also carefully took a few steps back and quickly left.
Even though she had defeated the number one genius of the Chi You Clan, Xia Yun's expression was still calm.
"Sigh, if I knew the Chi You Clan's younger generation was so weak, I wouldn't have used those tricks in the beginning …"
"I didn't expect the Chi You Clan to decline so much …"
The elders of the Xuanyuan Clan also had mixed feelings when they found out that they had won so easily.
One had to know that they had been planning for a long time for this Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, striving for a perfect plan.
They didn't expect the opponent to be unable to resist at all.
"Purple Jade, our Xuanyuan Clan has won this battle. According to the agreement, your Chi You Clan has to withdraw from the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda," Ji Zhuo said with a smile.
He was happy not only because he had taken control of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
More importantly, the Xuanyuan Clan's younger generation had almost crushed the Chi You Clan. Ji Zhuo didn't expect the Chi You Clan's younger generation to be so weak.
A clan needed continuous inheritance, and the younger generation represented the clan's future. If this continued, the Chi You Clan would decline sooner or later.
Hearing Ji Zhuo's words, the purple jade smiled and said, "Grand Elder Ji Zhuo, did you make a mistake?"
"What mistake?" Ji Zhuo's face darkened. He thought the purple jade wanted to go back on its words.
If the Chi You Clan didn't want to give up the control of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, this would be troublesome. After all, the Xuanyuan Clan didn't want to fight the Chi You Clan all out.
Even if the Xuanyuan Clan won, it would be a partial victory, and the losses it would bring were not something they wanted to see.

Chapter 2177
The elders of the Xuanyuan Clan were stunned when they heard Purple Jade's voice.
According to the bet, both clans would send five of their disciples to fight.
The Chiyou Clan had defeated four people in a row, and the Xuanyuan Clan had ignored the last person …
After all, even the most powerful Chiyou Mao Xue had been defeated.
"I see. I've been careless." Ji Yu sighed in relief. As long as the Chiyou Clan was willing to fulfill the bet, there was nothing else to worry about. "If that's the case, Xia Yun, you fight again."
At this time, Xian Ze said casually, "I've already said that the Chiyou Clan is a bunch of trash. Even if there were fifty of them, they would still be a bunch of trash. I don't know what they're thinking. One more is just asking for humiliation …"
Nobody from the Xuanyuan Clan cared about Xian Ze.
Xia Yun's expression did not change. He held the thin black sword and walked out slowly. Then, he transformed into a huge Aragami and stood there waiting.
"Brother Luo Zheng, we're counting on you!"
Cheng Wei who was sitting on the ground said. He was treating the big and small wounds on his body.
Purple Jade, Diao Yuan, and the other elders also looked at Luo Zheng …
They did not know how much potential Luo Zheng had.
However, as Cheng Wei said, Luo Zheng was able to kill a Middle Aragami in the Eternal Wasteland.
Now, Luo Zheng had activated the Chiyou Desolate Body and passed the Physical Calamity. His strength should have improved by leaps and bounds. He should not be underestimated.
Luo Zheng nodded. He stroked the emerald green sword in his hand and slowly walked towards the center of the wasteland.
At the same time, he softly uttered the word "Grow". His body began to expand rapidly.
In the blink of an eye, he had grown to a height of more than ten thousand feet …
However, when he transformed into a Aragami, he forgot about the sword in his hand. As his body expanded, he did not pay attention and the sword fell from his huge fingers …
It was Luo Zheng's first time using a Desolate Weapon. Although Diao Yuan told him that the Desolate Weapon could grow as his body grew, he forgot to tell him to infuse the Aragami's power into it.
For the Aragami, this action was like eating and drinking. They would never forget it.
Xia Yun who was standing in front of Luo Zheng was stunned for a moment before he understood what happened.
The Chiyou Tribe burst into laughter.
"No way, this brat doesn't even know how to use a Desolate weapon?"
"What is the Chiyou Clan trying to do? Why did they send such a guy …"
"I'm here to make fun of you!"
"Xia Yun will probably be able to subdue that brat in one move!"
Luo Zheng's clumsy appearance had indeed baffled many people. This kind of situation would only happen to a person who was using a desolate weapon for the first time.
They did not know that this was indeed the first time Luo Zheng had used a desolate weapon.
As for the Chiyou Tribe, their faces were somewhat ugly. Although many people had high expectations for Luo Zheng, it was a bit shameful for him to drop the ball as soon as he entered the arena.
"I'm sorry." Luo Zheng smiled faintly, as if he didn't care.
"It doesn't matter." Xia Yun shook her head and looked at Luo Zheng calmly. There was a trace of curiosity in her eyes.
After that, he dispersed the power of the Aragami and found the green longsword that he had lost. This time, his body expanded once again. The longsword also expanded along with his body.
"It's done."
Luo Zheng held the longsword with one hand and stared at Xia Yun.
He did not activate the Chiyou Desolate Body immediately. This Xia Yun had demonstrated a strong control over her body. Luo Zheng also wanted to test it out.
Xia Yun nodded and pinched the black rapier with her fingers. She said softly, "If you're ready, then I'm going to start!"
As soon as she finished speaking, Xia Yun's eyes suddenly shone with a sharp light.
"Swoosh!"
The black rapier that was more than four hundred feet long suddenly swung out like a snake coming out of its hole. It went straight to Luo Zheng's face.
Xia Yun had perfect control over her gigantic body. Regardless of strength or distance, she was precise to the smallest detail. This sword seemed ordinary, but the angle was extremely tricky!
Many Aragami would choose to retreat when facing this sword!
Only by retreating could they safely avoid this sword.
However, once they retreated, they would fall into Xia Yun's trap. Her next attack would be like a flood that broke through the dam. It would be endless until the opponent was completely trapped. Then, she would tear apart the opponent's defense bit by bit!
That was Xia Yun's strong control! She knew how to read the opponent and even the rhythm of the battle!
Everything would go according to her plan!
In all fairness, Mao Xue's strength was not inferior to Xia Yun. However, Mao Xue's temperament was too straightforward. It would be fine if she encountered other people. However, when she encountered Xia Yun, it would be difficult for her to utilize her advantage. She would be helpless when she was targeted. It was not surprising that she would lose to Xia Yun.
However, this time, Xia Yun encountered Luo Zheng.
"Phew …"
Luo Zheng did not retreat.
He only tilted his head slightly to one side.
The thin black sword flew past his cheek, and the distance between the tip of the sword and his cheek was less than a foot!
One should know that Luo Zheng and Xia Yun were both giants that were more than ten thousand feet tall.
After transforming into an Aragami, the width of a foot was equivalent to the width of an ordinary person's hair …
Luo Zheng turned his head and dodged the attack with such confidence. No matter how calm and indifferent Xia Yun's state of mind was, she was still shocked at the moment.
How was that possible?
Was it luck?
Xia Yun could not believe it.
At such a close distance, if he was not careful, his head would have been cut off by her sword. He actually dared to dodge the attack with luck?
Luo Zheng had a smile on his face. He seemed to be calmer than Xia Yun.
After noticing Luo Zheng's expression, Xia Yun frowned slightly. Then, she moved her arm again.
The rapier that was thrusting out suddenly changed direction. Once again, it was aimed at Luo Zheng's head.
However, Luo Zheng suddenly shrunk his neck. The black rapier sliced across Luo Zheng's head. It was still a foot away from Luo Zheng. This time, it managed to cut off a few strands of Luo Zheng's hair …
"This guy … It's not luck!"
Xia Yun was shocked.
Among the younger generation, Xia Yun had the most accurate control of space. Even her brother, Xia Feng, was not as good as her.
However, even she could not control it to such an extent …
A deep sense of danger rose in her heart. She realized that this unknown young man in front of her was not an ordinary person.
As she thought so, she swung the black rapier even faster!
However, Luo Zheng did not move his feet at all. His body either leaned back or swayed left and right. He actually managed to dodge all of Xia Yun's storm-like attacks.
In the distance …
When the elders of the Xuanyuan Clan saw this scene, their eyes widened in shock.

Chapter 2178
Xia Yun slashed out dozens of times in a row, but Luo Zheng easily dodged them all. For a moment, she became a little impatient.
She suddenly retreated a few steps and stomped on the ground with the tip of her foot, creating a three to four hundred feet deep pit. She was already holding the black rapier in her hand!
Seeing Xia Yun's action, her brother frowned slightly, "Yun 'er is a little impatient. But this time, let's see how that kid dodges!"
"Lingering Sword Net!"
"Swish swish swish swish swish …"
The black rapier was like a whip as it shot out from Xia Yun's hand.
The black rapier intertwined with each other, forming a complex net that enveloped Luo Zheng's head.
The net was very fine. No matter which direction Luo Zheng dodged, he could not escape!
"How will Luo Zheng break this move?" Mao Xue opened her eyes wide and looked at the scene with concern.
Seeing this move, Cheng Wei's handsome eyebrows slightly trembled.
Previously, he was completely defeated by Xia Yun's move. This black rapier net formed a cage that trapped him. No matter how he struggled, he could not break free from this cage …
Luo Zheng's eyes flickered and the corners of his mouth curled up slightly when he saw the black sword net coming at him from all directions.
The Desolate God's battle was essentially still a martial technique.
The only difference was that the weapon used was a desolate weapon, which was thousands of times bigger. The martial artist was also thousands of times bigger.
In terms of pure "technique", Luo Zheng was not afraid of anyone!
"Swish!"
Seeing the rapier net coming at him, Luo Zheng raised the rapier in his hand and stabbed it in one direction.
"Ding!"
A crisp sound rang out.
Luo Zheng blocked one of the black rapiers.
A large hole suddenly appeared in the rapier net …
Seeing Luo Zheng casually stab out, Xia Yun was shocked.
The sword technique she used required a high understanding of space. Once it was used, it would be continuous, like a cage that firmly trapped the opponent. The more he struggled, the deeper he would be trapped!
However, this sword technique was not perfect. There were four flaws in the rapier net.
As long as he attacked the four flaws, he would be able to break the sword net.
However, up until now, she had only met one opponent who was able to quickly find a flaw, and that was Xian Ze.
Xia Yun didn't expect that Luo Zheng could also see through the first flaw. Was this a coincidence, or did he really see through it?
"Oh?"
Xian Ze, who was lying on the ground, quietly observed this scene and finally got up from the ground.
"I didn't expect the Chi You Tribe to have such a person." Xian Ze's eyes widened, and a look of intense interest appeared on his face.
At this moment, Ji Zhuo and the other elders of the Xuanyuan Family frowned as well, their faces covered in a layer of haze.
"I didn't expect the Chiyou Race to be so secretive."
"That's not right. If there is really such a person in the Chiyou Clan, it is impossible that we don't know anything about it!"
"Xia Yun may not necessarily lose. Everyone, calm down!"
Even if Xia Yun lost, they still had Xia Feng. Even if Xia Feng lost, they still had the monstrous Xian Ze.
The Xuanyuan Clan could still afford to lose, but the Chiyou Clan only had one person left!
"Swoosh swoosh swoosh!"
The black sword in Xia Yun's hand didn't stop. In the blink of an eye, the hole created by Luo Zheng was filled with streaks of black light.
At the same time, Xia Yun began to rapidly circle Luo Zheng.
As she moved, the sword net also began to spin, and the changes within it became even more complicated. It would be more difficult for Luo Zheng to break through the sword net.
However, this small change was nothing to Luo Zheng …
In his eyes, the myriad of changes were nothing but a smokescreen. No matter how many changes there were, they were still the same.
Luo Zheng faintly smiled, and lightly waved his hand, thrusting out four times in a row!
"Ding ding ding ding!"
Following four crisp sounds, the sword net that Xia Yun had carefully arranged completely collapsed.
All of the black sword lights disappeared completely, and the black sword in Xia Yun's hand trembled unceasingly.
Xia Yun's heart sank. This guy had seen through all of her moves.
Her all-out attack was like child's play in front of Luo Zheng!
"Your sword moves are useless against me," Luo Zheng said indifferently.
From beginning to end, Luo Zheng stood in place, and didn't take a single step forward.
On the other hand, Xia Yun's continuous attacks were easily neutralized by him. Anyone could see the disparity between them.
Xia Yun pursed her lips.
She wasn't a woman who gave up so easily. Even if she knew that she was no match for him, she would still fight with all her might.
She lowered her body, and held the black sword horizontally. Her eyes flashed with an ethereal intent. After a moment of preparation, she suddenly swung the black sword.
"Huhuhuhu …"
The black sword turned into a long whip, and like a tornado, it crazily spun around her several times.
As the sword light spiraled, an extremely sharp black light separated from it, and shot straight at Luo Zheng!
"This sword move is not bad." Luo Zheng's eyes narrowed slightly.
Seeing this sword move, Mao Xue and Cheng Wei couldn't help but gasp.
They originally thought that Xia Yun had used all her strength to defeat them. They didn't expect that she still had such a killer move hidden!
"Luo Zheng, be careful. You can't take this move head-on!" Cheng Wei's eyes revealed a touch of worry.
"Not necessarily … I don't think Luo Zheng is serious at all," Mao Xue said.
Seeing the black sword light heading straight for Luo Zheng, Luo Zheng's eyes flashed, and his green sword actually pointed at the black sword light!
Seeing Luo Zheng's action, Zi Yu, Diao Yuan, and the other elders' faces suddenly tightened.
The others were also stunned. Was this kid courting death?
Xia Yun's eyes flashed with a touch of astonishment. She knew that Luo Zheng could take this sword move, but Luo Zheng's current action was no different from suicide!
However, the moment Luo Zheng's long sword touched the black sword light, Luo Zheng's arm suddenly twisted.
The long sword's body twisted slightly, and the black sword light seemed to be caught in a whirlpool, circling around Luo Zheng's long sword.
At the same time, Luo Zheng suddenly pulled the long sword, and Xia Yun felt a huge force coming from her hand.
Xia Yun didn't have time to throw away the black sword, and her whole body involuntarily collided with Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng had already placed the long sword horizontally in front of his chest. If she collided with Luo Zheng's long sword like this, she would be cut in two!
"Yun 'er!"
In the distance, Xia Feng roared, his eyes full of despair.
Xia Yun looked at the sword edge that was getting closer and closer, and her face was deathly pale. She only knew that she was going to die.

Chapter 2181

Chapter 2183
Xian Ze could instantly comprehend the True Meaning of Nature, but it wasn't because he truly understood the True Meaning of Nature.
