"Is it very strange?" Luo Zheng smiled faintly.
Luo Zheng's answer was a confirmation.
Xian Ze's eyes flashed, and the way he looked at Luo Zheng changed again.
He originally thought that Luo Zheng was just a talented Rage God …
No matter how powerful he was, there was an insurmountable gap between them.
Now, in Xian Ze's heart, he already saw Luo Zheng as the same kind of person.
"You really surprised me. No wonder you could defeat Xia Yun and Xia Feng so easily," Xian Ze said.
Xian Ze never thought that there would be someone like him who could cultivate the True Meaning of Dao in this world.
"However, if you want to win against me, you're still far from enough …" he said.
"Weng …"
Xian Ze's Xuan Yuan Desolate Physique released a dazzling golden light.
Other than the power of the Rage God, there was another unique aura being emitted …
"This aura … is also a type of True Meaning of Dao." Luo Zheng felt that unique aura, and his eyes narrowed slightly.
According to what Luo Zheng knew, the True Meaning of Dao was One Thought Good and Evil, the True Meaning of Chaos, the True Meaning of Nature, the True Meaning of Sword Fate, and the True Meaning of Destruction!
Among them, Luo Zheng had comprehended the True Meaning of Good and Evil from Elder Yuan. Although he wasn't able to completely comprehend it, he was close. Apart from that, he had also comprehended 99% of the True Meaning of Nature …
In the Divine Realm, there was a 《Incomplete Explanation of the Twelve True Meanings》, which meant that there were twelve True Meanings of Dao in the Divine Realm.
As for how many True Meanings of Dao there were in the chaos of the Divine Realm, Luo Zheng didn't know.
Every True Meaning of Dao was a path to the other side, and it was condensed from the blood and sweat of countless people.
This was the continuation of the Great World Cultivation Method. It was also a method to cultivate to the True God Realm and reach the Nirvana Realm.
The True Intent of the Dao emitted by the Immortal Marsh had a trace of the charm of the Space Divine Dao. While Luo Zheng was guessing in his heart, he didn't expect the Immortal Marsh to actually say it out loud …
"The mantra that Master passed down to me is the True Meaning of Void and Disillusionment." Xian Ze's right hand held a sharp dagger as he pointed it at Luo Zheng. "Master strictly forbade me from using these methods against my enemies, so I have never used them in front of others. Furthermore, I have no use for them …"
Xian Ze's words had a deeper meaning to them.
In the distance, Xia Yun and Xia Feng's expressions changed several times.
After Xia Feng lost to Xian Ze, Xian Ze became the most popular figure in the Xuan Yuan Clan.
However, Xia Feng was still somewhat unconvinced. After all, Xian Ze had only won by a narrow margin. The gap between them was very small.
Since then, Xia Feng had also cultivated extremely diligently, striving to defeat Xian Ze one day in the future …
Now that Xian Ze had said this, Xia Feng finally understood that this guy hadn't used his true strength at all!
"However, you are different." Xian Ze smiled seriously. "I believe Master won't blame me, right?"
As soon as he finished speaking, Xian Ze's figure flashed again, disappearing in front of everyone.
It wasn't just Luo Zheng who couldn't find Xian Ze …
Even powerful Aragami like Purple Jade, Diao Yuan, and Ji Zhuo of the Xuan Yuan Clan couldn't find this kid's figure.
"The True Meaning of Void and Disillusionment … Using this True Meaning, one can hide in the chaotic space?"
Luo Zheng stared at the empty space in front of him as he thought to himself.
He was no stranger to the Space Divine Dao. The Space Divine Dao also existed in his Infinity Ruler.
However, the Space Divine Dao belonged to the Universal Law, while the Space Divine Dao belonged to the Space Divine Dao of the Divine Realm.
Then, the True Meaning of Void and Disillusionment belonged to the Space Divine Dao of the Chaos?
"Whoosh!"
In the next instant, Xian Ze appeared beside Luo Zheng and slashed his dagger at him again.
Luo Zheng relied on his Eight Twists Flying Smoke, which was infused with the Dao of Nature, to dodge this attack as if it was natural.
However, this time, Xian Ze came prepared.
The instant he slashed out with his dagger, spiderweb-like cracks appeared on the surface of the dagger. These cracks spread throughout the space, and then, with a loud bang, it shattered!
"Crash!"
The shattered space quickly collapsed, forming an absolute void.
This void emitted an invisible force, which attracted everything in the surroundings to it. This kind of attraction was almost irresistible.
Luo Zheng had already dodged, but now, he was being pulled back involuntarily!
"Let's see how you escape this time!" Xian Ze smiled, and the dagger in his right hand stabbed at Luo Zheng.
Seeing this stab, Luo Zheng had no space to dodge. He snorted coldly, and suddenly twisted the longsword in his hand, stabbing at Xian Ze head-on.
"Ding ding ding ding ding …"
Xian Ze's dagger was pressed against Luo Zheng's longsword. At close range, the two's weapons constantly clashed.
Even with such large figures, they could still fight in a small area with dazzling speed.
Whether it was the Xuanyuan Clan or the Chiyou Clan, they were all dazzled. They could no longer tell who had the upper hand. They only knew that in a battle of this level, the slightest carelessness would determine the winner and loser, or even life and death.
"Luo Zheng hasn't used his Soul Strength." Cheng Wei quietly watched this scene.
"It seems that Luo Zheng hasn't used his full strength. He also wants to use this opportunity to temper himself," Mao Xue said with a complicated expression.
They were called geniuses in the Chiyou Clan and were respected by countless people. They were also doted on by the clan.
However, compared to Luo Zheng and Xian Ze, they were not even worth mentioning!
"There's something wrong with Xian Ze's dagger …" Purple Jade's gaze was fixed on the flying dagger, and a trace of worry appeared on its face.
Luo Zheng naturally noticed Purple Jade's problem.
When his longsword and the dagger collided, a faint white light would be left behind. After this white light appeared, it would quickly converge at the center of his dagger, forming a bright white ball of light.
Under the intense collision between the dagger and sword, the white ball of light on the black dagger became more and more eye-catching.
At the same time, Xian Ze's smile became more and more pleased, as if Luo Zheng was walking into his trap step by step …
"Turn into nothingness!"
When the ball of light became so bright that it was impossible to look directly at it, Xian Ze smiled malevolently and raised both daggers.
At that moment, it was as if two suns had risen.

Chapter 2184
When Xian Ze saw Luo Zheng retreating, the smile on his face grew wider.
The stronger the opponent, the more excited Xian Ze was.
Especially when Xian Ze knew that Luo Zheng had also comprehended the True Meaning of Dao, he was determined to trample this guy under his feet.
To Xian Ze, only by trampling on a true genius would he feel a sense of accomplishment. Xia Yun, Xia Feng, Mao Xue, Cheng Wei, and the others from the Chiyou race were clearly not worthy of the title of genius in his eyes.
"Wu!"
As the two sharp blades in Xian Ze's hands converged, the two balls of light merged into one.
His two sharp blades looked the same as Xia Yun's thin black sword, but in reality, they were worlds apart.
Xian Ze's master was an expert in refining tools, so he had tailored a Desolate Weapon for Xian Ze. Moreover, the two Desolate Weapons were made by imitating the famous Dual Stream Blades from the Mother World!
These two imitations of the Dual Stream Blades had opened up a void.
Only when he used the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment could he activate it.
Just now, when Xian Ze and Luo Zheng were crazily attacking each other, the powerful force did not disperse, but was continuously trapped in the void by the two sharp blades.
Now, it was being released by Xian Ze!
The surging force was mixed with the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment, forming a pure ball of energy. The energy within was extremely unstable, so it was also extremely destructive.
Even Xian Ze himself had to control it carefully!
"Whoosh!"
The blazing ball of energy smashed down towards Luo Zheng.
"This time, let's see how you handle it!"
In Xian Ze's opinion, although Luo Zheng had displayed the Dao of Nature, it was only mixed in with his movement technique. It was only a very shallow use of it!
However, what Xian Ze did not know was that in the Dao of Aragami, Luo Zheng might not be as good as him. After all, he had only cultivated the Aragami for a short period of time. Although the Chiyou race had crazily nurtured Luo Zheng, his foundation was still somewhat unstable …
However, in terms of the Dao of True Gods, Xian Ze's understanding was not at the same level as Luo Zheng's.
In fact, Luo Zheng had many ways to counter this move …
For example, he could directly use the Origin of Power to detonate this extremely unstable ball of energy.
However, even as the sphere of light shot toward Luo Zheng, an idea suddenly occurred to him, and he backed up. At the same time, the godhood in his inner world began to glow.
At the same time, the Quintessence Fragment inscribed with the Li Water Divine Dao emanated a dense Divine Dao!
"Divine Li Water Mirror!"
Luo Zheng waved his hand, and streams of Taoist Connotation of the Li Water Divine Dao quickly gathered in his hand, turning into a pale blue mirror.
The mirror was placed in front of him.
When Xian Ze saw the mirror in front of Luo Zheng, he was taken aback for a moment, then he grinned evilly.
He didn't know what Luo Zheng wanted to do, but it was naive and ridiculous to think that he could block his attack with a mirror.
People always had to pay for their own naivety, and the price was their own life. It seemed too easy for him to kill the genius of the Chiyou Clan just like that …
Xian Ze thought to himself as he watched the energy ball crash into the mirror.
At this moment, the Chiyou Clan members were all extremely nervous. Mao Xue clenched her fists so tightly that her fingernails dug deep into her palms.
Similarly, they couldn't understand Luo Zheng's moves …
Although they all believed in Luo Zheng's strength, and Luo Zheng must have his reasons for doing this, they couldn't help but worry when they saw Luo Zheng playing with fire like this.
Even Purple Jade's breathing became rapid at this moment.
"Die!"
Xian Ze spat out a single word.
However, the scene he had imagined didn't happen.
When the energy ball came into contact with the Divine Li Water Mirror, it suddenly rebounded toward Xian Ze!
Moreover, the energy ball's momentum was even more ferocious and faster than before!
What was this …
Xian Ze stared at the energy ball in disbelief.
In the blink of an eye, the energy ball was already very close to him. Xian Ze had no choice but to grit his teeth, and his huge body suddenly flashed, hiding in the void.
"Break!"
Luo Zheng raised his eyebrows slightly.
An invisible force shot out from behind the energy ball.
This energy ball was originally very unstable. After being pierced through by Luo Zheng's power origin, it instantly exploded!
Rumble!
A violent wave of energy spread wildly in all directions.
At the same time, the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment contained within the energy ball also continuously tore apart the surrounding space.
Seeing the power of the energy ball, even Luo Zheng was inwardly shocked.
Xian Ze's method was very crude, but it was indeed very powerful.
Come to think of it …
The Law of Space was very powerful in the universe, and the Space Divine Dao was also one of the most powerful Divine Daos. In that case, the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment should be one of the most powerful among the various True Meaning mental cultivation methods …
Unfortunately, Xian Ze himself couldn't control this energy ball.
A short while later …
In the void dozens of body positions away from Luo Zheng, a figure staggered out.
This figure was Xian Ze himself.
When the energy ball exploded, it tore apart the surrounding space. Even though he had escaped into the void, he was still affected.
At this moment, the golden light on the surface of Xian Ze's body had dimmed significantly, and his clothes were torn in several places.
Xian Ze's Xuan Yuan Desolate Body was far stronger than ordinary clansmen. If it had been Xia Feng or Xia Yun, they would have been severely injured even if they weren't torn to pieces by the energy ball.
The Xuan Yuan clansmen were all silent at this moment.
Every single one of them had an unsightly expression on their faces …
Ji Zhuo didn't make a sound either. He felt as if he had fallen into an ice cellar.
In Ji Zhuo's eyes, and in the eyes of the other elders of the Xuan Yuan Clan, even if everyone else lost, they would still win this battle.
That was because of Xian Ze.
This guy had already surpassed the level of Aragami, and was approaching the level of exiles, heading towards the Nirvana realm.
But facing this little guy from the Chiyou Clan who came out of nowhere, Xian Ze was actually at a disadvantage?
"What kind of method was that …" Xian Ze clenched his teeth and stared at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng gently withdrew his hand. The Dao of the Li Water Divine Dao dissipated, and the bright and clean mirror quietly shattered and quickly disappeared.
"It's just a small trick. Not worth mentioning," Luo Zheng said calmly.
Xian Ze's eyes were filled with hatred. "Indeed, it's just a small trick! If you think you can beat me like this, then you're dreaming! "
Then, he spread his arms. His body, which was even taller than a mountain, once again shot through the void and charged towards Luo Zheng!
Other than the True Intent of Dao, Xian Ze had another trump card.
The souls of the Aragami in this world were generally very weak.
But there were always some exceptions, and Xian Ze was one of them.
He had been nurtured by the efforts of several exiles. His soul was so powerful that it had far surpassed the strength of an ordinary Aragami …

Chapter 2185
Using one's soul to suppress an opponent would only happen when there was a large disparity in soul power.
Ever since Luo Zheng stepped into this world, he realized that the Aragami in this world didn't cultivate their souls …
Although the soul would naturally become stronger according to the length of time, the soul would naturally become stronger.
However, this process was very slow.
Just like Diao Yuan, Purple Jade, and the other Chiyou elders who had lived for countless years, their souls were still far inferior to Luo Zheng's without any special cultivation.
Today, Luo Zheng didn't plan on using his soul power, on the premise that he could deal with it himself.
However, he didn't expect Xian Ze to use this method first!
Xian Ze was still very confident in himself.
In a battle of souls, he definitely wouldn't lose to anyone of the same generation.
Moreover, his master had given him a set of "soul rings". Not only could these soul rings protect his soul, but they could also greatly strengthen his soul power. They were rare soul treasures. If his opponent's soul wasn't strong enough, he could rely on these soul rings to directly crush them!
"I'll tear your soul to shreds!"
Xian Ze revealed a sinister expression. He had never suffered such a great loss before. He wanted to kill Luo Zheng now!
That soul pressure instantly entered Luo Zheng's mind and went straight for Luo Zheng's soul.
Luo Zheng's soul quietly floated in his mind. Seeing that soul pressure coming straight for him, a faint smile appeared on his face.
After his soul fused with the Shadow Bearing Sword, Luo Zheng didn't fear even the soul of a Paragon True God …
However, the strength of Xian Ze's soul was only at the level of a True God's soul. The difference between him and Luo Zheng wasn't small. Moreover, a battle of souls was very similar to a battle of pure strength. The strong were strong, and the weak were weak. There were no tricks to speak of.
When Xian Ze's soul attack wrapped around Luo Zheng, it started to crazily tear at him.
In just a tenth of a breath's time, Xian Ze was already stunned.
His soul attack didn't cause any damage to Luo Zheng. At the same time, he felt that the surface of Luo Zheng's soul had started to twist and turn, like a small golden arrow.
Waves of sharp soul power were emitted from his body.
At this moment, Xian Ze's face finally revealed a look of shock.
How could this be …
Originally, he wanted to attack Luo Zheng in two steps. First, he would use a soul attack to disturb Luo Zheng's mind. Then, he would attack Luo Zheng's body.
But halfway through the first step, Xian Ze was dumbfounded. This guy's soul wasn't something he could shake.
"Swish —"
Xian Ze's reaction was extremely fast. He quickly withdrew his soul power from Luo Zheng's mind.
But at the same time, a smile appeared on Luo Zheng's face …
"Since you want to humiliate yourself, I will lend you a hand," Luo Zheng said indifferently with an expressionless face.
"Phew …"
An invisible soul pressure spread out from Luo Zheng's mind.
That spiritual pressure was almost tangible as it rushed toward Xian Ze's mind …
These soul arrows shot into Xian Ze's mind, heading straight for his soul.
Xian Ze was in a daze right now. He didn't know how to deal with Luo Zheng's soul attack!
Seeing those soul arrows piercing toward Xian Ze's soul, the soul ring above Xian Ze's soul suddenly released circles of golden light that protected him.
"Du du du du du …"
Countless soul arrows shot toward the soul ring.
Under the barrage of attacks, the soul ring above Xian Ze's head quickly dimmed. A clear crack quickly spread on the soul ring!
Once the soul ring was destroyed, Xian Ze's soul would be turned into a sieve by those soul arrows!
However, just as the soul ring was being attacked …
Tens of millions of kilometers away, inside the Evil Mountain Bone Pagoda, a middle-aged man suddenly opened his eyes.
The Evil Mountain Bone Pagoda was where the Xuanyuan Family gathered.
This middle-aged man was Xian Ze's master. He was also one of the exiles who had created the Xuanyuan Family, the Xuanyuan Guard, Di Jin!
Under normal circumstances, these exiles wouldn't make a move.
Even if the Xuanyuan Family's disciples were slaughtered, as long as they didn't enter the bone pagoda, the exiles wouldn't make a move.
If a certain family went overboard and killed their way into the bone pagoda, these powerful exiles would make a move.
Once, the Xuanyuan Family had killed their way into the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda. In the end, countless Xuanyuan Family's Desolate Gods were killed by the Chi You Exiles. After that, the Xuanyuan Family didn't take revenge. After all, the Xuanyuan Family was the one who broke the rules first.
The conflicts between the large families were only limited to the Desolate Gods, and not the exiles. This was an unwritten rule in this world.
The soul ring he had bestowed upon Xian Ze was created using a strand of his own soul.
Although it was only a tiny strand of his soul, the soul ring it formed was enough to withstand any soul attack in this world. After all, the souls of these Desolate Gods were pitifully weak.
At this moment, Di Jin discovered that the soul ring was under fierce attack!
This attack might not be a big deal to Di Jin, but how could the soul ring withstand it?
If the soul ring broke, Xian Ze's soul would probably be killed …
"Who is it? Whose soul attack is so powerful? "A trace of surprise appeared in Di Jin's eyes.
He knew that the Xuanyuan Family wanted to fight for the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, and had agreed to a battle with the Chi You Family.
It was Di Jin's idea to occupy the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
Although the space inside the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda wasn't large, it could become an important outpost for the Xuanyuan Family after occupying it.
Ji Zhuo had personally reported to him that the Chi You Family had agreed to the battle.
Di Jin was also very confident in Xian Ze. In his opinion, Xian Ze alone would be enough to achieve a complete victory, and the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda would be theirs.
But now, his soul was actually being suppressed by someone else? Who was this person?
A trace of surprise flashed in his eyes. With a gentle wave of his hand, a projection of a space appeared in front of him.
Di Jin's main cultivation was the True Meaning of Void Disillusionment. His control over space had reached perfection, and he was somewhat famous even among the Xuanyuan Guards!
"Who is this? His soul has already reached perfection at such a young age? No … he's almost there. He's only fused with the sword soul of a divine weapon? Is this the True Meaning of Good and Evil? "Di Jin felt some clues from Luo Zheng's soul pressure, and was very curious.
There were very few people who cultivated the True Meaning of Good and Evil.
Those old fellows from the Chi You Family had been exiled here for countless years, and there were many conflicts between the two families. However, Di Jin was very clear about the few exiles from the Chi You Family.
None of them cultivated the True Meaning of Good and Evil.
So where did this little fellow from the Chi You Family cultivate it?
Now was clearly not the time to think about this question. If he didn't stop him, Xian Ze would probably die here today.
He had invested countless hopes in Xian Ze, so he couldn't just let it be destroyed so easily.

Chapter 2186
At the edge of the wasteland, the Desolate Gods of Flying Scythe City were bewildered by this scene.
Luo Zheng and Xian Ze stood motionless on the ground.
When the Desolate Gods fought, they would rely on their bodies that were thousands of feet tall to fight against each other.
They had never seen a battle on the soul level.
Hence, when they saw the two of them standing motionless, they were dumbfounded. They had no idea what they were doing.
Even Mao Xue, Xia Feng, Xia Yun, and the others were intrigued by this scene.
As disciples of the Chiyou and Xuanyuan Clans, they were naturally more knowledgeable than ordinary Desolate Gods.
To a certain extent, they had even tempered their own souls.
However, compared to True Gods, the Desolate God Physique did not have any requirements for the soul. Their souls were not that strong. This was the first time they had seen a battle on the soul level.
"Just now, I felt that Xian Ze's soul became very strong in an instant." Mao Xue stared at Xian Ze in the distance.
Cheng Wei nodded slightly. "However, he seems to have been suppressed by Luo Zheng. In a battle on the soul level, Luo Zheng will not lose to him."
Luo Zheng's strength was his soul.
When Cheng Wei lured Luo Zheng into the Chiyou Clan, this was her goal.
However, after Luo Zheng's identity was revealed and his stunning performance, the others neglected this point.
Xian Ze in his mind was complaining incessantly.
Using his soul ring to launch a soul attack was one of his greatest trump cards.
No matter how strong Luo Zheng was, as long as he could not resist his soul attack, he would be easily defeated or even killed!
However, Xian Ze did not expect that after he used his soul attack, he would attract an even stronger counterattack …
And Luo Zheng's soul was far stronger than his!
"Du du du du du …"
More and more cracks appeared on his soul ring. Under the bombardment of those soul arrows, it would shatter at any time.
Once his soul ring shattered, his fate would be obvious!
Just as he was at a loss, he suddenly felt a warm feeling coming from his soul ring.
As that feeling flowed, the soul ring, which was originally full of cracks, began to rapidly repair and become abnormally stable. At the same time, it emitted rings of golden light!
"This is …"
Xian Ze revealed a look of surprise.
But in the blink of an eye, Xian Ze understood.
Master! 'Yes, it must be Master …'
Master once said that this world was too desolate, and it was very difficult to find materials to craft soul tools.
However, his master had promised to help him refine a magic treasure that could protect his soul. In the end, he still used a wisp of his own soul.
Although this wisp of soul had been refined into a Soul Ring, it was still Master's soul in essence. Master could sense it immediately and even control it!
Luo Zheng's eyebrows furrowed slightly as the golden light from the soul ring grew brighter.
"What a powerful soul treasure … No, this soul ring is a little strange!"
Under his relentless attacks, the soul ring should have been shattered, but it suddenly recovered to its original state.
Furthermore, Luo Zheng could feel that this Soul Ring had become ten times stronger!
No matter how sharp his Soul Arrow was, it was like an egg hitting a rock.
This definitely wasn't something Xian Ze could do. If that was the case, Xian Ze wouldn't have been suppressed by his soul force in the first place.
In the sky above Flying Sickle City, Di Jin was hidden in the void. He sighed slightly as he watched Luo Zheng's methods.
"I've taught you everything I know. I thought you would be able to run amok in this world, but the Chiyou Clan actually produced such an enchanting person … I can only help you here. You can forget about the battle of souls. Your strength isn't any weaker than his. If you activate the secret of the Spiritual Pivot, you might still have a chance!"
Di Jin had actually violated a great taboo by helping Xian Ze.
If the Chiyou Clan found out, it would probably cause a lot of trouble and even make him a laughingstock.
His own disciple couldn't beat the other party, and as a Nirvana Realm powerhouse of the mother world, he actually secretly helped him. This was too embarrassing.
However, Di Jin also valued the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda very much. He couldn't make Xian Ze admit defeat, so he could only use this method to help Xian Ze win this round.
Xian Ze felt a warm current surround his soul, and Di Jin's voice entered his mind.
Hearing his master's voice, Xian Ze was somewhat fearful.
The reason Xian Ze could run amok in the Xuanyuan Clan, and even the elders had to give him face, was also because of his master, Di Jin.
In front of Di Jin, Xian Ze had always been obedient.
Now that his master had taught him, he naturally agreed without hesitation …
"Yes, master! I originally wanted to take advantage of the situation and take down Luo Zheng, but I was too impatient. If I had used the secret of the Spiritual Pivot, I wouldn't have fallen into such a passive situation … "
After speaking, Xian Ze opened his eyes. Two rays of golden light shot out from them, and he stared at Luo Zheng, who wasn't far away.
Luo Zheng also chose to give up after using his soul attack for a long time to no avail.
He had doubts in his heart, but he still couldn't understand why Xian Ze's soul rings had become so strong in an instant.
However, now wasn't the time to dwell on this.
"I really didn't expect your soul to be so strong," Xian Ze said, looking at Luo Zheng with a complicated expression.
All along, Xian Ze had been a very arrogant person.
He had the right to be arrogant. After all, his master, Di Jin, had once said that in this world, other than the exiles, he was the most outstanding in terms of bloodline and talent!
Even in the Nuwa Clan, which was at the peak of its power, no one could compare to him.
This was indeed the case.
Since he was young, Xian Ze had never met an opponent who was on par with him.
Xia Feng and Xia Yun were at the peak of their power before him. However, Xian Ze only used 40% of his strength to defeat Xia Yun. Even when he defeated Xia Feng, he used less than 60% of his strength.
Such strength was enough for him to look down on all the geniuses in this world.
Now, someone had suddenly appeared who was stronger than him in the True God Realm, and in terms of soul power, could suppress him.
Xian Ze couldn't accept this at all. This feeling of defeat was like a sharp knife stabbing into his heart.
Only by killing Luo Zheng would Xian Ze be able to pull out that saber!
"However, you think you can defeat me like this?" Xian Ze continued.
At this moment, the golden light on the surface of Xian Ze's body started to change.
The golden light that covered his body seemed to have a life of its own, and started to flow slowly.
In the eyes of most Desolate Gods, the Xuanyuan Wilderness was a layer of golden light that was comparable to solid gold.
However, most people from the Xuanyuan Clan could only use the Xuanyuan Wilderness to this extent …
However, as a member of the Xuanyuan Clan, Xian Ze's bloodline could be listed in the Spirit Pivot Pavilion, and he could activate the secrets of the Spirit Pivot.
The relationship between the exiles and their clansmen in this world was actually quite distant. For example, Fu Er hadn't seen his daughter for so many years.
Therefore, very few exiles were willing to take the Desolate Gods as their disciples.

Chapter 2187
The Xuanyuan Clan also had differences in bloodline purity.
However, they weren't divided by grade, but by "pavilion".
There were a total of nine pavilions in the Xuanyuan Clan in the Mother World.
The descendants of the Xuanyuan Clan had different bloodlines, so they were all placed in different "pavilions".
The Spirit Pivot Pavilion ranked first.
However, there was still a gap between the Spirit Pivot Pavilion and the Chiyou Clan's "Royal" bloodline, but it was still quite outstanding.
Those whose bloodline was placed in the Spirit Pivot Pavilion could activate the secrets of the Spirit Pivot in their bloodline.
This was also Xian Ze's most hidden trump card.
Strands of golden light converged to form a mysterious picture.
This catalog was the Spirit Pivot Catalog.
There were very few people in the Xuanyuan Clan who could create the Spirit Pivot Catalog, and everyone's Spirit Pivot Catalog was different.
If Xian Ze was born in the Mother World, he would also be placed in the Spirit Pivot Pavilion to study his own catalog. The power contained in this catalog was very complicated, and involved the origin and secrets of the Xuanyuan Clan.
In this world, Xian Ze naturally didn't have the ability to study the catalog.
Di Jin was the one who helped him complete the inspiration for this catalog, and only a small part of it was unlocked.
After all, this wasn't the Mother World, and even the exiles' abilities were limited.
"Sizzle …"
The golden light circulated in the strange catalog, then turned into black light and returned to the surface of Xian Ze's body.
Soon, Xian Ze's entire body was covered by the black light.
His body, which was more than a thousand feet tall, was emitting black light, and looked very strange.
Not only did Luo Zheng and the Chiyou Clan find it strange, but the Xuanyuan Clan also looked at Xian Ze with strange eyes.
"This … is this still the Xuanyuan Desolate Body?"
"This black light is also the power of our bloodline?"
"It feels so strange …"
Some elders and descendants of the Xuanyuan Clan were also discussing in low voices.
They couldn't deny that Xian Ze's aura had greatly increased.
However, this power was too foreign to them, and they couldn't accept it. It was as if Xian Ze was no longer a member of the Xuanyuan Clan.
"That is indeed the power of our Xuan Yuan Family." Ji Mo looked at Xian Ze and said, "But, ordinary Xuan Yuan Family people can't have such power..."
Among the Xuanyuan Family, only the exiles and a few people knew that Xian Ze possessed the secret of the Spiritual Pivot, and Ji Zhuo was one of them.
Even now, Ji Mo still couldn't figure out why Xian Ze, who was born in this world, would suddenly possess such a powerful bloodline.
"I have never used the Secret of the Spiritual Pivot in a real battle." Xian Ze looked at the black light on his body and grinned at Luo Zheng. "You are the first!"

If the Chiyou Clan really had such a talented disciple, it was impossible for them not to have heard about it.
