Therefore, Ji Zhuo suspected that Luo Zheng was borrowed by the Chiyou Clan from other clans. If so, Luo Zheng couldn't have the Chiyou Wasteland bloodline. If he didn't have the Chiyou Wasteland bloodline, the Chiyou Clan would be considered to have violated the rules first, and this duel wouldn't count.
Hearing Ji Zhuo's words, Di Jin's eyes shone slightly. He came here in a hurry, and didn't notice this.
"Yes, let that kid prove that he's from the Chiyou Clan!"
"I've never heard of such a person in the Eternal Wasteland. He couldn't have jumped out from a rock!"
"Indeed, Luo Zheng's background seems to be unclear."
Xuanyuan Family people seemed to have caught a life-saving straw, and this was probably the only thing they could say.
In this regard, Xianze showed an indifferent look.
He didn't care about the South Heaven Bone Tower, neither did he care about Luo Zheng's background. He only knew that he had been completely defeated by Luo Zheng.
Seeing the people of the Xuanyuan Clan become noisy, the people of the Chiyou Clan remained calm.
Luo Zheng indeed didn't come from the Chi Spirit Bone Tower, but he was a genuine Chiyou Clan member, or rather, a Li Clan member!
Hearing Ji Zhuo's words, Zi Yu showed a weird smile, then said to Luo Zheng in a bland tone, "Since Grand Elder Ji Zhuo has doubts in his heart, Luo Zheng, please help him to clear up his doubts."
Luo Zheng nodded slightly.
It wasn't difficult for him to transform into the Chiyou Desolate Body, but he didn't have the chance to use it in the past few battles.
Xia Yun and Xia Feng posed no threat to Luo Zheng, and Luo Zheng could also suppress Xianze in the beginning.
After Xianze was controlled by Di Jin, Luo Zheng didn't have the chance to transform into the Bloodline Desolate Body.
Under everyone's gaze, Luo Zheng's thoughts changed slightly, and the bones and meridians in his body surged. Two heads and two pairs of arms grew out of his body, and he quickly transformed into a three-headed, six-armed Desolate Body!
A genuine three-headed, six-armed Desolate Body!
It was rare for a middle-level Desolate God to transform into a three-headed, six-armed Desolate God. This meant that Luo Zheng's bloodline was pure and rare, and it was normal for him to have such strength.
Seeing this, Di Jin snorted coldly in the air, then stepped into the void and disappeared in front of everyone.
Ji Zhuo cupped his fists at Zi Yu, and said nothing more. He just left with the Xuanyuan Family people.
As for the Desolate Gods who were quietly watching from afar, naturally, some were happy, while some were sad. After all, today's battle had determined the division of power within the South Heaven Bone Tower.

Chapter 2192
After the Xuanyuan Clan left, several people in charge of Flying Sickle City came up to congratulate the Chiyou Clan.
Among these leaders, two of them belonged to the Xuanyuan Clan. However, they chose to defect at the first opportunity.
After all, after the Xuanyuan Clan left, it would be impossible for them to take care of small fries. They would have to fight for their own survival in the future. Those without a foundation would easily defect.
However, the Chiyou Clan was too lazy to pay attention to them and sent them away with a few words.
Managing these cities didn't require the Chiyou Clan's disciples. The three major sects under the Chiyou Clan would naturally take care of everything.
"The Heavenly South Bone Pagoda has been sealed for 13,000 years. Today, it can finally be opened. Purple Jade, take everyone to open it." Fu Er smiled at his daughter.
Purple Jade nodded and looked at her father with some confusion. "Father, are we going back so soon?"
Fu Er shook his head and looked at the top of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda. "No, Father needs to investigate something …"
The Chiyou Clan's exiles were all watching the battle between Luo Zheng and Xian Ze.
However, they did not discover Di Jin who was hiding in the Void. The moment Di Jin fell out of the Void, they felt a powerful force penetrate through them.
That force almost disappeared in a flash, but Fu Er judged that it was this force that forced Di Jin out.
Who made a move at such a critical moment?
He didn't know whether the person who made the move was friend or foe, but he still wanted to find out.
He had already sensed that the force came from an extremely high direction. Judging from the direction and distance, it should be the top of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
"Mm …"
Purple Jade nodded reluctantly, but she was still a little unhappy.
She had never seen her father's face, but this time, because of Luo Zheng, her father had appeared twice. The difference was too big, and Purple Jade couldn't help but feel unbalanced.
Fu Er noticed his daughter's mood and smiled faintly, his lips moving slightly.
Her father's voice sounded in Purple Jade's ears. "Do you still remember the stories I told you? Maybe … it won't be long before we're not trapped in this world anymore! "
Purple Jade was stunned when she heard this.
Although she had never seen her father, she had often heard his voice when she was young.
Especially when he suffered setbacks, he would run to the large screen and let his father comfort him. Fu Er would also tell him many interesting stories.
And those stories came from the mother world.
In the mother world, there was the Dan Fenghai, Nuwa creating humans, the Fire God burning the world, countless ancient legends intertwined, and all kinds of bizarre divine abilities.
Hearing this, the cub pestered its father to take it to the mother world.
Later on, when the cub grew up, she found out that this world was actually a huge prison. Her father was a prisoner of this prison. Meanwhile, she and the other Aragami were just the children of these prisoners …
Leaving this world was simply an extravagant hope.
After reaching adulthood, Purple Jade was not too disappointed. After all, she was born in this world and was a native of this world.
However, now that it heard its father's words, the cub naturally couldn't react.
"Father, what do you mean by that?" Purple Jade asked with a true essence sound transmission.
Fu Er smiled slightly. "The way to leave this world is only a small possibility. You'll naturally know in the future."
Purple Jade did not know the news that Luo Zheng brought to the exiles, and Fu Er could not tell her about it. Now, he was just using it to comfort his daughter.
In the eyes of the exiles, Luo Zheng's news was not absolutely reliable.
However, in order to leave this place, as long as there was the slightest possibility, they would do it regardless of their own safety!
Then, Fu Er stretched out his hand and waved gently. A rippling vortex appeared in front of him, and he stepped into it, leaving the cub in a daze.
It was not until Diao Yuan called out "Great Elder" a few times that the cub reacted and said to everyone, "Everyone, follow me to open the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda!"
After the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda was opened, this bone pagoda would become the Chiyou clan's second stronghold!
If it was in the past, it would be a great joy for the cub. After all, this meant that the Chiyou clan had taken a big step forward in their expansion. In this world, there were many bloodline clans crowded in one bone pagoda, and the Chiyou clan was one of the few clans that could sit on two bone pagodas.
But just now, her father told her that they were going to leave this world.
Only then did the cub understand what her father had told her before they set off.
The outcome of this battle was not important. The most important thing was to protect Luo Zheng's safety. It seemed that her father was indeed planning something …
…
On the top of the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda.
9527 was very satisfied with the arrow it had just shot.
When it was in a good mood, it liked to sing softly.
That was a ballad that it had once sung, a kind of ballad that could penetrate the soul. Sometimes, 9527 would boast that it was the "Soul Catcher", and the rhythm of the soul fluctuations would intoxicate it.
However, that group of old-fashioned brain masters did not like it and often forbade it from singing in the spiritual world.
When 9527 was humming softly, it suddenly felt something.
The exiled Chiyou clan had come …
After realizing this, 9527 sank into the depths of the Bone Pagoda.
At the same time, the huge eyeball and the huge bow that it had created suddenly shattered, turning into wisps of white bone powder.
These bone powder did not scatter with the wind, but were quickly absorbed into the Bone Pagoda. Not a single trace of the original body's bones could be wasted!
After 9527 quietly disappeared, Fu Er walked out of the vortex at the top of the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda.
He wandered around the top of the Bone Pagoda for a while, and at the same time released his divine mind.
Fu Er's divine mind was very special. It was like a snake baring its fangs and brandishing its claws, constantly spreading at the top of the Bone Pagoda.
"Here!"
Fu Er's eyes flashed, and he immediately came to the side of the top of the Southern Heaven Bone Pagoda.
With his keen divine mind, he could feel the remnant soul fluctuations here, but when he looked at the Bone Pagoda, his face showed a trace of confusion.
The soul fluctuations seemed to have escaped into the interior of the Bone Pagoda.
After living in the Bone Pagoda for so many years, Fu Er was well aware of how monstrous the skeletons of the Primordial Chaos Primordial Gods were. No living creature could break into the Bone Pagoda and sneak inside …
"As expected, this Primordial Chaos Primordial God has its eyes on Luo Zheng," Fu Er murmured as he stood in place.
In the Na Huang Cave, the old man had come to this conclusion.
The Primordial Chaos Primordial God seemed to have merged its bloodline into Luo Zheng's body during Luo Zheng's physical tribulation, almost killing Luo Zheng …
This time, the Primordial Chaos Primordial God had personally forced Di Jin out of the Void?
He didn't know why a creature of this level had its eyes on Luo Zheng.
After all, the Primordial Chaos Primordial God had not made any moves for countless years, and it was also the Primordial Chaos Primordial God that had given these exiles a place to live.
So far, it seemed that the Primordial Chaos Primordial Chaos Primordial God had no ill intentions toward Luo Zheng.

Chapter 2193
Since 13,000 years ago, after the fierce battle between the Chiyou and Xuanyuan races, the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda had been sealed.
The two races made a compromise and shelved the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda. Together, they opened the Eternal Wasteland below the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda …
Today, the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda had fallen into the hands of the Chiyou race.
For the clansmen, especially Diao Yuan, Qi Chang, and the other Chiyou disciples, their emotions were surging.
Being able to help the Chiyou race open another bone pagoda could be said to be an unparalleled achievement, just like how the emperors of the mortals expanded their territory.
However, Purple Jade was a little absent-minded. Her beautiful eyes kept sweeping over Luo Zheng.
She didn't know what kind of changes this kid's arrival would bring to the Chiyou race, but her father's words were most likely because of Luo Zheng …
Could he really lead the Chiyou race out of this world?
Soon after, the bone ship slowly approached the middle section of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda. In the middle section of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, there was a crack that was hundreds of meters long. Through this crack, one could enter the bone pagoda.
Instead of saying that the clans were fighting for the bone pagoda, it was better to say that they were fighting for the "cracks" in the bone pagoda. Only by hiding in these cracks could they avoid the once-in-a-millennium Judgment Light.
Around the crack, there was a huge red seal that was tens of thousands of meters wide.
A crimson aura slowly hovered above the seal. This aura transmitted a sense of danger, as if it was issuing a warning to any creature that approached this place.
This seal was released by the exiles of the two races at the same time. Once it was removed, the powerhouses of the two races would naturally know.
"First Elder, it's time to unlock the seal." Diao Yuan looked at Purple Jade, who was still in a daze, and felt quite strange. He could only remind her.
Purple Jade came back to her senses, smiled awkwardly, and nodded.
Naturally, she should be the one to unlock the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
Then, under everyone's gaze, Purple Jade flew up, and her body began to expand …
"Huff, huff, huff …"
10,000 meters …
10,000 meters …
100,000 meters …
400,000 meters!
Purple Jade's huge body also gave Luo Zheng a rather oppressive feeling.
For an Aragami to be able to cultivate to such a level, in terms of pure strength, they were probably much stronger than a Sage!
When he had just arrived in this world, Luo Zheng had seen giants thousands of kilometers tall fighting in the Eternal Wasteland. Purple Jade's current height should be even taller than the giant he had seen before.
It was unknown how many desolate bones it had absorbed to reach such a level. Luo Zheng pondered.
Now that the arranged battle had ended smoothly, the next step was to find a way to leave this world. He did not know what plans the elder and company had, but regardless, he had to seize the time to absorb the desolate bones.
Everyone stood on the bone ship and looked up at the cub.
She reached out and slapped the seal …
"Boom!"
Under the explosion of the air, the surrounding space shook.
At the same time, the surface of the huge seal began to roll.
Purple Jade's palm, which was tens of thousands of feet wide, kept slapping the seal. When the seal was flipped to a certain extent, it began to shatter.
Soon, the seal dissipated with the wind.
After dispersing the power of the Rage God, the cub returned to its normal size and returned to the bone ship.
After everyone investigated the interior of the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, only a few of their clansmen were left behind to guard it.
According to the original plan, after occupying the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda, the Chiyou race would first move their major sects here. With this space, they would be able to absorb more people from the wilderness.
…
Luo Zheng followed Purple Jade back to the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda.
There were only about two years left before the Light of Judgment appeared once in a thousand years.
Although Little Yun and Fu Chu were still in a safe state, all decisions had to be made within two years.
After all, the Chiyou race alone might not be able to protect the siblings.
If they were not brought into the bone pagoda, everything would be purified once the Light of Judgment appeared, turning into smoke.
Even Luo Zheng himself had no chance of leaving this place.
After returning to the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda, Luo Zheng seized every moment to absorb the desolate bones …
Although the Chiyou race had a large stock of desolate bones, Luo Zheng's absorption method still shocked Wei Cong, who was supposed to distribute the desolate bones.
However, Wei Cong dared not say anything. After all, ever since Luo Zheng's return, the entire Chiyou race had been spreading all kinds of legends about the battle between Luo Zheng and the Xuanyuan race.
It could be said that the Chiyou race only relied on Luo Zheng to win the Heavenly South Bone Pagoda.
As for Mao Xue, Cheng Wei, and the others, they did not contribute at all.
Even Duo Mo, who had fought with Luo Zheng for the Na Desolate Cave, was now convinced by Luo Zheng's strength.
Six days later …
The old man silently descended in front of Luo Zheng.
He looked at the rich power of the Rage God in Luo Zheng's body and said calmly, "At this rate, it won't take long for you to reach the level of an upper-level Rage God …"
Luo Zheng opened his eyes and smiled. "I'm just afraid that there won't be enough time."
"Cultivating the Rage God is a shortcut. Time is not a big problem," the old man replied.
Luo Zheng nodded.
The power of the Rage God in these desolate bones should have been inherited from the predecessors. It was indeed much faster than cultivating in the great world.
"However, there's still something that I don't understand," Luo Zheng suddenly said.
"Please speak," the old man said.
"When I fought with Xian Ze, I saw that he had activated the power of his bloodline. It seems that the power of the Xuanyuan race's bloodline is very powerful. Why doesn't the Chiyou race's bloodline Desolate Body have a similar power?" Luo Zheng asked.
If the Chiyou race's three heads and six arms corresponded to the Xuanyuan race's golden light protection, then the power that Xian Ze used should be the power of his bloodline that had reached a certain level. That black light also posed a great threat to Luo Zheng.
The purity of Luo Zheng's bloodline was definitely not weaker than Xian Ze's. However, after passing the physical tribulation, Luo Zheng only had three heads and six arms. He did not obtain any bloodline power, nor had he heard anyone mention it.
The old man smiled and said, "Our Chiyou race's bloodline is not weaker than the Xuanyuan race's. However, you belong to the chief's direct bloodline. I can't predict when you will activate your bloodline power, but I believe that it will happen sooner or later."
After all, the Chiyou race had never produced a royal bloodline in all these years. Even these exiles did not know much about the bloodline power of the Chiyou race. The old man could not give Luo Zheng an answer. Luo Zheng needed to figure it out himself.
"Let's go. It's time," the old man said.
"Where to?" Luo Zheng asked.
"To visit the Nuwa race," the old man replied.
It would be difficult for the Chiyou race to escape from this prison alone. If they could borrow the power of the Nuwa race and gather the other races, the possibility of leaving this world would be greatly increased.
Moreover, the Nuwa race had bound the divine realm in the barbarian water bone pagoda. This problem had to be solved.

Chapter 2194
The old man took Luo Zheng with him, silently vanishing from the Chi Spirit Bone Pagoda.
The wardens had always been extremely mysterious. They traveled through this world silently, and even the exiles knew very little about them.
Now that they were planning to leave this prison, they had to be very careful.
The Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda was the fourth rib of the Primal Chaos Elder God.
There was a crack on this rib that was tens of billions of kilometers long. These cracks created a vast space within the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda, which was why it was the most suitable bone pagoda for this purpose.
As the most powerful race in this world, it was only natural that the Nuwa Clan would occupy this bone pagoda.
Beneath the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda was a vast expanse of the Eternal Wastelands.
However, ordinary people were unable to enter the Eternal Wastelands. Only the disciples of the nine major sects that relied on the Nuwa Clan could obtain an entry token and enter.
As the most powerful race in this world, no one was willing to cause trouble for them. Thus, even joining one of the subordinate sects of the Nuwa Clan was extremely difficult, let alone joining the Nuwa Clan.
It was for this reason that the Nuwa Clan was able to absorb all sorts of dazzling geniuses from this world!
Whoosh …
In the void, ripples of water spread out in circles.
The old man had already brought Luo Zheng out of the spatial tunnel. In a short period of time, he had already brought Luo Zheng across a distance of thirty billion kilometers.
Luo Zheng glanced at the distant Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda with a strange look on his face. "It seems that we're still quite far away from the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda …"
Visually, the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda was right in front of him, but if he flew, it would probably take quite a bit of time.
The old man revealed a wry smile. "There's nothing we can do about it. The Nuwa Clan has many rules. They don't allow outsiders to enter as they please!"
The Nuwa Clan forbade other exiles from traveling through the void and visiting the bone pagoda.
Even exiles had to abide by these complicated rules. In this world, the Nuwa Clan was the only clan that did so.
Luo Zheng nodded and followed behind the old man.
From where he was, Luo Zheng could see the divine domain high up in the sky.
This was the closest Luo Zheng had been to the Divine Domain since he had arrived in this world. The huge Divine Domain floated in the deep space, emitting a soft luster, like a gem of uniform texture.
However, in the middle of this "gem", there was a crimson band of light binding it!
This ribbon of light shot out from the top of the bone pagoda. Luo Zheng noticed that a part of it was still hidden in the bone pagoda. Through the huge crack in the middle of the bone pagoda, Luo Zheng could faintly see the scarlet light emitting from the rope …
The bone pagoda was 700 to 800 billion miles tall, and the distance between the bone pagoda and the Divine Realm was probably over a trillion miles. Moreover, the belt of light had wrapped around the Divine Realm, and was probably two to three trillion miles long.
Ji Hao wondered how the Nuwa Clan managed to create such a large-scale light band.
While Luo Zheng was thinking, a row of white dots suddenly appeared in front of him.
A group of Aragami wearing white armor flew over.
When those Aragami arrived in front of him, Luo Zheng noticed that the armor on those Aragami were made from bones.
This world was too desolate. Some of the necessities could only be made from bones that could never be exhausted in this world …
"Stop right there!"
The leader of the group of Aragami was waving a bone spear in his hand as he shouted at the old man and Luo Zheng.
This person's tone was very strict at first. However, after taking a glance at the old man, his expression suddenly became serious.
For an expert like this old man, just by standing there without saying a word, he gave off an unsurpassable feeling of vicissitudes of life. This feeling was completely emitted by his cultivation base. Even a blind man would know that he was extraordinary.
The Aragami spoke again in a very respectful manner, "My Flying Elephant City is under the rule of the Nuwa Clan. May I know why you are here? I can pass the message on your behalf!"
When the old man heard this, he smiled faintly, "If I let you pass the message on my behalf, I don't know how long it would take. I can do it myself."
These Aragami skills could only be passed to the nearest city. The city would then pass the message to the sect. The sect would then pass the message to the Nuwa Clan. The clan would then pass the message one by one. Indeed, it would take a long time.
Then, the old man put his hands behind his back. He stared at the Brute Water Bone Pagoda and moved his lips slightly.
These Aragami did not know who the old man was looking for. They were guarding the place and did not dare to leave …
Not long after, a rainbow-like spiral of light slowly appeared in front of the old man, and a figure appeared in the middle of this circular rainbow.
Because the rainbow-like light was blocking, only a silhouette could be seen. However, one could still see that the person had a graceful figure.
"Old Jin, you have not left the bone pagoda for so many years. What is it that you have to come here personally today?"
A soft voice gently floated out from the rainbow.
When the voice entered the ears, it was like sweet water. It was so sweet that it could melt one's soul.
"Important. Very important." The old man said faintly.
So the old man's surname was Jin. Luo Zheng thought to himself.
"Day after day, year after year in this world. Other than those weird-shaped bones, what else is important? Don't tell me that you have taken in a talented little girl and want to marry her to our Nuwa Clan? "
The sweet and soft voice revealed a trace of melancholy.
She seemed to have noticed Luo Zheng behind Elder Jin, so her last sentence was a joke.
These exiles were qualified to be imprisoned here. They were not nameless people in their mother world. Naturally, they felt helpless when they were in such a situation.
"Maybe these days can be gone forever?" Elder Jin smiled faintly.
How intelligent was the person in the rainbow? In an instant, she understood the meaning of Elder Jin's words. However, she did not have much interest in it. She just said lazily, "After so many years, we have tried countless methods. When have we ever succeeded?"
Elder Jin looked at the rainbow calmly and said faintly, "If there is a chance, are you willing to give it up?"
The person in the rainbow remained silent for a moment. Then she said, "No!"
"That's right." Elder Jin chuckled. "Let us in first!"
Then, the colorful vortex kept spinning. Elder Jin and Luo Zheng immediately went into it.
As for the pair of Aragami guards who were patrolling the shuttle and stopping Jin and Luo Zheng, they were staring blankly at the scene before them.
"Boss, boss." One of the guards patted the head of the Aragami guards. "She's gone!"
The Aragami guard regained his senses. He did not know who the woman was, nor did he know who Elder Jin was. However, such a person, no matter what the purpose of coming to the Nuwa Clan was, it was not something he could ask.

Chapter 2195
Luo Zheng stepped into the rainbow-like passageway. Immediately after, an elegant fragrance assailed his nostrils.
As far as the eye could see, he saw a world of colors ….
"This … is the interior of the bone pagoda?" Luo Zheng was stunned.
When Luo Zheng entered the bone pagoda of the Chiyou Race, he only felt that everything inside was very simple and crude.
The interior of the bone pagoda was a natural cave. The Chiyou Race had built some palaces and houses in it, and the materials were also made to make do.
However, in the vast cave of the Brutal Water Bone Pagoda, there were neatly planted jade-green plants and light yellow flowers. In the distance, there were mountains, waterfalls, and all kinds of beautiful buildings. It was like a fairyland.
"Haha, of course." That soft laughter was transmitted over faintly.
Not far in front of Luo Zheng, there was a tall woman. This woman wore a plain white dress and sat in front of a wide chair.
This woman was beautiful, and her eyes seemed to contain spring water, emitting a faint light.
Whether it was strength or fame, this woman far surpassed the other exiles in this world.
Even the Chiyou Race's Elder Jin, who had a five-star title, was not a match for this woman. It was also because of her that the Nuwa Race could firmly hold the title of the number one race.
However, this woman's aura was extremely restrained. At a glance, she looked like an ordinary person who had never cultivated before!
While Luo Zheng was sizing up the woman, the woman was also sizing up Luo Zheng.
"After so many years, your Chiyou Race hasn't improved at all. Except for a few old monsters, the younger generation is all fake. Old Jin, you've brought up a proud disciple this time?" The woman's eyes were sharp, and she could see through Luo Zheng's background at a glance.
She was very clear how difficult it was to cultivate an upper True God in this world. Even more valuable was that Luo Zheng seemed to have comprehended two True Intents of Dao at the same time.
Elder Jin smiled awkwardly …
The development of the Chiyou Clan in this world was indeed not as good as that of the Xuanyuan Clan and the Nuwa Clan. The reason why they could develop to such a scale was entirely because of the support of these exiles.
"Speaking of which, if you're really interested in a marriage alliance, there are many outstanding women in my clan. Can you arrange for the children to meet?" This woman was really interested in Luo Zheng, and actually went along with this topic.
This time, even Luo Zheng looked embarrassed.
Elder Jin chuckled. "These matters are up to the children themselves. I'm really here for an important matter!"
The woman withdrew her gaze from Luo Zheng and said, "Speak!"
Every exile desperately wanted to leave this world.
However, after making various attempts, they unanimously chose to give up.
How could it be so easy to escape from a cage constructed by a primal chaos ancient god?
But as long as there was a slim possibility, they would spare no effort to try.
"I'm afraid we'll have to start with Luo Zheng's background. Luo Zheng, you tell her," Elder Jin suddenly said with a Quintessential Sound Transmission.
The woman frowned. She didn't know what was so important about this youth's background that he had to be deliberately mystifying.
Luo Zheng nodded and said with a Quintessential Sound Transmission, "I'm not from this world. I come from the Realm of the Gods."
The woman didn't understand. She tilted her head and asked with a frown, "The Realm of the Gods? What kind of place is that?"
"The world above us," Luo Zheng said.
Hearing this, a trace of surprise appeared on the woman's face. "You came from that ship?"
In the woman's eyes, the world bound by the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda was a giant ship.
She didn't expect Luo Zheng to come from there!
Luo Zheng nodded, a trace of helplessness appearing on his face. "But this ship is bound by you. I can't leave …"
The woman still didn't understand. She continued to ask, "You said you came from that ship. Why did you enter the Chi You Clan?" She stared at Elder Jin in confusion.
Elder Jin smiled bitterly and said, "Because that ship was built by the Great Chieftain of my clan, Chi You. Luo Zheng is the Great Chieftain's grandson!"
The woman's eyebrows suddenly rose. She looked Luo Zheng up and down again, a strange light flashing in her eyes. Her interest in Luo Zheng grew even stronger.
"It sounds a bit like a fairy tale." The woman shook her head and smiled, as if she couldn't believe it.
Luo Zheng didn't care whether she believed him or not. He continued, "That's not the most important thing. The most important thing is that I have to return to that place. At the same time, the Nuwa Clan must release that ship!"
Ordinary people wouldn't dare to speak like this in front of her.
The woman looked at Luo Zheng with interest. She walked up and stared at Luo Zheng with her beautiful eyes. "It wasn't easy for me to bind this ship. I didn't expect it to dare to break free. If we drag this ship to this world, the Nuwa Clan will gain a lot of resources. Do you think I will let it go?"
"Besides … even if you really came from that ship, do you think you can leave this world with your strength?" The woman added.
Even if Luo Zheng and Elder Jin were telling the truth, and this ship really belonged to the Chi You Clan, and Luo Zheng was a direct descendant of the Chi You Clan, it still couldn't change anything.
How could she let that ship go just because of Luo Zheng's words?
Luo Zheng stared at the woman calmly and said, "That's why the Chi You Clan invited you to leave this world with us. I believe you will also make a choice!"
To the exiles, opening up this world was always the most important thing. Everything else was inconsequential.
"What a boastful little fellow." The woman's eyes looked past Luo Zheng and landed on Elder Jin. The more she listened, the more she felt that Luo Zheng's words were ridiculous.
However, she knew Elder Jin's character. A five-star Chi You Wei wouldn't bring a little fellow who was full of lies to fool her.
"Please listen to Luo Zheng." Elder Jin waved his hand.
Luo Zheng didn't care about the woman's reaction. He continued, "When I came to this world, I met the warden's clan."
"We see them every year. This group of cowards recently came to my Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda. It's not strange," the woman replied calmly.
"My experience is different." Luo Zheng shook his head slightly.
Luo Zheng recounted his encounter with Xiao Yun and Fu Chu.
As Luo Zheng spoke, the woman's face gradually became skeptical. After Luo Zheng finished, she stared at Elder Jin and said in a doubtful tone, "Old Ghost Jin, is this the possibility you spoke of?"
Elder Jin nodded. "Yes, you don't want to take this opportunity?"
Luo Zheng also stared at the woman.
As the decision-maker of the Nuwa Clan, her attitude determined everything.

Chapter 2196
For the exiles, being imprisoned here for an endless amount of time was the most terrible thing.
Time was the cheapest thing for them.
Even if Luo Zheng was really making things up, the exiles would still try to verify it. In any case, it was much better than hiding in the bone tower and doing nothing!
Besides, Old Devil Jin couldn't have specially brought Luo Zheng here to deceive her.
Luo Zheng secretly heaved a sigh of relief when he saw the woman's attitude.
Persuading these exiles was easier than he thought.
But it was also easy to understand. If it was Luo Zheng who had been imprisoned here for countless years, he wouldn't miss any small opportunity.
"That's straightforward," Luo Zheng smiled slightly.
The woman stared at Luo Zheng calmly and said, "In fact, many years ago, I was sure that the prison built by the ancient Gods of Chaos was absolutely perfect. There were no loopholes at all. All efforts were in vain. The only way to leave this world should be in the hands of the warden's family, but those guys are stubborn."
Saying so, she shrugged her thin shoulders and smiled helplessly.
The warden's family was like ghosts, occasionally patrolling quietly. No matter who tried to communicate with them, there was no result.
The woman had once set up a bewitching array, and even used her body to entice them, but to no avail. After various attempts, the Nuwa family could only choose to give up.
But this time, Luo Zheng told her that there was a conflict within the warden's family, and he even found two members of the warden's family!
This made her realize that this opportunity was extraordinary.
No wonder Old Devil Jin attached so much importance to it, and even personally brought Luo Zheng here.
The woman thought for a moment, then asked, "The brother and sister you mentioned, are they safe now?"
"They should be safe," Luo Zheng nodded.
"They can't enter the bone tower. When the Light of Judgment descends, they will undoubtedly die. We must act quickly," the woman said with a frown.
If it weren't for the Light of Judgment, which only appeared once in a thousand years, they would have a lot of time to plan this matter.
However, many things were often so coincidental. Thus, opportunities often flashed by. Whether or not one could seize them depended on one's luck …
"The fewer people who know about this, the better. I hope that the Nuwa Clan, the Xuan Mi Clan, the Star Deer Clan, and the other top-ranked clans will join us. However … the Xuanyuan Clan is an exception!"
The Xuanyuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan had always been fighting fiercely.
The conflict between the two clans was deeply rooted in their bloodlines. Elder Jin was naturally unwilling to leave with the Xuanyuan Clan.
The woman smiled. "That's your decision. I don't care."
When the Xuanyuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan fought, the Nuwa Clan had always been neutral. That was how the three major clans in this world maintained such a subtle relationship.
Then, the news was spread out by the Nuwa Clan, and it was secretly spread in the worlds at the bottom of the Chaos.
If Elder Jin were to take Luo Zheng to persuade those big clans, the effect would be greatly reduced.
However, when it came from the woman's mouth, the situation was naturally different. The exiles of the various large clans all responded with almost no hesitation.
One month later …
At the edge of the Eternal Wasteland.
A few scavengers lowered their heads and poked at the eerie white bones on the ground, hoping to find one or two desolate bones.
Although the Eternal Wasteland was almost dried up, there would always be one or two fish that escaped the net.
At that moment, spatial ripples spread out from the sky.
From the spatial ripples, figures stepped out and silently stood in the sky.
These people were all exiles of this world. They came from various major clans and all of them had their own bloodline inheritance.
The scavengers below all looked up at the sky and all of them had their mouths wide open.
They did not know that there were people in this world who could travel through the void and come and go as they pleased. In their eyes, the most powerful beings were those Desolate Gods that could transform into hundreds of feet tall.
Not far away, in the Wrought Iron Tribe …
Xiao Yun made a mark on a domino and calculated the time when Luo Zheng left.
Luo Zheng had left the Wrought Iron Tribe for almost a year. She did not know how he was doing now.
After absorbing the desolate bones left behind by Luo Zheng, Xiao Yun's cultivation level had increased by quite a bit. At the very least, it allowed her to have a firm foothold in the Wrought Iron Tribe.
However, Xiao Yun still had a huge hidden worry in her heart. If Luo Zheng was unable to bring her and Fu Chu into the bone pagoda within two years, they would completely disappear from this world.
"It is very difficult for ordinary people to join ordinary sects. However, Luo Zheng … he will not disappoint us." Xiao Yun stared at Fu Chu, who was silently cultivating not far away, and thought to herself.
At that moment, Xiao Yun suddenly felt an intense aura enveloping her.
That aura was very complicated, as though it was emitted by dozens of powerful beings.
Xiao Yun did not react immediately and was shocked. Fu Chu, who was not far away, also opened his eyes and looked at his sister with a frightened expression.
"Fu Chu, stay here and don't move. I will go out and take a look!"
After Xiao Yun said that, she rushed out of the straw hut.
How could Fu Chu be at ease with his sister? Fu Chu had never felt such an imposing aura before. How could such a powerful existence be stopped by her elder sister? He immediately followed her out.
The Wastelanders in the Wrought Iron Tribe were also in a panic.
These tribes were easily attacked by the surrounding factions. Not to mention those powerful sects, even some small factions in the cities could bring them disaster. The lives of these Wastelanders were as light as a feather.
"These people …"
When Xiao Yun saw the group of people in the sky, her eyes opened wide on her delicate and pretty face.
She could not sense the cultivation level of this group of people at all. However, this group of people was able to give her an intense pressure just by standing there motionlessly. This could only mean that their cultivation level had reached a level that she could not comprehend.
Fu Chu also followed her out and asked softly, "Sister, who are these people?"
"Why did you come out?" Xiao Yun stared at the sky and said to her brother in a reproachful tone.
Fu Chu smiled bitterly and said, "Is it safe to hide in the straw hut?"
If it was a blessing, it would not be a disaster. If it was a disaster, it could not be avoided. Fu Chu was more carefree than his sister.
Just as the two of them were bewildered, another spatial fluctuation came from the sky. Luo Zheng quietly came out from it.
When Xiao Yun saw Luo Zheng, her mouth opened wide and her fists clenched tightly.
At that moment, Xiao Yun only had one thought. Luo Zheng had succeeded. He had done it faster than she had expected!
In mid-air …
The Nuwa Race woman asked faintly, "Who are the siblings you mentioned?"
Luo Zheng looked around and saw Fu Chu and Xiao Yun at the entrance of the tribe.

Chapter 2197
After all, Xiao Yun was from the warden's clan. Although her cultivation wasn't high, her knowledge and experience were incomparable to that of ordinary people.
Therefore, she wasn't too flustered at the moment.
She already understood that Luo Zheng had brought the exiles here …
According to Xiao Yun's plan, Luo Zheng would first enter a large organization, establish a firm foothold, and then bring the sisters into the bone pagoda to temporarily hide.
After avoiding this round of judgment light, they would then plan their escape.
After all, Xiao Yun also knew that it was extremely difficult to enter the bone pagoda, let alone come into contact with the exiles.
She simply didn't expect that Luo Zheng would be able to come into contact with the exiles in such a short time, and gather so many of them in such a short time!
In fact, the exiles had almost all turned out for this operation.
This was probably the largest movement of the exiles in millions of years.
For this operation, they had gone through countless analyses and had adopted the best plan.
These exiles had never directly confronted the warden's clan, so they didn't know the true strength of the warden's clan.
After a discussion, Old Ghost Jin, Ming Wei, and the others of the Nuwa clan came to a decision. They would gather their strongest forces and first take away the "siblings" that Luo Zheng had mentioned.
For the exiles, this rare opportunity was worth it.
The premise was that Luo Zheng's words were true.
If Luo Zheng had deceived so many exiles, it was easy to imagine what would happen to him.
"You, you succeeded … I didn't expect you to bring so many people," Xiao Yun stared blankly at Luo Zheng.
Luo Zheng smiled faintly, "It can't be helped. This was their decision. They felt that if they brought you into the bone pagoda, they wouldn't be able to avoid the eyes and ears of the warden's clan, so they might as well face them head-on!"
Hearing this, Xiao Yun tilted her head and thought for a moment, then smiled faintly, "Your thoughts are so complicated!"
However, this was indeed a possibility.
The warden's clan might not be powerful, but they had the authority to control this prison. It really wasn't that easy to fight against them.
Whoosh …
A white figure flew down from the sky and landed in front of Xiao Yun.
It was the woman of the Nuwa clan, the most powerful exiled in this world.
Luo Zheng already knew that her name was Ming Wei.
Whether it was the Nuwa Clan, the outsiders, or even the exiles, they all addressed her as Sect Master Mingwei.
As it turned out, the Nuwa Clan had thousands of powerful 'leaders' in their mother world, and Ming Wei was one of them!
Ming Wei, Elder Jin, and a few exiles from the other races also descended one after another.
These exiles had been holding back for some time ever since they heard the news. Now was the time when they were the most nervous.
If it was proven that Luo Zheng was "lying" or that the siblings were lying, their hopes would be completely dashed, which was something they could not accept.
Even Ming Wei's beautiful face had a hint of nervousness. She sized up Xiao Yun and asked, "How can you prove that you're from the warden's family?"
"I can't prove it," Xiao Yun replied.
Hearing this, the expressions of the exiles changed, as if their hopes had been dashed in an instant.
However, Xiao Yun added, "My brother can prove it."
Hence, everyone's gaze turned towards Fu Chu, who was behind Little Yun.
Seeing so many Nirvana Realm powerhouses staring at him, Fu Chu looked a little nervous. He nodded at the crowd and said, "I can indeed prove it."
"Prove it to me." Ming Wei's calmness had a hint of authority that could not be disobeyed.
Fu Chu nodded and walked in front of Ming Wei. Then, he crossed his index finger and thumb, and a light brown light screen appeared in the middle.
Although the light screen was still in a state of chaos.
However, the exiles' eyes suddenly lit up.
These exiles were all powerful beings who had crossed over to the Nirvana Realm. Their understanding of energy was far beyond that of ordinary Aragami and True Gods. Just by looking at the energy emitted from the light screen, they could already sense that it was extraordinary.
After the light screen slowly became clear, Fu Chu said to Ming Wei, "Look, these are the curse chains behind you!"
Ming Wei looked at the light screen, and Elder Jin and the other exiles also squeezed forward.
In the light screen, they saw little golden worms with their heads and tails connected, spreading far away.
Ming Wei took a deep breath. "I've always been able to sense the existence of such a chain, but today is the first time I've seen it …"
One of the exiles saw the curse chain that bound him through Fu Chu. A reddish-gold lightning burst out from his hand, and the lightning turned into an electric blade that kept jumping. The electric blade contained an extremely terrifying power, as if it could destroy the world.
"Crack!"
The Exiled Outcast swung at the Curse Chain without hesitation.
Unfortunately, the two types of energy did not interact at all. The electric blade seemed to have slashed at nothing. From Fu Chu's light screen, the curse chain that bound the exiles did not move at all.
Fu Chu smiled faintly. "It's impossible to cut the curse chain with such a method."
The exiles nodded expressionlessly. How could he not know that the curse chain was not that easy to cut? It was just that this was the first time he saw it, and he could not help but want to try it.
"Is there a way to cut it?" Ming Wei looked at Fu Chu. Her expression had become much more amiable, and the smile on her face was somewhat charming.
"There is indeed a way." Fu Chu blinked and smiled. He could not help but feel proud to be surrounded by so many strong people at such a young age. He then said, "In our warden family, there are about thirty people who can cut the curse chain, and I'm one of them!"
"Then can you cut it now?" Elder Jin asked immediately.
Fu Chu shrugged his shoulders exaggeratedly. "Cutting the curse chain requires a special place, and my current cultivation level is not high enough!"
"Cultivation level? You want to cultivate to become a True God? "Ming Wei frowned.
She did not sense the power of the Rage God in Fu Chu's body, and at the same time, she sensed that he was trying to condense his quintessential essence.
If he cultivated to become a Rage God, it would be very simple. Desolate bones and even bloodline desolate bones were not worth mentioning to the exiles. However, if he wanted to cultivate a True God, it would be somewhat difficult …
"Yes." Fu Chu nodded. "But my progress is very average."
After they defected, they had been wandering around. How could they have the chance to cultivate?
Even after settling down in the Wrought Iron Tribe, Luo Zheng taught him a set of quintessential essence cultivation methods. However, this world was not suitable for quintessential essence cultivation. Even if Fu Chu's talent was good, his current cultivation level was far from becoming a True God warrior.
At this moment, Elder Jin and the other exiles focused their attention on Ming Wei.
Ming Wei pursed her lips and smiled faintly. "Although I have never taken in disciples, leave this matter to me!"

Chapter 2198
Since the ancient gods could not cross the Nirvana Realm due to the Desolate Body, the living beings in the mother world were immersed in it for a long time.
This meant that there was no way for those who came after them to ascend.
However, in that era, Nuwa appeared out of nowhere and comprehended the cultivation method of the great world.
She relied on the Qi of Chaos to open up a world within her body and reproduce countless living beings within the world within her body to form a large world before developing a divinity. She actually relied on such methods to cross the Nirvana Realm without relying on the Rage God's body, and it shocked the entire Mother World for a time.
Since then, the people in the mother world seemed to have discovered a new continent. They continuously explored the cultivation method of the great world, adding bricks and tiles to this path and bringing it to greater heights.
As Nuwa opened up the inner world and created countless people in it, the saying of Nuwa creating humans was passed down …
Therefore, the Nuwa Clan was not only the founder of the cultivation method of the great world, but they were also the best among them.
If they wanted to quickly increase Fu Chu's cultivation, Sect Master Ming Wei was the most suitable person to do it.
Hearing Sect Master Ming Wei's words, Fu Chu revealed a joyful smile.
This pair of siblings carried a great hatred. Their parents and close relatives were all killed by the Conservative Faction of the warden's clan.
Then, they wandered from place to place and curled up in this desolate land. The pain in their hearts was indescribable.
Now, he felt like he had endured through it all!
"Thank you, Master." Fu Chu was about to kneel down.
Ming Wei raised her eyebrows slightly, and Fu Chu could no longer kneel. He heard Ming Wei calmly say, "I don't accept disciples. You can follow me to cultivate, but I can't guarantee that I will give you too much time. You need to hurry!"
In the eyes of the exiles, Fu Chu had become their hope to leave this world.
No matter what Fu Chu needed, they would give it to him at all costs. However, he had to rely on himself for something like cultivation. There was nothing they could do about it.
"Yes." Fu Chu smiled and nodded.
"Don't be happy too early." Ming Wei said expressionlessly, "If you can't meet the requirements, I will use a secret technique to forcefully increase your cultivation. The price might be your life."
Hearing this, Fu Chu's face instantly revealed a bitter smile.
These exiles were not to be trifled with. In order to leave this world, they would do anything.
However, since Little Yun and Fu Chu had chosen this path, they were already mentally prepared. Even if it meant asking a tiger for its skin, they wouldn't hesitate.
"I'll bring this pair of siblings into the Barbarian Water Bone Pagoda." Ming Wei said to the other exiles, "Everyone knows how important this opportunity is to us. Please be on your guard. If there are any strange movements, please report to me immediately!"
With that, Ming Wei lightly tapped her toes on the ground.
Rainbow-like ripples spread out and enveloped Fu Chu and Little Yun.
Xiao Yun also knew that she had no choice now. She waved goodbye to Luo Zheng. Before Luo Zheng could react, the three of them disappeared in front of everyone.
The other exiles also used their own methods to travel through the void.
Only the Desolate Folks of the Wretched Iron Tribe were left standing there in a daze.
…
…
All the exiles carried an invisible curse chain on their backs.
This curse chain extended all the way to the farthest reaches of this world …
After passing through those bone pagodas and large swathes of the Eternal Wasteland, it gathered in an area of absolute darkness.
If Fu Chu used his talent to observe here, he would see millions or even tens of millions of curse chains gathered together. In this darkness, it was as if there was a monster with infinite tentacles.
After passing through the darkness, those curse chains were firmly fixed on the high platforms.
Occasionally, a new curse chain would appear on the high platforms. That meant that a new life was born in this world and a brand new curse chain would be connected to it.
On one of the rusty high platforms.
Two figures in long black robes stood there.
The black robes seemed to wrap the two of them from head to toe. Even their faces could not be seen.
If an exiled saw this scene, they would know that they were from the Warden Race.
All these years, the Warden Race had always used this image to patrol the bone pagodas.
It was just that they used a special method to send down illusory figures. The exiles were helpless against them.
"What a huge commotion. These exiles are getting more and more arrogant." A slightly hoarse voice came from the black robe. Behind the robe, a strand of bright purple hair gently fluttered.
"Even if they were lucky and found the two traitors, they wouldn't go so far as to come out in full force, right?" The other person echoed.
"This should be Sect Leader Ming Wei's idea. She's smart," the purple-haired man in black said indifferently. "She probably deduced that she could not hide the two traitors, so she gathered all the exiles. This way, she's forcing us to take action."
"These prisoners, do they really think they're so powerful? If they're forced into a corner, they'll settle everything in this world. None of them will survive, "the other person sneered.
"It's not time yet. Besides, settling everything is not a good thing for us," the purple-haired man in black said.
"Then what should we do?" the other person asked.
The purple-haired man in black said indifferently, "As far as I know, not all the exiles are on her side. For example, the Xuan Yuan Clan."
"You mean to use the Xuan Yuan Clan to fight against them? The Xuan Yuan Clan is not weak, but I don't think they'll be a match for the alliance of these large clans, right? "the other person asked doubtfully.
In terms of strength, the Xuan Yuan Clan was only on par with the Chi You Clan. There was still a big gap between them and the Nuwa Clan.
Moreover, the Nuwa Clan had joined forces with more than eighty percent of the large clans. The Xuan Yuan Clan alone was not enough.
"Indeed, the Xuan Yuan Clan alone is not enough. However, are the exiles who follow the Nuwa Clan really united?" the purple-haired man in black asked.
"What do you mean?"
"If I give some of them a chance to leave this world, do you think they'll be tempted?" the purple-haired man in black said indifferently.
The Warden Race controlled a lot of authority in this prison. The purple-haired man in black could decide who was free.
Fu Chu was one of them. If it were not for the conflict between the Conservatives and the Liberals, he would have started to gain some authority.

Chapter 2199
Just before the thousand-year time limit, all the major forces in this world began to make their moves.
The ninth rib of the Chaos Elder God, the North-Leaning Bone Tower.
This bone tower was not like the other bone towers that were vertical. Instead, it was slanted at a sixty-degree angle toward the north.
The North-Leaning Bone Tower was a relatively intact bone tower. There was not much space for hiding in it.
However, in such a small bone tower, there were four clans with bloodlines. One could imagine how limited the development space of these clans was.
The exiles among them were not strong enough to fight against the major clans. Naturally, they could not fight for a new bone tower.
More than ten thousand years ago, the four major clans in the North-Leaning Bone Tower had planned to take the Heavenly South Bone Tower. They hoped to trade with the Xuanyuan and Chiyou clans so that they could at least give them some space to enter the Heavenly South Bone Tower.
However, such a suggestion was ignored by the Xuanyuan and Chiyou clans …
As the number of clan members increased, the North-Leaning Bone Tower became more and more crowded.
Most of the time, the clan members moved around outside the bone tower. Only when the thousand-year time limit was up and the Judgment Light descended would they enter the bone tower to hide.
Over the past few years, the clan members had returned one after another. Naturally, it was very crowded.
At this moment, in the depths of the North-Leaning Bone Tower, there was a reddish-gold pillar engraved with a wolf, a lion, a crane, and an ape.
This was the symbol of the four major clans. The four major clans were the Spirit Wolf God Clan, the Celestial Lion Clan, the Hidden Crane Clan, and the Dark Ape Clan.
Below the golden pillar, a grey-robed exiled walked around and asked calmly, "Do you know what happened recently?"
Around this grey-robed man stood eight exiles. These eight exiles were the pillars of the four major clans.
Their strength was below average among the exiles. They could not fight against the exiles of the major clans. However, they were much stronger than the Aragami.
"I heard that the Nuwa Clan is doing something. They have joined forces with the Chiyou, Xuan Mi, Star Deer, and other major clans. However, I don't know what they are doing," a red-haired exiled exiled answered.
"These major clans are not very close to each other. Why are they suddenly joining forces?" another exiled exiled asked.
"Other than leaving this world, what else is worth their attention …"
The gray-robed Exile nodded and sighed. "Yes, it's said that they found a way to leave this world. Unfortunately, they didn't plan to bring us along …"
"Isn't it impossible to leave this prison? They are delusional! "The Red Bandit said hatefully.
Although they did not hold any grudges against the Nuwa Clan or the Chiyou Clan, they were still furious when they heard the news.
After all, they were all imprisoned in this world and couldn't wait to leave. What the Nuwa Clan was doing now made them feel like they had been abandoned!
"That's right, it's wishful thinking. We've tried so many times in the past, but we've never succeeded. This time, they're destined to fail again!" The other exiles echoed.
At this moment, the grey-robed exile said indifferently, "But it's said that they found two members of the warden's family. Moreover, these two people are traitors."
"What?!"
"How is this possible?!"
"The Warden Clan has never shown themselves. This must be a rumor!"
The exiles were shocked.
They did not want to believe it.
At the same time, they felt extremely disappointed. After all, they were willing to join the Nuwa Clan, but the Nuwa Clan looked down on them.
"Sigh, perhaps it's true this time." The grey-robed exile let out a long sigh.
The Nuwa Clan had made such a big fuss this time that they no longer planned to keep it a secret. Sect Master Ming Wei's actions were a disguised declaration of war against the Warden Clan.
"Do we really have no chance of joining them?"
"At the very least, we can do our part!"
"…"
Just as the exiles were discussing this, a figure descended from the sky.
A dark figure quietly descended into the northern bone pagoda. The black figure drifted over and immediately attracted the attention of the exiles.
"It's the Warden Clan." One of the exiles' face darkened.
None of the exiles welcomed these dark-skinned fellows.
Over the years, the Warden Clan had increased the frequency of patrols in the bone tower. They would patrol once every six months to a year.
They did not expect them to come again today.
Normally, these wardens would quickly leave after patrolling for a while. They never said a word, nor did they reveal their true faces.
However, this time, the Warden Clan member floated over to the exiles and slowly hovered in front of them.
All of the exiles raised their heads and looked puzzled. They did not know what this black figure wanted to do …
A short while later, the dark figure spoke abruptly, and the words he spoke caused the exiles to be tempted!
"Fellow exiles who have been imprisoned here, do you wish to obtain a chance to leave this world?"
At the same time …
In the Xuan Yuan Clan's Evil Mountain Bone Tower.
Due to the topography of the Evil Mountain Bone Tower, it formed a mountain of bones that resembled a pyramid. This was how the Evil Mountain Bone Tower got its name.
A young man sat at the top of this mountain of bones.
This man looked to be less than thirty years old. However, his slightly opened eyes revealed a sense of vicissitudes.
He was the most powerful exiled of the Xuan Yuan Clan, Yan Lai.
In front of Yan Lai, there was also a black figure floating. This black figure asked Yan Lai the same question in a low voice, "Do you want a chance to leave this world?"
Yan Lai's face was as calm as a still ancient well. However, his heart was pounding violently.
Yan Lai already knew what had happened to the Chiyou Clan and the Nuwa Clan. His Xuan Yuan Clan and the Chiyou Clan had always been at odds. It was the same here and in the mother world. Now that he had found a chance to leave this prison, the Xuan Yuan Clan naturally had no part in it.
However, Yan Lai would not give up. Given his character, no matter how powerful the Nuwa Clan was, his Xuan Yuan Clan would definitely stir up trouble. He would either take the Xuan Yuan Clan with him or leave with no one.
However, Yan Lai did not expect that the Warden Clan would take the initiative to show up. This was also the first time in countless years that they had communicated with him!

Chapter 2200
The black figure replied, "It's simple. Just kill two of them."
"You mean that pair of siblings?" Yan Hai asked with a smirk.
"Yes," the black figure replied.
The Xuanyuan Clan's intelligence network was much more powerful than the other clans. They knew more about the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan.
Moreover, the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan did not hide much about this matter. It was not difficult to find out.
Yan Hai looked at the black figure with interest and said, "So you're really afraid. That means that the pair of siblings is so important that you have no choice but to act."
The black figure was silent for a moment before replying, "Yes."
These exiles were extraordinarily intelligent. They only needed the slightest detail to discover the truth. It was too difficult to deceive them.
Therefore, the black figure admitted it directly.
Yan Hai pursed his lips and said, "I'm afraid the price is too high for me. After all, the Nuwa Clan and the Chiyou Clan should be doing their best to protect the pair of siblings. It will be very difficult to eradicate them. If I choose to join them, the Chiyou Clan will not be happy. However, if I pay a certain price, it won't be a big problem."
Yan Hai kept an eye on the black figure as he spoke.
Although Yan Hai could not see the black figure's expression, he could still guess what the black figure was thinking.
"The Xuanyuan Clan is indeed qualified to join them. However, even if you join them, it's impossible for you to leave this world," the black figure said. "I know you're testing me, but let me tell you this. Even if they find us through the pair of siblings and try to sever the curse chain, we'll wipe out this world. When that time comes, not only them, but also you and your Xuanyuan Clan will not be able to survive, even if you hide in the bone tower."
Yan Hai raised his eyebrows when he heard this.
He was trying to determine whether the black figure was telling the truth or not.
If the warden's clan could really do that, he would definitely not join the Nuwa Clan. He did not want to die with them.
However, if it was a lie …
This was a dilemma.
Then again, no matter which choice he made, it was easier than leaving this world.
Just as Yan Hai was considering, the black figure continued, "If you agree, the warden's clan will also help you behind the scenes. Apart from us, I believe there are many exiles who are willing to follow you."
The Nuwa Clan had only invited the top clans.
In addition to those large clans, there were also many smaller clans that were ranked at the bottom. Although these smaller clans were not that strong, they had quite a number of exiles. If they joined forces, they would be a considerable force.
Besides, the warden's family would be standing behind them.
Perhaps the warden's family would not choose to participate in the war directly, but this world was under their control, and they would certainly provide great assistance.
After pondering for a long time, Yan Hai's eyes flashed with determination. "I agree."
To be honest, Yan Hai might not hesitate if it was just the Chiyou Clan, but if the Nuwa Clan and a few other major clans were added into the mix, he was still very hesitant.
However, this opportunity was too important. He had to seize it.
In the other bone towers of this world, or in some hidden places, the wardens were persuading the exiles.
At first, it was difficult for the warden's family to convince them. After all, who would dare to go against those big families that were ranked at the top?
Therefore, the answers given by the exiles were all ambiguous. Their first choice was still to join the Nuwa Clan.
However, after the Xuanyuan Clan nodded, the weaker exiles quickly responded. After all, there was a big clan like the Xuanyuan Clan taking the lead. They just had to follow behind …
In the Nuwa Clan.
In the center of a huge hexagonal plaza, faint red ribbons of light formed a complicated pattern.
In the center of this pattern, Fu Chu was hanging upside down in midair. His eyes were tightly shut, and he did not move at all.
Above him was a huge shadow of Nuwa, holding him in its hands.
Ming Wei and Elder Jin had an agreement.
They set the time for two years later, after the Judgment Light descended.
Before that, Ming Wei was in charge of helping Fu Chu open up his inner world and doing her best to improve his cultivation.
"Buzz buzz buzz …"
As the huge shadow of Nuwa began to flicker, a powerful energy gathered in Fu Chu's dantian.
This energy was too violent. If his weak dantian were to withstand the impact of this energy, it would probably shatter into pieces …
Fu Chu, who had his eyes closed and had a peaceful expression, suddenly opened his eyes. His handsome face was twisted in pain, and his hands were waving like a drowning man.
Ming Wei looked at Fu Chu, and a look of approval appeared on her face. This child's talent was better than she had imagined.
"Fu Chu …"
Seeing her brother in such pain, Xiao Yun's face was filled with worry.
"It's alright. The Gatekeeper is doing it herself. Nothing will happen," a Nuwa Aragami comforted her.
"But the normal cultivation method is not this painful," Xiao Yun asked with a frown.
Inside the warden's family, there were also true gods who cultivated. Xiao Yun did not have the opportunity to do so back then. However, from what she had seen and heard, she knew that opening up a Divine Ocean was not that painful.
"It's because there's not enough time." The Aragami's eyes were sparkling as she stared at Fu Chu. Her face was filled with envy. "The Gatekeeper is directly helping Fu Chu construct his world. Naturally, it will be more painful. Such treatment is something that many of our core disciples would wish for …"
The Aragami of the Nuwa Clan rarely received such treatment. Now, many of them were looking at Fu Chu with envy.
With that said, Xiao Yun was relieved.
The Nuwa Aragami continued, "The Gatekeeper has instructed that if you're willing, I can transplant a Nuwa Clan bloodline into you. At the same time, I can provide you with desolate bones and bloodline desolate bones. You can use this time to cultivate."
"Transplant a bloodline …" Xiao Yun was slightly stunned.
Xiao Yun, who had lived in the Eternal Wasteland, naturally knew how precious a bloodline was.
Just a single bloodline desolate bone could be exchanged for unimaginable resources. However, the prerequisite for devouring these bloodline desolate bones was to possess the bloodline.
Bloodlines were naturally rarer than bloodline desolate bones. Furthermore, she could obtain a Nuwa Clan bloodline, which was the most powerful bloodline in this world …
After some consideration, Xiao Yun still rejected the offer.
Although she wanted to become stronger, and although the Freedom Party that she belonged to had betrayed the warden's family, she still needed to preserve her bloodline.
